Moreover, we identify a previously unacknowledged factor: the main GSM-Symbolic dataset contains a systematically shifted distribution of larger integers in problem texts relative to GSM-Base (K-S statistic = 0.12, p < 0.001), contradicting the original authors' claims. Controlling for this large number effect accounts for significance in roughly half the remaining cases. Among models with statistically significant performance deltas, we identify distinct, model-specific failure profiles - including fragility of variable binding, arithmetic limitations, and dual-task interference - underscoring that blanket claims about LLM reasoning are both statistically premature and mechanistically misleading.

</details>


### [148] [Beyond Binary Moral Judgment: Modeling Ethical Pluralism in AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28707)
*Aisha Aijaz,Rahul Goel,Arnav Batra,Raghava Mutharaju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将道德推理建模为规范伦理学理论上的概率分布（伦理多元主义），通过双流规范-语义架构和堆叠集成学习，在包含450个案例、15个细分子理论的基准上实现了88.89%的分类准确率，证明了结构化伦理表示对AI道德推理的改进作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI道德决策方法多采用标量或二元判断，缺乏可解释性和上下文信息，无法支持责任归属。需要更完善的方法来处理自主系统的道德推理。

Method: 提出规范伦理学单纯形框架，构建450个案例（15个子理论）的基准数据集，采用双流规范-语义架构，融合上下文特征与规范先验，通过顺序堆叠集成学习来拟合后果主义、美德伦理学、义务论三大理论及其子类别。

Result: 集成上下文和规范先验与语义嵌入显著提升分类性能，准确率达88.89%。消融实验表明结构化伦理表示超越类比推理，堆叠架构因渐进学习粒度而效果最佳。

Conclusion: 将伦理多元主义建模为概率规范分布能够支持类人道德推理、伦理分歧分析和未来AI对齐。

Abstract: Critical decision-making in socially consequential spaces is increasingly involving AI systems at varying capacities. Yet, despite the ubiquity of autonomous systems, most approaches to handling autonomous moral decision-making resort to scalar or binary judgments. These methods are insufficient for acceptable moral reasoning, as they provide little explanation, leaving out imperative contextual and theoretical information that must be included to support accountability. For this, we propose a framework to model moral reasoning as a distribution over normative ethical theories or ethical pluralism. We introduce a normative ethics simplex that integrates these theories. A benchmark of 450 cases across 15 fine-grained subtheories was also prepared for the purposes of stacked ensemble learning. These cases describe ethical dilemmas in natural language and have associated extracted contextual features. The implementation of the simplex was achieved via a two-stream normative-semantic architecture. This is followed by the fusion of normative information and a sequential, stacking ensemble to learn the best fit of the three broad theories: consequentialism, virtue ethics, and deontology, and the 15 subcategories. Our experiments demonstrate that the integration of contextual and normative priors with the semantic embeddings significantly improves the performance of the classification, displaying an accuracy of 88.89%. We conducted ablation studies to show that structured ethical representations contribute beyond analogical reasoning, and the chosen stacking architecture gives the best results due to the gradual learning of granularity. Ethical pluralism is also analyzed through entropy, confidence, and visualization. Thus, modeling ethical pluralism as a probabilistic normative distribution supports human-like moral reasoning, ethical disagreement analysis, and future alignment in AI systems.

</details>


### [149] [Thinking as Compression: Your Reasoning Model is Secretly a Context Compressor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28713)
*Guoxin Ma,Yibing Liu,Chengzhengxu Li,Yu Liang,Yan Wang,Yueyang Zhang,Kecheng Chen,Zhaohan Zhang,Zhiyuan Sun,Daiting Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Thinking as Compression (TaC)范式，利用思维模型自身的推理能力压缩长上下文，无需专门的压缩模块；进一步通过奖励驱动优化框架TaC-C实现紧凑可控的压缩，在4倍和8倍压缩比下相比最强基线分别提升17.4%/23.4%的F1和15.7%/21.7%的EM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有上下文压缩方法依赖复杂的压缩模块或专门的压缩训练，未能充分挖掘LLM自身的内在压缩能力。

Method: 提出TaC范式，直接提示思维模型生成思维轨迹作为压缩后的上下文；进一步提出TaC-C，通过奖励驱动的优化框架对思维输出进行约束，实现紧凑且可控的压缩。

Result: 在四个长上下文QA基准上，4倍压缩比下F1和EM分别超过最强基线17.4%和15.7%；8倍压缩比下分别超过23.4%和21.7%。

Conclusion: 思维模型自身的推理过程可作为一种有效的上下文压缩方式，结合简单的奖励优化能取得显著优于现有方法的压缩效果。

Abstract: Context compression aims to shorten long context inputs with minimal information loss for LLM inference acceleration. While existing methods have shown promise, they typically rely on complex compression modules or compression-specific training, leaving the intrinsic capabilities of LLMs underexplored. In contrast, this work reveals that a thinking model itself can naturally compress long contexts by organizing task-relevant information. We thus derive Thinking as Compression (TaC), a new compression paradigm that treats thinking itself as compressed context. Without relying on specific dedicated compressor, TaC directly prompts the thinking model to generate thinking traces as the shortened context, already outperforming most representative compression methods. Further, given that raw thinking output may struggle with budget control and shortcut behaviors, we introduce Thinking as Compression Constrained (TaC-C), leveraging a simple reward-driven optimization framework to elicit intrinsic thinking as compact and controllable compressed context. Experiments across four long-context QA benchmarks demonstrate that TaC-C consistently outperforms existing baselines. At 4x and 8x compression ratios, it surpasses the strongest competitor by 17.4% and 23.4% in average F1, and by 15.7% and 21.7% in average Exact Match Score (EM), respectively.

</details>


### [150] [OpenURMA: A Clean-Room Open Implementation of the Unified Bus Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28717)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了OpenURMA，这是华为UB（统一总线）传输层和事务层的首个开源洁净实现。UB通过将每应用端点状态与每主机传输状态解耦、将排序机制改为可选、并利用本地CPU加载/存储直接访问远程内存，解决了现代RDMA中NIC成为瓶颈的问题（即RoCE/InfiniBand因PCIe往返和每连接状态膨胀导致的延迟和吞吐瓶颈）。OpenURMA在RTL、SystemC模拟器和gem5三个层级实现，并与RoCEv2 RC进行对比。在64字节远程读取操作中，UB负载/存储路径实现了约500 ns的端到端延迟，比RoCEv2 RC（2186 ns）低4.37倍，吞吐量高出2.80倍，且仅占用U50约14%的LUT资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据中心RDMA受限于网络接口而非物理线路：RoCE/InfiniBand NIC为每对（应用，远程端点）维护连接状态，在1024应用扇出时需数百MB状态；64字节操作需四次PCIe往返，导致延迟比线路延迟高出数量级。这两个问题均源于RDMA从InfiniBand继承的基于PCIe的队列对（Queue Pair）抽象。

Method: 华为UB（2025年公开规范）改变了抽象方式：将每应用端点状态与每主机传输状态解耦（连接上下文呈加性增长），将排序机制改为可选，并通过本地CPU加载/存储指令访问片上总线控制器来直接操作远程内存。OpenURMA是UB传输层和事务层的开源洁净实现，在三个层级实现：综合级RTL（Alveo U50）、周期级双节点SystemC模拟器、gem5全系统模拟框架，每个层级均有匹配的OpenRoCE（RoCEv2 RC）基线进行对比。

Result: 在64字节远程读取操作中（UB规范第8.3节的LOAD vs RoCEv2 RC的READ），UB负载/存储路径实现约500 ns端到端延迟，比RoCEv2 RC基线（2186 ns）低4.37倍；吞吐量高出2.80倍；仅占用Alveo U50约14%的LUT资源。

Conclusion: UB通过改变抽象层次（去PCIe队列对、解耦状态、原生load/store访问远程内存）从根本上解决了RDMA的NIC瓶颈问题。OpenURMA作为首个开源实现，提供了闭源芯片无法实现的受控对比，验证了UB在延迟、吞吐和资源效率方面的显著优势。

Abstract: Modern datacenter RDMA is bottlenecked at the network interface, not the wire. A NIC running RoCE or InfiniBand holds per-connection state for every (application, remote-endpoint) pair - hundreds of megabytes at 1024-application fanout - and pays a four-traversal PCIe round trip on a 64-byte operation, inflating latency an order of magnitude beyond the wire. Both follow from the Queue Pair over PCIe abstraction RDMA inherits from InfiniBand.
  Huawei's Unified Bus (UB), a public 2025 specification, changes the abstraction: it decouples per-application endpoint state from per-host transport state so connection context grows additively, exposes ordering as opt-in, and reaches remote memory through native CPU load/store to an on-chip-bus controller. UB ships in Huawei's closed Ascend 950 silicon.
  OpenURMA is the first clean-room open implementation of UB's transport and transaction layers, realised at three tiers - synthesisable RTL on Alveo U50, a cycle-level two-node SystemC simulator, and a gem5 full-system scaffold - each with a matched OpenRoCE (RoCEv2 RC) baseline. The contribution is the implementation, harness, and controlled comparison closed silicon does not admit. On the canonical 64-byte remote fetch - LOAD on UB-spec Sec.8.3, READ on RoCEv2 RC - UB's load/store path delivers ~500 ns end-to-end, 4.37x below the matched baseline (2186 ns), sustains 2.80x higher throughput, and fits in ~14% of a U50's LUTs.

</details>


### [151] [LiveBrowseComp: Are Search Agents Searching, or Just Verifying What They Already Know?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28721)
*HuiMing Fan,Xiao Wang,Zheng Chu,Qianyu Wang,Zhuoyao Wang,Ming Liu,Bing Qin,XingYu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM搜索代理是否真正进行检索还是仅依赖内在知识。通过三个诊断实验发现，代理存在"内在知识依赖"（IKD）问题：即使有工具可用，它们仍主要依赖模型预存信息而非外部证据。代理在不使用工具的情况下能回答BrowseComp中44.5%的问题，超过一半的搜索查询来自内部生成假设而非检索线索。为此，作者提出了LiveBrowseComp基准，包含335个依赖90天内发布事实的问题，所有代理在该基准上的闭卷准确率低于2%，搜索增强得分相比BrowseComp下降25-40分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索基准（如BrowseComp）无法区分LLM代理是真正通过检索发现新信息，还是仅仅利用模型已编码的内在知识进行记忆验证。

Method: 设计三个诊断实验检测代理的内在知识依赖，并构建LiveBrowseComp基准——包含335个依赖近90天内发布事实的众包问题，排除了全球性重大事件，来源覆盖六个更新渠道。

Result: 代理能无工具回答BrowseComp中44.5%的问题；超半数搜索查询来自内部假设而非检索线索；移除支持证据后性能低于闭卷基线。在LiveBrowseComp上，所有代理闭卷准确率低于2%，搜索得分下降25-40分，模型排名不再可靠。

Conclusion: 静态搜索基准会奖励记忆验证而非证据驱动发现，LiveBrowseComp通过时间约束和事件排除设计，能更有效评估代理的真实搜索能力。

Abstract: Are LLM-based search agents genuinely searching, or using the web to verify what they already know? We study this question on BrowseComp with three diagnostics. Our analysis reveals Intrinsic Knowledge Dependence (IKD): even with tool access, agents often rely on intrinsic knowledge -- information encoded in the model before retrieval -- rather than on external evidence. Agents answer up to 44.5% of BrowseComp questions without tools, generate more than half of their search queries from internally produced hypotheses rather than retrieved leads, and perform worse than closed-book baselines when answer-supporting evidence is removed. These results suggest that static search benchmarks can reward memory-backed verification rather than evidence-driven discovery, conflating what agents already know with what they can find. We then introduce LiveBrowseComp, a deep-search benchmark designed to evaluate agents beyond intrinsic coverage. It contains 335 human-authored questions whose answers depend on facts published within the 90 days preceding benchmark construction, drawn from six updated sources and filtered to exclude globally salient events. On LiveBrowseComp, all evaluated agents fall below 2% closed-book accuracy, search-augmented scores drop by 25-40 points relative to BrowseComp, and prior model rankings no longer reliably predict performance. LiveBrowseComp is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/Forival/LiveBrowseComp.

</details>


### [152] [Multi-Adapter Representation Interventions via Energy Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28722)
*Manjiang Yu,Hongji Li,Junwei Chen,Xue Li,Priyanka Singh,Yang Cao,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MARI方法，通过多适配器表征干预和能量校准门控模块，实现自适应调整干预方向与强度，在保持通用能力的同时提升大模型对齐性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表征干预方法对所有输入施加固定的统一干预，但不同样本所需的干预方向和强度差异显著，这种无差别干预会损害模型在良性输入上的通用能力。

Method: 提出MARI框架：1）竞争式多适配器机制，由多个专家捕获非线性校正模式，自适应确定不同样本的干预方向和强度；2）基于能量的门控模块，利用内部传播动力学区分适合干预的输入。

Result: 在多种模型家族和参数规模上，MARI在TruthfulQA、BBQ和安全基准测试中达到最先进的对齐性能，同时在MMLU和ARC等通用任务上保持甚至提升能力。

Conclusion: MARI通过自适应干预策略有效解决了统一干预导致的通用能力退化问题，实现了对齐性能与通用能力的双赢。

Abstract: Representation intervention has emerged as a promising paradigm for aligning large language models toward desired behaviors without modifying model weights. Existing methods typically apply a fixed intervention uniformly across all inputs. However, we find that the appropriate intervention direction and strength vary substantially across samples, and such indiscriminate intervention leads to degradation of general capabilities on benign inputs. To address these challenges, we propose Multi-Adapter Representation Interventions via Energy Calibration (MARI). Specifically, we introduce a competitive multi-adapter mechanism in which specialized experts capture non-linear correction patterns and adaptively determine the appropriate intervention direction and strength for different samples. Furthermore, we design an energy-based gating module that leverages internal propagation dynamics to distinguish inputs that are applicable for intervention. Extensive experiments across diverse model families and parameter scales demonstrate that MARI achieves state-of-the-art alignment performance. Our method significantly improves performance on TruthfulQA, BBQ, and safety benchmarks, while maintaining and even improving general capabilities on tasks such as MMLU and ARC. Our code is available at https://github.com/V1centNevwake/MARI.

</details>


### [153] [AlphaTransit: Learning to Design City-scale Transit Routes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28730)
*Bibek Poudel,Sai Swaminathan,Weizi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AlphaTransit，一种结合蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）与神经策略-价值网络的公交网络规划框架，用于解决城市公交线路设计中的延迟反馈和路由交互欺骗问题。在Bloomington基准测试中，AlphaTransit在混合和全需求场景下分别达到54.6%和82.1%的服务率，优于单独使用强化学习或MCTS的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公交线路网络设计问题（TRNDP）中，路线扩展决策的反馈延迟且具有欺骗性：局部有效的扩展可能造成换乘瓶颈、冗余重叠或降低整体通行能力。现有方法缺乏在构建过程中对后续设计质量的提前评估能力。

Method: 提出AlphaTransit框架，将MCTS与神经策略-价值网络耦合：策略网络推荐路线扩展方向，价值网络估计下游设计质量，搜索过程利用这些预测精化每一步决策，在无需模拟器滚动的情况下实现构建过程中的决策时前瞻（lookahead）。

Result: 在Bloomington公交网络基准测试中，AlphaTransit在混合和全需求场景下服务率分别达54.6%和82.1%；相比无搜索的强化学习提升9.9%和11.4%，相比无学习指导的MCTS提升2.5%和11.2%。

Conclusion: 将学习引导与MCTS相结合比单独使用任一方法在公交网络设计中更有效，代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: Designing a transit network requires many sequential route extension decisions, but their quality is often visible only after the full network is assembled. This delayed-feedback challenge lies at the heart of the Transit Route Network Design Problem (TRNDP), where route interactions can be deceptive: an extension that appears useful locally can create transfer bottlenecks, produce redundant overlap, or reduce overall throughput. To guide route construction under delayed simulator feedback, we introduce AlphaTransit, a search-based planning framework for cityscale bus network design. AlphaTransit couples Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) with a neural policy-value network: the policy proposes route extensions, the value estimates downstream design quality, and search uses these predictions to refine each decision. This provides decision-time lookahead during route construction without running simulator rollouts inside the search tree. We evaluate AlphaTransit on a new Bloomington TRNDP benchmark with realistic road topology and censusderived demand, under mixed and full transit demand settings. In the Bloomington network, AlphaTransit attains the highest service rate in both demand settings, reaching 54.6% and 82.1%, respectively. Relative to reinforcement learning without search, these correspond to 9.9% and 11.4% service rate gains; relative to MCTS without learned guidance, they correspond to 2.5% and 11.2% gains. These results suggest that coupling learned guidance with MCTS is more effective than using either approach alone for transit network design. Our code and data are publicly available in https://github.com/poudel-bibek/AlphaTransit.

</details>


### [154] [Utility-Aware Multimodal Contrastive Learning for Product Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28733)
*Xiaohang Feng,Yiling Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种效用感知的多模态对比学习框架，通过将消费者需求纳入Utility-Aware InfoNCE损失函数，使生成的产品图像在保持语义一致性的同时优化商业表现（如需求提升），在Amazon和Airbnb数据集上优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI模型虽能生成与文本提示语义对齐的图像，但未直接优化市场表现——语义对齐并不等同于图像能促进销售，存在关键差距。

Method: 提出Utility-Aware InfoNCE损失函数，将消费者需求信号融入多模态对比学习，优化图像-文本表示空间向需求驱动的视觉线索偏移，并通过理论界验证其有效性。

Result: 在Amazon和Airbnb上，生成的图像在提升需求、保持保真度和文本-图像一致性方面均优于SOTA模型；保留了美学、独特性等属性的倒U型需求模式；人工实验验证了商业有效性。

Conclusion: 该效用感知组件可灵活嵌入新兴生成模型，提升直接商业用途，并兼具理论基础和实践价值。

Abstract: Product images strongly influence consumer decision-making in online marketplaces. Empowered by multimodal contrastive learning, generative AI can output images that closely align with text prompts. Yet existing generative AI models do not directly optimize marketplace performance. This is a critical gap, since semantic alignment alone does not guarantee that an image will sell. To address this limitation, we propose a \textit{utility-aware multimodal contrastive learning} framework that incorporates consumer demand into a novel Utility-Aware InfoNCE loss. Optimizing this utility-aware objective guides generation toward images that are both semantically coherent and demand-enhancing. This effect arises directly from a shift in the learned image-text representation space toward demand-driven visual cues, which we also validate through the theoretical bound of the proposed objective. In downstream applications on Amazon and Airbnb, product images generated and edited by our method outperform state-of-the-art models in increasing demand and preserving fidelity, while maintaining text-image consistency. Notably, our utility-aware framework preserves inverse U-shaped demand patterns for attributes such as aesthetics and uniqueness, improving demand-based performance while preserving fidelity and semantic consistency. Human-subject experiments further validate its commercial effectiveness. As generative AI technology continues to evolve, our utility-aware component can be flexibly embedded into emerging generative models to improve direct commercial use.

</details>


### [155] [CORE: Contrastive Reflection Enables Rapid Improvements in Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28742)
*Linas Nasvytis,Simon Jerome Han,Ben Prystawski,Satchel Grant,Noah D. Goodman,Judith E. Fan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出对比反思（CORE），一种非参数学习算法，通过比较成功与失败的推理轨迹生成自然语言洞察，在更少的训练样本和模型 rollout 下实现比参数化（GRPO）和非参数化（GEPA、RAG、MemRL）方法更快的推理能力提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可验证奖励的推理改进方法（如RLVR、提示优化）通常需要数百个训练样本和数千次模型 rollout，成本高昂甚至难以实现。为降低样本和计算开销，需要更高效的推理自我改进方法。

Method: CORE（Contrastive Reflection）是一种非参数学习算法，通过对比成功与不成功的推理轨迹，生成短的自然语言洞察（insights），这些洞察描述了推理策略和约束条件，捕捉成功与失败尝试之间的差异。

Result: 在四个推理任务上，CORE在使用更少rollout的情况下，比GRPO（参数化）和GEPA、RAG、MemRL（非参数化）方法实现更快的性能提升。在固定rollout预算下，仅需5个训练样本，CORE即达到或超过各基线的性能。此外，CORE比非参数基线更节省上下文token，以紧凑可解释的自然语言存储知识。

Conclusion: 将成功与失败推理轨迹之间的对比蒸馏为抽象且有用的自然语言洞察，比权重更新、提示优化或直接复用推理轨迹，提供了一条更高效、更可解释的模型自我改进路径。

Abstract: Language models can use verifiable rewards to improve at a wide variety of reasoning tasks. However, both parametric (e.g. RLVR) and non-parametric (e.g. prompt optimization) approaches to doing so typically require hundreds of training samples and thousands of model rollouts, making them expensive in the best case and intractable in the worst. To address this challenge, we introduce Contrastive Reflection (CORE), a non-parametric learning algorithm that compares past reasoning traces to generate insights: short natural-language descriptions of reasoning strategies and constraints that capture differences between successful and unsuccessful problem attempts. Across four reasoning tasks, we demonstrate that CORE enables more rapid improvement than both parametric (GRPO) and non-parametric (GEPA, episodic RAG, and MemRL) methods, while using fewer rollouts. Under fixed rollout budgets with as few as five training samples, we then show that CORE also achieves comparable or greater performance gains than each baseline. Finally, we highlight how CORE is also substantially more context-efficient than non-parametric baselines, requiring fewer prompt tokens while storing learned knowledge as compact, interpretable natural-language insights. Our results therefore suggest that distilling contrasts between successful and unsuccessful reasoning traces into abstract and useful insights can provide a more efficient and interpretable route to model self-improvement than weight updates, prompt optimization, or direct reuse of stored reasoning traces.

</details>


### [156] [CubePart: An Open-Vocabulary Part-Controllable 3D Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28763)
*Yiheng Zhu,Kangle Deng,Jean-Philippe Fauconnier,Inaki Navarro,Daiqing Li,Ava Pun,Yinan Zhang,Peiye Zhuang,Xiaoxia Sun,Maneesh Agrawala,Kiran Bhat,Tinghui Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CubePart提出了一种开放词汇、部件可控的3D网格生成框架，能够根据全局文本提示和用户定义的部件名称列表，生成一组语义对齐的部件网格，并组装成完整对象，可直接用于游戏引擎和动画脚本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式3D模型要么生成整体网格，要么生成与特定应用需求无法对齐的任意部件分解，而交互式3D资产（如游戏、仿真）需要支持动画、物理和脚本化行为的语义部件分解。

Method: 提出可扩展的数据流水线构建大规模开放词汇、部件标注的3D数据集；设计两阶段生成架构，分离全局形状合成与部件级解码；支持在推理时通过用户定义的部件名称列表显式控制部件结构。

Result: 生成的资产可直接集成到游戏引擎中，由动画和行为脚本驱动，无需手动后处理。

Conclusion: CubePart实现了开放词汇、部件级别的可控3D生成，弥合了生成模型与下游应用需求之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Interactive 3D assets used in games and simulation are typically decomposed into specific semantic parts to support animation, physics, and scripted behaviors, yet most generative 3D models produce either monolithic meshes or arbitrary part decompositions that cannot be aligned with application-specific requirements. We present CubePart, a generative framework for open-vocabulary, part-controllable 3D mesh generation that exposes part structure as an explicit inference-time control signal. Given a global text prompt and a user-defined parts schema expressed as an open-ended list of part names, our method generates a set of meshes - one per schema element - that assemble into a coherent object while respecting the specified semantic structure. To enable this capability, we introduce a scalable data pipeline to construct a large open-vocabulary, part-labeled 3D dataset, along with a two-stage generative architecture that separates global shape synthesis from part-level decoding. We demonstrate that the resulting assets can be directly integrated into game engines and driven by animation and behavior scripts without manual post-processing. Project Page: https://cubepart.github.io/

</details>


### [157] [SwarmHarness: Skill-Based Task Routing via Decentralized Incentive-Aligned AI Agent Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28764)
*Edwin Jose*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SwarmHarness 是一个去中心化计算共享协议，通过 DHT 发现、效用路由和 Shapley 值积分激励，使节点自组织成计算集群，无需中心化协调或区块链，并可作为分布式 AI 代理网络的基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大量计算资源（GPU 周期）闲置浪费，因缺乏激励对齐的协议让资源拥有者安全、盈利地共享。现有方案要么需要可信中心协调（云市场），要么依赖繁重区块链基础设施（Golem、BrokerChain），要么缺乏激励层（BOINC、Petals）。

Method: SwarmHarness 包含三个组件：(1) SwarmRegistry——基于分布式哈希表(DHT)的节点发现与能力通告；(2) SwarmRouter——基于能力、负载、延迟和信任的效用函数进行任务调度；(3) SwarmCredit——基于 Shapley 值近似的计算积分激励机制。节点提供服务赚取积分，消耗积分提交任务，空闲节点因积分流失而失去路由优先级，形成自调节参与经济。

Result: 节点向高奖励技能专业化，路由信号起到数字信息素作用，网络展现出类似生物集群的涌现集体智能。该协议可作为分布式 AI 代理网络的基础原语，使代理无需人类中介即可雇佣计算、路由子任务和结算积分。

Conclusion: SwarmHarness 提供了一种无需中心权威的去中心化计算共享协议，通过积分经济实现自调节参与，并具备支撑自主分布式 AI 代理网络的潜力。

Abstract: Vast quantities of compute (GPU cycles on personal workstations, idle inference servers, and edge devices between jobs) go unused because no incentive-aligned protocol exists for their owners to share them safely and profitably. Existing approaches either require a trusted central coordinator (cloud marketplaces), demand heavy blockchain infrastructure (Golem, BrokerChain), or lack an incentive layer entirely (BOINC, Petals). We propose SwarmHarness, a decentralised protocol in which HarnessAPI skill nodes self-organise into a compute swarm without any central authority. SwarmHarness has three interlocking components: a SwarmRegistry built on a Distributed Hash Table (DHT) for peer discovery and capability advertisement; a SwarmRouter that dispatches tasks to nodes using a utility function over capability, load, latency, and trust; and SwarmCredit, an incentive mechanism that attributes compute-credit rewards to contributing nodes via a Shapley-value approximation. Nodes earn credits by serving tasks and spend credits to submit them; idle nodes that never contribute drain credits and lose routing priority, creating a self-regulating participation economy. As nodes specialise toward high-reward skills and routing signals act as digital pheromones, the network exhibits emergent collective intelligence analogous to biological swarms. Beyond compute sharing, SwarmHarness is a foundational primitive for autonomous distributed AI agent networks in which agents hire compute, route subtasks, and settle credits without human intermediation.

</details>


### [158] [CaMBRAIN: Real-time, Continuous EEG Inference with Causal State Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28792)
*Abhilash Durgam,Nyle Siddiqui,Jeffrey A. Chan-Santiago,Qiushi Fu,Elakkat D. Gireesh,Mubarak Shah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CaMBRAIN——首个基于Mamba的状态空间模型，用于因果、实时推理的脑电图信号。通过多阶段自监督训练流水线增强长程记忆保持，在3个EEG数据集上达到SOTA性能，吞吐量比现有模型高10倍以上，首次实现对可变长度EEG信号的长程连续推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法处理EEG信号面临两大挑战：(1)基于注意力的模型随序列长度呈二次方缩放；(2)定长输入要求导致滑窗处理，无法全局理解整段信号。需要一种能实现因果、实时、长程连续推理的EEG模型。

Method: 提出CaMBRAIN——基于Mamba的因果状态空间模型。采用多阶段自监督训练流水线，专门针对EEG信号特点设计，鼓励隐藏状态保持长程记忆，同时保持SSM的线性时间复杂度。

Result: 在3个不同EEG数据集上达到SOTA性能，吞吐量比现有模型高10倍以上，实现了首个能够对可变长度EEG信号进行长程连续推理的模型。

Conclusion: CaMBRAIN证明了因果状态空间模型在长程EEG信号实时推理中的有效性，克服了注意力机制的二次复杂度瓶颈和定长输入限制，为临床EEG分析提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is a critical, non-invasive method to monitor electrical brain activity. EEGs can span anywhere from a couple seconds to multiple hours, posing a major hurdle for existing deep learning methods due to two major factors: (1) existing EEG models are predominantly built upon the attention mechanism, incurring quadratic scaling as the sequence length increases, and (2) raw EEG signals must be processed in a sliding-window fashion due to fixed-length input requirements, preventing global understanding of the entire signal. To this extent, we propose CaMBRAIN - the first Causal, Mamba-based state space model (SSM) capable of real-time inference of EEG signals, arguing that bidirectional approaches are needlessly expensive given the causal, unidirectional nature of EEG. However, training such a model is non-trivial, as crucial EEG events can be extremely brief - within fractions of a second - yet separated by long intervals spanning minutes. Current EEG methods use self-supervised objectives that optimize for signal reconstruction, but these are not well suited for streaming SSMs; they fail to explicitly train the hidden state to retain the salient long-range context needed for streaming inference. We therefore introduce a multi-stage self-supervised training pipeline specifically tailored to encourage long-range memory retention and strong performance on EEG signals, while preserving the linear-time complexity of state space models. CaMBRAIN achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) results across 3 different EEG datasets with >10x higher throughput than existing models, enabling the first model capable of long-range, continuous inference of variable-length EEG signals.

</details>


### [159] [Calibrating Conservatism for Scalable Oversight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28807)
*William Overman,Mohsen Bayati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出校准集体监督（CCO）方法，通过聚合多样辅助评分函数形成偏离保守基线的惩罚，并利用共形决策理论在线校准保守度，在保证违反率低于用户指定阈值的同时，实现对超越人类能力的自主AI系统的可扩展监督。在SWE-bench和MACHIAVELLI实验上，较弱监督者成功约束了对抗性误导的更强智能体，伦理违规率显著降低且与理论预测一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主规划与扩展环境交互的智能AI系统带来了根本性控制问题：人类如何在系统可能超越自身能力的情况下保持有意义的监督？现有可扩展监督方法依赖复杂假设、多为启发式或缺乏序贯场景下带统计保证的实用方法。

Method: 提出校准集体监督（CCO），聚合多样辅助评分函数为偏离保守基线的惩罚；受可获取效用保留启发，实现集体保守主义——动作面临与监督者关注度成正比的惩罚，高效用动作在监督者无异议时仍被选择，仅在关注累积时被覆盖；利用共形决策理论在线校准保守度，在无分布假设下提供有限时间界限保证。

Result: 在修改版SWE-bench上，较弱监督者成功约束了对抗性误导的更强智能体；在MACHIAVELLI上，CCO显著降低伦理违规同时保持奖励。两种设置下经验违规率与指定目标高度吻合，与理论预测一致。

Conclusion: CCO提供了一种实用的、带统计保证的可扩展监督方法，使人类（或较弱监督者）能够有效约束超越自身能力的AI系统，且理论违规率保证得到实验验证。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems capable of autonomous planning and extended environmental interaction pose a fundamental control problem: how can humans maintain meaningful oversight of systems that may exceed their own capabilities? Existing approaches to scalable oversight rely on complex assumptions, remain largely heuristic, or lack practical methods for sequential settings with statistical guarantees. We introduce Calibrated Collective Oversight (CCO), which aggregates diverse auxiliary scoring functions into a penalty measuring deviation from a conservative baseline. Inspired by Attainable Utility Preservation, CCO enables collective conservatism: actions face a penalty proportional to overseer concern, so high-utility actions are still selected when overseers find them unobjectionable and overridden only when concern accumulates. CCO calibrates this conservatism online using Conformal Decision Theory, ensuring that undesirable outcomes remain below a user-specified target threshold with finite-time bounds and no distributional assumptions. On a modified version of SWE-bench, weaker overseers successfully constrain an adversarially misaligned stronger agent; on MACHIAVELLI, CCO substantially reduces ethical violations while preserving reward. In both settings, empirical violation rates closely match the specified targets, as predicted by the theory.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Economic Nonlinear Model Predictive Control for Microgrids with Generator Up and Downtime Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27553)
*Jürgen Gutekunst,Armin Nurkanovic,Ekaterina Kostina,Hans Georg Bock,Robert Scholz,Amer Mesanovic*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对含离散决策变量（发电机运行时间与启动成本）的多阶段最优潮流问题，提出用凸二次近似替代非线性AC潮流方程，将混合整数非线性规划简化为混合整数二次约束规划，并通过附加时间耦合约束来保证NMPC方案的递归可行性与稳定性，在6节点微电网仿真中验证了效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经济非线性模型预测控制在多阶段最优潮流问题中取得进展，但引入发电机运行时间和启动成本等离散决策变量后，问题变为大规模混合整数非线性规划，计算极其困难，亟需一种实用且可求解的方法。

Method: 1) 用凸二次近似替换非线性AC潮流方程，将MINLP转化为MIQCP，利用CPLEX等求解器高效求解；2) 针对发电机运行时间和启动事件约束导致的递归可行性失效问题，提出附加时间耦合约束；3) 基于周期耗散性假设证明控制器的稳定性。

Result: MIQCP方法显著降低了计算复杂度，可在合理时间内求解；提出的时间耦合约束成功保证了递归可行性；在6节点微电网不同需求场景下的仿真验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 凸二次近似与时间耦合约束的结合为含离散决策变量的NMPC-OPF提供了一种实用且理论上可靠的求解方案，在保证递归可行性与稳定性的同时大幅降低了计算负担。

Abstract: Recently there has been a lot of progress in the development of economic nonlinear model predictive control (NMPC) schemes for multistage optimal power flow (OPF) problems. However, the additional inclusion of discrete decision variables to model generator runtimes and generator startup costs can amount to large scale mixed-integer nonlinear programs (MINLPs) that are computationally very challenging. This work investigates the practical approach that replaces the nonlinear AC power flow equations by convex quadratic approximations. In combination with the discrete generator dynamics this leads to a mixed-integer quadratically constrained program (MIQCP) which is of significantly lower complexity and can be solved in reasonable time by off-the-shelf solvers such as CPLEX. We further show that simple terminal constraints are not sufficient to guarantee recursive feasibility of the NMPC scheme if constraints on generator runtime and on the number of generator startup events are present. To address this challenge we propose the use of additional time-coupled constraints and prove the resulting recursive feasibility property. Based on the assumption of periodic dissipativity of the underlying system we can prove stability of the proposed controller. To illustrate our results, we present simulations of a realistic 6-bus microgrid under different demand scenarios.

</details>


### [161] [Automated Synthesis of Lyapunov Functions for Multi-Agent Systems under Jointly Connected Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27576)
*Shuyuan Zhang,Lei Wang,Qing-Guo Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了联合连通拓扑下多智能体系统的一致性跟踪问题，通过自动合成多项式Lyapunov函数并利用平方和分解方法，在二次Lyapunov函数不存在的情况下实现了多项式时间内的一致性验证，并将分布式非线性控制协议扩展至二阶多智能体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决联合连通拓扑下多智能体系统的一致性跟踪问题，克服传统二次Lyapunov函数在该场景下不存在的局限性。

Method: 提出了分布式非线性控制协议，通过构造和合成更一般的多项式Lyapunov函数，利用多元多项式的平方和分解（SOS）方法在多项式时间内高效合成Lyapunov函数，同时生成多项式耦合函数，并将方法扩展至二阶系统。

Result: 建立并证明了联合连通拓扑下一阶多智能体系统的一致性判据；在二次Lyapunov函数不存在的情况下实现了多项式时间内的一致性验证；生成了伴随的多项式耦合函数；成功将协议扩展至二阶多智能体系统并保证了二阶一致性验证。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于平方和分解的多项式Lyapunov函数自动合成方法能有效解决联合连通拓扑下多智能体系统的一致性跟踪问题，且该方法可推广至二阶系统。

Abstract: This article investigates the consensus tracking problem of multi-agent systems under jointly connected topology through automated synthesis of Lyapunov functions. Based on the proposed distributed nonlinear control protocol, several consensus criteria for first-order multi-agent systems are established and certified by the construction and synthesis of more general polynomial Lyapunov functions. By employing sum-of-squares decomposition for multivariate polynomials, we can efficiently synthesize polynomial Lyapunov functions to achieve consensus verification in polynomial time, although the widely used quadratic Lyapunov functions do not exist. Moreover, polynomial coupling functions for our proposed protocol are concomitantly generated. Furthermore, the distributed nonlinear control protocol is extended to deal with second-order multi-agent systems, while ensuring second-order consensus verification. Finally, an example is presented to demonstrate the efficacy of our method.

</details>


### [162] [OptiLoop: Coordination-in-the-Loop Verification and Repair for LLM-Generated Optimization Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27630)
*Yujia Xu,Zhiheng Wang,Thi Dinh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出OptiLoop，一种针对LLM生成的优化智能体进行协同校验与修复的流水线。通过ADMM共识协议进行短时间协调运行，提取行为与静态证据，实现从局部代码修复到修正公式化修复的递进式错误修复，在40个测试场景中将目标匹配率从66.0%提升至93.0%，社会匹配率从68.5%提升至89.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化决策问题中，LLM生成的本地优化智能体虽然能通过编译和局部校验，但在协调场景下常存在语义错误（如成本误表述、约束范围错误、激励响应不正确等），这些错误仅在协调过程中表现为系统性行为失败而非不可行性。因此需要一种在协同过程中进行验证与修复的方法。

Method: 提出OptiLoop流水线：(1) 从文本生成本地优化智能体；(2) 通过ADMM风格共识协议进行短时有界协调运行，以固定参考对手方进行验证；(3) 提取结构化行为证据与静态证据；(4) 执行证据驱动的修复，对结构性失败从局部代码修复升级为修正公式化修复，并可复用先前实例的经验教训。

Result: 在40个未见测试场景上，OptiLoop-Full相比强局部验证基线，目标匹配率从66.0%提升至93.0%，社会匹配率从68.5%提升至89.0%；平均目标差距从15.3%降至3.5%，平均社会差距从7.6%降至2.0%。

Conclusion: 对于部署在去中心化决策循环中的生成式优化智能体，正确性应在循环内部而非通过孤立执行来验证。

Abstract: Many decentralized decision problems require multiple parties to coordinate on shared decisions while keeping objectives, constraints, and data private. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising way to lower the barrier to participation by generating local optimization agents from natural-language specifications. In coordination settings, however, executability is not enough: a generated agent may compile, solve, and pass local checks while still being semantically wrong, for example by misrepresenting costs, mis-scoping constraints, or responding incorrectly to incentives. Such errors often surface only during coordination, as systematic behavioral failures rather than infeasibility. We propose coordination-in-the-loop verification and repair for LLM-generated optimization agents. We instantiate this idea with an Alternating Direction Method of Multipliers (ADMM)-style consensus protocol and introduce OptiLoop, a pipeline that generates local optimization agents from text, verifies them through short, bounded coordination runs against a fixed reference counterparty, extracts structured behavioral and static evidence, and applies evidence-driven repair. When failures are structural rather than implementational, OptiLoop escalates from localized code fixes to corrected-formulation repair, and it can additionally reuse episodic lessons from prior instances. On 40 held-out test scenarios, OptiLoop-Full improves objective match from 66.0% to 93.0% and social match from 68.5% to 89.0% relative to a strong local-validation baseline, while reducing mean objective gap from 15.3% to 3.5% and mean social gap from 7.6% to 2.0%. These results show that, for generated optimization agents deployed inside decentralized decision loops, correctness should be validated in the loop itself rather than through isolated execution alone.

</details>


### [163] [Global Convergence and Error Propagation in Neural Gradient Flows: A Riemannian Optimization Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27779)
*Shixin Zheng,Yiwei Wang,Haizhao Yang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在最小化移动方案框架下建立了神经网络的几何收敛理论，证明了在特定条件下神经网络优化子问题具有测地凸性和强凸性，并推导了不完全内层求解器误差传播下的函数空间跟踪界，数值实验验证了高斯-牛顿型内层求解器的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为神经网络优化建立严格的几何收敛理论，弥补现有方法在MMS框架下缺乏几何分析与收敛保证的不足。

Method: 将神经MMS每一步重构为希尔伯特空间中增量集合上的最小化问题；证明增量集是光滑无边嵌入子流形；建立参数空间中的预条件（高斯-牛顿型）梯度流与黎曼梯度流的等价关系；在严格内部局部化条件和显式数据条件下验证子问题的测地凸性和强凸性；通过指数映射实现离散伴随后获得线性收敛；传播内层求解器误差和神经近似误差得到函数空间跟踪界。

Result: 不完全神经迭代收敛到全局最优的O(δ)邻域；非线性回归和潜扩散测试中，高斯-牛顿型内层求解器相比一阶基线方法轨迹误差更小且所需内层迭代数显著减少。

Conclusion: 所提出的几何收敛框架为神经MMS优化提供了理论保证，高斯-牛顿型内层求解器在效率和精度上优于一阶方法。

Abstract: We develop a geometric convergence theory for neural-network optimization within the minimizing movement scheme (MMS) framework. Reformulating each neural MMS step as a minimization over the set of increments in a Hilbert space, we show that under a $C^2$ network with locally non-degenerate Jacobian this increment set is a boundaryless smooth embedded submanifold, on which a natural preconditioned (Gauss--Newton-type) gradient flow in parameter space induces exactly the Riemannian gradient flow. Under a strict interior-localization condition and an explicit data condition, the reached sublevel set is geodesically convex and the subproblem objective is geodesically strongly convex on it; both the continuous Riemannian gradient flow and its discrete companion via the exponential map converge linearly to the unique subproblem minimizer. Propagating finite-time inner-solver inexactness and neural-approximation error through the MMS iterations yields a uniform function-space tracking bound and an explicit trajectory budget, so the inexact neural iterates converge to an $O(δ)$-neighborhood of the global minimum. Numerical experiments on nonlinear regression and a small-scale latent-diffusion testbed indicate that the Gauss--Newton-type inner solver achieves smaller trajectory errors with substantially fewer inner iterations than first-order baselines.

</details>


### [164] [Polling Set Construction and Worst-Case Complexity for Direct Search under Polyhedral Convex Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27814)
*Lindon Roberts,Clément W. Royer*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文为直接搜索方法（无导数优化）建立了新的理论基础，使其能处理一般凸约束并具有复杂度保证，特别针对多面体凸约束构造了包含近似切锥外方向的轮询集，理论证明了这种做法的合理性，数值实验表明添加额外方向显著提升了实际性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接搜索方法与基于插值的模型方法之间存在理论差距：模型方法已为约束问题建立了统一框架并具有复杂度保证，而直接搜索方法在这方面仍缺乏。本文旨在填补这一空白，为直接搜索方法建立类似于模型方法的统一理论。

Method: 针对多面体凸约束，构造满足新理论要求的轮询集，这些轮询集必然包含近似切锥外的方向。理论分析结合数值实验验证。

Result: 新理论为直接搜索方法处理一般凸约束提供了复杂度保证；包含切锥外方向的轮询集在理论上被证明是必要的，且数值上显著提升了性能。

Conclusion: 本文为约束优化中的直接搜索方法建立了与模型方法对等的理论基础，并验证了实际启发式策略（添加切锥外方向）的理论正确性和实际有效性。

Abstract: Direct search is one of the most popular derivative-free optimization paradigms, that relies on exploring the variable space using polling directions. To analyze and implement direct search, one typically relies on positive spanning sets. This concept is somewhat decorrelated from interpolation-based sets used in model-based algorithms, another class of derivative-free optimization methods. This discrepancy is even more pronounced for constrained problems, where recent advances in the interpolation-based setting have produced a unified picture that is still lacking in the direct-search case. In this paper, we introduce a new theoretical underpinning for direct-search methods, that can be defined for general convex constraints and lead to complexity guarantees, as in the model-based setting. By focusing on polyhedral convex constraints, we are able to construct polling sets that meet our new theoretical requirements. In particular, our polling sets necessarily include directions outside of the approximate tangent cone, giving theoretical justification to existing practical heuristics which incorporate this idea. Our numerical results confirm that adding these extra directions significantly improves practical performance.

</details>


### [165] [Optimal Spectral Design with Prior Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27837)
*Anton J. Kleywegt,Johannes Milz,Mohit Singh,Weijun Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类谱设计问题，其中先验正半定信息矩阵通过设计向量构建的秩一矩阵和进行更新，目标为特征值的对称凸函数。利用特征值松弛和Schur-Horn定理，提出了多项式时间算法求解该非凸问题，并展示了在无导数优化中的应用潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一经典最优实验设计准则（A-、D-、E-最优性），并解决基于模型的无导数优化中采样方向对回归模型条件数和精度的影响问题。

Method: 使用紧的特征值松弛获得凸重构，并应用Schur-Horn定理构造简单的多项式时间算法。

Result: 通过算法计算出了最优谱设计，并在无导数优化中的数值实验展示了谱设计的潜力。

Conclusion: 所提出的凸重构和多项式时间算法有效解决了非凸的谱设计问题，且在无导数优化中有良好应用前景。

Abstract: We study a class of spectral design problems in which a prior positive semidefinite information matrix is updated by a sum of rank-one matrices constructed from chosen design vectors subject to a bound on their Euclidean norm. The objective of a spectral design problem is any symmetric convex function of the eigenvalues of the updated information matrix. This framework unifies classical optimal experimental design criteria, including A-, D-, and E-optimality. It also arises in model-based derivative-free optimization, where sampling directions determine the conditioning and accuracy of regression models. Although the objective is symmetric and convex in the eigenvalues, the optimization problem with design vectors/matrix as decision variables is nonconvex, and optimal solutions of their convex relaxations may not be feasible for the spectral design problem. We use tight eigenvalue relaxations to obtain a convex reformulation, and we apply the Schur--Horn theorem to construct a simple polynomial-time algorithm for solving the spectral design problem. We illustrate the optimal spectral designs computed by our algorithm. Moreover, a small set of numerical experiments shows the potential of spectral designs for derivative-free optimization.

</details>


### [166] [Stability of Quadratically Regularized Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27883)
*Alberto González-Sanz,Marcel Nutz*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了二次正则化最优传输（QOT）在边际、运输成本函数和正则化参数扰动下的定量稳定性理论，核心是对偶势函数的L∞稳定性结果，并首次证明了二次运输成本下最优耦合支撑集的Hausdorff距离局部Lipschitz稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QOT是熵正则化最优传输的稀疏替代方案，但其在扰动下的稳定性理论尚不完善，特别是最优支撑集的稳定性此前未被研究过。

Method: 从随边际变化的L2空间误差界出发，利用势函数的自界性质推导出对边际均匀的局部L∞-Lipschitz界，进而获得最优耦合及其支撑集的稳定性。

Result: 建立了对偶势函数的L∞稳定性结果；证明了最优耦合及其支撑集在边际、成本函数和正则化参数扰动下的稳定性；首次得到二次成本下最优支撑集在Hausdorff距离下的局部Lipschitz稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了正则化最优传输中支撑集稳定性理论的空白，为QOT的理论基础和应用提供了重要保障。

Abstract: Quadratically regularized optimal transport (QOT) is a sparse alternative to entropic optimal transport. We develop a quantitative stability theory for QOT under perturbations of the marginals, the transport cost function, and the regularization parameter. The centerpiece is an $L^\infty$-stability result for the dual potentials. Starting from an error bound in an $L^2$-space that varies with the marginals, we use a self-bound for the potentials to derive a local $L^\infty$-Lipschitz bound that is uniform over marginals. This bound also yields stability of the optimal coupling and of its support. In particular, we show that for the quadratic transport cost, the support of the optimal coupling is locally Lipschitz in Hausdorff distance under perturbations of the marginals. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first stability result for the optimal support in regularized optimal transport.

</details>


### [167] [Online Sketched Newton-Raphson](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27985)
*Jean-Luc Lupien,Yuen-Man Pun,Youssef Diouane,Iman Shames,Antoine Lesage-Landry*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出在线草图牛顿-拉夫逊方法（OSNR），利用草图技术降低二阶在线凸优化中矩阵求逆的计算复杂度（从立方降至更低），并在三个应用场景（在线求根、无约束OCO、时变等式约束OCO）中实现次线性动态遗憾界，同时OSNR-EC扩展保证次线性累积约束违例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二阶在线凸优化方法虽然能实现更紧的遗憾界和更好的经验性能，但每次迭代需要矩阵求逆，计算复杂度为问题规模的立方，限制了其在大规模问题中的应用。

Method: 引入草图（sketching）技术对二阶信息矩阵进行近似，提出在线草图牛顿-拉夫逊方法（OSNR），避免精确矩阵求逆，降低计算复杂度。

Result: OSNR在在线求根、无约束OCO和时变等式约束OCO三个场景中均实现次线性动态遗憾界；OSNR-EC扩展在等式约束下实现次线性累积约束违例；数值实验显示即使在低采样率下仍保持高性能。

Conclusion: 草图技术可有效缓解二阶OCO方法的计算瓶颈，在保持理论保证的同时大幅提升计算效率，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: In online convex optimization (OCO), a decision-maker is confronted with an unknown environment and seeks to play an optimal sequence of decisions on a short time-scale using only past information. Recent advances in second-order OCO methods have demonstrated tighter regret bounds and improved empirical performance over traditional first-order methods. However, this performance comes at a cost: a matrix inversion is now required, which scales with the cube of the size of the problem. In this work, we propose sketching to mitigate this limitation. Specifically, we present the online sketched Newton-Raphson method (OSNR) which preserves the tight regret bounds obtained with second-order methods while presenting a strict computational improvement in terms of complexity. We discuss three application scenarios of OSNR: online root finding, unconstrained OCO, and time-varying equality-constrained OCO, and present their respective regret and a constraint violation bound for the latter. In all three applications, OSNR achieves sublinear dynamic regret bounds. For the equality-constrained case, the extension OSNR with equality constraints OSNR-EC is shown to yield sublinear cumulative constraint violation. Finally, we illustrate the performance of OSNR and OSNR-EC on two numerical examples, viz., online position tracking and optimal power flow, and observe that OSNR and OSNR-EC exhibit high performance even at low sampling rates.

</details>


### [168] [Convergence of empirical subgradients for optimal transport-based objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28134)
*Tam Le*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于采样传输成本定义的参数化目标函数，证明了其次微分的图形收敛性，确保了子梯度方法在总体问题中一致收敛到稳定点。通过风险规避优化、公平约束学习和切片Wasserstein问题等案例，揭示了平滑参数化在统计一致性与优化之间提供良好接口，而非平滑代价和模型可能在大样本极限下出现不稳定的导数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优传输在分布学习、分布约束和不确定性建模中广泛应用，但实际中传输损失通常通过样本计算（如一维排序公式或切片Wasserstein代价），其统计与优化的一致性理论尚不完善。本文旨在建立采样传输目标函数的次微分收敛性理论，并分析不同参数化方式对优化稳定性的影响。

Method: 通过证明采样传输成本定义的参数化目标函数的次微分在图形意义上收敛到总体目标函数的次微分，从而建立统计一致性下的优化理论基础。分别考虑平滑与非平滑参数化情形，并在风险规避优化、公平约束学习、切片Wasserstein问题等场景中验证理论结果。

Result: 证明了采样传输目标函数的次微分图形收敛到总体目标次微分，确保标准子梯度方法能一致地逼近总体问题的稳定点。平滑参数化在统计一致性与优化之间提供了有利的接口，而非平滑代价和模型在大样本极限下导数不稳定。

Conclusion: 平滑参数化为基于采样传输成本的优化问题提供了统计一致性与优化收敛性的桥梁；非平滑传输代价和模型在大样本下导数不稳定，需谨慎使用。理论结果在风险规避、公平约束和切片Wasserstein等多个应用场景中得到验证。

Abstract: Optimal transport is widely used to learn distributions, enforce distributional constraints, and model uncertainty. In applications, transport losses are often computed from samples through tractable representations, such as one-dimensional sorting formulas or sliced Wasserstein costs, making them practical components in training pipelines. We study parameterized objectives defined by sampled transport costs and prove graphical convergence of their subdifferentials to the subdifferential of the population objective. In particular, this ensures that standard subgradient methods consistently approach stationary points of the population-level problem. We illustrate the results in several settings, including risk-averse optimization, fairness-constrained learning, and sliced Wasserstein problems. Our analysis highlights that smooth parameterizations provide a favorable interface between statistical consistency and optimization. By contrast, transport objectives with nonsmooth costs and models may exhibit unstable derivatives in the large-sample limit.

</details>


### [169] [Dynamic Stabilisation of Boundary Control Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28189)
*Mohamed Fkirine,Lassi Paunonen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 针对希尔伯特空间上的抽象线性边界控制系统，设计了基于观测器的控制器，给出了指数、强和多项式稳定的条件，并建立了闭环系统的外部适定性。在1维波动方程、2维分布式控制/观测波动方程以及非均匀SCOLE模型上验证了该方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为抽象线性边界控制系统设计基于观测器的反馈控制器，并建立稳定性条件及闭环系统的适定性理论。

Method: 基于观测器的控制器设计方法，利用算子理论分析闭环系统的稳定性（指数、强、多项式稳定），并验证外部适定性。

Result: 给出了指数、强和多项式稳定的充分条件，证明了闭环系统的外部适定性，并在三个具体系统中实现控制设计。

Conclusion: 所提出的观测器反馈控制器能够有效稳定一类抽象线性边界控制系统，适用于多个物理模型。

Abstract: We design observer-based controllers to stabilise abstract linear boundary control systems on Hilbert spaces. Our main results introduce conditions for exponential, strong, and polynomial stability, and establish external well-posedness of the closed-loop system. We design controllers for a one-dimensional wave equation, a two-dimensional wave equation with distributed control and observation, and a non-uniform SCOLE model.

</details>


### [170] [De-risking solutions to optimization problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28240)
*Daniel Bienstock,Blake Sisson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出一种切割平面方法，通过调整优化问题的解来减少风险暴露特征（如资产或资源集中度），该方法与风险表示形式无关，能快速降低风险指标而不显著增加名义成本，或证明无法实现此类调整。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的优化模型往往只关注名义成本最小化，忽略了解决方案中存在的风险暴露特征（如资产/资源集中），导致在实际应用中面临脆弱性。需要一种能够有效降低风险特征且不显著增加成本的方法。

Method: 开发了一种切割平面方法，该方法通过迭代调整优化问题的解来减少风险暴露特征。该方法对风险的具体表示形式保持不可知（agnostic），并借鉴了优化中一阶方法的技术。

Result: 该方法能够在快速迭代中降低适当的风险指标，同时不显著增加名义成本；或者在无法实现调整时给出证明。

Conclusion: 提出了一种通用的、与风险表示无关的切割平面框架，能够在控制成本的前提下有效降低解决方案的风险暴露特征，为风险规避优化提供了一种新的高效工具。

Abstract: We develop a cutting-plane methodology that adjusts solutions to optimization problems so
  as to reduce features that bring about exposure to risk, such as concentration of assets
  or resources. The methodology is agnostic to the representation of risk. Our procedure aims
  to reduce the appropriate risk metric without accruing a significant increase in nominal
  cost, rapidly, or proves that such an adjustment is not possible. The underlying approach
  borrows from techniques used in first-order methods for optimization.

</details>


### [171] [Hautus-Type Criteria for Controllability and Stabilizability of Backward-Structured Stochastic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28242)
*Jingrui Sun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对后向随机线性系统，建立了精确可控性和可稳定性的尖锐Hautus型判据（随机Popov-Belevitch-Hautus检验的推广），发现随机Hautus阻碍不是左特征向量或任意对称特征矩阵，而是Lyapunov型算子的正半定特征矩阵，并证明精确可控性等价于不存在与控制方向正交的非零正半定特征矩阵，可稳定性则由仅施加于非稳定谱部分的锥约束Hautus条件刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将确定性线性系统中经典的Popov-Belevitch-Hautus（PBH）检验推广到后向随机线性系统，建立针对精确可控性和可稳定性的随机Hautus型判据。

Method: 通过分析Lyapunov型算子的谱性质，引入正半定特征矩阵作为随机Hautus阻碍；利用锥约束（正半定性）和谱约束（仅关注非稳定谱部分）来刻画可控性和可稳定性；辅以有限秩和Gramian刻画以及可控性分解。

Result: 精确可控性等价于不存在与控制方向正交的非零正半定特征矩阵；仅排除所有具有相同正交性的对称特征矩阵是充分但不必要的（即锥约束是尖锐的）；可稳定性由相同的锥约束Hautus条件仅施加于Lyapunov型算子的非稳定谱部分来刻画；此外建立了有限秩和Gramian刻画、可控性分解，并证明了精确可控性蕴含可稳定性。

Conclusion: 后向随机线性系统的可控性和可稳定性由Lyapunov型算子的正半定特征矩阵这一锥约束主导，形成了"谱约束+锥约束"的双重约束框架，为随机系统的控制理论提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: This paper develops sharp Hautus-type criteria, stochastic counterparts of the classical Popov-Belevitch-Hautus test, for exact controllability and stabilizability of backwardstructured stochastic linear systems. The main finding is that the stochastic Hautus obstruction is not a left eigenvector, as in deterministic linear systems, nor an arbitrary symmetric eigenmatrix, but a positive semidefinite eigenmatrix of a Lyapunov-type operator. We prove that exact controllability is equivalent to the absence of such nonzero positive semidefinite eigenmatrices that are orthogonal to the control directions. This cone restriction is sharp: excluding all symmetric eigenmatrices with the same orthogonality property is sufficient but not necessary. We further show that stabilizability is characterized by the same cone-restricted Hautus condition imposed only on the nonstable spectral part of the Lyapunov-type operator. Thus the stochastic Hautus theory developed here is governed by a simultaneous spectral restriction and cone restriction. In addition to these criteria, we provide finite-rank and Gramian characterizations underlying exact controllability, establish the corresponding controllability decomposition, and show that exact controllability implies stabilizability.

</details>


### [172] [Machine Learning for Exact Time Series Aggregation in Generation Expansion Planning with Energy Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28356)
*Jakub Rybka,Luca Santosuosso,Thomas Klatzer,Sonja Wogrin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的迭代时间序列聚合（TSA）方法用于发电扩展规划（GEP），该方法通过机器学习估计边际成本指导聚类，在显著降低计算负担的同时能够评估最优性间隙，实现了精确的时间聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缓解大规模发电扩展规划模型的计算负担，同时克服传统时间序列聚合方法完全基于启发式、无法评估最优性间隙的局限。

Method: 提出迭代时间序列聚合方法，利用机器学习估计GEP模型的边际成本，将边际成本作为聚类特征指导时间序列聚合，保留原全规模模型的活跃约束集。

Result: 数值结果表明，相比传统仅依赖输入数据分析的TSA方法，纳入估计边际成本作为聚类特征显著提升了时间聚合的质量，且能够实现精确的时间聚合。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于机器学习边际成本估计的迭代TSA方法能够在保持解的质量的同时大幅降低GEP问题的计算负担，是一种有效的规模缩减技术。

Abstract: This paper investigates a generation expansion planning (GEP) problem encompassing renewable, thermal, and storage technologies while simultaneously optimizing market participation, operational expenditures, and capital investment. To alleviate the computational burden of the GEP model, we propose a novel iterative time series aggregation (TSA) method that constructs a temporally aggregated counterpart of the original full-scale GEP model. Unlike traditional TSA methods, which are purely heuristic, our method enables the assessment of the optimality gap between the aggregated and full-scale models. Moreover, by leveraging machine learning-based estimates of the GEP model marginal costs, the algorithm guides TSA to construct an aggregated model that preserves the active constraints of its full-scale counterpart, which has been shown to yield exact temporal aggregation. Numerical results show that incorporating estimated marginal costs as clustering features substantially improves the quality of temporal aggregation compared with traditional TSA methods that rely solely on input data analysis.

</details>


### [173] [Operator-Norm Transfer and Cohomological Rigidity for Quaternionic Quasi-Lie Structures with Application to Sliding Mode $β$-Exponential Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28385)
*Nassim Athmouni,Nejib Brahmia,Tarek Fajraoui,Fehmi Mabrouk*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文发展了四元数拟Lie结构的算子理论与上同调工具，建立了算子范数转移、上同调刚性转录以及投影Jacobi缺陷的广义单侧Lipschitz条件三个主要结果，并以此为基础构建了基于上同调匹配条件替代逐点验证的鲁棒滑模控制框架，通过积分滑模面和迭代LMI方案实现β-指数稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为四元数拟Lie结构建立算子理论与上同调分析工具，并以此为基础发展一种更高效的鲁棒滑模控制方法，用上同调匹配条件替代传统逐点验证，降低验证复杂度。

Method: 运用算子理论、上同调方法、可测选择的一致选取论证以及迭代线性矩阵不等式（LMI）方案。

Result: 建立了三个主要定理：（1）四元数反同构下的精确算子范数转移；（2）上同调刚性结果转录为显式双线性修正Ω，使括号在可容许半径球内精确满足Jacobi恒等式；（3）投影Jacobi缺陷满足广义单侧Lipschitz条件。应用方面，构建了基于积分滑模面和迭代LMI方案的鲁棒控制框架，实现β-指数稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文为四元数拟Lie结构提供了纯分析的理论框架，控制应用中的数值模拟留待后续论文；结果限于齐次拟Lie情形，Sobolev扩展为猜想性质，算法终止性依赖于LMI解映射的充分连续性假设。

Abstract: We develop operator-theoretic and cohomological tools for quaternionic quasi-Lie structures, with sliding mode control as a motivating application. Three main results are established. First, an exact operator-norm transfer under the quaternionic anti-isomorphism $a \mapsto \bar{a}$, which enables quantitative bounds from~\cite{Athmouni2026} to transfer between left- and right-module conventions with no constant factor. Second, a transcription of the cohomological rigidity result of~\cite{Athmouni2026} into a form usable here: in the homogeneous case, under a local cohomological non-obstruction hypothesis, an explicit bilinear correction $Ω$ produces a bracket satisfying the Jacobi identity exactly on a ball of admissible radius, with all quantitative constants expressed through $C_{2}$ and the admissible radius. Third, the projected Jacobi defect is shown to satisfy a generalized one-sided Lipschitz condition with computable constants, obtained via a uniform-selection argument handling state-dependence of the measurable selection. As an application, we develop a robust control framework with a cohomological matching condition replacing pointwise verification: an integral sliding surface yields $β$-exponential stability via an iterative linear matrix inequality (LMI) scheme. The work is purely analytical; closed-loop numerical simulations for multidimensional systems are deferred to a companion paper. The framework is restricted to the homogeneous quasi-Lie case; the Sobolev extension is conjectural, and algorithm termination is established conditionally on sufficient continuity assumptions on the LMI solution map.

</details>


### [174] [Birkhoff interpolation models for optimization with some available derivatives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28503)
*Jeffrey Larson,Matt Menickelly,Evan Toler*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Birkhoff插值的无导数优化框架，该框架能够处理仅部分导数可用的场景，通过混合函数值和偏导数信息构建局部多项式模型，并在信赖域方法中自适应选择导数信息，从而统一了无导数和基于导数的优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实际科学计算应用中（如仿真、伴随法启用的组件、遗留软件或部分可微模型），常常出现只有部分导数可用的情况。现有的Hermite插值方法要求在所有插值点获取全部可用导数，这限制了灵活性。因此需要一种能够处理任意导数可用模式的插值框架。

Method: 1) 引入Birkhoff插值框架，允许任意模式的导数可用性；2) 建立插值系统适定性条件及完全二次插值模型的模型精度界；3) 开发信赖域框架，在保持适定性插值集的同时选择性融入导数信息；4) 该方法是现有基于插值的无导数优化算法的推广。

Result: 在CUTEst测试问题集合上，使用合成生成的导数可用性模式进行了评估，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法弥合了无导数优化和基于导数优化之间的鸿沟，为处理部分导数可用问题提供了一种统一且灵活的理论框架与算法实现。

Abstract: We consider interpolation-based derivative-free optimization in settings where only some derivatives are available. Such situations arise naturally in scientific computing applications involving simulations, adjoint-enabled components, legacy software, or partially differentiable models. We introduce a Birkhoff interpolation framework that permits arbitrary patterns of derivative availability and enables the con- struction of local polynomial models using mixtures of function values and partial derivative informa- tion. In contrast to Hermite interpolation approaches, the proposed framework does not require all available derivatives to be queried at every interpolation point. We develop conditions under which the resulting interpolation systems are poised and establish corresponding model-accuracy bounds for fully quadratic interpolation models. We develop a trust-region framework that maintains poised interpolation sets while selectively incorporating derivative information. The method generalizes an established class of interpolation-based derivative-free optimization algorithms and naturally bridges derivative-free and derivative-based settings. We evaluate the proposed approach on a collection of CUTEst test problems with synthetically generated derivative-availability patterns.

</details>


### [175] [Myerson Interaction Index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28529)
*Jorge González-Ortega,Elisenda Molina,Juan Tejada*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了Myerson交互指数(MII)，将Shapley交互指数扩展到受图结构限制的通信合作博弈中，建立了图上交互指数的形式化框架，并基于组件效率、公平性和否决权联盟一致性给出了MII的公理化特征。还分析了网络诱导的交互，以区分图拓扑效应与博弈内在协同效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Shapley交互指数无法处理通信结构受限的合作博弈场景，缺乏在图上对玩家间交互进行度量的形式化工具。

Method: 将Shapley交互指数扩展到图受限合作博弈中，提出Myerson交互指数(MII)，通过组件效率、公平性和否决权联盟一致性三条公理进行特征化，并引入网络诱导交互分析来区分拓扑效应与内在协同效应。

Result: 建立了图上交互指数的形式化框架，给出了MII的公理特征描述，并能有效区分图拓扑引起的交互与博弈内在的交互。

Conclusion: MII为分析结构化环境中的联盟行为提供了稳健工具，在社交网络分析和可解释人工智能领域具有潜在应用价值。

Abstract: This paper introduces the Myerson interaction index (MII), an extension of the Shapley interaction index to cooperative games with communication structures restricted by graphs. We establish a formal framework for interaction indices on graphs and provide an axiomatic characterization of the MII based on component efficiency, fairness, and veto partnership consistency. Furthermore, we analyse network-induced interaction to distinguish between effects arising from the graph topology and those stemming from the game's intrinsic synergies. This framework offers a robust tool for analysing coalitional behaviour in structured environments, with potential applications in social network analysis and explainable artificial intelligence.

</details>


### [176] [Model Predictive Control for Constrained Linear Positive Systems on Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28550)
*Roland Schurig,David Ohlin,Anders Rantzer,Emma Tegling,Rolf Findeisen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了正线性系统在存储和流量容量约束下的模型预测控制（MPC），利用无约束问题的解析结构构建了显式次优容许控制器，得出了图可计算的性能界和最小镇定预测时域，并通过凸规划高效求解最优界和时域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正线性系统广泛存在于路由、物流和舱室建模中，但状态（存储）和输入（节点间流量）的容量约束显著增加了问题复杂度，现有方法难以提供显式MPC保证。

Method: 利用无约束问题的解析结构，构造显式次优容许控制器；基于该控制器推导图可计算的性能上界和最小镇定预测时域长度；通过凸规划高效求解最优界和时域。

Result: 得到了图可计算的性能界和最小镇定预测时域长度；证明了系统结构使得通常不可获得的显式MPC保证成为可能。

Conclusion: 系统结构（正线性系统的关联形式）能够为带容量约束的MPC提供显式保证，包括性能界和最小镇定时域，这些保证通常难以获得。

Abstract: Positive systems describing networks with inherently non-negative states and inputs arise naturally in routing, logistics, and compartmental modelling. We consider problems modelled as positive linear systems in incidence form with linear cost. The addition of capacity constraints on states (storage) and inputs (flows between nodes) significantly increases the problem complexity. Leveraging the analytic structure of the unconstrained problem, an explicit suboptimal admissible controller is constructed. This yields graph-computable performance bounds and a minimum stabilising horizon length for a model predictive controller without terminal conditions. A convex program enables efficient computation of the optimal bound and horizon. These results highlight how system structure enables explicit MPC guarantees that are typically not available.

</details>


### [177] [Implicit Regularization in Perturbed Deep Matrix Factorization: Spectral Conditions and Stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28613)
*Jingzhe Wang,Hung-Hsu Chou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了扰动深度矩阵分解中低秩隐式正则化的稳定性，分析了梯度下降在无噪声和有噪声情况下的低秩相位现象，并证明了扰动对迭代复杂度和特征值恢复的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在目标矩阵被噪声扰动的情况下，深度矩阵分解中梯度下降的低秩隐式正则化是否仍然稳定，以及扰动如何影响算法的收敛性和特征值恢复能力。

Method: 首先推导了无噪声场景下梯度下降呈现低秩相位的充分谱条件，揭示了目标谱、初始化和步长对低秩区间的联合影响；然后分析了扰动梯度下降动力学，给出了收敛保证并量化了扰动对迭代复杂度和特征值恢复的影响；最后证明了低秩相位在扰动下仍可保持，并给出了对扰动大小的显式依赖关系。

Result: 推导了无噪声条件下梯度下降低秩相位的充分谱条件；给出了扰动梯度下降的收敛性保证，量化了扰动对迭代复杂度和特征值恢复的影响；证明了低秩相位在扰动下仍能保持，且依赖于扰动大小。

Conclusion: 深度矩阵分解中梯度下降的低秩隐式正则化在噪声扰动下仍具有稳定性，数值实验支持了理论结果。

Abstract: This paper studies the stability of low-rank implicit regularization in perturbed deep matrix factorization, where the target matrix is corrupted by a noise matrix. We first derive sufficient spectral conditions under which gradient descent exhibits a low-rank phase in the noiseless setting. These conditions show how the target spectrum, initialization, and step size jointly determine the existence of a nonempty low-rank interval. We then analyze the perturbed gradient descent dynamics, proving convergence guarantees and quantifying how the perturbation affects iteration complexity and eigenvalue recovery. Finally, we show that the low-rank phase persists under perturbation, with explicit dependence on the perturbation size. Numerical experiments support the theoretical findings.

</details>


### [178] [Disjunctive Sum of Squares](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28674)
*Amir Ali Ahmadi,Sanjeeb Dash,Yixuan Hua,Bartolomeo Stellato*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的“析取平方和”方法，用于证明多项式的非负性。与传统的单一代数恒等式平方和（SOS）方法不同，该方法通过多个可并行求解的代数恒等式来认证非负性。主要结果是两个析取Positivstellensatz定理，分别构建了基于半定规划的收敛下界层次和免优化层次。该方法还结合了分支定界算法，并在多项式优化、共正性优化和组合优化问题上进行了数值实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统平方和（SOS）方法在证明多项式非负性时，通常需要单个高次代数恒等式，这导致半定规划问题的规模随多项式次数快速增大，计算负担沉重。本文旨在通过引入析取平方和（DSOS）概念，允许多个低次代数恒等式并行求解，从而降低每个恒等式的次数至原多项式的次数，并构建更高效的优化层次结构。

Method: 本文提出了析取平方和（DSOS）方法，核心是允许多个代数恒等式并行地共同证明多项式非负性。主要结果包括：(1) 析取Positivstellensatz定理：证明每个代数恒等式的次数可以保持与原始多项式次数相同；(2) 基于此定理，构造了半定规划（SDP）的收敛下界层次，其中最大半定约束的规模在整个层次中固定；(3) 第二个析取Positivstellensatz定理导出免优化的层次结构，并专门应用于矩阵共正性的证明；(4) 将DSOS方法与分支定界算法结合，形成完整的求解框架。

Result: 理论方面，成功证明了两个析取Positivstellensatz定理，给出了保持低次数的代数恒等式存在性保证。算法方面，构建了固定SDP规模的收敛层次和免优化层次。实验方面，在多项式优化、共正性优化和组合优化问题上进行了数值实验，验证了DSOS方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 析取平方和（DSOS）方法为多项式非负性认证提供了一种新的并行化框架，通过多个低次代数恒等式替代单个高次恒等式，有效降低了计算复杂度。该方法不仅构建了两种基于SDP的优化层次，还能与分支定界算法自然结合，在多种优化问题上具有实用潜力。

Abstract: We introduce the concept of disjunctive sum of squares for certifying nonnegativity of polynomials. Unlike the popular sum of squares approach where nonnegativity is certified by a single algebraic identity, the disjunctive sum of squares approach certifies nonnegativity with multiple algebraic identities which can be found in parallel. Our main result is a disjunctive Positivstellensatz proving that we can keep the degree of each algebraic identity as low as the degree of the polynomial whose nonnegativity is in question. Based on this result, we construct a semidefinite programming based converging hierarchy of lower bounds for the problem of minimizing a polynomial over a compact basic semialgebraic set, where the size of the largest semidefinite constraint is fixed throughout the hierarchy. We further prove a second disjunctive Positivstellensatz which leads to an optimization-free hierarchy for polynomial optimization. We specialize this result to the problem of proving copositivity of matrices. Finally, we describe how the disjunctive sum of squares approach can be combined with a branch-and-bound algorithm and we present numerical experiments on polynomial, copositive, and combinatorial optimization problems.

</details>


### [179] [Adaptive Partitioning in Column Generation for Nested Paths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28692)
*Bart van Rossum,Rolf van Lieshout,Alexandre Jacquillat*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的列生成定价算法，通过自适应子路径分桶和悲观-乐观迭代定价，有效解决嵌套路径问题中的子路径枚举难题。在平衡多周期车辆路径问题和鲁棒铁路乘务调度问题上的实验表明，该算法相比基准方法实现了最高13倍的加速，并显著提升了求解质量和最优性证明能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌套路径问题中路径变量可分解为子路径序列，子路径需满足局部资源约束，路径还需满足全局资源约束。现有列生成定价算法需要枚举所有非支配子路径，计算代价高昂，亟需一种避免枚举的高效定价算法。

Method: 提出自适应分桶定价算法：(1) 将子路径按全局路径资源消耗自适应划分为桶；(2) 每个桶由其最小简化成本的子路径表示；(3) 在悲观定价（求解受限主问题获取上界）和乐观定价（放宽约束获取下界）之间迭代；(4) 通过自适应精化过程在有限次迭代内缩小上下界差距。

Result: 在平衡多周期容量车辆路径问题上，相较最先进的列生成基准方法实现了最高13倍的加速，分支定价切割算法求解的实例数量是子路径基准方法的三倍。在鲁棒铁路乘务调度问题上，实现了最高三倍加速，并得到了最优性差距在1%以内的原始解。

Conclusion: 提出的自适应分桶定价算法能有效避免子路径枚举，显著提升列生成求解嵌套路径问题的效率，在多个应用场景中均取得了优于现有方法的性能表现。

Abstract: We study a class of nested path problems, in which every path-based variable can be decomposed into a sequence of subpaths. Subpaths must satisfy local resources, while paths must satisfy additional global resources. This paper develops a new exact pricing algorithm in column generation for these problems that avoids the enumeration of non-dominated subpaths. The algorithm relies on adaptive partitioning of subpaths into buckets characterizing the consumption of global path resources. The algorithm represents each bucket by its subpath of minimum reduced cost, and iterates between pessimistic and optimistic pricing steps to combine subpaths into paths while maintaining upper and lower bounds on the minimum reduced cost. An adaptive refinement procedure closes the gap in a finite number of iterations. We demonstrate the effectiveness of the algorithm on two applications. For the balanced multi-period capacitated vehicle routing problem, we obtain speed-ups of up to a factor of 13 over a state-of-the-art column generation benchmark, and the resulting branch-price-and-cut algorithm solves three times as many instances to optimality as a subpath-based baseline. For the robust railway crew scheduling problem, we obtain speed-ups of up to a factor of three and produce primal solutions within 1% of optimality.

</details>


### [180] [Robust Markov Decision Processes on Continuous State Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28706)
*Mengmeng Li,Yifan Hu,Daniel Kuhn,Yan Li*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续状态空间下具有结构矩形模糊集的无限时域鲁棒马尔可夫决策过程，提出了基于动态规划的随机一阶方法用于鲁棒策略评估与优化，分别实现了$\widetilde{\mathcal O}(1/ε^2)$的样本复杂度，该结果在连续状态空间鲁棒MDP中是新的，并可推广至零和马尔可夫博弈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒MDP研究多集中于离散状态空间，连续状态空间下鲁棒策略评估与优化的样本复杂度尚未得到充分研究，缺乏高效且具有理论保证的算法。

Method: 利用鲁棒MDP的动态规划形式，提出随机一阶方法进行鲁棒策略评估，并建立高概率收敛保证；在此基础上结合近似策略迭代方法寻找ε-最优策略；所提方法同样适用于零和马尔可夫博弈。

Result: 鲁棒策略评估和优化均达到$\widetilde{\mathcal O}(1/ε^2)$的样本复杂度，该结果在连续状态空间鲁棒MDP中是全新的；在零和马尔可夫博弈中，该方法严格改进了现有连续状态空间的样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 本文为连续状态空间鲁棒MDP提供了首个高效的样本复杂度保证，所提方法具有理论上的最优性或接近最优性，且可直接迁移至零和博弈场景，具有重要的理论价值。

Abstract: We study infinite-horizon robust Markov decision processes (MDPs) on continuous state spaces with structured rectangular ambiguity set. The proposed ambiguity set falls within the convex hull of unknown generating kernels. We utilize the dynamic formulation of the corresponding robust MDPs, and subsequently introduce a stochastic first-order method for robust policy evaluation. We establish its high probability convergence to the robust value function, which in turn leads to an $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(1/ε^2)$ sample complexity. This high probability accuracy certificate is then used in an approximate policy iteration method that finds an $ε$-optimal policy with $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(1/ε^2)$ samples. The obtained sample complexities for both robust policy evaluation and optimization appear to be new for robust MDPs with continuous state spaces. Of independent interest, the proposed method is also directly applicable to zero-sum Markov games, which seems to strictly improve the existing sample complexities for continuous state spaces.

</details>


### [181] [Preference-Shaped Expected Hypervolume and R2 Improvement: Exact Computation and Monotonicity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28746)
*Michael T. M. Emmerich*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了超体积指标和R2指标在贝叶斯多目标优化中的偏好变换性质，证明了超体积相关指标的精确计算条件，揭示了R2精确积分改进不是普通超体积而是标量化空间体积，并提出了对应的有限和算法、求积方法及高斯代理公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯多目标优化中，超体积指标和R2指标虽常用于算法设计，但二者的几何本质不同。本文旨在精确阐明哪些偏好变换能保持精确计算、帕累托兼容性和单调性，哪些变换会改变底层几何结构。

Method: 1) 超体积方面：通过Deng表示重新审视经典EHVI，提出乘积密度加权EHVI在愿望坐标下的形式，讨论基于锥的EHVI作为线性锥变换后的普通EHVI，并与截断EHVI区分开。2) R2方面：证明精确积分R2改进一般不是普通目标空间加权超体积，其障碍是低维的（勒贝格密度超体积无法检测切比雪夫标量化可检测的边界贡献），进而证明ER2I是标量化空间体积，即切比雪夫影子测度。

Result: 超体积方面：明确了各类EHVI变体的精确计算条件和单调性保持情况（截断EHVI可能违反方差单调性）。R2方面：ER2I不是目标空间超体积，而是标量化空间中的切比雪夫影子体积。基于此表示提出了离散R2的有限和ER2I算法、精确积分R2的求积方法，以及以标量高斯期望改进积分形式表达ER2I的高斯代理公式。

Conclusion: 超体积与R2指标在几何上本质不同，但通过适当的空间变换（标量化空间视角），R2改进可以转化为可精确计算的形式，为贝叶斯多目标优化提供了新的理论工具和算法框架。

Abstract: This paper studies preference-shaped expected improvement criteria for Bayesian multiobjective optimization. We consider two indicator families which are often used for similar algorithmic purposes, but which are geometrically different. The hypervolume indicator is based on a dystopian reference point and measures dominated volume in objective space. The R2 indicator is based on a utopian point and evaluates approximation sets through weighted Tchebycheff scalarization envelopes. The purpose of the paper is to make precise which preference transformations preserve exact computation, Pareto compatibility, and monotonicity properties, and which transformations change the underlying geometry. On the hypervolume side, we revisit canonical EHVI through the Deng representation, formulate product-density weighted EHVI in desirability coordinates, discuss cone-based EHVI as ordinary EHVI after a linear cone transformation, and separate these cases from truncated EHVI, where variance monotonicity may fail. On the R2 side, we prove that exact integral R2 improvement is not, in general, an ordinary objective-space weighted hypervolume. The obstruction is lower-dimensional: Lebesgue-density hypervolume cannot see certain boundary contributions that Tchebycheff scalarizations still detect. We then show that exact integral R2 improvement is exactly a scalarization-space volume, namely the measure of the Tchebycheff shadow between the incumbent scalarization envelope and the reference envelope. This representation yields finite-sum ER2I algorithms for discrete R2, quadrature methods for exact integral R2, and an achievement-space Gaussian surrogate formulation in which ER2I is an integral of scalar Gaussian expected improvements.

</details>


### [182] [Learning Approximate Solutions to Multiparametric Generalized Nash Equilibrium Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28757)
*A. Bemporad,T. Tatarenko*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于学习的多参数广义纳什均衡问题求解方法，利用Nikaido-Isoda间隙函数作为训练损失，避免了对昂贵数据集的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多参数广义纳什均衡问题(GNEPs)的求解在计算上非常昂贵，传统方法需要大量在线计算。同时，收集真实解的数据集成本高且困难，需要一种更高效的近似求解方法。

Method: 采用Nikaido-Isoda间隙函数作为训练损失，仅需最优响应数据；通过值函数代理近似每个智能体的最优最优响应成本，将双层优化转化为单层学习问题；同时建立了强变分稳定性假设下连续参数变分GNE选择存在的充分条件。

Result: 训练后的神经网络能够提供近似GNE解，相比在线求解器实现了数个数量级的加速。在不同问题类别上的数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了多参数GNEPs的近似求解问题，显著提升了计算效率，并提供了开源Python库。

Abstract: We propose a learning-based approach for approximating solution mappings of multiparametric generalized Nash equilibrium problems (GNEPs) with coupling in both objectives and constraints. Rather than solving a standard regression problem on a training dataset of GNEP solutions, which are expensive and possibly difficult to collect, we use the Nikaido-Isoda (NI) gap function as a training loss, which requires only best-response data. To avoid bilevel optimization, a value-function surrogate approximates each agent's optimal best-response cost and is substituted into the NI loss, yielding a single-level learning problem. Learning approximate solutions to standard multiparametric programming problems is a special case of the approach. We also establish new sufficient conditions for the existence of a continuous parametric variational GNE selection under a strong variational stability assumption that generalizes strong monotonicity. The trained neural network delivers approximate GNE solutions with speedups of several orders of magnitude over online solvers. Numerical experiments on different problem classes confirm the effectiveness of the approach. A Python library is available at https://github.com/bemporad/mpfit.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [183] [Personalized Observation Normalization for Federated Reinforcement Learning in Simulation Environments with Heterogeneity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27385)
*Yiran Pang,Zhen Ni,Xiangnan Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出个性化观测归一化（PON）方法，通过各智能体本地维护运行均值和方差来归一化状态输入，解决异构环境下联邦强化学习（FedRL）中状态转移动态差异导致的输入分布不一致和参数更新不平衡问题。实验证明PON能加速训练并取得更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦强化学习在异构环境中面临挑战：不同智能体的状态转移动态差异导致输入分布不一致，聚合时出现参数更新不平衡，影响全局策略的训练效果。

Method: 提出个性化观测归一化（PON）方法，每个智能体在本地使用持续更新的运行均值和方差对原始状态输入进行归一化，确保本地特征的尺度一致性而不在聚合时相互掩盖。

Result: 在异构MuJoCo任务上，PON方法相比基线方法加速了训练过程并取得了更优越的性能。

Conclusion: 共享归一化参数在异构环境中效果不佳，必须采用个性化统计量；PON有效解决了FedRL在异构环境中的输入分布不一致问题。

Abstract: Federated reinforcement learning (FedRL) enables multiple agents to collaboratively train a global policy without sharing raw data, making it ideal for privacy-sensitive applications. However, FedRL faces challenges in heterogeneous environments where differing state-transition dynamics lead to non-identical input distributions and imbalanced parameter updates during aggregation. Therefore, this paper develops a personalized observation normalization (PON) method, allowing each agent to locally normalize raw state inputs using a continuously updated running mean and variance. This design ensures consistent scaling of local feature without overshadowing across agents during aggregation. Furthermore, we demonstrate that sharing normalization parameters across agents is ineffective due to the diverse local input distributions, which highlights the necessity of personalized statistics. Experiments on heterogeneous MuJoCo tasks show that our developed PON accelerates training and achieves superior performance compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [184] [IGADA-IoT: IoT Sensor Energy Optimization in Wireless Sensor Networks Driven by Automatic Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27397)
*Mingchun Sun,Rongqiang Zhao,Muhammad Abdul Munnaf,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种信息间隙引导的物联网传感器自动数据增强框架(IGADA-IoT)，采用层次化多生成器协同与调度策略，通过信息间隙-模型性能联合评估与闭环方法，有效提升WSN数据增强的针对性和准确性，在多个下游模型上平均准确率提升7.27%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据增强方法依赖单一生成器和经验性确定的数量，未能建立动态信息间隙与多生成器之间的映射，且忽略了生成样本的异质性，同时缺乏联合考虑信息间隙和模型性能的评估与闭环方法。

Method: 提出IGADA-IoT框架：1) 层次化多生成器协同与调度策略(HMGCS)，增强生成样本分配的针对性和合理性；2) 信息间隙-模型性能联合评估与闭环方法(IGMP-EC)，增强增强决策的准确性，缓解欠增强和过增强风险。

Result: 多个下游模型平均准确率提升7.27%；相比先进数据增强方法平均准确率提升8.67%；相比单个生成器平均准确率提升7.24%；在UCR Archive和真实部署数据集上验证了准确性和泛化性。

Conclusion: 提出的IGADA-IoT框架通过层次化多生成器协同和闭环评估机制，有效解决了WSN中数据增强的针对性和决策准确性问题，实现了显著的性能提升和良好的泛化能力。

Abstract: In wireless sensor networks (WSNs), data augmentation is a novel method to improve sampling-frequency decision performance, thereby enabling energy optimization for IoT (Internet of Things) sensors. However, existing methods rely on a single generator and empirically determined quantities, failing to establish a mapping between dynamic information gaps and multiple generators, and overlooking the heterogeneity of generated samples. Moreover, an evaluation and a closed-loop method that jointly considers the information gap and the model performance are lacking. To address these issues, we propose an information gap-guided IoT sensor automatic data augmentation framework (IGADA-IoT) with hierarchical multi-generator collaboration and scheduling over multiple rounds. Capabilities of different generators are jointly utilized to reduce the information gaps. In the IGADA-IoT, a hierarchical multi-generator collaboration and scheduling strategy (HMGCS) is proposed to enhance the targetedness and rationality of generated sample allocation. An information gap-model performance joint evaluation and closed-loop method (IGMP-EC) is proposed to enhance the accuracy of augmentation decisions, and to mitigate the risks of under-augmentation and over-augmentation. Experimental results show that the IGADA-IoT improves the average accuracy of multiple downstream models by 7.27%. Compared with advanced data augmentation methods, the average accuracy is improved by 8.67%. Compared with the individual generators, the average accuracy is improved by 7.24%. Furthermore, public IoT sensor datasets from the UCR Archive and real-world deployments demonstrate the accuracy and generalizability of the proposed method.

</details>


### [185] [A Simple State Space Model Excels at Multivariate Time Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27406)
*Hassan Saadatmand,Geoffrey I. Webb,Hamid Rezatofighi,Mahsa Salehi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了状态空间模型（SSM）在时间序列分类（TSC）中的应用，发现轻量级对角SSM（S4D）在效率和准确率上均优于复杂的Mamba类模型，并提出了改进变体MS4和MS4N，在59个数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Mamba类架构在时间序列分类中表现出色但复杂度高，而更广泛的SSM设计空间（如对角SSM S4D）尚未被系统探索。本文旨在回答：在TSC任务中，增加模型复杂性是否必要？

Method: 首次系统比较对角SSM（S4D）与输入依赖SSM（Mamba家族）在TSC上的表现；提出MS4（线性输入投影+通道混合机制）和MS4N（归一化变体，稳定状态动力学）两个轻量级改进。

Result: 在59个数据集（MONSTER和UEA基准）上，S4D一致优于Mamba变体；MS4/MS4N在效率和准确率上超越Mamba类模型，且MS4N匹配或超越参数量大2-10倍的深度学习模型。

Conclusion: 轻量级结构化SSM可作为TSC中追求复杂性的有说服力的替代方案。

Abstract: Structured state space models (SSMs) have recently emerged as a promising foundation for sequence modeling, with Mamba-based architectures demonstrating strong performance through input-dependent state transitions, albeit at considerable complexity. However, their application to time-series classification (TSC) has been largely limited to Mamba-style architectures, leaving the broader SSM design space underexplored. We present the first systematic study spanning diagonal SSMs (S4D) and input-dependent SSMs (Mamba family) on large-scale TSC benchmarks, asking whether such complexity is necessary for top performance. Our results reveal a surprising finding: S4D consistently outperforms Mamba-based variants in both accuracy and efficiency, challenging the assumption that increased complexity translates to meaningful gains in TSC. Building on this, we introduce MS4, lightweight modifications to S4D via a linear input projection and channel-mixing mechanism, and MS4N, a normalized variant that stabilizes state dynamics with negligible overhead. Evaluated on 59 datasets across MONSTER (up to 60 million samples, 50K timesteps, 82 classes) and the UEA benchmark, against 15 baselines, MS4 and MS4N consistently outperform Mamba-based models while remaining more efficient, and MS4N matches or surpasses competing deep learning models that are roughly 2x and 10x larger in parameters. These results position lightweight structured SSMs as a compelling alternative to scaling complexity for TSC.

</details>
