### [186] [$E^3$-Agent: An Executable and Evolving Agent for Resource Management of Edge Generative Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27428)
*Rui Bao,Yaping Sun,Zhiyong Chen,Feng Yang,Meixia Tao,Nan Li,Wenjun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出E³-Agent，一个用于边缘AIGC资源管理的可执行且可演化的智能体。它通过分离快速路径路由器（毫秒级调度）和慢速路径大语言模型元控制器（处理环境变化），实现从执行反馈中在线学习，在动态边缘环境下将平均延迟降低65%-73%，接近Oracle最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘生成式推理部署面临两个实际挑战：每设备每模型的性能在部署时往往未知，且由于用户驱动的语义事件、后台负载和设备变动而呈现非平稳性。离线调优的固定资源管理策略在此类动态环境中变得脆弱且维护成本高昂。

Method: E³-Agent采用双路径架构：快速路径路由器负责毫秒级调度决策，慢速路径由事件驱动的LLM元控制器通过工具接口暴露的控制面（包括风险门控、路由器配置、快速性能校准）来应对环境漂移。智能体从执行反馈中在线学习，持续适应未知且时变的服务时间映射。

Result: 在MLPerf驱动的离散事件模拟器上，覆盖冷启动热身和三种动态场景（语义动态、设备变动、隐藏漂移），E³-Agent相比最佳静态基线降低延迟65%-73%，与在线全信息Oracle的差距仅为7%-10%，并能有效抑制语义退化下的卡顿率。

Conclusion: E³-Agent通过将LLM元控制器与快速调度路径结合，实现了在未知且动态的边缘环境中持续自适应性能优化，验证了将大语言模型作为边缘资源管理元控制器的有效性。

Abstract: Edge deployments of generative inference increasingly face two practical realities: per-device per-model performance is often unknown at deployment time, and it is non-stationary due to user-driven semantic events, background load, and device churn. Consequently, a resource manager that is tuned offline under a fixed regime can become brittle and expensive to maintain. This paper presents $E^3$-Agent, an executable and evolving agent for edge artificial intelligence generated content (AIGC) resource management. $E^3$-Agent separates a fast-path router that makes millisecond-level dispatch decisions from a slow-path, event-driven large language model (LLM) meta-controller that mitigates regime shifts through a small, explicit control surface exposed via a tool interface, including risk gating, router configuration, and rapid performance calibration. The agent learns online from execution feedback and continuously adapts to unknown and time-varying service-time mappings. We evaluate $E^3$-Agent in a discrete-event simulator driven by MLPerf-derived device-model measurement priors, covering cold-start warmup and three dynamic regimes: semantic dynamics, device churn, and hidden drift. Across the dynamic scenarios, $E^3$-Agent reduces average latency by 65%-73% compared to the best static baseline, stays within 7%-10% of an online full-information Oracle used for evaluation, and effectively suppresses stutter rate under semantic degradation.

</details>


### [187] [Tackling Multimodal Learning Challenges with Mixture-of-Expert: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27431)
*Liangwei Nathan Zheng,Wei Emma Zhang,Olaf Maennel,Lin Yue,Weitong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该综述系统回答了“混合专家模型（MoE）如何有效解决多模态学习挑战”这一核心问题，从三个关键视角（高效多模态引擎、多模态表示学习器、多模态适配器）全面梳理了MoE在多模态学习中的方法、进展与未来方向，并指出了可解释路由、专家通信、模态集成和终身多模态学习等关键研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有综述要么单独评估多模态学习，要么单独评估MoE，忽略了二者之间的独特交互作用；缺乏一份全面且系统地回顾MoE方法如何应对多模态挑战的综述。

Method: 本文从三个关键视角进行综述：(1) MoE作为高效多模态引擎——通过将计算成本与参数增长解耦、利用选择性专家激活减少模态冗余来实现可扩展的多模态建模；(2) MoE作为多模态表示学习器——整合互补的多意见专家知识以丰富对齐和交互表示；(3) MoE作为多模态适配器——提供模块化灵活机制以建模模态不平衡和模态缺失等不完美数据场景。

Result: 通过广泛的文献回顾，识别出关键研究空白，包括可解释路由、专家通信、模态集成和终身多模态学习。

Conclusion: 该综述为未来研究构建了基础，致力于发展可解释且可持续的多模态混合专家系统。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) presents a naturally compatible and scalable framework for multimodal learning, demonstrating strong adaptability across diverse modalities and tasks. Despite its growing success, a comprehensive and systematic review on the MoE metho addressing multimodal challenges remains lacking. Existing surveys tend to evaluate either multimodal learning or MoE independently from method taxonomy, overlooking the unique interplay between them. This survey fills that gap by answering a central question: \textit{How does MoE effectively resolve multimodal challenges?} We approach this from three key perspectives: (1) \textbf{MoE as an Efficient Multimodal Engine:} enabling scalable multimodal modeling by decoupling computational cost from parameter growth and mitigating modality redundancy through selective expert activation; (2) \textbf{MoE as a Multimodal Representation Learner:} integrating complementary multi-opinion expert knowledge to enrich alignment and interaction representations; and (3) \textbf{MoE as a Multimodal Adapter:} providing a modular and flexible mechanism to model imperfect data scenarios such as modality imbalance and missing modality. Through our extensive literature review, we identify critical research gaps, including interpretable routing, expert communication, modality integration, and lifelong multimodal learning. We position this survey as a foundation for future research toward interpretable and sustainable multimodal Mixture-of-Experts system.

</details>


### [188] [Metric-Aware PCA as a Linear Instance of Geometric Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27456)
*Michael Leznik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统地将度量感知主成分分析（MAPCA）定位在几何深度学习框架中，建立了从对称群、等变性、不变性到几何先验的精确对应词典，并通过唯一性定理证明了IPCA的特殊地位，最后拓展到核PCA、谱图方法和深度MAPCA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将MAPCA纳入几何深度学习框架，揭示其与对称群、等变性等核心概念之间的理论联系，从而统一两种看似独立的方法论。

Method: 通过六个轴（域、对称群、等变性、不变性、架构原语、几何先验）构建MAPCA与几何深度学习之间的精确词典；利用唯一性定理刻画IPCA作为MAPCA族中在任意对角缩放下等变且投影到作用不动点集的唯一线性数据衍生度量。

Result: 建立了MAPCA与几何深度学习的完整对应关系；证明了IPCA的唯一性定理；提出了向核PCA、谱图方法和深度等变网络的三个扩展桥梁。

Conclusion: MAPCA可以视为几何深度学习框架中的线性原语，其度量对应几何先验，正交群对应对称群，解在该群下等变且谱不变，约束条件对应等变网络中的Schur型权重约束。

Abstract: Geometric deep learning organises neural architectures around the symmetries of their data domain, with the choice of symmetry group serving as a geometric prior that determines what representations can be learned. Metric-Aware Principal Component Analysis (MAPCA) parameterises principal component analysis by a positive-definite metric matrix, with a canonical subfamily interpolating between standard PCA and output whitening and a diagonal-metric point recovering Invariant PCA (IPCA). This paper positions MAPCA within the geometric deep learning framework. The metric is read as the geometric prior; the orthogonal group preserving it is the symmetry group it induces; MAPCA solutions are equivariant under this group with the resulting spectrum invariant; and MAPCA's defining constraint is the linear analogue of the Schur-type weight constraints used in equivariant networks. Across six axes - domain, symmetry group, equivariance, invariance, architectural primitive, and geometric prior - we construct a precise dictionary between MAPCA and geometric deep learning. The technical anchor is a uniqueness theorem characterising IPCA as the unique linear data-derived metric in the MAPCA family that is equivariant under arbitrary diagonal rescaling and projects onto the fixed-point set of the action, equivalent under normalisation to the variance-maximisation criterion in its precise form. The paper closes with three bridges: kernel PCA as the nonlinear extension, spectral graph methods as MAPCA on graphs, and a deep MAPCA construction extending the positioning into deep equivariant networks

</details>


### [189] [Comparative Analysis of Liquid Neural Networks and LSTM for Sequential Pattern Recognition: Robustness, Efficiency, and Clinical Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27467)
*Ye Kyaw Thu,Thazin Myint Oo,Thepchai Supnithi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对液态神经网络（LNNs/CfC）与RNN、LSTM在四种时序模态（神经形态事件数据、笔画绘制、手写识别、生理时间序列）上进行基准测试，并通过时间丢弃法进行压力测试，结果表明LNNs在参数效率和对稀疏数据的鲁棒性方面显著优于传统模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RNN和LSTM基于离散时间步长建模，难以捕捉真实物理过程的连续时间动态，因此需要探索能更好建模连续时间演化的神经网络架构（如LNNs/CfC）。

Method: 在四种不同时序模态（N-MNIST、QuickDraw、IAM、PhysioNet Sepsis-3）上进行综合基准测试，并采用时间丢弃法（temporal dropout）模拟数据缺失场景，以评估模型鲁棒性。

Result: LNNs在参数效率和对数据稀疏场景的鲁棒性方面一致优于传统RNN和LSTM，特别是在时序原生领域和临床环境中表现突出。

Conclusion: LNNs（CfC网络）通过连续微分方程建模隐状态演化，在处理稀疏、不规则的时序数据时具有显著优势，是传统离散时序模型的有效替代方案。

Abstract: Traditional Recurrent Neural Networks (RNNs) and Long Short-Term Memory (LSTM) units operate on discrete time steps, often failing to capture the fluid temporal dynamics of real-world physical processes. Liquid Neural Networks (LNNs), specifically Closed-form Continuous-time (CfC) networks, address this by modeling the hidden state evolution as a continuous differential equation. In this paper, we conduct a comprehensive benchmarking study across four distinct sequential modalities: neuromorphic event-based data (N-MNIST), stroke-based drawing (QuickDraw), visual handwriting (IAM), and physiological time-series (PhysioNet Sepsis-3). Furthermore, we perform a rigorous stress test using temporal dropout to evaluate model robustness against missing data. Our findings reveal that LNNs consistently provide superior parameter efficiency and significantly higher robustness in natively temporal domains and clinical environments where data sparsity is prevalent. This extended preprint provides additional background on related datasets and the LNN theoretical lineage, supplemented with a detailed appendix documenting our full implementation and experimental settings.

</details>


### [190] [Architecture-driven Shift: towards a lightweight selector for capturing the trends of logit shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27469)
*Zhong Ye,Yu Hu,Ruilin Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出架构驱动偏移量（ADS）作为计算高效的代理指标，用于在持续学习中评估预训练模型的"可塑性-稳定性"平衡能力。ADS通过将logit偏移解耦为架构依赖和数据依赖两部分，利用少量数据样本即可高效计算，并在175种以上不同架构上验证了其与真实logit偏移的强单调相关性（最低斯皮尔曼相关系数0.731），同时可作为预期校准误差的轻量级代理用于模型选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习中选择合适的预训练模型对于平衡"可塑性-稳定性"至关重要，但直接计算logit偏移的计算成本过高，而现有理论分析因假设均匀隐藏层宽度而无法适用于实际异构架构。因此需要建立异构架构与logit偏移之间的理论关系，并开发高效的计算代理指标。

Method: 将logit偏移解耦为架构依赖和数据依赖两部分，定义架构驱动偏移量（ADS）。其推导基于三个机制组件：（1）权重矩阵梯度谱范数与层宽的缩放关系；（2）新任务的优化路径长度；（3）宽网络中的渐近任务冲突。通过少量数据样本即可计算ADS。

Result: 超过175种不同架构上的实验表明，ADS与真实logit偏移之间存在强单调相关性（最低斯皮尔曼相关系数为0.731）。在三个数据集、六种场景下，ADS可作为预期校准误差（ECE）的轻量级代理，用于可靠的持续学习模型选择。

Conclusion: ADS是一种高效且理论上合理的代理指标，能够在不显著增加计算成本的情况下捕捉不同预训练架构在持续学习中的logit偏移趋势，为大规模模型选择提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Continual Learning (CL) is a practical paradigm to utilize power of deep pre-trained neural networks, but which pre-trained model has a better ability to balance ``Plasticity-Stability", deserving to be chosen? The logit shift serves as a natural proxy because it represents the logit shift in CL scenarios. However, obtaining the logit shift requires huge computational cost, which hinders large-scale model selection. Existing theoretical analyses fail to offer an efficient alternative because of the assumption of uniform hidden layer widths, which ignores the structural heterogeneity (variable width and depth) of real-world architectures. This raises a critical question: what theoretically relationship can be identified between heterogeneous architecture and logit shift on prior tasks (that the model has been trained on)? To answer the question, we decouple logit shift into architecture dependency and data dependency to establish our framework, which reveals that the combination of two dependency, defined as Architecture-driven Shift (ADS), that can capture the logit shift tendency well computable with few data samples. Specifically, for a well-optimized model on prior tasks, higher ADS is associated with a larger logit shift after training on the current task, which derived based on three mechanistic components: (1) spectral norm scaling of weight matrix gradients with layer width, (2) the optimization path length of the new task, and (3) the asymptotic task conflict in wide networks. Extensive empirical results across more than 175 diverse architectures demonstrate a strong monotonic correlation (the weakest Spearman's $r_s=0.731$) between ADS and logit shift. Practically, we demonstrate that ADS can serve as a lightweight proxy of the expected calibration error, which is a widely used metric for reliable CL model selection, on three datasets across six scenarios.

</details>


### [191] [Detect by Yourself: Self-Designing Agentic Workflows for Few-Shot Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27470)
*Tairan Huang,Qiang Chen,Yili Wang,Yueyue Ma,Changlong He,Xiu Su,Yi Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SignGAD框架，将图异常检测从训练固定检测器重新定义为设计任务条件化检测工作流，通过选择合适图编码和检测器设计来利用任务特定异常证据，并采用受保护的最终重拟合策略提升有限监督下的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图异常检测方法面临两个关键挑战：1）固定管线限制了在不同图任务间的适应性；2）弱证据无法显式地将上下文和结构异常信号纳入检测过程。

Method: 提出SignGAD框架：1）将图异常检测重新表述为设计任务条件化检测工作流的新范式；2）通过构建检测工作流选择合适图编码和检测器设计以利用任务特定异常证据；3）引入受保护的最终重拟合策略，通过校准重拟合接受度来增强有限监督下的可靠性。

Result: 在多个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，SignGAD相比最先进方法取得了优异性能。

Conclusion: SignGAD框架在图异常检测任务上具有有效性，通过自适应工作流设计克服了固定管线和弱证据的局限。

Abstract: Graph anomaly detection aims to identify anomaly nodes in attributed graphs and plays an important role in real-world applications. However, existing graph anomaly detection methods still face two key challenges: 1) fixed pipelines, which restrict their adaptability across different graph tasks under limited supervision; 2) weak evidence, which prevents them from explicitly incorporating contextual and structural anomaly signals into the detection process. In this paper, we propose a novel framework, self-designing agentic workflows for few-shot graph anomaly detection (SignGAD). Specifically, we propose a novel paradigm that reformulates graph anomaly detection task from training a fixed anomaly detector to designing task-conditioned detection workflows. By constructing detection workflows, SignGAD selects suitable graph encodings and detector designs to exploit task-specific anomaly evidence. Meanwhile, we introduce a guarded final refit strategy to refine the selected workflow by calibrating refit acceptance, enhancing reliability under limited supervision. Extensive experiments conducted on several real-world datasets demonstrate that SignGAD achieves strong performance against state-of-the-art methods, highlighting its effectiveness on graph anomaly detection tasks.

</details>


### [192] [HEAL: Resilient and Self-* Hub-based Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27475)
*Mohamed Amine Legheraba,Stefan Galkiewicz,Maria Gradinariu Potop-Butucaru,Sébastien Tixeuil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HEAL是一种新型跨层去中心化学习框架，通过自组织自修复的P2P覆盖网络结合联邦学习、Gossip和Epidemic学习的优势，利用Elevator算法动态选择聚合节点，在无故障场景下性能接近联邦学习，同时在节点崩溃和高动态环境中优于Gossip和Epidemic学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化学习方法（联邦学习存在单点故障和服务器脆弱性；Gossip/Epidemic学习收敛慢）无法同时兼顾鲁棒性、可扩展性和收敛效率，亟需一种既能保持完全去中心化又能实现快速收敛的框架。

Method: 提出HEAL框架，采用跨层设计，底层构建自组织自修复的P2P覆盖网络，上层利用Elevator算法动态提升部分节点为聚合器，融合联邦学习的聚合机制与Gossip/Epidemic学习的信息传播方式。

Result: 在无崩溃环境中，HEAL性能与联邦学习相当；在节点崩溃和高动态（churn）环境中，HEAL的收敛速度和模型质量均优于Gossip学习和Epidemic学习。

Conclusion: HEAL成功将联邦学习的高效聚合能力引入完全去中心化场景，同时具备容错性和自愈能力，为去中心化学习提供了一种兼顾性能与鲁棒性的新范式。

Abstract: Decentralized learning enhances privacy, scalability, and fault tolerance by distributing data and computation across nodes. A popular approach is Federated learning, which relies on a central aggregator, yet faces challenges such as server vulnerabilities, scalability issues, privacy risks and most importantly, the single point of failure. Alternatively Gossip Learning and Epidemic Learning offer fully decentralization through peer-to-peer exchanges of model updates, ensuring robustness and privacy, at the price of slower model convergence. In this work, we introduce a novel decentralized learning framework called HEAL. HEAL is the first cross-layer decentralized learning framework that exploits an optimized self-organizing and self-healing underlying P2P overlay combining the strengths of Federated Learning, Gossip and Epidemic Learning. Leveraging the recently proposed Elevator algorithm, HEAL promotes dynamically chosen nodes to act as aggregators. Through simulations, we demonstrate that HEAL has similar performances to that of Federated Learning in crash-free settings, while being fully decentralized and fault-tolerant. In crash and churn prone environments HEAL outperforms Gossip and Epidemic Learning.

</details>


### [193] [Balancing Fidelity and Diversity in Diffusion Models via Symmetric Attention Decomposition: Hopfield Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27476)
*Hyunmin Cho,Woo Kyoung Han,Kyong Hwan Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过分解注意力矩阵为对称和反对称部分，分别解释为能量景观结构和循环驱动，借由Hopfield稳定性度量量化特征检索稳定性，并提出通过调节循环来控制生成质量与多样性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对Transformer中注意力矩阵的几何和动力学意义理解不足，缺乏系统的方法来量化检索特征的稳定性及其与生成质量-多样性权衡之间的关系。

Method: 将pre-softmax注意力矩阵QK^T分解为对称部分和反对称部分，对称部分对应能量景观结构，反对称部分对应循环动力学；基于对称部分推导Hopfield式稳定性度量；通过调节循环（反对称部分）来控制生成中的保真度-多样性权衡。

Result: Hopfield式稳定性度量与生成中的保真度-多样性权衡之间存在有意义的关联；通过修改底层动力学的循环（反对称分量）可实现对该权衡的可控调节。

Conclusion: 注意力矩阵的对称-反对称分解为理解Transformer的联想记忆机制和生成行为提供了新的视角，并提供了一个可控的实用工具来调节生成质量与多样性。

Abstract: We characterize the pre-softmax attention matrix $\mathbf{QK^\top}$ in transformers as an associative memory matrix encoding pairwise associations between input features. By decomposing this matrix into its symmetric and skew-symmetric parts, we interpret the symmetric component as governing the structure of the energy landscape, and the skew-symmetric component as driving circulation on that landscape. Leveraging the energy formulation induced by the symmetric component, we derive Hopfield-style stability measures that quantify the stability of retrieved features. We observe meaningful correlations between Hopfield-style stability measures and the fidelity-diversity trade-offs in generation. Finally, we propose a controllable knob to modulate this trade-off by modifying the circulation of the underlying dynamics. Code is available at our GitHub (https://github.com/hyeon-cho/Attention-Symmetric-Decomposition).

</details>


### [194] [Resource-Constrained Affect Modelling via Variance Regularisation Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27479)
*Kosmas Pinitas,Konstantinos Katsifis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出方差正则化剪枝（VR）框架，在模型稀疏化过程中显式引入跨参与者稳定性约束，使情感计算模型在80%稀疏度下仍保持竞争性CCC性能，无需额外微调，适用于资源受限的交互式环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型剪枝方法仅优化稀疏度，未考虑参数移除对不同个体鲁棒性的影响，导致在跨用户场景下可靠性下降。亟需一种能同时平衡计算效率与跨参与者稳定性的剪枝策略。

Method: 提出Variance-Regularised Pruning (VR)框架，在剪枝过程中不仅考虑平均预测误差，还评估每个连接对预测准确性和跨用户变异性的联合贡献，优先保留在分布差异下仍保持可靠的参数。

Result: 在AGAIN数据集（包含9种情感诱发游戏环境的唤醒度标注）上，VR方法在80%稀疏度下无需额外微调即可保持与全模型竞争的CCC性能。

Conclusion: VR框架支持开发紧凑且鲁棒的情感模型，能够在资源受限的真实世界交互环境中可靠运行，为情感计算系统的实际部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Affective computing systems are increasingly embedded in pervasive and interactive environments, such as adaptive games, assistive technologies, and resource-constrained platforms, where computational efficiency must be balanced with reliability across diverse users. Model pruning offers an effective way to reduce computational demands, yet existing approaches typically optimise for sparsity alone, without accounting for how parameter removal impacts robustness across individuals. In this work, we introduce Variance-Regularised Pruning (VR), a pruning framework that explicitly incorporates cross-participant stability into the sparsification process. Rather than relying solely on average prediction error, VR evaluates each connection based on its joint contribution to both prediction accuracy and variability across users, prioritising parameters that remain reliable under distributional differences. We evaluate the proposed approach on the AGAIN dataset, which includes arousal annotations collected across nine affect-eliciting game environments. Experimental results demonstrate that VR maintains competitive Concordance Correlation Coefficient (CCC) performance even at 80\% sparsity without additional fine-tuning, highlighting its suitability for deployment in real-world, resource-limited affect-aware systems. Overall, the proposed framework supports the development of compact, robust affective models that can operate reliably in real-world interactive environments.

</details>


### [195] [Energy-Structured Low-Rank Adaptation for Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27482)
*Longhua Li,Lei Qi,Qi Tian,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出E²-LoRA方法，通过将知识集中和排序到主要秩中，解决持续学习中正交子空间方法的能量扩散问题，实现参数更新的低秩结构保持，并设计了动态秩分配策略平衡稳定性与可塑性，在多个基准上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正交子空间方法在持续学习中虽能缓解任务干扰，但存在能量在基向量间扩散的问题，阻碍知识紧致且耗尽了未来任务的容量。

Method: 1) 理论证明：参数更新引起的输出特征漂移本质上是低秩的，沿此漂移的主方向保留参数可最小化输出重建误差；2) E²-LoRA：通过显式排序和集中知识到主要秩中；3) 动态秩分配策略：联合优化能量保留和模型可塑性，平衡稳定性与可塑性。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，E²-LoRA达到了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: E²-LoRA通过能量集中与排序解决了正交子空间方法的容量耗尽问题，动态秩分配策略有效平衡了持续学习中的稳定性-可塑性困境。

Abstract: While orthogonal subspace methods try to mitigate task interference in Continual Learning (CL), they often suffer from energy diffusion across the basis, hindering knowledge compaction and exhausting capacity for future tasks. We observe that output feature drift induced by parameter updates is inherently low-rank, and theoretically prove that preserving parameters along the principal directions of this drift minimizes the output reconstruction error. Motivated by this, we propose \textbf{E}nergy-Concentrated and \textbf{E}nergy-Ordered \textbf{Lo}w-\textbf{R}ank \textbf{A}daptation (E$^2$-LoRA). By explicitly ordering and concentrating knowledge into leading ranks, E$^2$-LoRA frees capacity for subsequent tasks. Furthermore, we design a dynamic rank allocation strategy to balance stability and plasticity by jointly optimizing energy retention and model plasticity. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks demonstrate that E$^2$-LoRA achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [196] [Federated Learning for Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection in Industrial Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27486)
*Khayyam Nosrati,Martin Uray,Saverio Messineo,Olaf Sassnick,Stefan Huber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对联邦学习框架下多变量时间序列异常检测面临的数据集挑战，提出一个包含循环动态特性的新数据集，并在该数据集和公共基准数据集上评估了多种MTSAD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集无法同时满足规模、标签准确性和无常见缺陷的需求，且离散工业自动化中常见的循环过程行为在MTSAD研究中未被充分探索。

Method: 设计了一个具有循环动态特性的数据集（源于离散自动化过程的重复性），并在该数据集和公共基准数据集上评估了多种MTSAD方法。

Result: 提出了新数据集，并在多个数据集上进行了MTSAD方法评估，但摘要未给出具体数值结果。

Conclusion: 循环过程行为对MTSAD具有重要意义，新数据集有助于更全面地评估联邦学习下的异常检测方法。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) has broadened the horizon for multivariate time series anomaly detection (MTSAD). However, benchmarking such anomaly detection methods within FL paradigm poses data-centric challenges. The existing datasets do not counteract these challenges since they do not simultaneously provide sufficient scale, accurate labels, and freedom from common flaws. In addition, the role of cyclic process behavior, which is common in discrete industrial automation, remains underexplored for MTSAD for the current state of research. This paper aims to shed more light on the literature and address these gaps by introducing a dataset designed with cyclic dynamics arising from the repetitive nature of discrete automation processes and evaluates selected MTSAD methods on both the proposed dataset and a public benchmark dataset.

</details>


### [197] [GenSBI: Generative Methods for Simulation-Based Inference in JAX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27499)
*Aurelio Amerio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GenSBI是一个基于JAX的开源库，实现了流匹配、分数匹配和去噪扩散三种生成模型，提供三种可互换的Transformer架构（包括一种新提出的联合密度估计架构），用于模拟推演中的密度估计，在标准基准上达到近理想的C2ST分数（0.50-0.56）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最广泛使用的SBI库基于PyTorch，而许多研究者使用JAX开发前向模型和分析流程，缺乏原生的JAX选项。

Method: 在JAX中实现流匹配、分数匹配和去噪扩散三种生成方法；提供三种基于Transformer的架构（SimFormer、Flux1及新提出的Flux1Joint用于联合密度估计）；通过统一接口解耦生成方法、神经骨干网络和推理模式；支持自定义架构和领域特定嵌入网络；提供从训练到后验校准的端到端工作流。

Result: 在标准SBI基准测试上，以最小的任务特定调优实现了近理想的平均C2ST分数（0.50-0.56，理想值为0.50），所有测试配置下后验覆盖均良好校准。

Conclusion: GenSBI为JAX用户提供了一个原生、灵活且高效的SBI工具，验证了其框架的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: Flow and diffusion generative models have established themselves as widely adopted density estimators for simulation-based inference (SBI), extending naturally from neural posterior estimation to likelihood and joint density estimation. Their principled optimization objectives and freedom from architectural constraints have driven rapid adoption across the natural sciences. Yet the most widely used SBI libraries remain PyTorch-based, leaving researchers who develop their forward models and analysis pipelines in JAX without a native option. We present GenSBI, an open-source library that implements flow matching, score matching, and denoising diffusion entirely in JAX. The library offers three transformer-based architectures - SimFormer, Flux1, and a novel Flux1Joint that extends gate-modulated transformer blocks to joint density estimation - all interchangeable through a unified interface that decouples generative method, neural backbone, and inference mode. GenSBI provides an end-to-end workflow from training through posterior calibration (SBC, TARP, LC2ST) and supports custom architectures with domain-specific embedding networks. We validate the framework on standard SBI benchmarks, achieving near-ideal mean C2ST scores (0.50-0.56, where 0.50 is ideal) on SBIBM tasks with minimal per-task tuning and well-calibrated posterior coverage across all tested configurations. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/aurelio-amerio/GenSBI.

</details>


### [198] [SparseOpt: Addressing Normalization-induced Gradient Skew in Sparse Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27541)
*Mohammed Adnan,Rohan Jain,Tom Jacobs,Ekansh Sharma,Rahul G. Krishnan,Rebekka Burkholz,Yani Ioannou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现批归一化(BN)对动态稀疏训练(DST)有负面影响，导致收敛变慢。为此提出稀疏感知优化器SparseOpt，在保持稀疏性的同时加速收敛并提升泛化性能，通过理论和实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DST方法虽能降低计算量，但收敛速度远慢于稠密训练，往往需要相近的训练时间才能达到相似精度。现有研究忽视了归一化层与稀疏训练的交互影响，需系统性地解决此问题以使DST在实际中与稠密训练具有竞争力。

Method: 提出SparseOpt稀疏感知优化器，针对批归一化在稀疏训练中的负面影响进行优化。通过理论分析和实验验证BN对稀疏训练的不利影响，并设计专门优化器缓解该问题。

Result: 在CIFAR-100和ImageNet上使用ResNet模型进行实验，结果表明SparseOpt在收敛速度和泛化性能上均优于现有DST方法，实现了更快的收敛和更好的泛化。

Conclusion: 首次系统性地研究了批归一化、稀疏层和DST之间的交互作用，揭示了当前归一化层在稀疏训练中的局限性，提出的SparseOpt使DST向实际中与稠密训练竞争迈出了重要一步。

Abstract: Dynamic Sparse Training (DST) methods train neural networks by maintaining sparsity while dynamically adapting the network topology. Despite the promise of reduced computation, DST methods converge significantly slower than dense training, often requiring comparable training time to achieve similar accuracy. We demonstrate both analytically and empirically that Batch Normalization (BN) adversely affects sparse training, and propose SparseOpt, a sparsity-aware optimizer, to address this. Experiments on ResNet models across CIFAR-100 and ImageNet demonstrate consistently faster convergence and improved generalization with our proposed method. Our work highlights the limitations of current normalization layers in sparse training and provides the first systematic study of the interaction between Batch Normalization, sparse layers, and DST, taking a significant step toward making DST practically competitive with dense training.

</details>


### [199] [The Fundamental Limits of Fraud Detection in Card Payment Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27557)
*Gaurav Dhama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信息论角度证明，支付欺诈检测的性能瓶颈并非模型架构，而是支付生态系统中固有的信息质量缺陷（延迟、删失、污染、反事实缺失），并推导出这些缺陷以乘法方式降低学习率下限，指出改善报告质量比增加模型复杂度更能有效降低遗憾值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管模型架构不断进步，但卡支付欺诈检测的性能提升却趋于停滞。作者认为这并非函数逼近或优化失败，而是支付生态系统固有的结构性信息损伤所致，现有文献对此缺乏系统性理论分析。

Method: 将卡授权形式化为一个具有延迟、删失、污染和反事实缺失反馈的序列决策问题，推导出极小极大遗憾下界，证明信息损伤以乘法方式进入可达到学习率的分母，并分析发行方异质性对可学习性的影响。

Result: 信息损伤（延迟、删失、污染、反事实缺失）在可达到学习率的分母中以乘法方式相互作用；改善发行方报告质量或减少删失比增加模型复杂度能更大程度降低遗憾值；发行方之间的异质性会进一步恶化可学习性。

Conclusion: 支付网络欺诈检测在理论上比标准在线学习设置更困难，生态系统信息质量是关键瓶颈，应优先投资于报告基础设施、争议处理质量和选择性探索策略。

Abstract: Card payment fraud detection is usually framed as a supervised classification problem. Although this approach has generated practical progress, improvement has remained incremental despite major advances in model architecture. We argue that this is not mainly a failure of function approximation or optimization, but a consequence of structural information impairments inherent to the payment ecosystem.
  We formalize card authorization as a sequential decision problem with delayed, censored, corrupted, and counterfactually missing feedback. We derive a minimax regret lower bound showing that these impairments enter multiplicatively in the denominator of the achievable learning rate. The bound implies that improving issuer reporting quality or reducing censorship can yield larger reductions in the regret floor than increasing model complexity. We also show that heterogeneity across issuers worsens learnability beyond what average impairment rates suggest.
  The paper contributes a theory of why fraud detection in payment networks is fundamentally harder than in standard online learning settings, identifies ecosystem information quality as the key bottleneck, and provides a theoretical basis for prioritizing investments in reporting infrastructure, dispute process quality, and selective exploration. The paper is theory-first and does not rely on proprietary transaction data.

</details>


### [200] [Information-theoretic Multimodal Representation Learning for Electrocardiogram Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27583)
*Phu X. Nguyen,Konstantinos Kontras,Wei Dai,Huy Phan,Christos Chatzichristos,Paul Pu Liang,Bert Vandenberk,Maarten De Vos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MERIT框架，基于信息论视角联合心电图信号结构保持与临床语义整合，通过双分支预训练（掩码心电图建模+心电图-文本对比对齐）学习更具信息量的心电图表征，在PTB-XL等基准上显著提升分类、零样本评估和临床文本生成性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态方法将心电图与临床报告对齐以融入诊断语义，但临床报告未能保留心电图波形中丰富的生理结构信息，特别是在从粗粒度诊断类别到细粒度形态的多层次抽象上存在不足。

Method: 从信息论视角推导可优化目标，联合保持信号结构与整合临床语义；提出MERIT双分支预训练框架，包含掩码心电图建模和心电图-文本对比对齐两个分支。

Result: 在PTB-XL All上F1提升超3%，SubClass上F1提升超5%；零样本评估中SubClass AUC提升+2.66%、F1提升+2.11%；在多种分布偏移设置下具有鲁棒性；用于心电图条件临床文本生成时，在ROUGE和METEOR等指标上均有提升。

Conclusion: MERIT学习的心电图表征更具信息量和临床意义，尤其适用于细粒度临床应用。

Abstract: Electrocardiograms (ECGs) are widely used non-invasive measurements of cardiac activity and play a central role in clinical diagnosis. Recent multimodal approaches align ECG signals with clinical reports to incorporate diagnostic semantics, but clinical reports often fail to preserve the rich physiological structure of ECG waveforms, particularly across multiple levels of abstraction ranging from coarse diagnostic categories to fine-grained morphology. To address this limitation, we formulate ECG representation learning from an information-theoretic perspective and derive a tractable objective that jointly preserves signal structure and integrates clinical semantics. Based on this principle, we propose \textbf{MERIT} (Multimodal ECG Representation via Information Theory), a dual-branch pretraining framework combining masked ECG modeling with ECG--text contrastive alignment. Extensive experiments on PTB-XL and additional benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements over prior methods, including gains exceeding $3%$ F1 on PTB-XL All and $5%$ F1 on SubClass classification. In zero-shot evaluation, MERIT further improves performance by up to $ +2.66\%$ AUC and $ +2.11\%$ F1 on PTB-XL SubClass, while also demonstrating robustness under multiple distribution-shift settings. Moreover, leveraging the learned ECG representations for ECG-conditioned clinical text generation with large language models improves text quality across several metrics, including ROUGE and METEOR. Together, these results demonstrate that MERIT learns more informative and clinically meaningful ECG representations, particularly for fine-grained clinical applications.

</details>


### [201] [Gradient Transformer: Learning to Generate Updates for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27591)
*Binh-Nguyen Nguyen,Khang Tran,NhatHai Phan,Issa Khalil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无数据知识蒸馏框架，通过梯度变换器（Grad-Transformer）将基于私有数据微调的小模型更新向量转换为大模型更新向量，使资源受限组织无需共享私有数据即可完成大模型微调，实验表明该方法在语言建模和推理任务上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多组织缺乏计算资源在私有（不可共享）数据上微调大语言模型（LLMs）以提升效用，而单独微调小语言模型（TinyLMs）效果不佳，存在资源与隐私瓶颈。

Method: 提出无数据知识蒸馏框架，核心是梯度变换器（Grad-Transformer），利用影子数据集学习从TinyLM更新向量到LLM更新向量的映射关系，使第三方可在不访问组织私有数据的情况下，根据TinyLM更新向量生成LLM更新向量，支持多组织协作联合更新LLM。

Result: 在语言建模和推理任务上的大量实验表明，Grad-Transformer显著优于最先进的知识蒸馏基线，即使在严格差分隐私保护下仍表现优异。

Conclusion: 该框架实现了在保护私有数据且无需大量计算资源的前提下，有效微调大语言模型，兼具性能提升与成本效率。

Abstract: Many organizations lack computational resources to fine-tune large language models (LLMs) on private (unshareable) data for better utility, while fine-tuning tiny language models (TinyLMs) alone performs poorly. To address this bottleneck, we propose a data-free knowledge distillation framework that generates LLM update vectors based on TinyLMs fine-tuned on private data. An update vector is a vector of parameter changes from an initial model to its fine-tuned version on a dataset, capturing the effect of cumulative gradient steps during fine-tuning. The key idea of our framework is a novel Gradient Transformer that transforms TinyLM's update vectors into LLM's update vectors. As derived from shadow datasets, Grad-Transformer captures the correlation between TinyLM and LLM update vectors, enabling third-party providers to generate LLM update vectors given the organization's TinyLM update vectors without accessing the organization's private data. The framework supports multi-organization collaboration to jointly update LLMs, improving performance and cost-efficiency. Extensive experiments across language modeling and reasoning tasks show that Grad-Transformer remarkably outperforms state-of-the-art knowledge distillation baselines, even under strict differential privacy protection.

</details>


### [202] [The Energy Blind Spot: NVIDIA's Flagship Edge AI Hardware Cannot Support Process-Level Energy Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27599)
*Deepak Panigrahy,Aakash Tyagi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对ASUS Ascent GX10（GB10 SoC）平台进行能量可观测性审计，发现该平台仅暴露GPU瞬时功耗（通过NVML），CPU能量计数器、INA电源轨监控、IPMI/BMC、SCMI powercap协议均不可用。尽管MediaTek固件已通过未文档化的ACPI接口（SPBM）计算每轨能量，但NVIDIA表示"没有计划暴露CPU轨信息"。因此，x86上通过RAPL实现的每进程能量归因在该平台上不可复现。本文形式化了能量归因AI的硬件需求规范，提出了基于外部直流计量结合GPU减法的临时校准桥接方案，并识别了通过SCMI powercap的标准路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic AI工作负载在边缘部署（如GB10桌面AI系统）中能量开销巨大——编排结构导致能耗比线性基线高4.33倍，多步推理任务中OOI可达7.63倍，且CPU侧处理占总延迟90.6%和动态能耗44%。然而，当前硬件平台缺乏足够的能量可观测性，无法进行精细化的每进程能量归因，阻碍了低功耗AI系统的优化。

Method: 对ASUS Ascent GX10（GB10 SoC）进行系统性能量可观测性审计，检查所有支持的软件接口（CPU计数器、INA电源轨、IPMI/BMC、SCMI powercap、NVML等）；发现MediaTek固件通过未文档化的ACPI接口（SPBM）计算每轨能量；与NVIDIA沟通确认CPU轨信息不计划暴露；形式化能量归因AI的硬件需求规范；提出外部DC计量结合GPU减法的校准桥接方案。

Result: GB10平台仅可通过NVML获取GPU瞬时功耗；无CPU能量计数器、无INA电源轨监控、无IPMI/BMC、无SCMI powercap；MediaTek固件已内部计算每轨能量但未对用户暴露；NVIDIA明确无计划暴露CPU轨信息；每进程能量归因在支持的接口上不可复现。

Conclusion: 倡导低功耗计算社区将能量可观测性作为一等硬件需求；形式化规范了能量归因AI的硬件要求；提出了临时校准桥接方案和SCMI powercap标准化路径。

Abstract: Agentic AI workloads - where a single user goal triggers multi-step orchestration, tool calls, retries, and failure recovery - are being targeted for edge deployment, with NVIDIA, Dell, HP, ASUS, MSI, Acer, and Gigabyte all shipping GB10-based desktop AI systems in 2026. We recently demonstrated that orchestration structure dominates agentic energy cost, with workflows consuming 4.33x more energy per successful goal than linear baselines and OOI reaching 7.63x for multi-step reasoning tasks. Separately, Rajat et al. show that CPU-side processing accounts for up to 90.6% of total latency and 44% of total dynamic energy in agentic workloads. We report a systematic energy-observability audit of the ASUS Ascent GX10 (GB10 SoC) and find that the platform exposes no CPU energy counter, no INA power-rail monitor, no IPMI/BMC, and no SCMI powercap protocol through any supported software interface. The only on-device energy telemetry is instantaneous GPU power via NVML. We further discover that the MediaTek firmware already computes per-rail energy internally via an undocumented ACPI interface (SPBM), but NVIDIA states there are "no plans to expose CPU rail information." On-device per-process energy attribution - as performed on x86 via RAPL - is therefore not reproducible on this platform through supported interfaces. We formalize a hardware requirements specification for energy-attributed AI, propose an interim calibration bridge using external DC metering combined with GPU subtraction, and identify a standards-track path via SCMI powercap. Our findings motivate the low-carbon computing community to demand energy observability as a first-class hardware requirement.

</details>


### [203] [Evaluating Local Explainability Metrics for Machine Learning Models on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27618)
*Tomás Pereira,João Vitorino,Eva Maia,Isabel Praça*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了局部可解释性技术（LIME、Kernel SHAP、Feature Ablation）在复杂表格分类任务中的可信度，通过对32个数据集和多种机器学习模型的基准测试发现，解释质量与模型预测性能并不总是相关，数据集复杂性和特征分布才是影响解释可靠性的主要因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管可解释性技术被广泛用于理解AI行为，但生成的解释可能并不可靠，尤其是在处理复杂表格数据时。现有研究缺乏对解释可信度的系统评估，因此需要探究局部可解释性技术在实际表格分类任务中的忠实性、鲁棒性和复杂性。

Method: 针对LIME、Kernel SHAP和Feature Ablation三种局部可解释性技术，在32个数据集上对不同类型机器学习模型进行基准测试。评估三个属性：对模型预测的忠实性、对输入数据变化的鲁棒性、解释的复杂性。通过模型性能范围分析将样本分为"共识正确"（所有模型预测正确）和"共识错误"（所有模型预测错误）两组进行比较。

Result: 解释质量与模型的预测性能并不总是相关；数据集复杂性和特征分布是影响解释质量和可靠性的主要因素，而非模型本身的预测表现。

Conclusion: 局部可解释性技术的可信度受到数据集固有特性的显著影响，研究人员和实践者在使用这些解释技术时需要谨慎，不能仅依赖模型性能来判断解释的可靠性。

Abstract: Despite the wide use of explainability techniques to attempt to understand the behavior of Artificial Intelligence (AI), the generated explanations may not always be reliable. An explanation can appear plausible to humans but fail to capture the internal reasoning of a model, particularly when dealing with complex tabular data. This paper studies the trustworthiness of local explainability techniques when applied to complex tabular classification tasks, considering evaluated metrics for three main properties: faithfulness to the model's predictions, robustness to input data variations, and complexity of the explanation itself. A benchmark was performed for Local Interpretable Model-Agnostic Explanations (LIME), Kernel SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP), and Feature Ablation techniques, across 32 datasets and different types of machine learning models. Model performance ranges were analyzed to identify two groups: consensus-correct, which are samples that all models predicted correctly, and consensus-wrong, samples that all models predicted incorrectly. The obtained results demonstrate that that the explanations are not always correlated with a model's predictive performance. Instead, dataset complexity and feature distributions seem to be the main factors affecting explanation quality and reliability.

</details>


### [204] [Reward Transfer from Inverse Reinforcement Learning: A Coupled Minimax Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27834)
*Guang-Yuan Hao,Lars van der Laan,Aurélien Bibaut,Nathan Kallus*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了将逆强化学习从源环境学到的奖励迁移到目标环境进行强化学习的问题，提出联合求解源和目标环境的贝尔曼方程系统的方法，相比于顺序方法可消除源环境贝尔曼残差的一阶影响，并提供了理论保证和实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专家演示通常在有控制的环境（如模拟器）中收集，但实际应用环境可能不同。如何在环境变化的情况下，将从演示中学到的奖励有效迁移到新环境中的强化学习，是一个自然且重要的问题。

Method: 将问题建模为源和目标环境之间的联合贝尔曼方程系统，开发针对目标软Q函数的最小最大估计器。提出耦合方法（联合求解源和目标方程系统），并与顺序方法（先估计源奖励再代入目标控制问题）进行对比。

Result: 耦合方法消除了源环境贝尔曼残差的一阶影响；给出了两种方法的局部行为刻画、有限样本软Q函数误差界以及软控制策略的遗憾界保证；通过败血症模拟器实验验证了理论比较结果。

Conclusion: 联合求解源和目标环境的奖励迁移方法优于顺序方法，在理论上和实证上都证明了耦合方法的优势。

Abstract: We study the transfer of rewards learned using inverse reinforcement learning from expert demonstrations in one environment to reinforcement learning in a new, different environment. This arises naturally when demonstrations are collected in a controlled environment. We formulate the problem as a joint system of Bellman equations across the source and target environments and develop minimax estimators for the target soft-$q$-function. Whereas a sequential solution approach first estimates the source reward and then plugs it into the target control problem, a coupled approach solves the source and target system of equations jointly. We show that, in contrast to the sequential approach, the coupled approach removes the first-order influence of source Bellman residual error. We characterize the local behavior of each approach, develop finite-sample soft-$q$-function error bounds, and prove regret guarantees for the resulting soft-control policy. An empirical investigation using a sepsis simulator validates the theoretical comparison.

</details>


### [205] [Supervised Distributional Reduction via Optimal Transport and Dependence Maximization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27619)
*Sai-Aakash Ramesh,Archit Sood,Andrew Corbett,Tim Dodwell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出监督式分布约简（SDR）算法，通过融合Gromov-Wasserstein目标与显式依赖最大化，学习兼顾数据几何结构与任务预测信号的紧致表示，并支持构建非平稳高斯过程核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据约简需要在压缩保真度与预测性能之间取得平衡，现有的有监督分布约简方法探索不足，难以在学习紧致表示的同时保留任务相关信号。

Method: 基于Fused Gromov-Wasserstein（FGW）目标对齐输入分布的 relational structure 与代表性点集，并引入显式依赖最大化项增强嵌入对预测信号的捕获能力，从而得到兼顾几何结构与监督信息的紧致表示。

Result: SDR能产生反映几何结构与监督信息的紧致表示，并自然诱导出数据依赖的非平稳几何结构，可用于构建自适应高斯过程核。

Conclusion: SDR为有监督表示学习与非平稳核设计提供了基于最优传输的新视角，在兼顾压缩与预测保真度方面具有优势。

Abstract: Learning representations that capture both intrinsic data geometry and target-relevant structure remains a fundamental challenge, particularly in settings where data reduction must balance compression with predictive fidelity. While distributional reduction-encompassing joint clustering and dimensionality reduction-offers a principled way to summarize data, its supervised variants remain relatively under-explored, despite the importance of retaining task-relevant signal for downstream prediction and decision-making. We propose Supervised Distributional Reduction (SDR), an algorithm for learning target-aware representations by combining optimal transport with explicit dependence maximization. SDR builds on the Fused Gromov-Wasserstein (FGW) objective to align the relational structure of the input distribution with a set of representative points, while augmenting it with a direct dependence term that encourages the learned embeddings to capture predictive signal more explicitly. This results in compact representations that reflect both geometric structure and supervision. Beyond representation learning, SDR naturally induces a data-dependent, non-stationary geometry that can be leveraged for settings such as Gaussian Process (GP) modelling. By redefining distances through target-aware distributional alignment, SDR enables the construction of adaptive kernels that respond to local variations in both data geometry and supervision, offering an optimal transport-based perspective on non-stationary kernel design.

</details>


### [206] [Continual Learning in Modern Hopfield Networks with an Application to Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27975)
*Ken Takeda,Masafumi Oizumi,Ryo Karakida*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过现代Hopfield能量分析扩散模型在持续学习中的遗忘机制，证明高能量、离群样本更容易被遗忘，并据此提出基于能量的重放样本选择策略，在MHN和扩散模型实验中验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型作为基础模型被广泛使用，需要通过序列微调进行自适应，这使得持续学习成为一个重要问题。然而，在生成模型中进行持续学习时，任务变化后所学分布的哪些方面最容易丢失、应该优先重放哪些样本等问题尚不明确。

Method: 利用现代Hopfield网络（MHN）与扩散模型之间的已知联系，将MHN中的分析迁移到扩散模型。定义内在遗忘为任务变化后Hopfield能量的增加。在可解场景中证明高能量、离群样本的能量增幅大于聚类样本，即位于尖锐孤立盆地中的样本更容易遗忘。进一步分析记忆重放，证明重放对高能量样本特别有效，从而提出基于能量的重放样本选择策略。

Result: 在MHN和两种扩散模型（Stable Diffusion和像素空间DDPM）的持续学习实验中，Hopfield能量能够追踪基于重构的遗忘；重放实验显示出与能量相关的遗忘缓解效果，与MHN分析一致。

Conclusion: Hopfield能量可以有效刻画和预测扩散模型在持续学习中的遗忘模式，基于能量的样本选择策略能优先重放易遗忘的高能量样本，为生成模型的持续学习提供理论指导。

Abstract: Generative models, including diffusion models, are increasingly used as foundation models and adapted through sequential fine-tuning, making continual learning an essential problem setting. However, continual learning in such generative models remains poorly understood: after a task change, what aspects of the learned distribution are most easily lost, and what replay samples should be prioritized? We address these questions through the modern Hopfield energy. Recent links between modern Hopfield networks (MHNs) and diffusion models allow analyses in MHNs to be transferred to diffusion models. We introduce intrinsic forgetting as an increase in Hopfield energy after the task change. In tractable settings in an MHN, we prove that high-energy, outlier-like samples undergo a larger energy increase than cluster-like samples, implying that samples located in sharp, isolated basins are more forgettable. We further analyze memory replay and show that replay is particularly effective for high-energy samples, enabling an energy-based selection of replay samples. We validate these predictions in experiments on MHNs and two diffusion models under continual-learning settings: Stable Diffusion and a pixel-space DDPM. In these diffusion models, Hopfield energy tracks reconstruction-based forgetting, and replay experiments reveal energy-dependent mitigation of forgetting that is consistent with the MHN analysis.

</details>


### [207] [Hurwitz Quaternion Multiplicative Quantization for KV Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27646)
*Kabir Swain,Sijie Han,Daniel Karl I. Weidele,Mauro Martino,David Cox,Antonio Torralba*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需校准的KV缓存压缩方法HQMQ，将四元数乘法量化应用于大型语言模型，通过Hurwitz群和随机辅助码本的乘积组合实现高效压缩，在多种现代模型上以约5比特达到FP16精度，压缩比最高达5.05倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的KV缓存占用巨大内存，现有量化方法通常需要校准数据和额外的存储开销，且难以处理现代模型中异常值主导的架构。本文旨在设计一种无需校准、高效且能处理异常值的KV缓存压缩方法。

Method: 将K/V的每4个元素视为一个四元数，将其单位方向量化为两个四元数的乘积：q_p来自24元素的Hurwitz群2T（S^3上24-cell的顶点），q_s来自每层每头的S个随机单位四元数次级码本。乘法组合产生24S个有效码字但仅需存储S个参数。采用每批次中位数乘子的异常值提取步骤（C=3，无需校准）处理异常值。

Result: 在Mistral-7B、Llama-3-8B、Qwen2.5-7B、Qwen3-8B和gpt-oss-20b五个模型上评估。HQMQ在约5比特下匹配FP16精度（ppl差异0.02-0.10）。在Qwen2.5-7B和Qwen3-8B上，当naive int4崩溃至10^4+ ppl时，HQMQ+Med3×恢复FP16质量。在相同比特下Pareto支配naive int 3-1900倍。零-shot准确率在3.79比特下匹配FP16。与最强校准基线KIVI-4相比，在3.79比特下以少16%比特且无需校准，在CoQA、TruthfulQA、GSM8K上分别接近1、0.6、2.3个百分点。

Conclusion: HQMQ是一种无需校准的高效KV缓存压缩方法，通过四元数乘法量化和异常值处理，在极低比特下保持FP16质量，显著优于现有方法，最高可实现5.05倍压缩（如Llama-3-70B的128k上下文缓存从43GB降至8.5GB）。

Abstract: We propose \textbf{Hurwitz Quaternion Multiplicative Quantization (HQMQ)}, a \textbf{calibration-free} method for KV cache compression of large language models. HQMQ treats each 4-element chunk of K or V as a quaternion and quantizes its unit direction to the \emph{product} $q_p \cdot q_s$, where $q_p$ ranges over the 24-element Hurwitz group $2T$ (the 24 vertices of the 24-cell on $S^3$, pairwise angle $60^\circ$) and $q_s$ ranges over a per-(layer, head) secondary codebook of $S$ \emph{random} unit quaternions. The multiplicative composition yields $24S$ effective codewords at $S$ stored parameters; random initialization suffices because left-multiplication is an $S^3$ isometry, so seeded codebooks vary in end-task ppl by $<1.5\%$. A per-batch median-multiplier outlier extraction step ($C{=}3$, no calibration) handles modern outlier-heavy architectures. We evaluate on five modern open models: Mistral-7B (dense MHA), Llama-3-8B and Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen3-8B (dense GQA), and gpt-oss-20b (sparse MoE). On Mistral-7B and Qwen3-8B, HQMQ matches fp16 within $0.02$--$0.03$ ppl points at $\sim$5 bits. On Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen3-8B, where naive int4 collapses to $10^4{+}$ ppl, HQMQ + Med3$\times$ recovers fp16 quality within $0.02$--$0.10$ ppl points at $\sim$5 bits. HQMQ Pareto-dominates naive int by $3$--$1900\times$ at matched bits across all five models, and downstream zero-shot accuracy matches fp16 at $3.79$ bits on Mistral. Against the strongest calibrated KV-quantization baseline, HQMQ at $3.79$ bits matches KIVI-4 ($\sim 4.5$ bits) within ${\sim}1$ pt on CoQA, $0.6$ pts on TruthfulQA, and $2.3$ pts on GSM8K, at $16\%$ fewer bits and without a calibration pass. At the storage level, HQMQ delivers up to $5.05\times$ KV compression, shrinking a Llama-3-70B 128k-context cache from 43 GB to 8.5 GB.

</details>


### [208] [Learning to Bid in Repeated Second-Price Auctions with Dynamic Values and Aggregated Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28133)
*Benjamin Heymann,Otmane Sakhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究动态价值下的重复拍卖竞价学习问题，提出结合插件估计与微分方程最优策略的学习方法，实现了近最优的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有竞价学习研究大多假设竞拍者价值是静态的或独立同分布的，但实际场景中竞拍者的价值往往具有动态依赖性（如当前价值取决于上次成功竞拍后的时间间隔）。因此需要研究动态价值下的竞价学习问题。

Method: 提出一类结合插件估计器与微分方程最优策略表征的学习方法；针对分段线性原语和一般光滑原语分别设计了置信界算法，无需显式随机化。

Result: 分段线性原语下达到$\widetilde{O}(\log N)$遗憾界，一般光滑原语下达到$\widetilde{O}(N^{1/3})$遗憾界，均为近最优。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法在动态价值重复拍卖场景中实现了理论上的近最优遗憾保证，并通过数值实验验证了有效性。

Abstract: We study the problem of learning to bid when the bidder's value is dynamic, i.e., when the current value depends on past outcomes. Specifically, we consider a bidder participating in repeated second-price auctions whose value depends on the time elapsed since their last successful bid, with auctions arriving in continuous time and only aggregated feedback revealed at the end of the horizon. Such a bidder must (1) balance the immediate benefit of winning the current auction against its impact on future values and (2) learn unknown environmental parameters. We derive regret bounds for a class of learning methods that combine plug-in estimators with a differential-equation characterization of the optimal policy, and show that a specific confidence bound algorithm learns the optimal policy with a near optimal regret of $\widetilde{O}(\log N)$ for piecewise linear primitives, and $\widetilde{O}(N^{1/3})$ for general, smooth primitives, achieving these regrets without explicit randomization. These theoretical results are supported by numerical experiments.

</details>


### [209] [Faster Thermal Profiling of a Lunar Rover with Machine Learning Adapted Finite Difference Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27651)
*Samuel Weber,Zaki Hasnain,Souma Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种物理信息机器学习（PIML）框架，用于月球车的热分析。该框架通过迁移神经网络（TNN）实现环境自适应粗网格划分，并嵌入可微有限差分热仿真器以保持物理一致性。相较于高保真仿真，该框架在保持物理一致性的同时，将预测精度提升50%-39%，计算速度提升3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主空间系统在极端热环境下运行需要精确且高效的热建模，但高保真物理仿真计算成本高，简化模型和查表法精度不足，因此需要一种兼顾精度与效率的替代方案。

Method: 提出PIML框架，包含：(1) 迁移神经网络（TNN）根据热载荷和初始条件自适应确定3D有限差分节点划分（粗网格）；(2) 嵌入可微有限差分热仿真器强制执行物理一致性并支持高效训练；(3) 上采样层从粗网格解重建高分辨率温度场。

Result: 相比固定粗网格物理模型，PIML框架预测精度提升50%；相比纯数据驱动ANN模型，精度提升39%；计算速度比高保真仿真快3倍，且保持物理一致的温度分布。

Conclusion: 所提PIML框架在月球车热建模任务中有效平衡了精度与效率，为极端热环境下自主空间系统的热分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Autonomous space systems operating in extreme thermal environments require accurate and efficient thermal modeling to support both pre-mission system design and onboard autonomy. For lunar rovers, large temperature gradients, radiative heat transfer, and variable surface conditions make reliable thermal prediction especially challenging. High-fidelity physics-based simulations provide accurate results but are computationally expensive, while simplified models and lookup-table approach often lack sufficient accuracy. Physics-informed machine learning (PIML) offers a promising alternative by combining data-driven models with embedded physical knowledge. This paper presents a PIML framework for thermal analysis of a simplified lunar rover with internal heat sources, where machine learning enables environment-adaptive coarse meshing. The proposed architecture integrates a transfer neural network (TNN) that adaptively determines 3D finite-difference nodalization based on thermal loads and initial conditions, enabling more accurate coarse-mesh calculations. A differentiable finite-difference thermal simulator is embedded within the framework to enforce physical consistency and support efficient training, while an upscaling layer reconstructs high-resolution temperature fields from the coarse-grid solution. The proposed PIML approach is evaluated against high-fidelity fine-mesh simulations, low-fidelity fixed coarse-mesh models, and a purely data-driven artificial neural network (ANN). Results show that the PIML framework improves prediction accuracy by 50% and 39% relative to the coarse-mesh physics model and ANN model, respectively, while maintaining physically consistent thermal distributions. Computationally, the framework is also 3x faster than high-fidelity simulations, demonstrating an effective balance between accuracy and efficiency for thermal modeling of lunar rover systems.

</details>


### [210] [Decentralized Parameter-Free Online Learning with Compressed Gossip](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27831)
*Tomas Ortega,Hamid Jafarkhani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DECO-EF算法，一种结合coin-betting预测与压缩差分gossip的去中心化参数无关在线学习算法，首次在压缩通信下实现期望的次线性网络遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化在线方法通常需要依赖视界、比较器尺度等问题参数选择学习率，而压缩通信会引入额外的需要控制的不一致性问题。本文旨在设计一种无需调参、对压缩通信鲁棒的去中心化在线学习算法。

Method: DECO-EF算法结合了coin-betting预测与基于压缩差值的gossip机制。每个代理维护一个干净的累积状态和一个压缩跟踪器，在gossip步骤中仅通信压缩后的状态差值，并通过误差反馈机制补偿压缩误差。

Result: 在压缩通信条件下，证明了DECO-EF具有期望的比较器自适应网络遗憾界。据我们所知，这是首个在压缩通信下为参数无关去中心化在线学习提供期望次线性网络遗憾保证的工作。

Conclusion: DECO-EF在去中心化在线学习中同时实现了参数无关性（无需调参至视界、比较器范数或学习率）和对压缩通信的鲁棒性，具有理论保证。

Abstract: We study decentralized online convex optimization when agents communicate over a graph and messages may be compressed. Classical decentralized online methods typically require learning-rate choices that depend on the horizon, comparator scale, or other problem parameters, while compressed communication introduces additional disagreement that must be controlled. We propose DECO-EF (DEcentralized COin-betting with Error Feedback), a decentralized parameter-free online learning algorithm that combines coin-betting predictions with compressed difference-based gossip. Each agent maintains a clean accumulated state and a compressed tracker, and communicates only compressed state differences during gossip steps. The method is parameter-free in the online-learning sense: it does not tune to the horizon, the comparator norm, or the learning rate. We prove expected comparator-adaptive network-regret bounds for DECO-EF under compressed communication. To the best of our knowledge, this gives the first expected sublinear network-regret guarantees for parameter-free decentralized online learning under compressed communication.

</details>


### [211] [Transferable Reinforcement Learning via Probabilistic Latent Embeddings and Dynamic Policy Adaptation for Sim-to-Real Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27659)
*Gengyue Han,Yiheng Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于概率潜变量嵌入和动态策略适应的强化学习框架，通过元学习推断环境上下文、结合分布强化学习动态调整风险水平，实现从模拟器到真实环境的安高效策略迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度强化学习智能体在模拟器中训练后部署到真实世界时，因Sim2Real差距（未建模系统动态）导致性能下降或安全违规；现有零样本方法（鲁棒安全RL、域随机化）以性能退化或残余安全风险为代价，亟需更安全高效的迁移方法。

Method: 将不同环境上下文建模为约束马尔可夫决策过程（CMDP）族；利用元强化学习中的潜上下文变量从模拟经验中推断环境潜在表征；引入分布强化学习，根据潜变量的估计精度动态调整部署策略的风险水平，在早期部署阶段优先安全，后期通过快速策略适应提升效率。

Result: 所提框架在Sim2Real差距下实现了安全且高效的策略迁移，早期阶段能保障安全，后期通过策略适应恢复性能，优于现有零样本方法。

Conclusion: 通过概率潜变量嵌入结合动态风险调整，有效解决了Sim2Real迁移中安全与效率的权衡问题。

Abstract: Due to limited resources and public safety concerns, deep reinforcement learning (RL) agents for many cyber-physical systems (e.g., autonomous vehicles) are first trained in simulators. However, when deployed in real world environments, they often suffer from performance degradation or safety violations because of the inevitable Sim2Real gap. Existing zero-shot approaches, such as robust safe RL and domain randomization, mitigate this issue but typically at the cost of degraded performance or residual safety risks when experiencing unmodeled system dynamics. To address these limitations, we propose a novel reinforcement learning framework that enables safe and efficient policy transfer via probabilistic latent embeddings and dynamic policy adaptation. We consider a family of Constrained Markov Decision Processes (CMDPs) under different environment contexts. By leveraging latent context variable in meta-RL, the proposed framework infers the latent representation of the environment from simulated experiences. Furthermore, it incorporates a distributional RL formulation, which allows risk levels of the deployed policy to be adjusted dynamically, based on the estimation accuracy of the latent context variable. This strategy promotes safety at the early deployment stage and improves efficiency through fast policy adaptation under the Sim2Real gap.

</details>


### [212] [Parameter-Efficient Generative Modeling with Controlled Vector Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28267)
*Peyman Morteza*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Chow-Rashevskii定理的连续时间生成建模框架，通过学习少量标量控制函数来调制固定向量场，构建具有表达力的流模型，实现参数高效的生成建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统连续归一化流需要学习高维无约束向量场，参数效率低。本文旨在通过少量可学习控制通道与固定向量场的组合，实现更具结构性和参数效率的生成模型。

Method: 基于Chow-Rashevskii定理，利用括号生成（bracket-generating）固定向量场的李代数张成整个空间，通过学习少量标量控制函数调制这些向量场来构造速度场，使用连续归一化流似然目标进行训练。

Result: 在合成分布上进行了概念验证实验，验证了该框架的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种结构化的、可解释的生成模型，学习标量输出通道的数量可独立于环境维度，是标准向量场参数化的参数高效几何替代方案。

Abstract: We introduce a continuous-time generative modeling framework, motivated by the Chow-Rashevskii theorem, that builds expressive flows from a small set of fixed vector fields and learned scalar controls. Instead of learning an unconstrained high-dimensional vector field, our framework constructs the velocity by modulating fixed vector fields with learned scalar control functions. When the fixed fields are bracket-generating, their Lie algebra spans the ambient space, providing a mechanism for expressive transport with only a small number of learned control channels and offering a parameter-efficient geometric alternative to standard vector-field parameterizations. This decoupled formulation yields a structured and interpretable generative model in which the number of learned scalar output channels can be chosen independently of the ambient dimension. We formulate an expressivity principle showing that, under suitable controllability and well-posedness assumptions, such controlled flows can transport a source distribution to a target distribution. We train the resulting model using a continuous-normalizing-flow likelihood objective and present proof-of-concept experiments on synthetic distributions.

</details>


### [213] [How the Optimizer Shapes Learned Solutions in Equivariant Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27662)
*Teodor-Mihai Stupariu,Andrei Manolache*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文比较了Muon和Adam优化器在等变与几何架构下的表现，发现Muon在ModelNet40上持续优于Adam，并分析了其到达的检查点具有更大的Hessian曲率、更规则的损失面以及更高秩的权重与表示，表明优化器设计与几何归纳偏置的交互值得进一步关注。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等变神经网络通常难以优化，甚至不如约束较少的架构。现有工作多通过架构修改（如约束放松或近似等变性）来解决，而优化器的作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在点云和分子学习任务中，对比Muon与Adam在多种等变和几何架构上的表现；通过在ModelNet40上训练，利用Hessian估计、损失面可视化、权重与中间表示的谱性质对检查点进行分析。

Result: Muon在所有架构上一致优于Adam；其检查点具有更大的Hessian曲率总结值、更规则的损失面，以及更高稳定秩和有效秩的权重与表示。

Conclusion: 优化器设计与几何归纳偏置之间的交互值得社区进一步研究。

Abstract: Equivariant neural networks encode geometric symmetries by construction, yet they are often difficult to optimize and can underperform less constrained architectures. A growing body of work addresses this through architectural modifications such as constraint relaxation or approximate equivariance, while the role of the optimizer remains comparatively underexplored. We study this direction by comparing Muon and Adam across several equivariant and geometric architectures under pointcloud and molecular learning settings. On ModelNet40, where the comparison is clearest, Muon consistently improves over Adam across all architectures considered. We then analyze the trained ModelNet40 checkpoints through Hessian estimates, loss surface visualizations, and spectral properties of learned weights and intermediate representations. The checkpoints reached by Muon have larger Hessian curvature summaries but more regular loss surfaces, and their learned weights and representations have higher stable and effective ranks. These observations suggest that the interaction between optimizer design and geometric inductive bias deserves further attention from the community.

</details>


### [214] [Adaptive Bandit Algorithms for Contextual Matching Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28290)
*Shiyun Lin,Simon Mauras,Vianney Perchet,Nadav Merlis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究匹配市场中基于上下文特征的赌博机学习问题，分别在随机上下文和对抗上下文两种场景下提出了自适应算法，并给出了相应的遗憾界分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在匹配市场中，玩家的效用与手臂上下文呈线性关系，上下文微小变化可能完全重构稳定匹配基准，导致其他玩家产生巨大遗憾。现有研究缺乏对这种上下文结构带来的学习困难的处理。

Method: 针对随机上下文，引入了最小偏好差距来刻画学习难度，并设计了全自适应算法；针对对抗上下文，提出了一种在任意上下文下仍然有效的遗憾概念，并通过自适应算法实现次线性遗憾。

Result: 随机上下文下实现了实例相关的多对数遗憾上界，并在温和分布假设下建立了匹配的实例无关遗憾上下界；对抗上下文下实现了实例无关的次线性遗憾界。

Conclusion: 本文系统解决了匹配市场中上下文赌博机学习的核心挑战，填补了该领域在随机和对抗上下文下的理论空白。

Abstract: We study bandit learning in matching markets, where players and arms constitute the two market sides, and the players' utilities are linear in the arm contexts. In each round, new arms arrive with observable contexts. Then, the algorithm matches them to players, aiming to minimize each player's regret against a stable matching benchmark. This contextual structure creates significant complexity: subtle context shifts can slightly alter one player's utility while completely reconfiguring the underlying benchmark, causing large regret spikes for others. We address this in two settings: stochastic contexts, drawn from a latent distribution, and adversarial contexts, which may be arbitrary. For the stochastic case, we introduce a novel minimum preference gap to capture learning difficulty and provide a fully adaptive algorithm with an instance-dependent poly-logarithmic regret upper bound. We also establish matching instance-independent regret upper and lower bounds under a mild distributional assumption. For the adversarial setting, we propose a tractable regret notion that remains valid under arbitrary contexts and achieves an instance-independent sublinear regret bound via an adaptive algorithm.

</details>


### [215] [Aligning LLMs with Human Uncertainty: A Beta-Bernoulli Calibrator for LLM Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27668)
*Hui Dai,Ryan Teehan,Parsa Torabian,Mengye Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Beta-Bernoulli校准器(BBC)，利用二元结果和人类聚合预测信号，将点估计转化为事件似然的分布，实现更优校准和准确性的概率预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM概率预测方法通常仅从二元结果中学习，未能充分利用人类聚合预测中蕴含的群体概率估计和预测者一致程度等丰富信息。

Method: 提出Beta-Bernoulli校准器(BBC)，建模事件似然 p~Beta(α,β) 和结果 y~Bernoulli(p)，以均值作为校准后的点预测，方差作为 epistemic uncertainty，同时利用二元结果和人类预测进行监督。

Result: BBC在校准性和准确性上优于传统事后校准方法和专门微调的预测模型，且轻量高效、泛化性好；其捕捉的epistemic uncertainty比语言化置信度更可靠地指示预测误差。

Conclusion: 通过结合人类聚合预测信号与Beta-Bernoulli概率建模，可有效提升LLM概率预测的质量和不确定性量化能力。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting estimates the likelihood of uncertain future events. To improve LLM forecasting, existing methods typically learn from binary outcomes to output verbalized forecasts. However, while aggregated human forecasts contain rich information in both the crowd probability estimate and the degree of agreement among forecasters, how to utilize these signals remains underexplored. To address this, we propose the Beta-Bernoulli Calibrator (BBC), which converts an initial point estimate forecast from any model into a distribution over event likelihood, using supervision from both binary outcomes and human forecasts. BBC models event likelihood $p \sim \text{Beta}(α, β)$ and outcome $y \sim \text{Bernoulli}(p)$, with the mean as the calibrated point forecast and the variance as the epistemic uncertainty. Our results show that BBC generally provides better calibrated and more accurate forecasts than both traditional post-hoc calibration methods and models fine-tuned specifically for forecasting, while remaining lightweight and having good generalization. We also show that the epistemic uncertainty captured by BBC is a more reliable predictor of forecasting error than verbalized confidence.

</details>


### [216] [Latent Diffusion for Missing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28427)
*Alberte Heering Estad,Ignacio Peis,Jes Frellsen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将扩散模型从像素空间迁移到由鲁棒VAE学习的潜在空间，用于处理高缺失率（MCAR）数据。实验表明，潜在扩散模型在50%缺失率下仍保持高质量，而像素空间扩散随缺失率增加性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于像素空间的扩散模型在处理高度不完整训练数据时性能严重退化，亟需探索更鲁棒的生成框架用于缺失数据填补。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段使用鲁棒VAE从不完整观测中学习紧凑语义特征作为隐式填补器；第二阶段在所得潜在空间上训练扩散模型。

Result: 在MCAR设定下，潜在扩散模型在高达50%缺失率时仍保持稳定和高样本质量；像素空间扩散模型性能随缺失率增加渐进退化。下游填补任务中潜在扩散一致优于像素空间扩散。

Conclusion: 潜在空间建模能缓解零填充输入带来的伪影放大，为不完整数据学习提供更鲁棒的生成先验，是像素空间扩散在缺失数据问题上的强有力且实用的替代方案。

Abstract: Diffusion models have emerged as powerful generative approaches for missing-data imputation, yet most existing methods operate directly in data space and degrade when training data are heavily incomplete. We investigate whether shifting diffusion to a learned latent representation improves robustness under missing-completely-at-random (MCAR) corruption. To this end, we propose a two-stage framework: a robust VAE-based imputer first learns compact semantic features from incomplete observations, and a diffusion model is then trained in the resulting latent space. Across training missing rates, we perform a controlled comparison against pixel-space diffusion models under the same incomplete-data setting. The latent diffusion model maintains high sample quality and remains stable up to 50\% missingness, while pixel-space diffusion degrades progressively as missingness increases. For downstream imputation, latent diffusion also achieves consistently better performance than pixel-space diffusion. These findings indicate that latent-space modeling mitigates artifact amplification from zero-imputed inputs and provides a more robust generative prior for incomplete-data learning. Overall, our results support latent diffusion as a strong and practically useful alternative to pixel-space diffusion for missing-data problems.

</details>


### [217] [When do complex-valued neural networks help? A study of representation, geometry, and optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27673)
*Ashutosh Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了复数神经网络（CVNNs）相较于各类实值基线模型在多个领域（合成射频、量子波函数预测、EEG分析）中的表现，发现复数表示在特定任务（如PSK符号识别、相位相关任务）中有优势，但并非普遍优于实值模型。研究还揭示了RadioML 2018.01A基准测试中的评估偏差，说明CVNNs应被视为结构化归纳偏置，其优势依赖于表示方式、对称性和优化条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复数输入本身并不能决定复数算术何时能改善学习效果，因为标签信号可能包含在幅度、相位、它们的耦合或某种对称性中，而这些实值模型在合适的坐标下也能表示。因此需要系统地评估CVNNs相较于各类实值基线的实际优势。

Method: 采用"表示优先"的评价方式，将CVNNs与笛卡尔实值、极坐标、仅相位、仅幅度、参数匹配实值、FLOP匹配实值六类基线模型进行对比。在合成射频任务、量子波函数预测、EEG解析信号实验等多样化场景中测试，并通过梯度分析、学习率×激活函数双因素实验等方法分析优化行为。

Result: PSK任务偏向相位敏感和复数模型，QAM任务偏向幅度模型，混合PSK+QAM任务复数仅微弱优势；载波相位旋转破坏坐标依赖模型。在RadioML 2018.01A上，共享试次选择下CReLU复数模型领先22.94 PP，独立每族调优后差距缩至2.46 PP，梯度分析表明差距源于实值基线高学习率第一步不稳定。

Conclusion: CVNNs应被视为结构化归纳偏置（structured inductive biases），其优势取决于任务中的表示、对称性和优化条件，而非普遍优越的架构。

Abstract: Complex-valued Neural Networks (CVNNs) are often motivated by domains where information is naturally encoded in magnitude and phase. Yet complex-valued inputs alone do not determine when complex arithmetic improves learning: the label signal may lie in amplitude, phase, their coupling, or a symmetry that real-valued models can also represent under suitable coordinates. We study this through a representation-first evaluation of CVNNs against Cartesian real, polar, phase-only, magnitude-only, parameter-matched real, and FLOP-matched real baselines. Across synthetic RF tasks, complex representations are useful but not universally superior. PSK-only tasks favor phase-aware and complex-valued models, QAM-only tasks favor magnitude-based models, mixed PSK+QAM gives only a small complex-valued advantage, and unseen carrier-phase rotations break coordinate-dependent models without augmentation. Similar patterns appear beyond RF: in quantum-wavefunction prediction, momentum is invisible to $|ψ|$ but recoverable from phase, while EEG analytic-signal experiments show that phase locking, amplitude bursts, and phase-amplitude coupling each favor different coordinate views. We also identify a benchmarking artifact on RadioML 2018.01A. Under matched-shared-trial selection, a CReLU complex model exceeds the best real baseline by 22.94 PP; under independent per-family tuning on the same data and 16-trial search space, the gap collapses to 2.46 PP. Gradient analysis traces the inflated gap to high-learning-rate first-step instability in real baselines, while complex parameter coupling distributes the loss signal more robustly. A learning-rate $\times$ activation factorial confirms the failure is primarily hyperparameter-driven. Overall, CVNNs are best viewed as structured inductive biases whose gains depend on representation, symmetry, and optimization, not as universally superior architectures.

</details>


### [218] [Heterogeneous Parallelism for Multimodal Large Language Model Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27678)
*Yashaswi Karnati,Kamran Jafari,Akash Mehra,Li Ding,Pranav Prashant Thombre,Ali Roshan Ghias,Shifang Xu,Parth Mannan,Yu Yao,Hao Wu,Eric Harper,Ashwath Aithal,Nima Tajbakhsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向多模态大语言模型训练的高效异构并行框架。该框架允许端到端计算图中的不同模块独立设置并行布局（TP/CP/PP/DP/EP）和秩放置，支持共享GPU上的共置执行和分离秩集上的非共置执行。核心挑战是保持边界张量语义：前向激活需按目标布局重排，反向梯度需回传至源布局。为此设计了边界通信器实现前后向布局变换，并扩展了调度机制。实验表明，共置异构方案最高提升TFLOPS/GPU达49.3%，非共置异构方案提升聚合token吞吐量达13.0%、TFLOPS/GPU达9.6%，且损失收敛性与同构基线一致。系统已作为Megatron-LM扩展开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多模态基础模型训练中模态覆盖扩展、上下文窗口增长以及编码器与LLM规模差异加大，单一LLM为中心的TP/CP/PP/DP/EP布局限制了吞吐量。这种耦合导致编码器被迫继承LLM驱动的分片和放置策略，增加通信开销、限制编码器并行度或约束LLM调度，尤其在长上下文场景中矛盾突出（LLM需要上下文并行处理融合的多模态序列，而编码器输入仍保持有界）。

Method: 提出异构并行抽象，允许端到端计算图中各模块使用独立的并行布局和秩放置，支持共置（共享GPU）和非共置（分离秩集）两种执行模式。关键设计包括：1）边界通信器，实现前向激活按目标布局重排和反向梯度回传至源布局的布局变换；2）调度扩展，适配两种放置模式的执行调度。

Result: 在多模态工作负载和不同GPU规模下，共置异构方案提升TFLOPS/GPU最高49.3%，非共置异构方案提升聚合token吞吐量最高13.0%、TFLOPS/GPU最高9.6%。损失收敛性与同构基线一致。

Conclusion: 异构并行通过解耦模块间的布局和放置自由度，在多模态训练中找到了更优的操作点，有效缓解了单一LLM中心化并行策略带来的瓶颈，系统已作为Megatron-LM扩展开源。

Abstract: Foundation model training is becoming multimodal, from post-training pipelines to large-scale pretraining. As modality coverage broadens, context windows grow, and encoder LLM scales diverge, a single LLM-centric TP/CP/PP/DP/EP layout increasingly limits throughput. This coupling forces encoders to inherit LLM-driven sharding and placement choices that can add communication, limit encoder parallelism, or constrain the LLM schedule; the mismatch is most pronounced at long contexts, where LLM context parallelism is needed for the fused multimodal sequence but encoder inputs remain bounded. We present heterogeneous parallelism for multimodal large language model training, an abstraction that lets modules in one end-to-end graph use independent layouts and rank placements, supporting colocated execution on shared GPUs and non-colocated execution on disjoint rank sets. The key challenge is preserving boundary tensor semantics across independent layouts: forward activations must be materialized for the destination layout, while backward gradients must be routed back to the source layout. We address this with boundary communicators that implement forward and backward layout transforms, plus scheduling extensions for both placement modes. We evaluate optimized homogeneous, colocated heterogeneous, and non-colocated heterogeneous configurations across multimodal workloads and GPU scales to characterize when added layout and placement freedom exposes a better operating point. Across this sweep, colocated heterogeneity improves TFLOPS/GPU by up to 49.3%, while non-colocated heterogeneity improves aggregate token throughput by up to 13.0% and TFLOPS/GPU by up to 9.6%. We validate loss convergence parity against homogeneous baselines and release the system as an open-source Megatron-LM extension.

</details>


### [219] [Optimal ridge regularization revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28679)
*Jack Timmermans,Sergio A. Alvarez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种迭代算法，用于在固定数据矩阵X和加性各向同性噪声条件下，从生成参数中数值计算岭回归的最优正则化强度，并证明了其在有限噪声水平下的收敛性。实验表明，结合基于样本的参数估计，该方法在多种样本规模、纵横比和噪声水平下均能实现近似的随机X泛化性能，额外计算成本仅为一次（欠参数化）或两次（过参数化）初步岭回归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在固定X设定下，从生成参数中高效计算最优正则化强度，并验证其在实际样本估计场景下的泛化效果。

Method: 提出迭代数值求解最优正则化强度的算法，并基于样本协方差和噪声方差估计进行实现。

Result: 在合成数据上，该方法在多种样本规模、纵横比和噪声水平下均达到近最优的随机X泛化性能，额外计算成本极低。

Conclusion: 所提迭代程序能有效确定最优正则化强度，且在实际样本估计下泛化性能接近最优，计算开销小。

Abstract: We consider $L^2$-regularized linear (ridge) regression over a finite data sample $X$ with bounded covariance and linear prediction targets $y$ with additive isotropic noise of finite variance. We present an iterative procedure to compute the optimal regularization strength numerically from the generative parameters in the fixed-$X$ setting and prove its convergence at limited noise levels. Our experimental evaluation over synthetic data shows that the proposed procedure combined with sample-based parameter estimates attains near-optimal random-$X$ generalization across a wide range of sample sizes, aspect ratios, and noise levels, at an added computational cost equivalent to one preliminary ridge regression in the underparameterized regime and two in the overparameterized case.

</details>


### [220] [Test-Time Collective Action: Proxy-Based Perturbations for Correcting Algorithmic Harms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27689)
*Meghana Bhange,Ulrich Aïvodji,Elliot Creager*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出测试时集体行动（TTCA）框架，允许共享查询访问的用户群体在不参与平台训练的情况下，通过代理机制优化通用扰动来纠正算法对弱势子群的性能差异，实验表明适度规模的集体行动可显著缩小准确率差距且查询成本更低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有算法公平性方法依赖平台端修复，受影响的用户群体缺乏外部杠杆来纠正算法对特定子群的性能差距，而现有的算法集体行动方法需要平台在修改后数据上重新训练，用户难以控制。

Method: 提出TTCA框架：集体共享黑盒API查询权限以提取平台代理模型，优化每个类别的通用扰动，成员在提交时对各自输入施加该扰动，无需平台配合。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和FairFace上实验表明，适度规模的集体能缩小大部分子群准确率差距；扰动可跨架构迁移；提升了最差组准确率、均等机会差距和差异性影响；查询预算分析显示集体攻击比个体单独攻击更节省成本。

Conclusion: 测试时集体行动为平台端修复不可用或延迟时，用户提供了一种有效的矫正干预手段。

Abstract: When machine learning systems under-perform for particular subgroups, affected users typically have no way to correct these disparities without relying on platform-level fixes. Existing approaches to algorithmic fairness rely on provider-centric approaches to correct these failures, leaving users with no external lever when faced with harm. Recent work in Algorithmic Collective Action shows that coordinated users can steer an algorithmic system toward a collective goal, but the existing mechanisms require the provider to retrain on the collective's modified data which users may not have control over. We propose Test-Time Collective Action (TTCA), a framework through which a group of users who share query access to the platform, can correct disparities affecting under-served subgroup without participating in the platform's training loop. We implement this through a proxy-based mechanism where the collective pools query access to a black-box API to extract a proxy of the platform, then optimizes a per-class universal perturbation against the proxy. Each member applies this perturbation to their own inputs at submission time, requiring no cooperation from the platform. We empirically evaluate the mechanism on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and FairFace, showing that modestly-sized collectives close most of the subgroup accuracy gap, transfer across architectures (a small proxy can attack a larger platform), and improve worst-group accuracy, equal-opportunity gap, and disparate impact. A query-budget analysis comparing a per-user black-box attack baseline shows that pooling is cheaper than each subgroup member attacking alone. Test-time collective action thus offers corrective intervention to users when platform-side remediation is unavailable or delayed.

</details>


### [221] [Principled Algorithms for Optimizing Generalized Metrics in Multi-Label Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28767)
*Mehryar Mohri,Yutao Zhong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于H-一致性的多标签学习框架MMO，通过设计可分解的代理损失函数，实现对广义线性分式指标（如F-measure、Jaccard指数）的非渐近优化，并在大规模高稀疏数据集上验证了其优越性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经验效用最大化（EUM）框架虽适用于多标签分类中的复杂评价指标（如F-measure、Jaccard指数），但已有理论结果多局限于渐近贝叶斯一致性，缺乏非渐近保证和实用的学习算法。

Method: 在EUM框架下基于更强的H-一致性概念设计新型代理损失函数，证明其组合形式的代理函数可精确分解为O(l)时间复杂度；在此基础上提出MMO算法族，用于优化广义线性分式指标。

Result: 在MS-COCO和Reuters-21578等大规模高稀疏数据集上的实验表明，MMO算法在深度学习场景下具有鲁棒的可扩展性，性能优于现有最先进的连续基线方法。

Conclusion: 本文同时提供了理论严谨性和实际有效性，为通用多标签指标优化建立了原则性的学习算法框架。

Abstract: Many real-world classification tasks require predicting multiple labels per instance, necessitating the optimization of complex evaluation metrics such as the $F$-measure and Jaccard index. While the Empirical Utility Maximization (EUM) framework is natural for these population-level metrics, existing theoretical results are largely limited to asymptotic Bayes-consistency. In this paper, we develop principled learning algorithms for optimizing a broad class of generalized metrics within the EUM framework, grounded in the stronger notion of $H$-consistency. Our key contribution is the design of novel surrogate loss functions for multi-label learning that admit provable $H$-consistency bounds, enabling optimization with non-asymptotic guarantees tailored to the hypothesis class and finite samples. Crucially, we prove these combinatorially formulated surrogates decompose exactly, operating in strictly $O(l)$ time without approximations. Building on this foundation, we introduce MMO (Multi-Label Metric Optimization), a new family of algorithms for optimizing generalized linear-fractional metrics. We validate our approach through extensive experiments, demonstrating robust scalability and superior performance over state-of-the-art continuous baselines on large-scale datasets (MS-COCO, Reuters-21578) in high-sparsity, deep learning regimes. Our results offer both theoretical rigor and practical effectiveness for general multi-label metric optimization.

</details>


### [222] [Bayesian Deployment Approval for Learned Landing Controllers under Finite Rollout Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27720)
*Fei Jiang,Lei Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种贝叶斯审批框架，用于在有限 rollout 证据下评估学习型自主着陆控制器的部署就绪性，通过后验概率推理量化不确定性，解决了传统强化学习评估指标（如累积奖励和经验成功率）可能导致的过度自信问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习评估使用累积奖励和经验成功频率等经验指标，但这些指标在有限仿真轨迹下无法提供足够的统计证据来支撑部署决策，容易导致对部署就绪性的过度自信判断。

Method: 提出贝叶斯审批框架：1）基于触地安全满足度构建概率化着陆能力公式；2）利用贝叶斯后验推断量化学习策略的真实部署能力不确定性；3）引入后验审批概率和后验部署风险用于部署导向评估；4）设计支持渐进式 rollout 测试中批准/拒绝/继续决策的顺序验证框架。

Result: 基于PPO和SAC控制器的仿真实验表明，经验成功率和奖励优化在有限验证证据下会产生过度自信的部署解读，而后验审批推断能提供更不确定性校准的部署就绪性评估。

Conclusion: 所提框架建立了传统强化学习评估与不确定性下部署导向验证之间的实用统计连接，可推广至更广泛的学习型自主系统类别。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning and data-driven autonomous controllers are commonly evaluated using cumulative reward and empirical success frequency under finite simulation trajectories. However, such empirical metrics do not necessarily provide sufficient statistical evidence regarding deployment readiness under uncertainty. This work develops a Bayesian approval framework for learned autonomous landing controllers under finite rollout evidence. A probabilistic landing capability formulation is introduced based on touchdown safety satisfaction under uncertain operating conditions, while Bayesian posterior inference is used to quantify uncertainty regarding the true deployment capability of learned policies. Posterior approval probability and posterior deployment risk are further introduced for deployment-oriented evaluation, together with a sequential validation framework supporting approve/reject/continue decisions during progressive rollout testing. Simulation experiments using PPO and SAC controllers demonstrate that empirical success and reward optimization may produce overconfident deployment interpretation under limited validation evidence, whereas posterior approval inference provides a more uncertainty-calibrated assessment of deployment readiness. The proposed framework provides a practical statistical connection between conventional reinforcement-learning evaluation and deployment-oriented validation under uncertainty and may be generalized to broader classes of learned autonomous systems.

</details>


### [223] [NUCLEUS-MoE: Unified Model of Pool Boiling for Liquid Cooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27722)
*Arthur Feeney,Xianwei Zou,Sheikh Md Shakeel Hassan,Siddhartha Rachabathuni,Aparna Chandramowlishwaran*

Main category: cs.LG
