TL;DR: NUCLEUS是一个基于混合专家模型的池沸腾代理模型，通过邻域注意力、符号距离场重初始化及专家路由机制，统一建模不同流体类别和沸腾条件下的饱和/过冷沸腾，在极端流体上超越先前模型，并展现出零样本与少样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两相沸腾的模拟因相变、湍流与输运的强耦合以及对流体性质与热力学条件的极端敏感性而困难重重。现有基于学习的代理模型要么局限于特定条件，要么局限于特定流体，缺乏泛化能力，需要为每种场景单独训练模型。

Method: 提出NUCLEUS混合专家模型，结合邻域注意力机制捕获局部交互、符号距离场重初始化保证界面一致性，以及专家路由机制实现无监督的专家分工专业化，用一个统一架构替代多个专用代理模型的集合。

Result: 在高保真池沸腾仿真数据上训练，NUCLEUS联合建模了饱和与过冷沸腾，覆盖电介质、制冷剂和低温流体三类流体，在极端流体上解决了先前模型的失败模式；专家路由展现出空间结构与专业化；性能匹配或超越基线模型；在Opeon 2P50等下游任务上展现出零样本与少样本泛化能力。

Conclusion: 混合专家模型是实现沸腾动力学统一代理建模的可扩展路径，为科学ML中更广泛的泛化奠定了基础。

Abstract: Two-phase boiling enables heat transfer rates an order of magnitude higher than single-phase cooling, but it remains difficult to model due to the strong coupling between phase change, turbulence, and transport, as well as extreme sensitivity to fluid properties and thermodynamic conditions. Existing learning-based surrogates are either condition- or fluid-specific, limiting generalization and requiring separate models. We present NUCLEUS, a mixture-of-experts model for pool boiling that replaces collections of specialized surrogates with a single architecture. NUCLEUS combines neighborhood attention, signed distance field reinitialization for interface consistency, and expert routing that exhibits emergent specialization across distinct boiling dynamics.
  Trained on high-fidelity simulations of pool boiling, NUCLEUS jointly models saturated and subcooled boiling across three fluid classes (dielectrics, refrigerants, and cryogens), resolving failure modes of prior models on extreme fluids. We show that expert routing exhibits coherent spatial structure and specialization without explicit supervision. Quantitatively, NUCLEUS matches or exceeds baselines while maintaining physical consistency across heterogeneous boiling configurations. We also show zero-shot and few-shot generalization capabilities on downstream tasks such as a new fluid (Opteon 2P50 developed for immersion cooling). These results demonstrate that mixture-of-experts models are a scalable pathway toward unified surrogate modeling of boiling dynamics and lay the groundwork for broader generalization across scientific ML.

</details>


### [224] [Can Entry-Wise Clipping Give Spectral Control of Stochastic Gradients?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27733)
*Zitao Song,Cedar Site Bai,Zhe Zhang,Brian Bullins,David F. Gleich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对训练不稳定性（如loss spikes）源于随机梯度噪声的问题，提出了一种基于逐元素裁剪的贝叶斯最优估计器，在Cauchy污染噪声下具有O(ε^{-4})收敛保证，并在NanoGPT预训练中节省约7%的训练token，与Muon结合可额外节省约2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练中的不稳定性（如loss spikes）通常由随机梯度噪声引起。语言训练数据中的稀有表达和多层组合导致噪声呈现重尾分布，且小批量平均无法完全消除。现有方法（如向量范数裁剪、谱归一化）在结构保留和计算成本之间存在权衡，缺乏能够在两者之间取得平衡的解决方案。

Method: 论文首先通过一阶扰动分析揭示了重尾噪声的局部化特性，即逐元素方法能够实现谱控制。基于此，在Gaussian信号先验下推导出贝叶斯最优逐元素估计器的可计算替代形式（即平滑收缩方法）。该方法在Cauchy污染噪声下具有O(ε^{-4})收敛保证。

Result: 实验表明：1）平滑收缩改进Adam在NanoGPT预训练上的表现，节省约7%的训练token；2）在谱归一化（如Muon）之前应用逐元素裁剪，可在Muon基础上额外节省约2%的训练token。

Conclusion: 重尾梯度噪声的局部化特性使得简单的逐元素方法能够实现谱级别的控制，从而在保持结构的同时降低计算成本。该方法可作为现有优化器（Adam、Muon）的即插即用模块，有效提升训练效率。

Abstract: Training instabilities such as loss spikes are frequently the result of stochastic gradient noise. Because of rare expressions in language training data, and multiple layer composition, the noise impact is heavy-tailed and survives mini-batch averaging. Existing remedies trade off structure against cost: vector-norm clipping ignores the matrix structure of weight updates, while spectral normalization (e.g., Muon (Jordan et al., 2024)) respects it at additional cost. We show that this trade-off can be balanced. Real gradient noise appears to be similar to entry-wise heavy-tailed contamination, and a first-order perturbation analysis reveals a localization property of such noise, under which a simple entry-wise method achieves spectral control. Exploiting this, we derive a tractable surrogate for the Bayes-optimal entry-wise estimator under a Gaussian signal prior. We establish $O(ε^{-4})$ convergence guarantee under Cauchy-contaminated noise. Empirically, we find that smooth shrinkage improves Adam on NanoGPT pretraining, saving ${\sim}7\%$ of training tokens. We further find that applying the entry-wise clipping before spectral normalization yields a ${\sim}2\%$ token saving on top of Muon.

</details>


### [225] [Learn from your own latents and not from tokens: A sample-complexity theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27734)
*Daniel J. Korchinski,Alessandro Favero,Matthieu Wyart*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于具有组合结构的概率上下文无关文法生成的数据，潜在预测（如data2vec/JEPA）相比监督学习或token级SSL，能将样本复杂度从指数级降低到常数级（相对于树深度L）。同时发现，显式堆叠的多尺度层次结构（如H-JEPA）在很大程度上是冗余的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成模型（从扩散模型到大语言模型）虽然性能卓越，但所需训练数据量远大于生物学习系统。潜在预测范式（如data2vec和JEPA）虽有强实证结果，但其理论基础薄弱，核心问题包括：潜在预测究竟能在多大程度上提升数据效率？堆叠多尺度层次结构是否有益？

Method: 以概率上下文无关文法（PCFG）生成具有组合结构的数据（捕获自然语言和图像的组成性），理论分析证明潜在预测的样本复杂度为O(log L)常数级；通过三种方式验证：(i) 层次聚类算法，(ii) 端到端神经网络（预测器-聚类器模块通过梯度下降预测自身潜在表示），(iii) data2vec的首次样本复杂度分析（证明其隐式执行层次潜在预测）。

Result: 对于树深度L的组合结构数据：监督/SSL需要O(exp(L))指数级样本；潜在预测仅需O(1)常数级样本（对数因子除外）。理论界得到三种实证验证。data2vec被证明隐式进行层次潜在预测，因此显式堆叠（如H-JEPA）基本冗余。

Conclusion: 潜在预测显著提升数据效率（从指数到常数），而显式多尺度层次堆叠并非必要。这为理解自监督学习中预测自身潜在表示的优势提供了理论基础，并暗示了更简洁模型设计的可能性。

Abstract: Generative models, from diffusion models to large language models, achieve remarkable performance but at a cost in training data orders of magnitude larger than what biological learners require. An alternative paradigm has emerged in which networks are trained to predict their \emph{own} latent representations of related views or masked regions, as in data2vec and JEPA -- an idea related to predictive-coding accounts of the cortex. Despite strong empirical results, the theoretical understanding of these methods remains limited. Central questions include: by how much does latent prediction actually improve data efficiency? Is there a benefit to stacking such methods into multi-scale hierarchies? We answer both using as data a tractable probabilistic context-free grammar that captures the compositional structure of natural language and images. Such a grammar generates strings of visible tokens by recursively applying production rules along a tree of hidden symbols of depth $L$. For such data, supervised or token-level SSL require a number of samples \emph{exponential} in $L$ to recover the latent tree; we prove that latent prediction achieves this with a number of samples \emph{constant} in $L$, up to logarithmic factors. We confirm this bound with (i) a hierarchical clustering algorithm, (ii) an end-to-end neural network whose predictor-clusterer modules predict their own latents at each level via gradient descent, and (iii) the first sample-complexity analysis of data2vec, which we show implicitly performs hierarchical latent prediction. This suggests that explicit stacking such as H-JEPA is largely redundant.

</details>


### [226] [Explicit Critic Guidance for Aligning Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27736)
*Zhengyang Liang,Qihang Zhang,Ceyuan Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种状态对齐的隐空间演员-评论家框架用于扩散模型后训练，通过让扩散模型自身作为时间步条件价值函数，直接在噪声隐状态上预测价值，实现轨迹级PPO训练、稳定的演员-评论家优化以及推理时引导，并扩展至多奖励优化缓解奖励黑客问题，在UNet和DiT骨干上均优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线强化学习方法在扩散模型对齐中面临两个主要限制：沿去噪轨迹分配细粒度信用（fine-grained credit assignment）的能力不足，以及难以实现稳定的基于价值的优化。

Method: 提出状态对齐的隐空间演员-评论家框架，扩散模型自身作为时间步条件价值函数，直接在噪声隐状态上预测价值；支持轨迹级PPO训练；采用简单的条件化和价值预训练策略实现稳定优化；评论家可复用于推理时引导；扩展至多奖励联合训练以缓解奖励黑客问题。

Result: 在UNet和DiT骨干上，该方法在单奖励和多奖励基准上均优于先前的组相对强化学习和演员-评论家基线方法，测试时引导进一步提升了生成质量。

Conclusion: 该框架通过状态对齐的隐空间演员-评论家设计有效解决了扩散模型后训练中信用分配和稳定优化的问题，同时具备良好的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: Online reinforcement learning is becoming increasingly important for aligning diffusion models with non-differentiable objectives. However, existing methods still face limitations in assigning fine-grained credit along denoising trajectories and in realizing stable value-based optimization. We propose a state-aligned latent actor-critic framework for diffusion post-training, in which the diffusion model serves as its own timestep-conditioned value function and predicts values directly on noisy latent states. This enables trajectory-level PPO training, supports stable actor-critic optimization with simple conditioning and value pretraining strategies, and naturally allows the learned critic to be reused for inference-time steering. We further extend the framework to multi-reward optimization, where joint training with complementary rewards helps alleviate reward hacking. Across both UNet- and DiT-based backbones, our method consistently outperforms prior group-relative RL and actor-critic baselines on single-reward and multi-reward benchmarks, while test-time steering provides additional gains in generation quality.

</details>


### [227] [Worker Disagreement Reveals Sharp Directions in Local SGD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27739)
*Tolga Dimlioglu,Kristi Topollai,Anna Choromanska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在分布式深度学习训练中，局部SGD（Local SGD）中各个工作节点之间的平均差距（worker-average gap）能够作为黑塞矩阵主导特征子空间的无代价估计器，并通过理论和实验验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络训练中损失几何高度各向异性，少数陡峭的主导黑塞方向与大量平坦方向共存，梯度倾向于与主导方向对齐，但稳定训练需要沿平坦方向推进。直接基于黑塞矩阵的方法估计主导子空间成本高昂，需要更高效的替代方案。

Method: 从理论上证明，局部SGD中工作节点平均差距的协方差结构受随机梯度噪声和黑塞曲率共同塑造，使得节点差异沿曲率敏感（陡峭）方向增大。因此，工作节点平均差距可作为黑塞矩阵主导子空间的无黑塞估计器。

Result: 在MLP、CNN和Transformer上的实验表明，由工作节点平均差距构成的子空间能够捕获梯度中位于黑塞主导特征空间中的大部分成分。

Conclusion: 工作节点平均差距提供了一种廉价且有效的黑塞主导子空间估计方法，可用于分析损失几何并指导优化。

Abstract: Deep neural network training often exhibits highly anisotropic loss geometry, where a few sharp dominant Hessian directions coexist with a large flatter bulk. Gradients tend to align disproportionately with these dominant directions, although stable progress often requires movement through flatter bulk directions. Estimating the dominant subspace is therefore useful but costly with direct Hessian-based methods. We show that standard Local SGD exposes this geometry through worker disagreement. We theoretically show that the worker-average gap covariance is shaped by stochastic-gradient noise and Hessian curvature, causing workers to disagree along sharp, curvature-sensitive directions. Thus, worker-average gaps provide a cheap Hessian-free estimator of the dominant subspace. Experiments on MLPs, CNNs, and Transformers show that subspaces formed by worker-average gaps capture a substantial fraction of the gradient component lying in the dominant Hessian eigenspace.

</details>


### [228] [High-Fidelity Industrial Crash Dynamics Prediction via Geometry-Aware Operator Learning with Memory-Efficient Low-Rank Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27758)
*Deepak Akhare,Mohammad Amin Nabian,Corey Adams,Sudeep Chavare,Sanjay Choudhry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoTransolver框架，用于工业级汽车碰撞高保真代理建模，通过几何感知算子学习实现多尺度形变预测，并引入FLARE机制降低内存开销约2倍，在保险杠横梁和整车碰撞数据集上验证了其准确性与实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统有限元求解器在汽车碰撞优化中计算成本过高，而现有算子学习框架难以应对工业级碰撞分析中复杂的几何结构、接触非线性和快速瞬态变形共存的问题。

Method: 提出GeoTransolver框架，包含几何感知的交叉注意力机制；系统评估了三种时序预测策略（一次性预测、时间条件预测、自回归滚动预测）；引入基于快速低秩注意力路由引擎（FLARE）的注意力骨干改进。

Result: 在保险杠横梁和整车碰撞数据集上，GeoTransolver能够准确捕捉多尺度几何上下文、塑性变形模式和关键位置的加速度曲线；一次性预测策略在精度、训练开销和推理延迟方面达到最优；FLARE改进将内存开销降低约2倍，同时提升长程高频瞬态预测精度。

Conclusion: 几何感知的算子学习（GeoTransolver）为复杂、安全关键的汽车动力学高保真代理建模提供了实际可行的解决方案，展示了其在大规模工业碰撞分析中的应用潜力。

Abstract: Automotive crashworthiness optimization remains a safety-critical challenge, requiring the management of large-scale nonlinear structural deformations and energy dissipation through iterative, high-fidelity simulations. While traditional finite element solvers are computationally prohibitive, emerging operator learning frameworks provide rapid surrogate predictions; however, applying them to industrial-scale crash analysis, where complex geometry, contact nonlinearities, and rapidly evolving transient deformation coexist, remains an open challenge. In this paper, we demonstrate that the GeoTransolver framework provides a viable solution for accurate, high-fidelity crash dynamics prediction at industrial scale. Benchmarked on complex bumper beam and full-vehicle crash datasets, GeoTransolver captures multi-scale geometric context and accurately resolves plastic deformation patterns as well as acceleration profiles at critical occupant locations. Beyond the architecture itself, we propose and systematically evaluate a suite of temporal prediction recipes, including one-shot, time-conditional, and autoregressive rollout strategies, demonstrating that the one-shot approach achieves state-of-the-art accuracy with significantly reduced training overhead and inference latency. As a secondary contribution, we introduce a Fast Low-rank Attention Routing Engine (FLARE)-based modification to the GeoTransolver attention backbone that reduces memory overhead by approximately 2x while further improving predictive accuracy for O(N) long-range, high-frequency transients, preserving the geometry-aware cross-attention strengths of the base framework. Our results highlight the practical viability of geometry-aware operator learning for high-fidelity surrogate modeling of complex, safety-critical automotive dynamics.

</details>


### [229] [A Paired Testing Protocol for Batch-Conditioned Refusal Robustness in LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27763)
*Sahil Kadadekar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究揭示批处理条件（单独、同步批处理或连续批处理调度）会引发语言模型安全标签翻转（0.16%真实翻转率），但未发现普遍的安全-能力倾向偏移；通过消融实验验证了VLLM_BATCH_INVARIANT内核可消除翻转；建议采用精确堆栈验证协议进行安全评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型安全评估将服务配置视为固定背景，忽略了批处理条件这一未测试的处理变量——同一提示在单独评估、同步批处理或连续批处理调度中可能产生不同结果。

Method: 合成四项锚定研究形成配对测试协议：研究A（本地发现+评分校正裁决+真批处理确认）、研究B（跨模型泛化测试）、研究C（连续批处理组合测试）、研究D（批处理不变核消融实验）。

Result: 本地测试安全标签翻转率（0.51%）高于能力标签（0.14%），但经裁决63个候选行仅剩17个真实行为翻转（校正全集率0.16%）；15模型扩展未发现安全-能力偏移（翻转比0.94x，对齐类型无显著关联p=0.942，输出不稳定性为最强脆弱性筛选器r=0.909）；vLLM标准版复现22/55翻转，启用批处理不变内核后降为0/55。

Conclusion: 建议采用精确堆栈验证：在实际批处理设置下评估拒绝行为，将安全提示与能力控制配对，分别报告低率定向翻转与聚合零效应。

Abstract: Safety evaluations of language models often treat serving configuration as fixed background infrastructure, but batch condition is an untested treatment variable whenever the same prompt may be evaluated alone, in a synchronized batch, or inside a continuous-batching scheduler. We synthesize four artifact-backed studies into a paired testing protocol: Study A combines local discovery, scorer-corrected adjudication, and true-batching confirmation; Study B tests cross-model generalization; Study C tests continuous-batch composition; and Study D runs a batch-invariant-kernel ablation. The local test finds safety-label changes more often than capability-label changes (0.51% vs. 0.14%), but adjudication of 63 candidate rows leaves only 17 genuine behavioral flips, implying a corrected full-set rate of 0.16%. The 15-model extension finds no detectable universal safety-over-capability skew: flips are near parity (0.94x), alignment type has no detectable association ($p=0.942$, $η^2=0.033$), and output instability is the strongest tested fragility screen ($r=0.909$, bootstrap 95% CI [0.65, 0.97]). In the targeted kernel ablation, standard vLLM reproduces 22/55 label flips on current score-flip candidates, while enabling VLLM_BATCH_INVARIANT=1 reduces the same test to 0/55 flips; the composition test separately finds no aggregate effect at 4.7pp sensitivity. The testing recommendation is exact-stack validation: evaluate refusal at the served batch setting, pair safety prompts with capability controls, and report low-rate directional flips separately from aggregate null effects.

</details>


### [230] [Restoring the Sweet Spot: Pass-Rate Weighted Self-Distillation for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27765)
*Zehao Liu,Yuanpu Cao,Jinghui Chen,Vasant G. Honavar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SC-SDPO通过引入$\sqrt{p(1-p)}$权重修正SDPO的KL优势缺乏难度感知的问题，实现隐式课程学习，在科学推理和工具使用基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SDPO提供密集的token级信用分配，但其基于KL的优势函数缺乏GRPO中天然存在的难度感知机制（即群体相对优势自然聚焦于中等难度问题），导致对不同难度问题的学习效率不均衡。

Method: 从GRPO的优势归一化出发，通过可学习性框架分析归一化奖励，发现归一化吸收了方差项$p(1-p)$，使得每个问题的梯度残留$\sqrt{p(1-p)}$缩放因子。据此提出SC-SDPO，对每个问题的SDPO损失加权$[\hat{p}(1-\hat{p})]^{1/2}$，该权重通过on-policy rollout的批自适应归一化零成本获得，形成动态跟踪模型能力的隐式课程。

Result: 在科学推理和工具使用基准上，SC-SDPO相比SDPO在Qwen3-8B上提升+3.2/4.3（mean@16/maj@16），在OLMo-3-7B上提升+1.8/+3.0，且训练动态稳定。

Conclusion: SC-SDPO通过简单的难度感知权重修正了SDPO的缺陷，以零成本方式引入了隐式课程学习，显著提升了强化学习微调效果。

Abstract: Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (SDPO) provides dense token-level credit assignment for reinforcement learning with large language models by leveraging the model's own feedback-conditioned predictions as a self-teacher. Unlike GRPO, however, whose group-relative advantage naturally concentrates learning on a sweet spot of intermediate-difficulty questions, SDPO's KL-based advantage lacks an implicit notion of difficulty awareness.
  We analyze this gap through the lens of GRPO's advantage normalization. Extending the learnability framework to normalized rewards, we show that normalization absorbs the variance term $p(1-p)$, equalizing leading-order learnability across questions and leaving $\sqrt{p(1-p)}$ as the sole residual scaling factor in the per-question gradient. This analysis yields a simple prescription: weight each question's SDPO loss by $[\hat{p}(1-\hat{p})]^{1/2}$, resulting in SC-SDPO, a scale-consistent variant of SDPO.
  The proposed weights are obtained as a zero-cost byproduct of on-policy rollouts with batch-adaptive normalization, inducing an implicit curriculum that dynamically tracks the model's evolving competence. Experiments on scientific reasoning and tool-use benchmarks demonstrate that SC-SDPO consistently improves over SDPO, yielding gains of +3.2/+4.3 (mean@16/maj@16) on Qwen3-8B and +1.8/+3.0 on OLMo-3-7B, while preserving stable training dynamics throughout optimization.

</details>


### [231] [Fine-Tuning Dynamics of In-Context Factual Recall in Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27774)
*Ruomin Huang,Eshaan Nichani,Jason D. Lee,Rong Ge*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究上下文学习如何利用存储在模型参数中的事实知识。作者提出了"上下文事实回忆（IC-recall）"任务，分析了一个单层transformer在该任务上的微调动态，证明了模型会收敛到一种特定的成对注意力模式，并且微调只需要极少的样本（与存储知识量成对数多项式关系）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论工作通常假设上下文学习仅从提示中提取信息，但实际应用中模型常常需要同时从参数中检索事实知识。作者希望从理论上理解这一行为，弥合理论与实践之间的差距。

Method: 形式化定义了IC-recall任务：给定包含(主体, 答案)对的上下文（由隐藏关系生成）和一个查询主体，模型需要同时推断隐藏关系并从参数存储的知识三元组中检索答案。知识通过一个简单的MLP联想记忆存储。作者分析了单层transformer在此任务上的监督微调动态。

Result: 证明了模型能成功执行IC-recall，收敛到一种特定的成对注意力模式；微调所需样本数仅与存储知识量成对数多项式关系。实验验证了理论预测，并表明即使MLP层是预训练而非人工构建的，成对注意力模式也会出现。

Conclusion: 上下文学习中的事实知识检索可以通过特定的注意力模式实现，且该能力可以通过极少量样本的微调获得，这为理解大规模语言模型如何结合上下文信息与内部知识提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In-context learning \ -- performing tasks based on examples given in the prompt \ -- is an important capability that has emerged in large language models and has received significant attention in both theory and practice. Existing theoretical work often focuses on settings where the learning uses information purely from the prompt. However, many practical instances of in-context learning require the model to retrieve factual knowledge stored in the model's parameters, with the context serving to identify which knowledge is relevant. In this work, we study how in-context learning leverages factual knowledge recall. We formalize this behavior by introducing the \emph{in-context factual recall (IC-recall)} task, where a transformer is provided a context of (subject, answer) pairs generated from a hidden relation, along with a query subject, and must both infer this hidden relation and retrieve the corresponding answer. Factual knowledge is modeled by the transformer having access to a simple pre-constructed MLP associative memory storing (subject, relation, answer) triplets. We analyze the supervised fine-tuning dynamics of a one-layer transformer on IC-recall data and prove that the model successfully performs IC-recall by converging to a particular pairwise attention pattern. This fine-tuning stage requires a very small number of samples \ -- only polylogarithmic in the number of stored knowledge triplets. Experiments verify our theoretical predictions and show that the pairwise attention pattern emerges even when the MLP layer is pretrained instead of constructed.

</details>


### [232] [Revisiting ML Training under Fully Homomorphic Encryption: Convergence Guarantees, Differential Privacy, and Efficient Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27782)
*Yvonne Zhou,Mingyu Liang,Ivan Brugere,Danial Dervovic,Yue Guo,Antigoni Polychroniadou,Min Wu,Dana Dachman-Soled*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对全同态加密（FHE）下的机器学习训练进行理论收敛性分析，并设计了一种适配加密计算的差分隐私（DP）训练算法，通过多项式逼近激活函数和损失函数实现近似梯度下降的收敛性证明，同时避免逐样本梯度裁剪，提升了计算效率和数据隐私保护能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FHE下的机器学习训练缺乏理论收敛性分析，且标准DP-GD中逐样本梯度裁剪在加密计算下计算成本过高，亟需一种兼顾理论保证、计算效率和隐私保护的加密训练方法。

Method: 1) 基于多项式逼近激活函数和损失函数实现FHE兼容的近似梯度下降；2) 设计无需逐样本梯度裁剪的差分隐私集成机制；3) 提出数据无关的超参数选择策略和多项式逼近的理论指导。

Result: 在理论上证明了近似梯度下降的收敛性；相比标准DP-GD，计算效率显著提升且效用相当；实现了数据无关的超参数选择和多项式逼近策略。

Conclusion: 该工作从理论上推进了高效、隐私且安全的加密机器学习训练的可行性，为敏感数据上的隐私保护学习提供了新的理论基础和实用方案。

Abstract: We present the first theoretical convergence analysis of machine learning training under fully homomorphic encryption (FHE), combined with a differentially private (DP) training algorithm tailored to encrypted computation. Our approach improves computational efficiency over standard differentially private gradient descent (DP-GD) while achieving comparable utility. In particular, we prove convergence of approximate gradient descent using polynomial approximations of activation and loss functions, which are required for FHE compatibility. To preserve privacy in downstream tasks, we integrate differential privacy without relying on costly per-sample gradient clipping, enabling scalable encrypted learning. We also provide data-independent hyperparameter selection and theoretically grounded strategies for polynomial approximation which can be of independent interest. Together, these contributions advance the feasibility of efficient, private, and secure machine learning on sensitive data.

</details>


### [233] [Locality-Aware Redundancy Pruning for LLM Depth Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27786)
*Vincent-Daniel Yun,Youngrae Kim,Woosang Lim,YoungJin Heo,Minkyu Kim,Sunwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种训练无关的一次性深度剪枝框架 LoRP，通过表征局部性指导层剪枝，在多种 LLM 族中提升困惑度和下游任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 在网络深度上存在表征冗余，现有一次性剪枝方法依赖局部层重要性或固定的冗余假设，缺乏对架构间冗余分布差异的考虑。

Method: 提出 LoRP 框架：引入表征局部性分数（RLS），基于全局层间隐藏状态相似度计算；使用小校准集计算成对层相似度，按表征相似性聚类，根据簇内残余冗余分配剪枝。

Result: 在多种 LLM 族上，LoRP 在困惑度和下游任务准确率上均有提升。

Conclusion: LoRP 通过表征局部性感知的冗余剪枝，有效提升了 LLM 推理效率，且无需重新训练。

Abstract: Large language models are known to contain representational redundancy across network depth, making depth pruning an effective approach for improving inference efficiency. Existing one-shot pruning methods rely on local layer importance or fixed redundancy assumptions across architectures. We propose Locality-Aware Redundancy Pruning (LoRP), a training-free one-shot depth pruning framework guided by representation locality. We show that inter-layer redundancy can be either localized or globally distributed depending on the LLM architecture. To characterize this phenomenon, we introduce Representation Locality Score (RLS), derived from global inter-layer hidden-state similarity. Using a small calibration set, LoRP computes pairwise layer similarity, clusters layers by representational similarity, and allocates pruning according to residual intra-cluster redundancy. Experiments across diverse LLM families show improvements in both perplexity and downstream task accuracy.

</details>


### [234] [Knowing When to Ask: Segment-Level Credit Assignment for LLM Tool Use](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27788)
*Abhijit Kumar,Zoey Wu,Mohit Suley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CARL方法，通过强化学习训练评论员模型，让语言模型学会判断何时需要调用外部工具（如计算器），在保持准确率的同时减少不必要的工具调用，在5个基准测试上提升6.7-9.7个百分点的精确匹配准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类知道何时需要求助（如347×28需要计算器而2+2不需要），但语言模型缺乏这种元认知能力。现有方法（基于提示或轨迹级强化学习）无法让模型学会识别自身知识边界，导致要么过度依赖工具，要么在需要工具时错误回答。

Method: 提出CARL（Competence-Aware Reinforcement Learning），通过在模型自身 rollout 上训练一个评论员（critic），利用自然工具使用边界（如代码围栏分隔符、上下文块转换）将轨迹分解为独立片段，从单一二元结果中为每个片段分配独立信用，无需外部评判或步骤级标注。

Result: 评论员能以AUC 0.93（7B模型）区分参数可解和需要工具的问题。在5个基准上，7B模型EM提升6.7点，3B模型提升9.7点；Musique上增益最大（7B +8.3 EM，3B +9.0 EM）。模型在参数可回答问题上减少53%的工具调用，同时保持约10 EM点的更高准确率。3B提升幅度是7B的1.4倍。

Conclusion: CARL通过能力感知的强化学习成功教会语言模型判断自身知识边界，在需要时调用工具、在不需要时依靠自身参数知识，尤其对参数容量较小的模型增益更大。

Abstract: Humans know when to reach for help e.g. $347 \times 28$ warrants a calculator while $2+2$ does not. Language models do not. Prompt-based approaches can instruct a model when to invoke tools, but this scaffolding does not teach it to recognize the boundary of its own knowledge. RL approaches that assign a single outcome reward to the whole trajectory fare no better: trajectory-level credit cannot isolate which tool call in a successful episode actually helped, nor penalize unnecessary calls. We propose \textbf{CARL} (\textbf{C}ompetence-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{R}einforcement \textbf{L}earning), which trains a critic on the model's own rollouts to learn where parametric knowledge suffices and where it needs external help. By decomposing each rollout at natural tool-use boundaries (e.g., code fence delimiters and context block transitions), CARL assigns independent credit to each segment from a single binary outcome, without external judges or step-level annotations. As a result, erroneous tool calls, incorrect extractions, and unnecessary calls each receive appropriately signed advantages. The trained critic captures the model's domain competence: it separates parametrically solvable from tool-dependent questions with AUC 0.93 at 7B. On five benchmarks spanning arithmetic, multi-hop factual QA, and numerical reasoning over financial tables, CARL improves exact-match accuracy by 6.7 points at 7B and 9.7 points at 3B over the best RL baseline, with the largest gain (+8.3 EM at 7B, +9.0 EM at 3B) on Musique. The model issues 53\% fewer tool calls on parametrically answerable questions while remaining ${\sim}10$ EM points more accurate on them. Gains are largest at small scale: the 3B improvement is $1.4\times$ the 7B improvement, suggesting that knowing when to ask disproportionately benefits models with smaller parametric memory.

</details>


### [235] [SYNAPSE: Neuro-Symbolic Visual Thought-to-Text Decoding via Topological Semantic Denoising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27790)
*Akshaj Murhekar,Abhijit Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SYNAPSE是一个轻量级神经符号框架，通过在推理时进行符号正则化，利用常识图谱和潜在样例净化EEG语义候选，在不微调LLM的情况下提升脑电解码文本生成的语义稳定性，同时保护生物特征隐私。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇EEG到想象文本解码系统对生物噪声高度敏感，受损的神经投影会导致冻结语言模型产生幻觉或语义不稳定的生成内容。

Method: 提出SYNAPSE框架，通过推理时的符号正则化（利用常识图谱结构和潜在样例净化EEG语义候选）来稳定神经文本生成，无需端到端LLM微调，并将原始EEG处理完全限制在编码器堆栈内以保护生物特征隐私。

Result: 在多个EEG解码基准和不同冻结LLM后端上，相比无约束提示基线取得一致提升；在目标标签消融下具有鲁棒性；性能与资源密集型的微调系统相当。

Conclusion: SYNAPSE通过轻量级神经符号方法有效提升了EEG解码的语义稳定性，兼顾了隐私保护，是资源高效且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models have accelerated open-vocabulary EEG-to-imagined-text decoding, where non-invasive neural activity recorded during visual perception is translated into coherent natural language descriptions of viewed stimuli. However, existing systems remain highly vulnerable to biological noise, where corrupted neural projections induce hallucinated or semantically unstable generation in frozen language models. We introduce SYNAPSE (Symbolic Neural Alignment for Precise Semantic Extraction), a lightweight neuro-symbolic framework that stabilizes neural text generation through inference-time symbolic regularization. By purifying EEG-derived semantic candidates using commonsense graph structure and latent exemplars, SYNAPSE improves semantic stability without end-to-end LLM fine-tuning. Experiments across popular EEG decoding benchmarks and multiple frozen LLM backends demonstrate consistent gains over unconstrained prompting baselines, robustness under object-label ablation, and performance commensurate with substantially more resource-intensive fine-tuned systems, while preserving biometric privacy by localizing raw EEG processing entirely within the encoder stack.

</details>


### [236] [Density-aware Sample-specific Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27809)
*Qiyuan Wang,Yao Li,Raymond K. W. Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分布密度比的后门攻击方法，通过将触发样本引导至干净数据的低密度区域，在攻击成功率和抗防御能力上显著优于现有方法，并揭示了当前防御范式的根本性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击方法在微调或剪枝等训练后防御下容易被消除，缺乏能够抵抗这些防御的攻击方法。

Method: 提出双层优化框架，通过条件时间得分匹配估计密度比，并优化混合模型目标将触发样本放置在干净数据分布的低密度区域。

Result: 在MNIST、CIFAR-10、GTSRB和TinyImageNet上实现99%以上攻击成功率；微调防御下后防御ASR比最强基线高50-85个百分点；神经剪枝防御下完全免疫，所有剪枝阈值下均无神经元被识别移除。

Conclusion: 当前防御范式存在根本性缺陷，需要开发超越干净分布支撑集的防御方法。

Abstract: Despite recent progress in backdoor attacks, existing methods remain susceptible to post-training defenses that erase the backdoor through fine-tuning or pruning. We revisit the core objectives of backdoor attacks and derive principled criteria characterizing optimal sample-specific trigger construction under a Bayes-optimal model of the victim's training. Our analysis reveals that both attack success and clean-accuracy preservation are simultaneously optimized when triggered samples are steered into low-density regions of the clean data distribution, a distributional condition that controls all moments of the poisoned distribution at once rather than a handful of input-space summary statistics. We introduce a bilevel optimization framework that estimates density ratios via conditional time-score matching and optimizes a mixture-model objective to place triggered samples in these sparse regions. Extensive evaluations on MNIST, CIFAR-10, GTSRB, and TinyImageNet demonstrate that our method achieves above 99\% attack success rate before defense and retains 50--85 percentage points higher post-defense ASR than the strongest baselines under fine-tuning defenses. Against neuron-pruning defenses, the method exhibits complete immunity, with zero neurons identified for removal across all pruning thresholds. These results expose a fundamental gap in current defense paradigms and underscore the need for defenses that operate beyond the support of the clean distribution.

</details>


### [237] [ReSAE: Residualized Sparse Autoencoders for Multi-Layer Transformer Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27819)
*Prathyush Poduval,Calvin Yeung,Neel Desai,Mohsen Imani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出残差化稀疏自编码器（ReSAEs），通过拟合一幅射图去除层间线性可预测结构，使后续层SAE专注于解释残差而非完整激活，从而减少解码器冗余、提升多层替换下的交叉熵恢复和稀疏探测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统稀疏自编码器逐层独立训练，但Transformer残差流激活在层间高度耦合，导致不同层字典重复编码相同的向前传递信息，多层干预时产生单层行为无法预测的交互效应。

Method: 在选定层之间拟合一幅射映射（affine map），将后续层的SAE训练目标从完整激活改为相对于该映射的残差，再通过拟合的仿射链将重建映射回原始激活空间，从而保持与普通SAE相同的干预评估协议。

Result: 在Pythia-1.4B和Gemma-2-9B上，残差化减少了解码器冗余，在大多数设置下提升了稀疏探测和定向扰动性能；尽管重建的原始激活方差更少，但在教师强制和足够稀疏条件下，多层替换时的交叉熵恢复更好。

Conclusion: 去除线性可预测的跨层结构是多层SAE干预的有效默认策略，ReSAEs保留了对模型下游计算最相关的激活成分。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders are usually trained one layer at a time, even though transformer residual stream activations are strongly coupled across depth. This creates a practical problem for multi-layer interventions: different layerwise dictionaries can spend capacity representing the same carried-forward information, and replacing several layers at once can produce interactions that are not predicted by single-layer behavior. We introduce Residualized Sparse Autoencoders (ReSAEs), which fit an affine map between selected layers and train each later-layer SAE on the unexplained residual rather than on the full activation. Reconstructions are mapped back into the original activation space through the fitted affine chain, so ReSAEs can be evaluated with the same intervention protocols as ordinary SAEs. On Pythia-1.4B and Gemma-2-9B, residualization reduces decoder redundancy and improves sparse probing and targeted perturbation in most tested settings. Despite reconstructing less of the raw activation variance, ReSAEs recover more transformer cross entropy under multi-layer replacement. This gain is clearest under teacher-forcing and at sufficient sparsity online, indicating that ReSAEs preserve the components of the activation most relevant to the model's downstream computation. These results suggest that removing linearly predictable cross-layer structure is a useful default for multi-layer SAE interventions.

</details>


### [238] [CAREF: Calibration-Aware Regularization for Explanation Faithfulness Without Rationale Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27835)
*Naphat Nithisopa,Teerapong Panboonyuen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CAREF是一个参数高效的微调框架，通过校准感知正则化联合优化预测准确性和解释忠实度，核心是利用统一损失函数LSCED耦合熵校准与令牌级稀疏控制，无需监督信号即可提升模型的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在微调大语言模型时难以同时保证预测准确性和解释忠实度，且通常需要额外的监督信号。作者旨在提出一种无需理性监督、参数高效且能统一优化准确性与可解释性的微调框架。

Method: 提出CAREF框架，核心是校准感知正则化（LSCED损失），它将基于熵的校准与令牌级别稀疏控制结合为一个统一的训练目标。通过该损失函数，在微调过程中同时优化预测准确性和解释忠实度，无需外部理性标注。在此基础上设计了轻量级变体CAREF-AQ。

Result: 在四个NLE基准（COS-E、ECQA、ComVE、e-SNLI）上使用Flan-T5评估，CAREF-AQ变体仅用6.43%的可训练参数即达到最佳平均准确率（89.04）和解释对齐度（81.00 nBERT），优于LoRA和AdaLoRA。

Conclusion: CAREF是首个将熵正则化和稀疏正则化统一到单一训练目标中的可解释LLM微调方法，证明了校准感知正则化能够在不增加额外监督和大量参数的前提下同时提升准确性与解释忠实度。

Abstract: We introduce CAREF, a parameter-efficient fine-tuning framework that jointly optimizes predictive accuracy and explanation faithfulness via calibration-aware regularization. At its core, CAREF couples entropy-based calibration with token-level sparsity control through a single unified loss, the Calibration-Aware Regularization for Explanation Faithfulness (LSCED), without requiring rationale supervision. Evaluated on four NLE benchmarks (COS-E, ECQA, ComVE, e-SNLI) with Flan-T5, our lightweight CAREF-AQ variant attains the best average accuracy (89.04) and explanation alignment (81.00 nBERT) using only 6.43% of trainable parameters, outperforming LoRA and AdaLoRA. To our knowledge, CAREF is the first method to unify entropy and sparsity regularization in a single training objective for interpretable LLM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [239] [From Detection to Mechanism: Cross-Attention Graph Neural Networks Enable Drug-Drug Interaction Type Prediction An Ablation Study with Acetylsalicylic Acid Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27861)
*Juergen Dietrich*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统消融研究了三种图神经网络（GNN）架构在药物-药物相互作用（DDI）预测中的表现，发现双MPNN+四头交叉注意力（CrossAtt）在多类机制分类上比简单拼接提升+0.186 F1-macro（+45%），但二分类AUC仅提升+0.012（+1.3%），证实原子级分子间通信特异地促进了机制类型分类；三元架构因训练不稳定表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常将DDI二分类检测与多类机制分类混为一谈，缺乏对两种任务本质差异的深入分析。本文旨在通过系统性消融实验，揭示不同GNN架构在这两个任务上的表现差异，特别是探究原子级分子间交互机制（如交叉注意力）对机制类型分类的独特贡献。

Method: 在包含38,337个阳性对、86种交互类型的公开基准数据集上，比较三种GNN架构：双MPNN+拼接（Concat）、双MPNN+四头交叉注意力（CrossAtt）、三元MPNN+交互图（Ternary）。所有模型在相同训练条件（n=61,339对）下训练，并设立10个乙酰水杨酸（ASA）药物对作为留出验证集。

Result: CrossAtt在多类F1-macro上比Concat提升+0.186（+45%），而二分类AUC仅提升+0.012（+1.3%）；CrossAtt在ASA留出集上10/10完全正确预测机制类型，Ternary为0/10；所有架构存在两个一致的失败案例。

Conclusion: 原子级分子间通信（交叉注意力）特异地赋能机制类型分类，对二分类检测影响甚微；三元架构因训练不稳定而性能不佳；存在架构无关的结构性局限，需与毒性研究协同理解。

Abstract: Predicting whether two drugs interact (binary detection) is a substantially dif- ferent task from predicting the mechanism type of that interaction (multi-class classification). This study presents a systematic ablation study of three Graph Neural Network (GNN) architectures for drug-drug interaction (DDI) prediction on a publicly available benchmark dataset comprising 38,337 positive pairs across 86 interaction types. Three architectures are compared under identical training conditions (n = 61,339 pairs): a siamese dual Message Passing Neural Network (MPNN) with concatenation (Concat), a dual MPNN with four-head cross-attention (CrossAtt), and a ternary MPNN incorporating an interaction graph (Ternary). CrossAtt improves multi-class F1-macro by +0.186 absolute (+45%) over Concat, while improving binary AUC by only +0.012 (+1.3%) - confirming that atom-level inter-molecular communication specifically enables mechanism-type classification. The ternary architecture underperforms despite equivalent training data, with its failure consistent with a training instability hypothesis. Validation on ten acetylsali- cylic acid (ASA) drug pairs, held out prior to training, demonstrates 10/10 correct DDI-type predictions for CrossAtt versus 0/10 for Ternary. Two consistent failure cases are identified across all architectures, linking to structural limits established in a companion toxicity study.

</details>


### [240] [SPAR: Support-Preserving Action Rectification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27877)
*Jiaxin Zhao,Weihang Pan,Xun Liang,Binbin Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SPAR框架，通过残差修正和潜在自模仿机制解决离线策略提升中价值最大化与数据分布拟合的冲突，在D4RL基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线策略提升面临价值最大化与数据分布拟合之间的固有冲突：样本内加权回归过于保守，抑制了分布尾部的高价值动作；而梯度方法存在拟合-优化梯度冲突，驱使策略偏离数据流形。

Method: 提出支持保持动作修正(SPAR)框架：1) 将全局学习重构成锚定冻结纯行为克隆策略的局部残差修正，在残差空间进行细粒度拟合和局部策略提升，收缩搜索空间；2) 引入潜在自模仿机制，利用潜在采样加权回归机制解决残差空间中的拟合-改进梯度冲突。

Result: 理论上证明了该机制消除了标准值梯度的流形法向漂移；在D4RL实验上，SPAR从次优基线中提取显著收益，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: SPAR通过残差空间的局部修正和潜在自模仿机制，有效平衡了离线策略提升中的拟合与优化目标，既保持了策略分布的支持集，又实现了有效的策略改进。

Abstract: Offline policy improvement faces an inherent conflict between maximizing value and fitting the data distribution. While in-sample weighted regression is stable, it suffers from over-conservatism that suppresses high-value actions in the distribution tail; conversely, gradient-based approaches often exhibit a fitting-optimization conflict of gradients, which drives the policy off the data manifold. To address this, we propose Support-Preserving Action Rectification (SPAR), which reframes global learning as a local residual rectification anchored to a frozen pure behavior cloning policy. This framework performs fine-grained fitting and local policy improvement in the residual space, thereby contracting the search space. We further introduce Latent Self-Imitation, utilizing a latent-sampling weighted-regression mechanism to address fitting-improvement gradient conflict in the residual space. Theoretically, we prove this mechanism eliminates the manifold-normal drift of standard value gradients, while extensive D4RL experiments show SPAR extracts significant gains from suboptimal baselines to achieve state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [241] [FedEHR-Gen: Federated Synthetic Time-Series EHR Generation via Latent Space Alignment and Distribution-Aware Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27892)
*Jun Bai,Ziyang Song,Yue Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FedEHR-Gen，首个面向分布式医院的时间序列合成电子健康记录生成的联邦学习框架，通过两阶段学习（联邦自编码器+联邦时序条件变分自编码器）解决高维稀疏性和跨医院异质性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EHR生成模型多为集中式，需要跨医院数据池化，在真实数据共享受限场景下不可行；直接联邦建模因高维稀疏性和跨医院异质性而崩溃或发散。

Method: 两阶段学习范式：(1) 联邦自编码器将高维稀疏EHR特征投影到紧凑潜空间，采用逐层匹配聚合机制对齐各医院局部编码器；(2) 在对齐潜空间上训练联邦时序条件变分自编码器（TCVAE），使用分布感知聚合实现稳定的时序生成建模。

Result: 在eICU和MIMIC-III数据集上，FedEHR-Gen在生成保真度、下游任务效用和隐私风险方面与集中式训练相当，且持续优于标准联邦基线。

Conclusion: FedEHR-Gen有效解决了联邦场景下EHR生成的去中心化难题，在保护隐私的同时实现了接近集中式训练的性能。

Abstract: Synthetic Electronic Health Record (EHR) generation provides a promising avenue for data augmentation and cross-hospital modeling in privacy-constrained healthcare settings. However, most existing EHR generative models are centralized and require pooling data across hospitals, which is often infeasible when real-world data sharing is restricted. While federated EHR generation offers a natural solution, direct federated modeling often collapses or diverges due to the high dimensionality, sparsity, and cross-hospital heterogeneity of EHR data. In this work, we propose FedEHR-Gen, the first federated framework for synthetic time-series EHR generation across distributed hospitals. FedEHR-Gen uses a two-stage learning paradigm. First, we introduce a federated autoencoder that projects high-dimensional and sparse EHR features onto a compact latent space. To ensure semantic consistency across hospitals, we develop a layer-wise matching aggregation mechanism that aligns local encoders into a unified global latent space. Second, operating on this aligned latent space, we train a federated temporal conditional variational autoencoder (TCVAE) with distribution-aware aggregation, enabling stable temporal generative modeling under severe cross-hospital heterogeneity. Extensive experiments on the eICU and MIMIC-III datasets demonstrate that FedEHR-Gen achieves generation fidelity, downstream utility, and privacy risk comparable to centralized training, while consistently outperforming the standard federated baseline.

</details>


### [242] [Where LLM Annotators Fail: Label-Free Learning on Graphs with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27913)
*Safal Thapaliya,Jiatan Huang,Chuxu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Cluster-Aware Noise Estimation (CANE)，一种无需真实标签即可估计LLM标注噪声的图学习框架，利用聚类条件可靠性估计来筛选和纠正伪标签，在多个图基准上优于现有无标签方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图上的节点分类通常需要大量人工标注，成本高昂。虽然可以利用LLM为少量节点生成低成本标签，但这些标签存在噪声。现有方法通常将噪声建模为全局或类别条件，忽略了LLM标注错误在同一类别内不同特征空间聚类间的显著差异，因此需要更精细的噪声估计方法。

Method: 提出CANE框架：首先对节点特征进行聚类，然后估计每个聚类内LLM标注的可靠性（无需真实标签），基于聚类条件的可靠度估计来决定哪些伪标签值得信任、哪些需要纠正，最终用于训练GNN进行节点分类。

Result: 在多个图基准数据集和不同GNN骨干网络上，CANE均优于最强的无标签基线方法，在聚类条件噪声更明显的数据集上提升最大。

Conclusion: LLM标注噪声不仅是类别相关的，更是区域相关的（聚类条件），考虑这种精细化的噪声结构能有效提升无标签图学习的性能。

Abstract: Node classification on graphs often requires labeled nodes, yet obtaining labels at graph scale is expensive. When node attributes contain semantic content, such as paper abstracts, web pages, or product descriptions, large language models (LLMs) can provide low-cost supervision by annotating a small subset of nodes. However, these LLM-generated labels are noisy, and existing label-free graph learning methods usually treat this noise as either global or class-conditional. We find that LLM annotation errors are not only class-dependent but also region-dependent: within the same class, reliability can vary sharply across feature-space clusters. In light of this, we propose Cluster-Aware Noise Estimation (CANE), a label-free learning framework that estimates cluster-conditional LLM reliability without ground truth labels, and uses this estimate to decide which pseudo-labels to trust, and which labels to correct. Across various graph benchmarks and GNN backbones, CANE improves over the strongest label-free baselines, with the largest gains on datasets exhibiting stronger cluster-conditional noise.

</details>


### [243] [Cyclical Entropy Eruption: Entropy Dynamics in Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27954)
*Wendi Li,Shawn Im,Sharon Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现智能体强化学习中存在一种称为“循环熵爆发”（cyclical entropy eruption）的新现象，表现为训练过程中熵值周期性急剧上升并逐渐回落，而非像单轮推理强化学习中熵值持续走低。这一动态过程分为三个阶段，且爆发期间产生的退化模式（如句子重复、幻觉）会在循环间积累。为此，作者提出SEAL（分离增强型智能体学习）方法，通过轻量级辅助损失在表示空间中分离正确与错误轨迹，从而稳定训练并提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体大语言模型在现实任务中广泛应用，但智能体强化学习的训练动力学尚未被充分理解，限制了我们对训练不稳定的诊断和更有效算法的设计。本文旨在揭示并解释这一未被充分探索的现象。

Method: 将循环熵爆发分解为三个阶段，提供理论与实证分析；提出SEAL辅助损失函数，在表示空间中将正确与错误轨迹分离，从根源上抑制熵爆发。

Result: 在多个基准、模型和强化学习算法上的实验表明，SEAL能够稳定训练并提升智能体的下游性能，同时有效缓解熵爆发带来的退化模式积累问题。

Conclusion: 循环熵爆发是智能体强化学习中特有且关键的动态现象，理解并控制它对提升训练稳定性和最终性能至关重要；SEAL提供了一种轻量级且有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Agentic large language models are increasingly used to solve real-world tasks by reasoning over goals, invoking tools, and interacting with external environments. Reinforcement learning provides a natural framework for improving these behaviors, and recent agent RL methods have achieved strong results across domains. However, the training dynamics of agent RL remain poorly understood, limiting our ability to diagnose instabilities and design more effective training algorithms. In this work, we identify a previously underexplored phenomenon in agent RL, which we term cyclical entropy eruption. Unlike single-turn reasoning RL, where entropy typically collapses and stays low, agent RL training exhibits unique recurring cycles of sharp entropy eruption and gradual subsidence. We decompose this dynamic into three phases and provide theoretical and empirical analyses of each, explaining the mechanisms underlying its cyclical oscillation. We further show that degenerate patterns such as sentence duplication and hallucination, once acquired during eruption, can persist and accumulate across cycles. Motivated by these findings, we propose SEAL (Separation-Enhanced Agent Learning), a lightweight auxiliary loss that separates correct and incorrect trajectories in representation space, directly targeting the root cause of entropy eruption. Experiments across multiple benchmarks, models, and RL algorithms demonstrate that SEAL stabilizes training and yields stronger downstream agent performance.

</details>


### [244] [Law of Neural Interaction: Depth-Width Shape, Interaction Efficiency, and Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27989)
*Wenjie Sun,Jinning Yang,Shuai Zhang,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出神经交互概念（将叠加从参数空间扩展到梯度空间），发现固定预算下模型存在高效交互区间，深度宽度比（R_D/W）影响资源利用效率和泛化能力，且该区间随预算扩展保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在固定预算下资源利用效率存疑，前人证明叠加（superposition）是损失的重要来源，本文旨在探究资源利用效率与泛化之间的关系。

Method: 基于神经特征假设（Neural Feature Ansatz），将叠加概念从参数空间扩展到梯度空间，定义为神经交互（neural interaction）；通过调整深度宽度比（R_D/W）将模型置于高效交互区间。

Result: 固定预算下，良好泛化伴随高效神经交互；高效交互区间随预算扩展保持稳定；靠近该区间的小规模密集LLM在MMLU-Pro上表现更好。

Conclusion: R_D/W通过影响资源利用效率进而影响泛化能力，为模型形状初始化和泛化机制理解提供新见解。

Abstract: The guidance of scaling laws has increased the resource demands of modern large language models (LLMs), yet it remains questionable whether these models utilize resources effectively under a fixed budget. Previous research has proved superposition as a key contributor to loss. By leveraging the Neural Feature Ansatz, we extend superposition from parameter space to gradient space and define it as neural interaction. We find that under a fixed budget, good generalization is usually accompanied by efficient neural interactions, and the model can be placed in an efficient interaction interval by adjusting its depth-width ratio ($R_{D/W}$). In addition, as the budget scales up, the efficient interaction interval of the model remains relatively stable. By comparing existing small scale dense LLMs, we observe that models operating near this interval tend to perform better on the MMLU-Pro benchmark. Our findings reveal that the $R_{D/W}$ influences resource utilization efficiency and thereby affects generalization, providing insights into model shape initialization and the understanding of model generalization mechanisms. Code for Neural Interaction Law is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Neural_Interaction_Law-D788

</details>


### [245] [Geometry-Correct Diffusion Posterior Sampling with Denoiser-Pullback Curvature Guidance and Manifold-Aligned Damping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27990)
*Seunghyeok Shin,Minwoo Kim,Dabin Kim,Hongki Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于阻尼高斯-牛顿修正的扩散后验采样方法，通过逐噪声水平的曲率建模替代传统标量引导权重，在多个逆问题任务中实现了竞争性能且速度更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统扩散后验采样中数据一致性更新依赖人工调节的标量引导权重，且在高刚度、算子依赖的曲率下采样不稳定。

Method: 在扩散状态坐标下引入逐噪声水平的阻尼高斯-牛顿修正；通过单侧曲率模型避免正向去噪器雅可比计算；使用扩散校准的秩一阻尼与去噪器残差对齐；每个修正通过矩阵自由GMRES结合自动微分求解；采用具有闭式漂移/噪声分裂的方差保持朗之万转移进行采样。

Result: 在FFHQ和ImageNet上的多个逆问题中，PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS指标与基线相当，运行速度显著更快；在加速MRI重建中，PSNR/SSIM达到基线最佳水平。

Conclusion: 所提方法有效替代了人工调参的标量引导，在保持采样质量的同时提升了稳定性和效率，尤其适用于对曲率敏感的逆问题。

Abstract: Diffusion posterior sampling conditions diffusion priors on measurements, but data-consistency updates are typically scaled by hand-tuned guidance weights and can destabilize sampling under stiff, operator-dependent curvature. We replace scalar guidance with a per-noise-level damped Gauss--Newton correction computed in diffusion-state coordinates. The correction pulls likelihood gradients back through the denoiser, uses a one-sided curvature model that avoids forward denoiser Jacobians, and applies diffusion-calibrated rank-one damping aligned with the denoiser residual. Each correction is solved with matrix-free GMRES using automatic differentiation, and sampling proceeds with a variance-preserving Langevin transition with a closed-form drift/noise split. On FFHQ and ImageNet across inverse problems, it achieves competitive PSNR/SSIM/LPIPS while running markedly faster than most of the compared baselines; on accelerated MRI reconstruction, it achieves the best PSNR/SSIM among the compared baselines.

</details>


### [246] [Patched-DeltaNet: Token-Level Event-Driven Memory for Linear-Time Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27992)
*Tae-Gyun Lee,Junyoung Park,Kyu Won Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Patched-DeltaNet提出了一种结合时间序列分块与门控Delta网络的架构，通过令牌级事件驱动记忆机制实现异常检测，在SMD基准上以O(L/P)的计算复杂度取得ROC-AUC 0.957和PA-F1 0.822的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的模型（如PatchTST）在时间序列异常检测中表现优异，但其O(L²)的计算复杂度严重限制了在资源受限环境中的部署，亟需一种高效且性能优越的替代方案。

Method: 提出Patched-DeltaNet架构，核心创新包括：(1) 时间序列分块机制提取局部语义片段；(2) 门控Delta网络基于误差驱动的更新规则，仅在发生显著物理变化（即delta）时更新循环状态，实现令牌级事件驱动记忆，有效滤除背景噪声并捕获突发异常漂移。

Result: 在Server Machine Dataset (SMD)基准上，在统一评估约束和相同计算预算下严格超越近期架构，ROC-AUC达0.957，PA-F1达0.822，计算复杂度降至理论最小值O(L/P)。

Conclusion: Patched-DeltaNet通过分块与Delta网络的协同作用，验证了令牌级事件驱动记忆机制的有效性，在显著降低计算复杂度的同时实现了更优的异常检测性能，适用于资源受限场景。

Abstract: Time series anomaly detection is critical for maintaining the reliability of mission-critical systems. While Transformer-based models like PatchTST have shown remarkable performance, their $\mathcal{O}(L^2)$ computational complexity severely limits deployment in resource-constrained environments. In this paper, we propose Patched-DeltaNet, a novel architecture combining time-series patching with Gated Delta Networks. By integrating these paradigms, we hypothesize and demonstrate the emergence of token-level event-driven memory, whereby the patching mechanism extracts local semantic chunks, while the error-driven DeltaNet updates its recurrent state exclusively when significant physical changes, defined as deltas, occur. This synergy effectively filters out background noise and captures sudden anomalous drifts. Our rigorous experiments on the Server Machine Dataset (SMD) benchmark demonstrate the structural superiority and sample efficiency of Patched-DeltaNet. By strictly outperforming recent architectures under unified evaluation constraints and identical compute budgets, our model yields an ROC-AUC of 0.957 and PA-F1 of 0.822, while drastically reducing computational complexity to the theoretical minimum of $\mathcal{O}(L/P)$.

</details>


### [247] [Learning Compositional Latent Structure with Vector Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28007)
*Niclas Pokel,Benjamin F. Grewe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出向量网络（VN），一种层级循环架构，每层用可复用的秩-1权重原子库替代固定权重矩阵，通过局部能量最小化推断稀疏的活动原子及其系数，实现输入特定的低秩权重组合，在组合泛化任务上显著优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络将不同计算模式存储在共享权重矩阵中，当熟悉的结构以新组合出现时难以选择性复用或适应，缺乏组合泛化能力。

Method: 提出向量网络（VN），每层维护一个可复用的秩-1权重原子库；对每个输入，通过最小化局部能量（联合约束自底向上输入重构和自顶向下反馈一致性）推断稀疏活动原子及其系数，组成样本特定的低秩权重矩阵；学习仅更新被选中的权重原子，通过推断系数缩放的局部残差信号驱动。

Result: 在1D信号、2D空间解码、N体动力学和组合MNIST四个组合性基准上，VN在分布内匹配强基线，在需要以新方式重组熟悉因子的分布外场景中，误差通常降低约一个数量级。

Conclusion: 向量网络通过将组合泛化构建为架构和推理过程的结构性属性，而非将多种行为塞入共享稠密参数基质的脆弱的副产品。

Abstract: Deep networks are powerful function approximators, but they typically store many different computations in shared weight matrices, making it difficult to selectively reuse or adapt parts of them when a familiar structure appears in novel combinations. We introduce the Vector Network (VN), a hierarchical recurrent architecture in which each layer replaces a fixed weight matrix with a library of reusable rank-1 weight atoms. For each input, VN minimizes a layer-local energy to infer a sparse set of active weight atoms and their coefficients, jointly constrained by bottom-up input reconstruction and top-down feedback consistency. These weight atom coefficients then compose an input-specific low-rank weight matrix for that sample. After convergence, slow learning updates only the selected weight atoms through local residual signals scaled by the inferred coefficients. We evaluate VN on four compositional benchmarks spanning 1D signals, 2D spatial decoding, N-body dynamics, and compositional MNIST. VN matches strong baselines in distribution while often achieving out-of-distribution error about an order of magnitude lower when familiar factors must be recombined in novel ways. Vector networks thus make compositional generalization a structural property of the architecture and inference process rather than a brittle byproduct of fitting many behaviors into one shared dense parameter substrate.

</details>


### [248] [AOE: Exhaustive Out-of-Distribution Detection via Recalibrating Outlier Labels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28021)
*Fengqiang Wan,Qing-Yuan Jiang,Yang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应置信异常暴露（AOE）方法，通过温度缩放生成自适应软标签，在保留OOD样本与ID类别语义关系的同时扩大分离边界，有效提升了OOD检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于异常暴露（OE）的OOD检测方法使用均匀标签最大化OOD样本熵，但理论上证明均匀标签会导致"过度软化效应"，忽略OOD样本与ID类别间的关系，产生次优边界。

Method: 提出自适应置信异常暴露（AOE），利用可学习的温度参数对模型预测进行温度缩放，生成自适应的软标签作为OOD样本的监督信号。该软标签既保留了类别关系信息，又趋近高熵分布。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明AOE方法有效，能够抑制OOD样本的过度自信预测并扩大ID与OOD的分离边界。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析和实验验证，证明了显式利用OOD样本与ID类别的关系可以提升OOD检测性能，AOE是一种简单有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is essential for deploying machine learning models in open-world and safety-critical scenarios, where test inputs may deviate from the training distribution and overconfident predictions on unknown samples can lead to unreliable decisions. Outlier Exposure (OE) has emerged as a promising OOD detection paradigm by introducing auxiliary outliers during training to enlarge the margin between in-distribution (ID) and OOD samples. Existing OE-based methods typically enlarge this margin by employing uniform labels to maximize the entropy of OOD samples over ID categories. However, we theoretically show that uniform labels inevitably disregard the relations between OOD samples and ID categories, termed the over-softening effect, leading to a suboptimal margin bound. Our theoretical analysis further reveals that explicitly exploiting such relations can instead yield improved OOD detection performance. Motivated by this insight, we propose \underline{A}daptive Confidence \underline{OE} (AOE), a simple yet effective method that leverages temperature scaling to recalibrate outlier labels. Specifically, AOE generates adaptive soft targets from temperature-scaled model predictions for OOD samples, where the learnable temperature smooths the prediction distribution without fully erasing class-wise relational information. By supervising OOD samples with these adaptive soft targets, AOE preserves the semantic proximity between OOD samples and ID categories while encouraging the softened targets to approach a high-entropy distribution, thereby suppressing overconfident OOD predictions and enlarging the separation margin. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of AOE.

</details>


### [249] [BPPO: Binary Prefix Policy Optimization for Efficient GRPO-Style Reasoning RL with Concise Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28028)
*Qingfei Zhao,Huan Song,Shuyu Tian,Jiawei Shao,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出二元前缀策略优化（BPPO），通过仅使用组内最短正确和最短错误完成作为更新单元，并聚焦前缀优化，在保持竞争性精度的同时实现最高6.08倍加速，并将响应长度减少约30-50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO在训练推理模型时更新所有采样完成，成本高昂且会强化冗长的推理轨迹。作者研究是否所有完成都提供同样有用的更新信号，发现同类完成梯度方向高度相似，而正误对提供更显著的对比信号。

Method: 提出BPPO方法：(1) 仅使用组内最短正确和最短错误完成作为紧凑更新单元；(2) 保留全组优势归一化；(3) 自适应完成调度；(4) 前缀聚焦优化，只更新响应前缀，避免强化冗余后缀。

Result: 在GSM8K、MATH和Geo3K上，BPPO相比GRPO实现最高6.08倍加速，保持竞争性精度，并将平均响应长度减少约30-50%，无需显式长度惩罚。

Conclusion: 通过选择性采样和前缀优化，BPPO显著提升GRPO风格推理RL的训练效率，同时自然产生更简洁的推理轨迹。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is widely used for training reasoning models, but updating all sampled completions in each group incurs substantial cost and can reinforce verbose reasoning trajectories. In this paper, we study whether all completions provide equally useful update signals in GRPO-style reasoning RL. Our gradient-similarity analysis shows that, within the same prompt group, same-class completions often induce highly similar update directions, whereas correct-incorrect pairs provide more distinct contrastive signals. Motivated by this observation, we propose Binary Prefix Policy Optimization (BPPO), which uses the shortest correct completion and the shortest incorrect completion as a compact update unit while preserving full-group advantage normalization. BPPO further improves efficiency with adaptive completion scheduling and prefix-focused optimization; by updating only response prefixes, it avoids reinforcing redundant suffixes and encourages more concise responses. Experiments on GSM8K, MATH, and Geo3K show that BPPO achieves up to 6.08x speedup over GRPO while maintaining competitive accuracy, and reduces mean response length by approximately 30-50% without modifying the reward with an explicit length penalty.

</details>


### [250] [SPARD: Defending Harmful Fine-Tuning Attack via Safety Projection with Relevance-Diversity Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28030)
*Shuhao Chen,Weisen Jiang,Yeqi Gong,Shengda Luo,Chengxiang Zhuo,Zang Li,James T. Kwok,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SPARD是一种针对大语言模型有害微调攻击的防御框架，通过安全投影交替优化与相关性-多样性数据选择，在保持高任务精度的同时显著降低攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微调大型语言模型往往会削弱其安全对齐，有害微调攻击进一步利用对抗性数据移除安全防护并诱导不安全行为，亟需有效的防御方法。

Method: SPARD框架包含两个核心组件：(1) SPAG：在效用更新与显式安全投影之间交替优化，利用安全数据集强制执行安全约束；(2) 基于相关性-多样性行列式点过程（RD-DPP）选择紧凑的安全数据，平衡任务相关性与安全覆盖范围。

Result: 在GSM8K和OpenBookQA数据集上，针对四种有害微调攻击的实验中，SPARD consistently取得最低的平均攻击成功率，显著优于现有最优防御方法，同时保持高任务精度。

Conclusion: SPARD为防御大语言模型的有害微调攻击提供了一种有效的解决方案，兼顾了安全性与任务性能。代码已开源。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large language models often undermines their safety alignment, a problem further amplified by harmful fine-tuning attacks in which adversarial data removes safeguards and induces unsafe behaviors. We propose SPARD, a defense framework that integrates Safety-Projected Alternating optimization with Relevance-Diversity aware data selection. SPARD employs SPAG, which optimizes alternatively between utility updates and explicit safety projections with a set of safe data to enforce safety constraints. To curate safe data, we introduce a Relevance-Diversity Determinantal Point Process to select compact safe data, balancing task relevance and safety coverage. Experiments on GSM8K and OpenBookQA under four harmful fine-tuning attacks demonstrate that SPARD consistently achieves the lowest average attack success rates, substantially outperforming state-of-the-art defense methods, while maintaining high task accuracy. Code is available at https://github.com/shuhao02/SPARD.

</details>


### [251] [RW-TTT: Batched Serving for Request-Owned Test-Time Training State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28053)
*Jian Yang,Zhizhuo Kou,Yao Tian,Hao Zhang,Han Chen,Sirui Han,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RW-TTT，一种为支持测试时训练（TTT）的LLM推理服务设计的批处理框架。通过给每个解码步骤打上所有者、版本和读写操作标签，仅批处理兼容阶段，并将更新仅提交给所有者，避免了TTT流之间状态污染的问题。在单GPU上，8个InPlace-TTT流达到274.61 tok/s聚合吞吐，相比顺序执行快9.31倍，相比每流独立副本快3.44倍，且不损失长上下文基准性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理服务假设共享静态权重，而TTT需要在生成过程中动态更新请求专属状态（如快速权重、低秩增量或流式学习器状态），导致无法直接批处理：串行执行正确但慢，朴素批处理会破坏请求状态。需要一种既能利用批处理加速又能保证TTT状态隔离的推理服务方案。

Method: 提出RW-TTT框架，核心设计包括：(1) 为每个解码步骤打上所有者、版本和READ/WRITE操作标签；(2) 仅批处理兼容阶段（同操作类型且无冲突依赖的步骤）；(3) 更新仅提交给所属的所有者流，避免跨流状态污染。

Result: 在单GPU上运行8个InPlace-TTT流，RW-TTT达到274.61 tok/s聚合吞吐，是串行服务的9.31倍，是每流独立副本的3.44倍（相同内存预算下）。在RULER长上下文基准测试中保持正确行为，并通过了所有者/版本一致性检查。

Conclusion: RW-TTT有效地解决了TTT推理服务中的批处理难题，在保证状态隔离和行为正确性的前提下大幅提升吞吐，为支持动态权重更新的LLM高效推理服务提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Test-time training (TTT) adapts an LLM during generation by reading and updating request-owned state, such as fast weights, low-rank deltas, or streaming learner state. This breaks batched LLM serving, which assumes shared static weights: serial execution is correct but slow, while naive batching can corrupt request state. We formulate this problem as read-write TTT serving and present RW-TTT , which tags each decode step with its owner, version, and READ/WRITE effect, batches only compatible phases, and commits updates only to the owner. On one GPU with eight fast-weight InPlace-TTT streams, RW-TTT reaches 274.61 aggregate tok/s, 9.31x over sequential serving and 3.44x over per-stream replicas under the same memory budget. It preserves behavior on RULER, a long-context benchmark, and passes owner/version checks.

</details>


### [252] [On the Learnability of Test-Time Adaptation: A Recovery Complexity Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28057)
*Zhi Zhou,Ming Yang,Shi-Yu Tian,Kun-Yang Yu,Lan-Zhe Guo,Yu-Feng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出测试时自适应（TTA）的理论学习框架，定义了(ε,δ)-恢复复杂度和(ε,ρ)-TTA可学习性，揭示了TTA在非平稳流中的基本极限与自适应-信息权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTA在非平稳测试流中虽取得实证成功，但其可学习性缺乏理论框架，现有方法无法同时对齐TTA目标、捕获持续演变的分布漂移以及内在信息约束。

Method: 提出(ε,δ)-恢复复杂度衡量漂移后恢复至目标超额风险所需时间，并扩展到(ε,ρ)-TTA可学习性衡量长期可靠性；引入非平稳测试流的离散代理变量以统一分析渐变和突变漂移，推导恢复复杂度的阶匹配上下界。

Result: 获得了恢复复杂度的阶匹配下界和上界，揭示了TTA的基本极限以及自适应-信息的内在权衡。

Conclusion: 该框架为TTA提供了统一的可学习性保证，补充了基于遗憾的分析方法，为理解TTA在非平稳环境中的理论能力奠定了基础。

Abstract: Test-time adaptation (TTA) aims to adapt models to maintain reliable performance on non-stationary test streams without requiring labeled data. Despite its empirical success, the learnability of TTA under non-stationary streams remains unexplored. A key challenge is the lack of a principled theoretical framework that simultaneously aligns with the TTA objective and captures both continuously evolving distribution shifts and intrinsic information constraints. To address this gap, we propose the first theoretical framework for studying the learnability of TTA and introduce $(ε,δ)$-Recovery Complexity and $(ε,ρ)$-TTA Learnability. Recovery complexity measures the post-shift time needed to maintain excess risk below a target level with high probability, and is further extended to TTA learnability, which measures the long-term reliability of TTA. Within this framework, we introduce a novel discrete surrogate for non-stationary test streams, enabling a unified and tractable analysis of both gradual and abrupt shifts. We derive order-wise matching lower and upper bounds on recovery complexity, revealing fundamental limits of TTA and an intrinsic adaptivity-information trade-off. These results provide unified learnability guarantees for TTA that complement regret-based analyses.

</details>


### [253] [PINE: Pruning Boosted Tree Ensembles with Conformal In-Distribution Prediction Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28068)
*Haruki Yajima,Yusuke Matsui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PINE是一种针对树集成模型的剪枝方法，通过保形校准在分布内区域保证预测等价性，在12个表格数据集上实现了高达30%的压缩率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有忠实剪枝方法虽然能保证整个输入空间上的预测等价性，但压缩率较低；而标准剪枝方法牺牲了决策一致性。PINE旨在在分布内区域提供强保证的同时提升压缩率。

Method: PINE通过单一参数α进行保形校准（conformal calibration），在分布内区域（in-distribution region）保持预测等价性，并控制该区域的大小。

Result: 在12个公开表格数据集上，PINE相比现有忠实剪枝方法，压缩率提升高达30%，同时保持可比的预测保留水平。

Conclusion: PINE在压缩率与预测等价性保证之间取得了更好的权衡，通过将保证从全局输入空间缩放到分布内区域，实现了更优的剪枝效果。

Abstract: Tree ensembles are machine learning models with strong predictive performance and interpretability, and remain widely used for tabular data. Standard pruning methods for tree ensembles typically optimize an accuracy-compression trade-off and may change a subset of predictions, potentially compromising decision consistency. Faithful pruning methods address this issue by preserving prediction equivalence over the entire input space, but this requirement leads to lower compression ratios. We propose PINE, a pruning method that provides strong guarantees within an in-distribution region. PINE preserves prediction equivalence within this region and controls the region size using a single parameter $α$ via conformal calibration. Experiments on 12 public tabular datasets show that PINE improves the compression ratio by up to $30\%$ while preserving predictions at a comparable level to existing faithful pruning methods.

</details>


### [254] [Measure-to-measure Regression with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28075)
*Matthew Vandergrift,Martha White,Yury Polyanskiy,Philippe Rigollet,Lazar Atanackovic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究度量到度量（M2M）回归问题，即从有限观测的输入-输出对中学习概率测度之间的映射。提出了两种基于Transformer的方法（静态M2M映射和动态M2M速度场），在合成实验、相互作用粒子系统和大规模患者来源类器官数据集上展示了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学问题（如细胞生物学）需要预测群体在未知变换下的演化，而群体自然表示为概率测度。现有方法在表达性和可扩展性上不足，亟需能够处理分布到分布回归的框架。

Method: 形式化了非线性M2M回归问题，提出两种基于Transformer的方法：(1) 静态M2M映射——将Transformer作为输入分布到输出分布的直接映射；(2) 动态M2M速度场——利用Transformer建模分布的演化速度场。方法利用Transformer固有的测度依赖和平均场结构来学习概率分布空间上的非线性映射。

Result: 在合成实验、相互作用粒子系统以及大规模患者来源类器官数据集（预测结直肠癌治疗反应）上验证了方法有效性，能够泛化到未见过的测度。

Conclusion: 提出的Transformer-based M2M回归方法具有表达性强、易用且可扩展的特点，为处理分布级回归问题提供了有效框架，在科学应用中具有潜力。

Abstract: Many learning problems require predicting how populations evolve under an unknown transformation. A natural representation for such populations is a probability measure, with point clouds as a key example. In this work, we study the measure-to-measure (M2M) regression problem, in which one seeks to learn a map between probability measures from a finite collection of observed input-output pairs. In contrast to classical regression, where individual samples are transformed independently, M2M regression treats entire distributions as the data points. This perspective is vital in certain scientific applications, for example, cellular and molecular biology, where cells are known to evolve not as independent data points but as a collection. However, few existing approaches address the problem of M2M regression with sufficient expressivity and scalability. We present a formalization of nonlinear M2M regression and introduce two easy-to-use, expressive, and scalable approaches to learn such operators: transformers as static M2M maps and transformers as dynamic M2M velocity fields. Our approach leverages the natural measure-dependent and mean-field structure of transformers to learn nonlinear M2M maps on the space of probability distributions. We illustrate the effectiveness of our proposed method to generalize to unseen measures on synthetic experiments, interacting particle systems, and a large-scale patient-derived organoid dataset for predicting treatment response in colorectal cancer.

</details>


### [255] [Benchmarking Inductive Biases for Multivariate Time-Series Anomaly Detection with a Robust Multi-View Channel-Graph Detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28103)
*Junhao Wei,Yanxiao Li,Bidong Chen,Yifu Zhao,Haochen Li,Dexing Yao,Baili Lu,Xudong Ye,Jietian Feng,Sio-Kei Im,Yapeng Wang,Xu Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的多元时间序列异常检测实验、分析和基准研究，在五个数据集上评估了十个代表性检测器，并提出了一种自适应检测器家族\ours{}，该模型在宏观平均VUS-ROC上达到0.675，比第二名LSTM-AE高出5.1个百分点，在有效性、效率和鲁棒性方面均表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多元时间序列异常检测研究缺乏统一的实验协议、跨数据集评估和公平的基准比较，导致不同方法之间的性能对比缺乏可重复性和可靠性。

Method: 本文提出了一个统一的实验框架，在五个数据集上评估十个代表性检测器（涵盖统计、重建、关联、频率和通用transformer家族），所有方法共享相同的窗口化、评分、硬件和度量协议。同时提出了\ours{}自适应检测器家族，结合NOTEARS约束的有向通道图视图与可选的patch-attention和时间关联视图。

Result: \ours{}在宏观平均VUS-ROC上达到0.675，比第二名LSTM-AE高出5.1个百分点，在所有五个数据集上均进入前三名。在噪声、通道缺失和时间偏移扰动下均获得最强的绝对VUS-ROC。此外，发现无单一偏置基线占优、绝对扰动VUS-ROC比保留比率更具信息量、MSDS是事件密集型部署负载而非稀疏点异常基准。

Conclusion: 统一的实验协议揭示了三个与方法无关的发现，验证了\ours{}在多元时间序列异常检测中的优越性能和鲁棒性，并发布了MSDS预处理协议、配置、脚本和种子级度量数据以促进可重复性研究。

Abstract: We present a unified experiment, analysis, and benchmark study of multivariate time-series (MTS) anomaly detection. Ten family-representative detectors -- spanning statistical, reconstruction, association, frequency, and generic-transformer families -- are evaluated on five datasets (SMD, MSL, SMAP, PSM, and MSDS) under effectiveness, efficiency, robustness, and cross-dataset generalisation. All methods share the same windowing, scoring, hardware, and metric protocols. Effectiveness, ablation, and robustness use three random seeds; cross-dataset transfer uses seed~0 because each extra seed requires $250$ source-target evaluations. The benchmark yields three method-independent findings: no single-bias baseline dominates; absolute perturbation VUS-ROC is more informative than retention ratios; and MSDS behaves as an event-dense deployment workload rather than a sparse point-anomaly benchmark. Under this protocol we also introduce \ours{}, an adaptive detector family combining a NOTEARS-constrained directed channel-graph view with optional patch-attention and temporal-association views. \ours{} achieves the best macro-average VUS-ROC ($0.675$, $+5.1$~pt over the second-best LSTM-AE), ranks first overall, and reaches the top-3 on all five datasets. Its wins on MSL and MSDS are narrow, while its average and robustness gains are larger: under the same three-seed robustness protocol for every method, it obtains the strongest absolute VUS-ROC across noise, channel dropout, and time-shift perturbations. We release the MSDS preprocessing protocol, configurations, scripts, and seed-level metric dumps.

</details>


### [256] [Long Live The Balance: Information Bottleneck Driven Tree-based Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28109)
*Hao Jiang,Shurui Li,Tianpeng Bu,Bowen Xu,Xin Liu,Qihua Chen,Hongtao Duan,Lulu Hu,Bin Yang,Minying Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于信息瓶颈理论的IB-Score指标来评估大模型在线强化学习中的探索-利用平衡，并设计了IB-TPO框架，通过IB引导的树采样策略和蒙特卡洛估计，在标准基准上相比GRPO提升2.9%-3.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型在线强化学习方法在复杂推理任务中常表现出探索-利用不平衡，导致优化不稳定和次优性能，缺乏有效的量化指标来评估和引导这种平衡。

Method: 提出IB-Score指标量化步骤级推理多样性与正确答案互信息之间的权衡；基于此设计IB-TPO框架，包含IB引导的树采样策略（相同token预算下多生成50%轨迹）和基于树结构的IB-Score蒙特卡洛估计方法。

Result: 在标准基准上，IB-TPO相比GRPO基线提升2.9%-3.6%，并超越其他SOTA在线RL方法；树采样策略在相同token预算下生成50%更多轨迹。

Conclusion: 基于信息瓶颈理论的探索-利用平衡量化方法能有效提升在线RL训练效率和效果，IB-TPO是一种有原则且高效的优化框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in online reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated promising performance in complex reasoning tasks. However, they often exhibit an imbalanced exploration-exploitation trade-off, resulting in unstable optimization and sub-optimal performance. We introduce IB-Score, a novel metric grounded in Information Bottleneck theory that evaluates policy's exploration-exploitation balance by quantifying the trade-off between step-level reasoning diversity and mutual information shared with the correct answer. Analysis based on IB-Score shows that popular online RL approaches (e.g., GRPO) with common regularizers fail to consistently maintain balance during training with suboptimal results. To address this, we propose Information Bottleneck-driven Tree-based Policy Optimization (IB-TPO), a principled framework that formulates IB-Score as a fine-grained optimization objective and utilizes a novel IB-guided tree sampling strategy that not only improves the efficiency of online sampling with 50% more trajectories under the same token budget, but also reuses the tree structure for effective IB-Score Monte Carlo estimation. Extensive experiments across standard benchmarks show that our method significantly outperforms GRPO baseline by 2.9% to 3.6% and also outperforms other state-of-the-art online RL approaches. Our code is available at https://github.com/alibaba/EfficientRL.

</details>


### [257] [Chreode: A Cell World Model for One-Step Temporal Dynamics and Perturbation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28111)
*Mufan Qiu,Genhui Zheng,Yinuo Xu,Ruichen Zhang,Ying Ding,Qi Long,Tianlong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chreode是一种单步细胞世界模型，通过结构化的残差转移算子实现动作条件化的细胞状态转变预测。该模型将分布演化从推理时转移到训练时，支持单步生成，并保留了Waddington-inspired的分解（下坡景观流、切向旋转动力学和随机扩散）。在240万细胞的鼠胚胎图谱上预训练后，微调效果优于现有方法，且预训练动力学表征可迁移至CRISPR扰动预测任务（GEARS的DE20 MSE降低12.4%），并能零样本生成与强动态OT基线相当的克隆命运分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的细胞状态转变预测方法要么拟合静态的控制-处理映射而忽略时间信息，要么在每个数据集上独立求解多步ODE/Schrödinger桥问题，缺乏一个统一的、可预训练的单步细胞世界模型来高效预测动作条件化的转录状态演化。

Method: 提出Chreode模型，采用结构化残差转移算子将分布演化从推理时转移到训练时，实现单步生成。模型分解为下坡景观流、切向旋转动力学和随机扩散三个组件。使用共享的scVI编码器和DiT-based动力学骨干网络，在包含7个数据集、240万细胞的鼠胚胎图谱上预训练。

Result: 微调后在Weinreb造血和Veres胰岛分化任务上Sinkhorn距离优于scratch模型、PI-SDE和PRESCIENT。预训练表征迁移至GEARS后，DE20 MSE从0.2121降至0.1858（相对提升12.4%）。零样本克隆命运分数与强动态OT基线相当。

Conclusion: 预训练的发育轨迹动力学编码了可迁移至CRISPR诱导状态转变的分化基元，因为两者共享相同的潜在几何结构中的细胞状态转变过程。

Abstract: Predicting how a cell will change its transcriptional state under a developmental signal or a genetic perturbation is the computational core of in-silico biology and the AI Virtual Cell program. Existing approaches either fit static control-to-treated maps that discard time, or solve multi-step ODE / Schrödinger-bridge problems on each dataset independently. We introduce Chreode, a one-step cell world model that predicts action-conditioned cell-state transitions through a structured residual transition operator. It shifts distributional evolution from inference time to training time, enabling single-pass generation while preserving a Waddington-inspired decomposition into downhill landscape flow, rotational in-tangent dynamics, and stochastic spread. The model is pretrained with a shared scVI encoder and a DiT-based dynamics backbone on a 2.4M-cell mouse embryonic atlas spanning 7 datasets. As a fine-tuning initialization, Chreode improves per-target Sinkhorn distance on Weinreb hematopoiesis and Veres islet differentiation over matched scratch models, PI-SDE, and PRESCIENT. As a transferable gene-state embedding for GEARS, the pretrained dynamics representation reduces shared-vocabulary DE20 mean squared error on Norman Perturb-seq from 0.2121 to 0.1858, a 12.4% relative improvement, without changing the GEARS training procedure. We interpret this transfer to perturbation prediction as evidence that pretrained developmental-trajectory dynamics encode differentiation primitives transferable to CRISPR-induced state shifts, since both involve cell-state transitions in a shared latent geometry. The pretrained backbone additionally produces zero-shot clonal fate scores on Weinreb that are competitive with strong dynamic-OT baselines.

</details>


### [258] [Adaptive Coarse-to-Fine Subgoal Refinement for Long-Horizon Offline Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28127)
*Kaiqiang Ke,Shenghong He,Chengdong Xu,Yuheng Luo,Xiangyuan Lan,Chao Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CFHRL方法，通过自适应递归细化远距离目标，在离线目标条件强化学习的长期任务中取得显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线GCRL在长期任务中面临远距离状态-目标对监督弱、值估计累积误差大的问题，而现有分层方法使用固定时间抽象或固定层次深度，与不同可达性水平的状态-目标对不匹配。

Method: CFHRL框架从最终目标开始递归提出中间子目标，利用基于经验回放的候选目标训练，通过学习的可达性成本估计判断何时停止细化，子目标只需提供可靠进展并减少剩余到达难度，不要求精确中点或全局最优。

Result: 在OGBench基准测试的多个长期任务上取得显著性能提升，消融实验验证了细化机制和停止机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 近似递归收缩的鲁棒性得到理论分析支持，子目标设计的宽松条件（仅需可靠进展）使得框架自适应匹配不同可达性范围的状态-目标对。

Abstract: Offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) is challenging in long-horizon tasks, where distant state--goal pairs provide weak supervision and value estimates become vulnerable to accumulated bootstrapping errors. Hierarchical methods mitigate this difficulty by introducing intermediate subgoals, but fixed temporal abstractions or fixed hierarchy depths can be mismatched to state--goal pairs with different reachability horizons. We propose Coarse-to-Fine Hierarchical Goal Reinforcement Learning (CFHRL), a fully offline GCRL framework that adaptively refines distant goals before execution. Starting from the final goal, CFHRL recursively proposes intermediate targets, trained from replay-supported candidates, and stops refinement once the current target is estimated to be locally executable by a learned reachability cost. The key idea is that a subgoal need not be an exact midpoint or globally optimal waypoint; it only needs to provide reliable progress and reduce the remaining reaching difficulty, enabling subsequent refinement over shorter horizons. A stylized analysis further supports the robustness of approximate recursive contraction. Experiments on OGBench show substantial gains on several long-horizon tasks, with ablations validating the proposed refinement and stopping mechanisms

</details>


### [259] [Self-Consistency via Marginal Sharpening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28142)
*Aleksei Arzhantsev,Otmane Sakhi,Nicolas Chopin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将推理时采样的目标从完整输出分布转向锐化的答案边际分布，并设计了一种纯自回归并行采样算法来近似实现这一目标，在数学和代码基准上显著优于传统功率采样方法，同时速度提升数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有功率采样方法针对完整输出分布进行锐化，优先选择模型下个体似然高的完整序列。然而推理任务中，一个完整输出混合了推理轨迹和最终答案，真正关键的是答案是否被多条合理的推理路径所支持。因此需要将目标从完整输出分布转移到答案边际分布。

Method: 提出一种纯自回归的并行采样算法，通过近似从锐化后的答案边际分布中采样，将自一致性从后验投票标准转化为推理时的优化目标，而非事后聚合。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上，该方法比标准功率采样获得更强的性能，同时速度提升数个数量级。

Conclusion: 答案边际分布是比完整输出分布更优的推理时采样目标；自一致性作为推理时目标比作为后验投票准则更有效；所提近似算法在性能和效率上均优于传统方法。

Abstract: Inference-time sampling can elicit strong reasoning abilities from language models without additional training. Existing power-sampling methods do so by sharpening the distribution over full generated outputs, favoring completions that are individually likely under the model. We argue that this is the wrong object to target for reasoning: a completion entangles a reasoning trace with a final answer, whereas what matters is whether an answer is supported by many plausible reasoning paths. We therefore shift the target from the full-output distribution to the sharpened answer marginal, making self-consistency an inference-time objective rather than a post-hoc voting criterion. Surprisingly, this marginal target admits an efficient approximation: we propose a simple, purely autoregressive parallel sampling algorithm that approximately samples from the sharpened answer marginal, eliciting stronger performance than standard power sampling on mathematics and coding benchmarks while being orders of magnitude faster.

</details>


### [260] [Sequential Neural Probabilistic Amplitude Shaping: Learning the Channel's Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28143)
*Mohammad Taha Askari,Lutz Lampe,Amirhossein Ghazisaeidi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提出了一种神经网络概率幅度成形方法，在考虑所有实现损耗的情况下优于现有方法，采用无块结构、易于实现的序列自回归编码器，兼容算术分布匹配，降低了速率损失并提高了可达信息率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率幅度成形方法存在实现损耗大、速率损失高的问题，亟需一种能够全面考虑实现损耗同时性能更优的方案。

Method: 提出了一种无块结构的序列自回归编码器，采用神经网络概率幅度成形，兼容算术分布匹配算法。

Result: 在考虑所有实现损耗的情况下，该方法优于现有方法，降低了速率损失，提高了可达信息率。

Conclusion: 所提出的神经概率幅度成形方法是首个在全面考虑实现损耗时仍能超越现有方案的方法，具有易于实现的优势。

Abstract: We present the first neural probabilistic amplitude shaping that outperforms existing methods while accounting for all implementation losses, using a block-less, easily implementable sequential autoregressive encoder compatible with arithmetic distribution matching, yielding reduced rate loss and higher achievable information rates.

</details>


### [261] [Sign-Aware Gated Sparse Autoencoders: Modeling Anticorrelated Features with Bi-Jump-ReLU Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28149)
*Bartosz Wieciech,Zmnako Awrahman,Marcin Czelej,Victor Hugo Jaramillo Velasquez,Wioletta Stobieniecka*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Sign-Aware Gated SAE (SA-GSAE)，通过双面门控稀疏与符号感知机制让单个潜变量同时编码正反两种极性（如“压力太高”与“压力太低”），避免标准SAE因非负性约束浪费字典容量。在Pythia-1B和SmolLM3-3B上，半宽SA-GSAE在3/6个细胞上帕累托主导全宽Gated SAE，其余3个R²差距≤0.025，且死亡比例降低0.35-0.62绝对值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准稀疏自编码器强制非负性，导致反相关概念（如“过高”vs“过低”）被迫使用不同潜变量，浪费字典容量。

Method: 提出SA-GSAE：带符号感知的双面门控稀疏机制。核心创新：极性敏感门控（选择正或负支撑）、有符号幅度路径（避免L1收缩）、辅助重建损失（防门坍塌）、Bi-Jump-ReLU激活（实现双极性共享），以及对称变体（r_i⁺=r_i⁻）。

Result: 在Pythia-1B和SmolLM3-3B的6个细胞上：半宽SA-GSAE在3个细胞上帕累托主导全宽Gated SAE；其余3个R²差距≤0.025（最大-0.008），死亡比例降低0.35-0.62。几何平均死亡比例降幅在MLP输出层达100-500倍，注意力层为2-4倍。门控和辅助损失是关键组件（去掉后LR降至0.27，98%死亡）。全宽版本在SmolLM3-3B残差流出现重建坍塌。

Conclusion: 双极性共享机制可有效替代标准SAE的非负性约束，在参数高效的同时提升特征利用率；推荐对称变体作为默认配置。
