Abstract: Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) extract interpretable features from Large Language Models, but standard variants enforce non-negativity, forcing separate latents for diametrically opposed concepts (e.g., "pressure too high" vs. "pressure too low") and wasting dictionary capacity when features are anticorrelated. We propose the Sign-Aware Gated SAE (SA-GSAE): two-sided gated sparsity with signed magnitude and auxiliary supervision. A polarity-sensitive gate selects support on either sign, a signed-magnitude path avoids L1 shrinkage, and an auxiliary reconstruction prevents gate collapse. Bipolar sharing - one latent encoding both signs along a shared direction - is realised via a new Bi-Jump-ReLU activation; parameter accounting shows sign-awareness stays parameter-efficient even when anticorrelated pairs are rare.
  On real LLM activations across three mid-depth hookpoints on Pythia-1B and SmolLM3-3B (6 cells, 3 seeds), a half-width SA-GSAE at width H strictly Pareto-dominates a full-width Gated SAE at 2H over the entire swept L0 overlap on 3 of 6 cells (both MLP-output hookpoints and resid-mid/Pythia-1B); on the remaining 3 it matches R^2 within 0.025 (max gap -0.008) while cutting dead fraction by 0.35-0.62 absolute. Sweep-geomean dead-fraction reductions are ~100x-500x on MLP-output cells and Pythia-1B resid, ~2x-4x on attention cells and SmolLM3-3B resid. Ablations show the two-sided gate and auxiliary loss are load-bearing (no auxiliary collapses LR to 0.27, 98% dead); tying r_i^+ = r_i^- is indistinguishable (|Delta R^2| = 0.0015), and we recommend this symmetric variant as default. MLP-output gains come from most latents carrying both polarities; on attention, bipolar structure concentrates in a small set of top latents. Full-width SA-GSAE exhibits a reproducible reconstruction collapse at SmolLM3-3B resid that the half-width entirely avoids.

</details>


### [262] [Off-Policy Learning to Reason Works Because It Is More Pessimistic Than You Think](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28150)
*Otmane Sakhi,Aleksei Arzhantsev,Imad Aouali,Flavian Vasile*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出大规模强化学习训练LLM时存在天然的异策略问题，传统PPO使用重要性权重校正分布偏移会导致高方差和熵坍塌。作者提出通过隐式悲观主义来理解异策略目标的有效性，并提出了稳定隐式目标分布的改进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模强化学习训练LLM时，由于生成延迟和异步更新，策略更新依赖旧策略收集的数据，导致学习本质上是异策略的。现有PPO方法用重要性权重校正分布偏移，但引入高方差和熵坍塌。本文旨在提供一种不使用重要性权重的异策略目标构建框架，并解释其有效性。

Method: 1. 提供一种直觉化的异策略目标构建方法，包含成功的异策略目标；2. 提出隐式悲观主义视角：这些目标实际上在向比名义目标更保守的目标策略优化；3. 提出一种原则性的改进方法，稳定诱导出的目标分布，改善异策略学习。

Result: 揭示了异策略目标的有效性来源于隐式悲观主义——它们隐式地控制了有效目标分布，使其更保守。某些实现选择之所以能提升稳定性，是因为它们隐式地稳定了有效目标分布。

Conclusion: 异策略学习无需依赖重要性权重校正，通过隐式悲观主义视角可以设计更稳定的异策略RL算法。提出的改进方法通过稳定诱导的隐式目标分布，能有效提升异策略学习效果。

Abstract: Large scale reinforcement learning has become a central tool for improving reasoning in large language models. At this scale, generation is often lagged or asynchronous, so updates are performed on data collected by older policies. This makes learning inherently off-policy. Most existing approaches nevertheless remain rooted in PPO-style trust-region objectives, treating training as approximately on-policy and using importance weights to correct distribution mismatch. These corrections can introduce high variance, destabilize optimization, and accelerate entropy collapse. Recent work suggests an alternative: rather than correcting the mismatch, one can embrace off-policy data and remove importance weights, often yielding stronger algorithms. In this paper, we provide an intuitive construction of off-policy objectives that include successful off-policy objectives and show that their effectiveness can be understood through implicit pessimism: they optimize toward target policies that are more conservative than their nominal objectives suggest. This perspective explains why some particular implementation choices improve stability: they implicitly control the effective target distribution. We then propose a principled modification that stabilize this induced distribution and improve off-policy learning.

</details>


### [263] [Temporal Hyperbolic Graph Representation Learning for Scale-Free Internet Routing and Delay Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28155)
*Yi-Ling Kuo,Hao-Yu Tien,Shih-Yu Tsai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出HERMIT框架，融合双曲时域图神经网络（HMPTGN）与随机森林回归器，联合进行链路预测和RTT预测。在2015-2024年大规模互联网数据集上，相比强随机森林基线提升6% RMSE，并在链路预测上超越现有双曲TGNN模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 互联网RTT预测对路由优化、QoS保障和流量工程至关重要，但由于长期时间依赖、路由动态变化和重尾延迟分布而极具挑战。现有TGNN在欧氏空间中建模，难以捕捉互联网路由图的分层和无标度结构，而双曲几何提供了更合适的表示空间。

Method: 提出HERMIT混合框架：1) 基于HMPTGN的双曲流形保持时域GNN，引入RTT感知边特征和可学习边编码器，用于建模演化链路状态和路由行为；2) 将学习到的双曲节点表示与历史RTT统计特征结合，输入随机森林回归器进行鲁棒延迟预测。

Result: 在2015-2024年大规模真实互联网数据集上：1) HERMIT相比仅使用历史RTT统计的强随机森林基线，RMSE提升6%，且减少重尾样本上的大误差；2) 在链路预测任务上超越HMPTGN和HTGN等现有双曲TGNN模型。

Conclusion: 结合双曲时域图学习与树基回归（随机森林）为真实互联网拓扑中的RTT预测提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Predicting Internet round-trip time (RTT) is critical for routing optimization, quality-of-service (QoS) provisioning, and traffic engineering, yet remains challenging due to long-term temporal dependencies, evolving routing dynamics, and heavy-tailed latency distributions. While Temporal Graph Neural Networks (TGNNs) can model evolving network topologies, most existing approaches operate in Euclidean space, which poorly captures the hierarchical and scale-free structure of Internet routing graphs. Hyperbolic geometry provides a more suitable representation space.
  We propose HERMIT (Hyperbolic Edge-aware RTT Modeling via Integrated Topology), a hybrid framework combining a hyperbolic manifold-preserving temporal GNN with a Random Forest regressor for joint link prediction and RTT prediction. Built on HMPTGN, HERMIT introduces RTT-aware edge features and a learnable edge encoder to improve modeling of evolving link states and routing behavior. The resulting hyperbolic node representations are combined with historical RTT statistics for robust latency prediction.
  We evaluate HERMIT on a large-scale real Internet dataset spanning 2015-2024. HERMIT consistently outperforms a strong Random Forest baseline using only historical RTT statistics, achieving a 6% RMSE improvement while reducing large errors on heavy-tailed samples. It also surpasses prior hyperbolic TGNN models, including HMPTGN and HTGN, in link prediction performance. These results demonstrate that combining hyperbolic temporal graph learning with tree-based regression provides a scalable solution for RTT prediction in real-world Internet topologies.

</details>


### [264] [Unification and Optimization of Robust Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28165)
*Jonas Hanselle,Valentin Margraf,Clemens Damke,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的设计空间，将分布鲁棒优化、标签平滑、Vicinal Risk Minimization和Mixup等多种鲁棒学习方法组织为参考分布增强、输入空间扰动、标签空间扰动和样本级聚合四个顺序阶段，每个阶段可选择悲观、中性或乐观立场。通过联合超参数优化，该方法在表格、图像和奖励建模基准上达到与最佳单一方法相当的性能，为不确定主导失效模式的从业者提供可靠默认方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒学习方法（如分布鲁棒优化、标签平滑、Mixup等）通常孤立开发，迫使从业者在任务主导失效模式不明确时，必须预先承诺单一失效模式，缺乏统一的框架来组合和配置多种鲁棒策略。

Method: 将现有方法沿三个共同设计轴组织，推导出将鲁棒学习分解为四个顺序阶段（参考分布增强、输入空间扰动、标签空间扰动、样本级聚合）的可训练过程，每个阶段可选择悲观、中性或乐观的立场，构成统一设计空间，通过联合超参数优化来组合和配置适合特定任务的鲁棒策略。

Result: 在表格数据、图像数据和奖励建模基准上，联合超参数优化与每个设置中最佳单一方法基线性能相当，为不确定主导失效模式的从业者提供可靠默认方案。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一设计空间能够有效组合多种鲁棒学习策略，通过联合超参数优化实现与最佳单一方法竞争的性能，解决了从业者需要预先承诺单一失效模式的问题。

Abstract: The literature has proposed various robust alternatives to empirical risk minimisation to address failure modes such as distribution shift, label noise and finite-sample degeneracies. Examples include distributionally robust optimization, label smoothing, vicinal risk minimization, and Mixup. However, such approaches are typically developed in isolation, forcing practitioners to commit a priori to a single failure mode even when the dominant mode for the task is unclear. To address this, we organize a broad class of existing methods along three common design axes and derive a tractable training procedure that decomposes robust learning into sequential stages (reference distribution enrichment, input-space perturbation, label-space perturbation, and sample-level aggregation), each with a choice of stance (pessimistic, neutral, or optimistic). This results in a unified design space in which joint hyperparameter optimization can compose and configure robustness strategies suited to the task at hand. Across tabular, image, and reward modeling benchmarks, joint hyperparameter optimization is competitive with the best single-method baseline in each setting, offering a reliable default for practitioners who do not know a priori which failure mode dominates their task.

</details>


### [265] [QuITE: Query-Based Irregular Time Series Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28166)
*JungHoon Lim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QuITE，一种即插即用的查询感知不规则时间序列嵌入模块，通过可学习查询令牌和单层自注意力机制，在不生成人工值或修改骨干架构的条件下，将不规则多元时间序列转换为骨干网络兼容的潜在表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不规则多元时间序列建模方法要么设计专门架构限制模型复用，要么通过插值引入人工值扭曲时间动态，缺乏一种既兼容现有模型又避免数据失真的通用嵌入方案。

Method: QuITE采用可学习查询令牌，通过单层自注意力聚合不规则观测值，直接生成与骨干网络兼容的潜在表示，作为即插即用的嵌入模块。

Result: 在多个真实世界基准上，QuITE在预测任务上平均相对提升高达54.7%，分类任务上提升15.8%，且兼容多种骨干架构。

Conclusion: 不规则时间序列建模的关键瓶颈在于传统嵌入层假设均匀采样，而非骨干架构本身；QuITE作为一种轻量级通用嵌入方案，可有效提升现有MTS模型在不规则数据上的性能。

Abstract: Irregular Multivariate Time Series (IMTS) are common in practice, yet their irregular sampling complicates effective modeling. Existing approaches typically either (i) design specialized architectures that limit the reuse of proven Multivariate Time Series (MTS) models, or (ii) map IMTS onto regular temporal grids through interpolation, which may distort temporal dynamics by introducing artificial values. To address these limitations, we propose a new input-embedding-based approach. We identify that the key bottleneck lies not in the backbone architecture, but in conventional embedding layers that assume uniform sampling. In this work, we introduce QuITE (Query-Based Irregular Time Series Embedding), a simple yet effective plug-and-play embedding module for IMTS. QuITE employs learnable query tokens to aggregate irregular observations through a single self-attention layer, directly producing backbone-compatible latent representations without artificial value generation or architectural modification. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks show that QuITE consistently improves MTS models, yielding average relative gains of up to $54.7\%$ in forecasting and $15.8\%$ in classification across diverse datasets and backbone architectures. Code is available at: https://github.com/Meaningfull9502/QuITE.

</details>


### [266] [Joint Training of Multi-Token Prediction in Reinforcement Learning via Optimal Coefficient Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28184)
*Zili Wang,Jiajun Chai,Lin Chen,Xiaohan Wang,Shiming Xiang,Guojun Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从优化视角重新审视多令牌预测（MTP）与强化学习（RLVR）联合训练失效的问题，将MTP对RL目标的影响分解为一阶相关项和二阶扰动惩罚项，统一解释了三种训练模式（Detach、交叉熵损失、策略损失）的成败原因，并提出最优系数校准（OCC）方法，通过在线追踪最优系数实现稳定高效的联合训练，在六个数学推理基准上达到或超越Detach基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前RLVR与MTP联合训练时性能下降，通常做法是切断MTP梯度，但缺乏对其根本原因的理论分析。本文旨在从优化视角揭示联合训练失效的数学机制，并设计一种能同时利用MTP收益而不损害RL性能的联合训练方法。

Method: 将MTP对RL目标的每步影响分解为一阶相关项（有益）和二阶扰动惩罚项（有害）；基于此统一框架分析Detach、交叉熵损失、策略损失三种模式的优缺点；提出OCC（最优系数校准）方法，通过对数概率代理在线追踪并自适应调整MTP与RL之间的最优系数，计算成本可忽略。

Result: 在六个竞赛级数学推理基准上，OCC方法一致地达到或超越了切断MTP梯度的基线方法，证明了联合MTP-RL训练可以超越传统Detach方案。

Conclusion: MTP与RL联合训练性能下降的根本原因在于二阶扰动惩罚项持续存在而一阶相关项衰减；OCC方法通过动态系数校准有效避免了这一问题，为未来在强化学习阶段充分利用预训练MTP模块提供了可行的理论指导和技术方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has emerged as the standard paradigm for improving reasoning capability of large language models, while Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) has been a widely adopted module in pretraining. Combining them is a natural approach, yet current RL practices detach MTP gradients because joint training degrades the performance. We revisit this failure from an optimization perspective. We show that the per-step effect of MTP on the RL objective can be decomposed into two terms: a first-order correlation and a second-order perturbation penalty. This decomposition unifies three MTP training regimes: Detach, Cross-Entropy loss, and Policy loss, and explains why each succeeds or fails. Further analysis of policy loss reveals that, although it aligns with intuition, performance still degrades: the correlation term decays while the quadratic penalty persists. Guided by the analysis, we propose Optimal Coefficient Calibration (OCC), an adaptive scheme that tracks the optimal coefficient online via a log-probability proxy at negligible cost. Across six competition-level mathematical reasoning benchmarks, OCC consistently matches or exceeds the detach baseline, delivering improved joint MTP-RL training performance.

</details>


### [267] [Hierarchical Synthetic Tabular Data Generation: A Hybrid Top-Down and Bottom-Up Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28198)
*Junfeng Nie,Alvin Jin,Xiaohui Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种分层混合自上而下和自下而上（H-TDBU）框架，通过解耦语义结构与随机纹理，在弱多模态金融基准上实现优于神经基线方法的合成表格数据生成性能，同时保持语义一致性和统计保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于纯生成模型或LLM的合成表格数据生成方法在处理数据异质性、逻辑一致性、罕见事件覆盖和低数据鲁棒性方面存在不足。

Method: 提出H-TDBU框架：自上而下路径构建结构驱动的逻辑约束和跨模态对齐规则，自下而上路径使用轻量级表格生成器学习局部统计模式，通过统一合成引擎中的迭代反馈循环整合两条路径。

Result: 在结合表格数据和情感文本数据的弱多模态金融基准上，H-TDBU方法在训练-合成-测试-真实性能上优于神经基线方法，同时保持了语义一致性。

Conclusion: 分层规则引导的合成为合成数据生成提供了兼顾可控性、语义连贯性和统计保真度的有效机制。

Abstract: Existing approaches for synthetic tabular data generation are based on either purely generative models or LLMs, both of which struggle with data heterogeneity, logical consistency, rare-event coverage, and robustness in low-data regimes. In this paper, we propose a hierarchical hybrid top-down and bottom-up (H-TDBU) framework that decouples semantic structures from stochastic texture. In the top-down path, structure-driven logical constraints and cross-modal alignment rules are constructed, while in the bottom-up path, lightweight tabular generators are used to learn local statistical patterns from real data. The two paths are consolidated in a unified synthesis engine with an iterative feedback loop. We evaluate the framework on weak multimodal financial benchmarks combining tabular and sentiment-text data. Experimental results show that our H-TDBU approach improves train-synthetic-test-real performance over neural baseline methods while preserving semantic consistency. Our results suggest that hierarchical rule-guided synthesis provides an effective mechanism for combining controllability, semantic coherence, and statistical fidelity in synthetic data generation.

</details>


### [268] [Geometry-First Generative Spatial Single-Cell Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28200)
*Ehtesamul Azim,Muhtasim Noor Alif,Tae Hyun Hwang,Yanjie Fu,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GEARS是一种几何优先的空间转录组与单细胞RNA测序整合框架，通过学习域不变表达编码器、扩散生成器和全局距离几何求解，在不依赖细胞类型标签或组织学图像的情况下，重建单细胞尺度的内在空间几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间转录组整合方法受限于固定网格和切片特定坐标系，尤其在非配对设定下问题突出；缺乏能够在不依赖细胞-斑点分配或标签的情况下重建单细胞级空间几何的通用框架。

Method: (1) 学习域不变表达编码器对齐ST斑点与解离细胞；(2) 训练置换等变生成器结合EDM预条件的扩散精炼器，在ST坐标提供的姿态不变监督下生成局部空间几何；(3) 推理时在多个重叠的scRNA-seq细胞子集上重构几何，聚合预测的成对距离，求解全局距离几何问题获得二维坐标和密集距离矩阵。

Result: 在全局距离保持、局部邻域保真度和空间分布对齐方面，GEARS一致优于空间映射和解卷积基线方法，并在跨截面泛化实验中表现优异。

Conclusion: GEARS通过几何优先策略实现了不依赖固定网格和标签的单细胞空间重构，为空间转录组数据整合提供了更灵活有效的方案。

Abstract: Single-cell RNA sequencing (scRNA-seq) profiles large numbers of cells but loses spatial context, whereas spatial transcriptomics (ST) preserves partial spatial structure at lower resolution. Most existing integration methods either deconvolve spot mixtures or map cells onto a measured spot lattice, which ties reconstructions to a fixed grid and slide-specific coordinate systems, a limitation that is especially problematic in unpaired settings. We propose GEARS, a geometry-first framework that reconstructs an intrinsic single-cell spatial geometry guided by ST, without relying on cell-type labels, histological images, or cell-to-spot assignment. GEARS first learns a domain-invariant expression encoder that aligns ST spots and dissociated cells, and then trains a permutation-equivariant generator with a diffusion-based refiner with EDM-style preconditioning to generate local spatial geometries under pose-invariant supervision derived from ST coordinates. At inference, GEARS reconstructs geometry on many overlapping subsets of scRNA-seq cells, aggregates predicted pairwise distances across subsets, and solves a global distance-geometry problem to obtain canonical two-dimensional coordinates and a dense distance matrix. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments, including cross-section generalization, show that GEARS consistently improves global distance preservation, local neighborhood fidelity, and spatial distribution alignment compared to strong spatial mapping and deconvolution baselines.

</details>


### [269] [Refining Multidimensional Video Reward Models via Disentangled Influence Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28203)
*Muyao Wang,Zeke Xie,Hideki Nakayama*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现视频评估中存在“维度异质性”现象：同一训练样本在不同评估维度上的可靠性差异显著。为此提出解耦影响框架，并设计维度解耦剪枝和维度解耦重加权两种策略，有效训练多维视频奖励模型，显著优于全局过滤方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于全局标量指标的数据筛选方法无法处理视频评估中各维度间监督可靠性的差异，导致多维视频奖励模型训练效果不佳。

Method: 提出解耦影响框架，高效估计每个样本在各评估维度上的监督风险；基于该框架设计两种维度解耦优化策略：维度解耦剪枝（移除极端高风险样本）和维度解耦重加权（对高风险监督进行软降权）。

Result: 实验表明，所提解耦策略在多个维度上显著优于全局过滤基线方法，训练出的奖励模型与真实标签的对齐程度更高。

Conclusion: 维度解耦的数据处理策略能更有效地应对视频评估中的维度异质性挑战，是训练高质量多维视频奖励模型的关键。

Abstract: As Text-to-Video (T2V) generation models continue to evolve, the complexity of video evaluation necessitates a fine-grained assessment across various axes. To address this, recent works have focused on developing Multidimensional Video Reward Models (MVRMs), which decompose the evaluation process to better align with the multifaceted nature of human visual perception. However, training effective MVRMs is fundamentally challenged by the complex nature of video data. In this work, we identify a critical phenomenon termed Dimensional Heterogeneity: the reliability of a training sample can vary substantially across evaluation dimensions, meaning that a sample may provide reliable supervision for one objective while inducing high supervision risk for another. Consequently, prevailing data-centric methods that filter based on global scalar metrics are ill-posed for T2V tasks. To address this, we propose a disentangled influence framework that that efficiently estimates dimension-specific supervision risk. Leveraging this framework, we introduce two dimension-disentangled refinement strategies: Dimension-Disentangled Pruning, which removes extreme high-risk samples, and Dimension-Disentangled Reweighting, which softly down-weights high-risk supervision. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our disentangled strategies significantly outperform global filtering baselines, yielding reward models with superior alignment to ground truth.

</details>


### [270] [Robust Contrastive Graph Clustering with Adaptive Local-Global Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28209)
*Lei Zhang,Fubo Sun,Haipeng Yang,Zhong Guan,Likang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合多尺度局部结构与全局语义的对比图聚类框架，通过注意力机制自适应融合不同传播深度的GNN特征和动态聚类原型，在8个真实数据集上取得了竞争性聚类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对比图聚类方法难以灵活捕获高阶局部结构，且常忽略复杂图中的全局语义，导致在结构碎片化、聚类边界模糊的真实图中节点表示次优。

Method: 局部层面：基于注意力加权自适应融合多传播深度的GNN拓扑特征；全局层面：通过注意力机制自适应聚合动态演化的聚类原型以引导节点表示；训练采用双视角对比学习，结合实例级和结构感知的混合损失。

Result: 在8个真实世界图数据集上取得了具有竞争力的聚类性能。

Conclusion: 所提框架通过多尺度局部结构与全局语义的联合建模，有效提升了图聚类的表示质量与簇间可分性。

Abstract: Graph clustering is essential in graph analysis for revealing structural patterns and node communities. Despite recent advances in self-supervised contrastive learning that have improved clustering via structural and attribute signals, existing methods still struggle to flexibly capture high-order local structures and often overlook global semantics in complex graphs. These limitations lead to suboptimal node representations, especially in real-world graphs with fragmented structures and ambiguous cluster boundaries. To address these limitations, a contrastive graph clustering framework is proposed to jointly integrate multi-scale local structures with global semantics via attention mechanisms. At the local level, GNN-based topological signals extracted from multiple propagation depths are adaptively fused through attention-based weighting to capture multi-scale neighborhood features. At the global level, semantic prototypes derived from dynamically evolving cluster centers are adaptively aggregated through attention to guide node representations and enhance inter-cluster separability. The model is trained under a dual-view contrastive learning paradigm with a hybrid objective that combines instance-level and structure-aware losses to improve representation robustness and discrimination. Experiments on eight real-world graph datasets demonstrate that our method achieves competitive clustering performance. Code is available at https://github.com/vege12138/w2.

</details>


### [271] [PhAME: Phenotype-Aware Molecular Editing via Latent Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28226)
*Łukasz Janisiów,Sebastian Musiał,Bartosz Zieliński,Dawid Rymarczyk,Tomasz Danel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PhAME是一个基于隐空间扩散框架的分子编辑方法，通过组合式无分类器引导机制，在保持与已知先导化合物结构相似性的同时，精准优化分子表型特征（如细胞形态、转录组扰动等高维生物信号），在对接分数优化和多模态表型生成等任务上达到了SOTA效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小分子药物发现需要同时优化候选分子的多种特性，而现有生成方法无法同时满足两个关键需求：向期望表型特征提供精确引导，同时保持与已知先导化合物的结构邻近性。

Method: 提出PhAME框架，将分子优化重构为预训练图基VAE隐空间中的编辑问题，核心创新是组合式无分类器引导机制，包含两个独立尺度分别控制表型条件引导和种子结构相似性，实现两个目标之间的可调节权衡。

Result: 在对接分数优化和多模态表型生成等多个基准测试中，PhAME在保持高化学有效性和新颖性的同时达到了最先进结果。

Conclusion: PhAME成功解决了在表型引导下进行分子优化时，精确表型引导与结构相似性保持之间的矛盾，为基于高维生物信号的小分子药物发现提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Small-molecule drug discovery requires simultaneous optimization of numerous properties of candidate molecules. These properties can be investigated through the analysis of high-dimensional biological signatures, such as cell morphology and transcriptomic perturbations, which provide a rich perspective on the underlying biological mechanisms. However, existing generative methods, which use those signatures for optimization, fail to meet two key requirements: providing precise guidance toward desired phenotypic signatures while maintaining structural proximity to a known hit. We introduce PhAME (Phenotype-Aware Molecular Editing), a latent diffusion framework that overcomes this challenge by recasting molecular optimization as editing in the latent space of a pretrained graph-based VAE. Our central contribution is a compositional classifier-free guidance scheme with two independent scales, one for the phenotype-conditioning and one for similarity to the seed structure, allowing practitioners to control the tradeoff between these two objectives. Empirical evaluations across diverse benchmarks, including docking score optimization and multimodal phenotypic generation, demonstrate that PhAME achieves state-of-the-art results while maintaining high chemical validity and novelty.

</details>


### [272] [IRDS: Interpretable RLVR Data Selection via Verifier-Coupled Sparse Autoencoder Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28247)
*Yuhan Li,Mingxu Zhang,Dazhong Shen,Ying Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: IRDS是一种基于稀疏自编码器（SAE）簇的可解释RLVR数据选择方法，通过可验证奖励的覆盖率目标选择模型失败但可学习的实例，在三个指令微调模型和六个数学推理基准上取得了最优准确率，同时计算成本降低一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法存在数据效率低下的瓶颈，且已有方法在子集级覆盖、验证信号利用和可解释性三个方面至少缺失其一，无法全面解决问题。

Method: 以稀疏自编码器（SAE）簇为基础选择RLVR训练实例，使选择过程在可识别的问题模式上可审计；提出验证器耦合的覆盖率目标，并通过贪心对数行列式最大化来求解。

Result: 在Qwen模型上超过最强基线+3.9/+4.0个百分点，在Llama-3.1-8B上超过+0.5个百分点，且运行成本比基于轨迹的基线低一个数量级。

Conclusion: IRDS通过可解释且高效的RLVR数据选择方法，显著提升了LLM推理能力，同时兼顾了覆盖率、验证信号利用和可解释性三个关键维度。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become a key technique for en- hancing LLM reasoning, yet its data ineffi- ciency remains a major bottleneck. Existing methods address this problem only partially, each missing at least one of subset-level cov- erage, verifier signal use, or interpretability. To address this gap, we present IRDS (Inter- pretable RLVR Data Selection), which selects RLVR training instances on a sparse autoen- coder (SAE) cluster basis so the selection itself is auditable on recognizable problem motifs. To select instances the model both fails on and can still learn from, we introduce a verifier- coupled coverage objective on the SAE basis and solve it by greedy log-determinant max- imization. Experiments on three instruction- tuned models and six math reasoning bench- marks show that IRDS achieves the highest overall accuracy, exceeding the strongest base- line by +3.9/+4.0 pp on the two Qwen models and by +0.5 pp on Llama-3.1-8B, while run- ning an order of magnitude cheaper than the trajectory-based baseline.

</details>


### [273] [Dynamic Topic Modeling with a Higher-Order Hypergraphical Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28269)
*Hanjia Gao,Hanwen Ye,Qing Nie,Annie Qu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于超图表示的非线性动态主题模型，通过分离词的共现与重复、结构化低秩分解及时序正则化，克服传统多项分布主题模型忽略高阶交互的局限，并在非凸优化下给出收敛保证，实验表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统动态主题模型将每个主题表示为单一概率向量，隐含地将词的出现与重复耦合在同一概率机制中，限制了词间的依赖结构并忽略了高阶交互信息，尤其在语义重叠的动态语料中问题突出。

Method: 引入超图文本表示：每篇文档建模为连接所有共现词的超边，重复强度编码为节点权重，从而分离词的出现与重复；基于此导出超图多项分布（非线性归一化）；构建结构化低秩因子分解框架，加入显式时序正则化约束主题-词分布演变；在非凸条件下推导局部收敛保证与非渐近误差界。

Result: 在合成数据与ICLR会议论文语料上的数值实验表明，该方法在一致性上持续优于现有的基于多项分布的主题模型。

Conclusion: 超图表示与非线性的归一化机制能更有效地捕捉动态语料中词的高阶交互结构，为动态主题建模提供了新的理论和实践范式。

Abstract: Dynamic topic modeling is widely used to analyze evolving trends in scientific literature, medical records, and social media. Traditional topic models represent each topic through a single probability vector on the multinomial simplex and implicitly couple word occurrence and repetition within one probabilistic mechanism. However, this formulation restricts the dependence structure among words and overlooks informative higher-order interactions, particularly in dynamic corpora with overlapping semantics. To address these limitations, we introduce a hypergraph representation of text where each document is modeled as a hyperedge connecting all co-occurring words, with repetition intensities encoded as node weights. This representation naturally separates word occurrence from repetition and induces a novel hypergraph-based multinomial distribution with a nonlinear normalization depending on the observed word set of each document. Building on this likelihood, we develop a dynamic topic modeling framework via structured low-rank factorizations with explicit temporal regularization on topic-word profiles. Moreover, we establish local convergence guarantees and derive non-asymptotic error bounds despite the intrinsic nonconvexity induced by bilinear factorization and document-specific nonlinear normalization. Numerical experiments on synthetic data and an application to the International Conference on Learning Representations (ICLR) corpus demonstrate consistent improvements over existing multinomial-based topic models.

</details>


### [274] [Commit to the Bit: Reactive Reinforcement Learning Done Right](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28276)
*Onno Eberhard,Claire Vernade,Michael Muehlebach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Committed Q-learning的新算法，用于在确定性观测（硬状态聚合）的有限环境中学习最优反应式策略。该算法在比传统q*-可实现性更弱的"rewire-robustness"假设下，证明了几乎必然收敛到最优反应式策略。算法核心是行为策略在进入一个特征时承诺执行单一动作，仅在特征变化时重新采样。分析中引入了准马尔可夫环境概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实环境中马尔可夫假设往往不成立（部分可观测或函数逼近导致非马尔可夫状态特征），需要研究在非马尔可夫条件下学习最优反应式策略的理论保证。

Method: 提出Committed Q-learning算法：行为策略在进入某一观测特征时"承诺"选择一个动作并持续执行，直到观测特征发生变化才重新采样动作。引入"准马尔可夫环境"概念作为分析工具，在"rewire-robustness"假设下证明收敛性。

Result: 证明了Committed Q-learning在rewire-robustness假设下几乎必然收敛到最优反应式策略。该假设严格弱于先前工作所需的q*-可实现性条件。

Conclusion: Committed Q-learning为非马尔可夫环境下的强化学习提供了理论收敛保证，拓宽了可处理的环境类别，且算法实现简单（经典Q-learning的变体）。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning algorithms are commonly analyzed (and designed) under the Markov assumption. This is unrealistic, as most environments encountered in practice are either partially observable, or require function approximation that restricts the agent to access non-Markovian state features. We consider the problem of learning an optimal reactive policy in a finite environment with deterministic observations (or equivalently, hard state aggregation). We introduce a new algorithm, Committed Q-learning, and prove almost-sure convergence to the optimal reactive policy under an intuitive assumption we call rewire-robustness. This assumption is strictly weaker than the $q_\star$-realizability condition used in prior work. Our algorithm is a variant of classical Q-learning in which the behavior policy commits to a single action upon entering a feature, and only resamples actions when the observed feature changes. A crucial part of our analysis is the introduction of quasi-Markov environments.

</details>


### [275] [AtomComposer: Discovering Chemical Space from First Principles with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28287)
*Bjarke Hastrup,Francois Cornet,Tejs Vegge,Arghya Bhowmik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AtomComposer——一种无需预训练数据、通过在线强化学习自主构建3D异构体的智能体，能在未见化学式中发现比基线多一个数量级的有效异构体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子生成模型依赖大规模预训练数据集，引入偏差并限制了新型化学空间的探索，缺乏无需预训练即可自主探索未知化学空间的方法。

Method: 采用在线强化学习，在化学计量约束下自主构建有效3D异构体；提出多组分训练方案，通过基于能量和有效性的奖励信号实现跨化学式的泛化。

Result: 在未见测试化学式上，相比单组分RL基线，能发现多达一个数量级更多的有效异构体。

Conclusion: 在线强化学习是从零开始可扩展探索化学构型空间的有效范式，有望推动无需训练数据的自主分子发现。

Abstract: Discovering novel stable molecules without training data remains a grand scientific challenge. Current molecular generative models are trained on large, pre-curated datasets, which introduce biases and limit exploration of novel chemistry. In contrast, we propose a new paradigm: autonomous, generalized agents capable of mapping vast, unknown chemical spaces without any pretraining. For the first time, we present AtomComposer, a self-guided agent that autonomously constructs valid 3D isomers under stoichiometric constraints and is trained exclusively online using reinforcement learning. Unlike existing approaches that generally overfit to a specific chemical formula, we establish a multi-composition training scheme that enables a broad generalization across diverse chemistry, guided by energy- and validity-based rewards. Our agent can discover up to an order of magnitude more valid isomers on unseen test formulas than existing single-composition reinforcement-learning baselines trained with per-step energy rewards. These results fulfill the promise of online reinforcement learning as a powerful paradigm for scalable, from-scratch exploration of chemical configuration space.

</details>


### [276] [ProRL: Effective Reinforcement Learning for Proactive Recommendation via Rectified Policy Gradient Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28293)
*Hongru Hou,Tiehua Mei,Denghui Geng,Jinhui Huang,Ao Xu,Hengrui Chen,Jiaqing Liang,Deqing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProRL框架，通过步进奖励中心化和位置特异性优势估计两种机制，解决强化学习在主动推荐系统中梯度估计存在的长度依赖偏差和高方差问题，显著提升了推荐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动推荐系统旨在通过中间推荐路径引导用户偏好向目标物品偏移，但现有策略梯度方法在路径奖励分解为步级奖励时存在梯度估计缺陷：一是路径级奖励分解为均值为正的步级奖励产生长度依赖偏差，使梯度偏向路径延伸而非有意义探索；二是用整个路径奖励加权每一步忽略了分解结构，导致梯度方差高。

Method: 提出ProRL框架，包含两种机制：(1) 步进奖励中心化，通过减去期望奖励来中和长度依赖偏差，确保路径延伸产生零期望梯度信号；(2) 位置特异性优势估计，利用奖励分解结构计算步依赖基线，降低梯度方差，使策略梯度精准优化路径质量。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，ProRL显著优于当前最先进的主动推荐系统。

Conclusion: 通过解决强化学习在主动推荐中梯度估计的两大缺陷，ProRL框架有效提升了主动推荐系统的引导性能。

Abstract: Proactive Recommender Systems (PRSs) aim to guide user preference shift toward target items by generating paths of intermediate recommendations. Reinforcement learning (RL) provides a principled framework for optimizing such sequential decision tasks, as path rewards can naturally capture both short-term acceptance and long-term guidance effectiveness. However, naively applying policy gradients to PRS results in deficient gradient estimation. We identify two deficiencies: (1) path-level rewards decompose into step-level rewards with positive mean, creating a length-dependent bias that causes gradients to favor path extension over meaningful exploration; (2) weighting each step by the entire path-level reward ignores the decomposition structure, leading to high gradient variance. To rectify these two deficiencies, we propose an effective RL framework ProRL with two novel mechanisms for proactive recommendation. First, Stepwise Reward Centering subtracts expected rewards to neutralize length-dependent bias, ensuring that path extension yields zero expected gradient signal. Second, Position-Specific Advantage Estimation leverages the reward decomposition structure to compute step-dependent baselines, reducing gradient variance. Together, these mechanisms yield policy gradients that precisely target path quality. Our experiments on three real-world datasets demonstrate that ProRL significantly outperforms state-of-the-art PRSs. Our code is available at https://github.com/hongruhou89/ProRL.

</details>


### [277] [Machine Learning methods for event classification and vertex reconstruction of the 12C + 12C reaction with the MATE-TPC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28296)
*Minghui Zhang,Xiaobin Li,Jie Chen,Ningtao Zhang,Fenhua Lu,Junrui Ma,Jiazhen Yan,Wanqin Tu,Xiaodong Tang,Bingshui Gao,Chengui Lu,Zhichao Zhang,Jinlong Zhang,Weiping Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用ResNet-50/34/18、VGG-19等深度学习模型对12C+12C融合反应的主动靶时间投影室(TPC)数据进行分类，模拟数据准确率约97%，实验数据约90%，并开发了CNN模型用于反应顶点重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在主动靶时间投影室(TPC)的现代核物理实验中，识别感兴趣的事件对于核反应研究具有挑战性，传统方法存在局限性，因此需要更有效的分析方法。

Method: 采用残差神经网络(ResNet-50/34/18)和VGG-19对12C+12C反应的弹性散射与融合反应事件进行分类；同时开发CNN模型用于反应顶点重建。

Result: 四种模型分类结果几乎一致，模拟数据准确率约97%，实验数据约90%；成功识别出传统方法误分类的事件；不同融合反应通道分类准确率约95%；CNN顶点重建提供了替代策略。

Conclusion: 机器学习技术能有效分类不同反应通道的事件并重建反应顶点，为未来复杂核反应数据分析铺平道路。

Abstract: In modern nuclear physics experiments, identifying events of interest is challenging for nuclear reaction studies with the active target Time Projection Chamber (TPC). In this work, machine learning techniques are employed to analyze the complex data of the 12C + 12C fusion reaction from a TPC named MATE (multi-purpose active-target time projection chamber for nuclear experiments). Specifically, we successfully applied Residual Neural Network (ResNet-50, ResNet-34 and ResNet-18) and Visual Geometry Group (VGG-19) to classify elastic scattering and fusion reaction events from the 12C + 12C reaction. The classification results of the four models are nearly identical, with accuracies of approximately 97% for the simulated data and 90% for the experimental data. Moreover, these approaches successfully identify some events that are misclassified by traditional methods. These models are also applied to classify events from different fusion reaction channels, with classification accuracies of approximately 95% on simulated data. In addition, a Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) model is developed to reconstruct the reaction vertex, providing an alternative strategy for vertex reconstruction. These results indicate that machine learning techniques can effectively classify reaction events from different channels and reconstruct the reaction vertex, thereby paving the way for future analyses of complex nuclear reaction data.

</details>


### [278] [T-GINEE: A Tensor-Based Multilayer Graph Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28300)
*Maolin Wang,Ziting Mai,Xuhui Chen,Zhiqi Li,Tianshuo Wei,Yutian Xiao,Wenlin Zhang,Wanyu Wang,Ruocheng Guo,Haoxuan Li,Zenglin Xu,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出T-GINEE框架，利用张量分解和广义估计方程显式建模多层网络层间依赖关系，在理论和实验上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多层网络分析方法通常独立处理各层或简单聚合，无法捕捉复杂的层间依赖关系，亟需能够显式建模跨层相关性的统计框架。

Method: T-GINEE框架包含三个核心创新：(1) CP张量分解通过共享潜在因子捕捉结构依赖；(2) 广义估计方程通过工作协方差矩阵建模层间相关性；(3) 灵活链接函数适配稀疏等数据特性。

Result: 理论分析证明了框架在温和条件下的一致性和渐近正态性；合成数据与真实数据实验验证了T-GINEE在多层层网络分析中的有效性。

Conclusion: T-GINEE为多层网络分析提供了一种能够显式建模层间依赖关系的统计正则化框架，优于传统独立/聚合处理方法。

Abstract: Traditional network analysis focuses on single-layer networks, real-world systems often form multilayer networks with multiple relationship types. However, existing methods typically fail to capture complex inter-layer dependencies by treating layers independently or aggregating them. To address this, we propose T-GINEE (Tensor-Based Generalized Multilayer-graph Estimating Equation), a statistical regularization framework combining tensor-based generalized estimating equations with task-specific loss to model cross-network correlations explicitly. Key innovations include: (1) CP tensor decomposition capturing structural dependencies via shared latent factors; (2) a generalized estimating equation framework modeling inter-layer correlations through working covariance matrices; and (3) a flexible link function accommodating characteristics like sparsity. Our theoretical analysis establishes consistency and asymptotic normality under mild conditions. Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets validate T-GINEE's effectiveness for multilayer network analysis.

</details>


### [279] [How Far Can Disaggregation Go? A Design-Space Exploration of Attention-FFN Disaggregation for Efficient MoE LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28302)
*Hanjiang Wu,Abhimanyu Rajeshkumar Bambhaniya,Sarbartha Banerjee,Tuhin Khare,Sudarshan Srinivasan,Suvinay Subramanian,Souvik Kundu,Madhu Kumar,Midhilesh Elavazhagan,William Won,Amir Yazdanbakhsh,Tushar Krishna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了MoE模型推理中从chunked-prefill、P/D分离到Attention-FFN分离（AFD）等多级分离调度策略的收益与权衡，通过结合设备内核测量与高保真网络仿真的框架，在严格TTFT/TPOT SLO约束下，AFD在DeepSeek-V3.2上实现了约4000 tokens/s的系统吞吐量，而非AFD部署不可行，并提炼出跨GPU分区注意力与FFN的设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型规模增长和TTFT/TPOT服务目标收紧，推理架构逐步走向分离化（chunked-prefill → P/D分离 → AFD）。对于MoE模型，注意力（内存受限）、专家FFN（计算密集）和MoE调度通信具有不同资源需求，AFD进一步将注意力与MoE-FFN放在不同GPU组上执行。这提出了核心问题：每种分离级别在何种条件下真正有效？

Method: 通过系统性的特征分析，覆盖不同输入/输出序列长度、前缀KV复用和用户延迟约束的真实工作负载。以chunked-prefill和P/D分离为基线，构建融合设备级内核测量与高保真网络仿真的评估框架，研究AFD在大规模部署中的收益与局限性。

Result: 在严格TTFT/TPOT SLO下，AFD在DeepSeek-V3.2上针对聊天、编程和代理编程工作负载维持约4000 tokens/s的系统吞吐量，而非AFD部署在这些场景下不可行。

Conclusion: 提炼出联合优化吞吐量与交互性的具体建议，包括如何根据工作负载和模型架构在GPU间分区注意力与FFN，为当前机架级/集群级部署以及未来分离式AI基础设施提供设计原则。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) inference has progressively disaggregated to keep pace with growing model sizes and tight TTFT and TPOT service-level objectives: from chunked-prefill aggregation, to prefill-decode (P/D) disaggregation, and most recently to operator-level Attention-FFN Disaggregation (AFD). This trend is especially important for mixture-of-experts (MoE) models, where memory-bound attention, compute-intensive expert FFNs, and MoE dispatch/combine communication create distinct resource demands. AFD further exposes this heterogeneity by placing attention and MoE-FFN execution on separate GPU groups. Each level of disaggregation deepens the scheduling design space across workload characteristics, resource allocation, and interconnect topology, raising the central question: when does each level actually pay off? We systematically characterize this trade-off for MoE inference across realistic workloads spanning input/output sequence lengths, prefix-KV reuse, and per-user latency constraints. Using chunked-prefill and P/D disaggregation as baselines, we study the benefits and limits of AFD at scale through a framework that fuses on-device kernel measurements with high-fidelity network simulation. Under strict TTFT/TPOT SLOs, AFD sustains around 4k tokens/s of system throughput on DeepSeek-V3.2 across chat, coding, and agentic-coding workloads, where non-AFD deployments are infeasible. We distill concrete takeaways for jointly optimizing throughput and interactivity, including how to partition attention and FFN across GPUs as a function of workload and model architecture, providing design principles for current rack- and cluster-scale deployments as well as future disaggregated AI infrastructure.

</details>


### [280] [Compositional Generalization in Autoregressive Models via Logit Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28304)
*Aakash Kumar,Maria Sofia Bucarelli,Emanuele Natale*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于自回归模型的新型组合策略，受扩散模型组合方法启发。在因子化条件假设下，该组合具有投影性——每个组件模型保持对其指定输出子空间的控制，避免模型间干扰。该性质在输出空间的平滑重参数化下仍保持，并得到特征空间定理。此外，当因子化假设和组件保证在目标长度上一致成立时，组合能保持长度泛化行为。这些结果为自回归系统中模型组合与合并的成功条件和稳定交互提供了原则性理解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归模型的组合是大语言模型跨任务行为或技能融合的核心难题，现有理解尚不充分。受扩散模型组合方法的启发，作者希望为自回归系统建立一种原则性的组合策略。

Method: 提出基于因子化条件假设的组合策略，借鉴扩散模型的组合方法；通过理论分析证明组合的投影性质，并扩展到特征空间定理和长度泛化性质。

Result: 证明了组合具有投影性（各组件模型保持对其指定子空间的控制，互不干扰）；该性质在平滑重参数化下仍保持；在满足均匀因子化假设和组件保证的条件下，组合能保持长度泛化行为。

Conclusion: 为自回归系统中模型组合与合并的成功条件和稳定交互提供了原则性理论理解，识别了交互稳定的条件。

Abstract: Composing autoregressive models remains a core challenge in understanding how large language models can combine behaviors or skills learned across tasks. We introduce a new and principled composition strategy for autoregressive systems, inspired by composition methods developed for diffusion models. Under a factorized-conditionals assumption, we show that the resulting composition is projective: each component model preserves control over its own designated subspace of the output distribution avoiding interference between models. This property is further preserved under smooth reparameterizations of the output space, yielding a feature-space theorem. Finally, we show that composition preserves length-generalizing behavior when the factorization assumptions and component guarantees hold uniformly at the target length. These results provide a principled understanding of when model composition and merging succeed in autoregressive systems and identify conditions under which their interactions remain stable.

</details>


### [281] [Learning to Assess the Reliability of Number-of-Runs Estimation in Stochastic Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28309)
*Sara Gjorgjieva,Eva Tuba,Tome Eftimov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个基于学习的自适应方法，通过分类器预测随机优化算法基准测试中所需运行次数估计的可靠性，在固定配置（相同优化器）场景下实现了对不可靠估计的高召回率检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模随机优化算法基准测试中，关键挑战已从是否需要重复运行转向如何在不增加不必要计算成本的前提下确定何时收集到足够证据。

Method: 在先前在线启发式方法基础上，利用离群点处理和偏度对称性检验自适应估计所需运行次数；从132,000次Nevergrad运行（COCO基准，24个问题，20维，10个实例，11个优化器）的标注结果中提取23个统计、无能量、形状与稳定性特征，训练分类器预测运行次数估计的可靠性，并通过少数类召回率优先检测错误估计。

Result: 在固定配置学习场景（同优化器数据训练与测试）下，运行次数可靠性可被学习，能够以高少数类召回率检测不可靠估计，但性能受限于固定配置内有限的数据多样性。

Conclusion: 运行次数估计的可靠性可以在固定配置场景中通过学习方法有效预测，实现不可靠估计的高召回率检测，但数据多样性不足限制了性能上限。

Abstract: In large-scale benchmarking of stochastic optimization algorithms, the key challenge is no longer whether repeated runs are needed for reliability, but how to determine when sufficient evidence has been collected without incurring unnecessary computational cost. We study a learning-based extension of a recent empirical online heuristic that adaptively estimates the required number of runs using outlier handling and skewness-based symmetry checks. Using annotated outcomes from 132{,}000 Nevergrad runs on COCO (24 problems in 20 dimensions, 10 instances each, 11 optimizers), we train classifiers on 23 statistical, energy-free, and shape and stability features to predict whether a run-number estimate is reliable, prioritizing detection of incorrect estimates via minority-class recall. We evaluate reliability prediction using a within-configuration learning setup, where models are trained and tested on data sharing the same optimizer. The results show that run-number reliability can be learned in a within-configuration scenario, enabling detection of unreliable estimates with high minority-class recall, although performance remains limited by the restricted data diversity within fixed configurations.

</details>


### [282] [Hybrid Neural World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28317)
*Pranav Lakshmanan,Paras Chopra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出混合神经世界模型，通过单网络连续时间视野预测物理动力学，隐式编码间断位置，生成误差图以识别激波、锋面和接触，支持纯代理模式（26-72倍加速）和回退模式（减少约一半残差误差），无需修改即可应用于反应扩散、可压缩欧拉和刚体碰撞动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经代理模型在物理动力学模拟中相比经典求解器可大幅加速，但在激波、锋面和接触等尖锐动力学事件中会静默失效，缺乏可靠的误差指示机制。

Method: 训练单个网络在物理状态空间中直接监督学习任意未来时间视野T的前向预测；通过预测轨迹的误差图隐式定位间断；提供两种运行模式：纯代理模式和基于误差图触发参考求解器回退的混合模式。

Result: 纯代理模式下CPU加速26-72倍；回退模式减少约一半残差误差；误差图与深度集成、学习型误差头、梯度幅度指示器和自适应共形预测等无监督基线方法相比具有竞争力或更优；无需校准集或控制方程知识。

Conclusion: 该方案在反应扩散、可压缩欧拉和刚体碰撞三种PDE环境中无需修改即可直接应用，实现了高效可靠的物理动力学模拟。

Abstract: Neural surrogates promise large speedups over classical solvers for physical dynamics but fail silently at sharp dynamical events such as shocks, fronts, and contact. We present hybrid neural world models for physical dynamics: a recipe for training and deploying multi-horizon surrogates in physical state space, where a single network with continuous horizon conditioning is trained with direct supervision against textbook reference solvers to predict any future state at horizon T in one forward pass. Although no part of the training data, loss function, or architecture supervises discontinuity location, the trained surrogate encodes it implicitly, recoverable from its forward passes alone as a per-trajectory error map that concentrates on shocks, fronts, and contacts, and stays small elsewhere. The map is competitive with or better than standard label-free baselines including deep ensembles, learned error heads, gradient-magnitude indicators, and locally-adaptive conformal prediction, while using only a single trained network and requiring no calibration set or governing-equation knowledge. The recipe supports two operating points. Mode 1 runs the surrogate alone for maximum throughput, with same-hardware CPU speedups of 26x to 72x against textbook solvers on the PDE environments. Mode 2 uses the error map to gate a reference-solver fallback, deferring uncertain trajectories and roughly halving the surrogate's residual error at the default operating point. The recipe applies without modification across reaction-diffusion, compressible Euler, and rigid-body collision dynamics.

</details>


### [283] [Learning the Error Patterns of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28328)
*Jinwoo Kim,Taylor Berg-KirkPatrick,Loris D'Antoni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出前缀过滤器（prefix filters）——针对特定领域和LLM的符号函数，用于捕获LLM在生成符合特定约束（如程序编译）的输出时出现的少量且集中的错误模式。同时提出Palla算法，可高效学习这些前缀过滤器。实验表明，Palla能将Qwen2.5-1.5B在TypeScript生成上的编译率提升60%以上，使其达到与Llama3.1-8B无约束时相近的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在生成具有特定有效性约束（如程序应能编译）领域的输出时，常常会出现少量但集中的错误模式（例如在生成TypeScript时误用Python函数名）。这些错误模式可以用少量约束来表示，并可以在实践中被学习。作者希望提出一种方法来自动捕获并纠正这些错误模式，从而提升LLM在受约束领域的生成质量。

Method: 提出前缀过滤器（prefix filters），作为针对每个领域和每个LLM的符号函数，用于捕获错误模式。提出Palla算法，能高效地在实际场景中学习这些前缀过滤器。学习到的前缀过滤器可以与约束采样算法结合，用于限制模型的输出。

Result: Palla在TypeScript生成任务上，将Qwen2.5-1.5B的编译率提升了60%以上，使其性能达到与Llama3.1-8B无约束时相当的水平。

Conclusion: 前缀过滤器能够定量分析LLM的错误模式，并有效提升模型在受约束领域的生成质量。Palla算法在实践中可以高效学习这些过滤器，具有良好的实用价值。

Abstract: When generating outputs for domains with specific validity constraints (e.g., a program should compile), LLMs often fail in a small number of focused ways: for example, by using Python function names when generating TypeScript. We observe that these error patterns can be represented using a small number of constraints that can be learned in practice. We propose \emph{prefix filters}, which are per-domain-and-LLM symbolic functions, as objects to capture the error patterns, Palla as an algorithm to learn prefix filters efficiently in practice, and implement Palla. Prefix filters learned by Palla i) help us quantitatively analyze the error patterns of LLMs, and ii) can be used to constrain the outputs of a model via constrained sampling algorithms. For example, Palla boosts compile rates for Qwen2.5-1.5B on TypeScript generation, by over 60%, allowing Qwen2.5-1.5B to achieve similar performance to Llama3.1-8B unconstrained.

</details>


### [284] [Dimensionality Reduction for Robust Federated Learning: A Theoretical Analysis and Convergence Guarantee](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28335)
*Shiyuan Zuo,Jiashuo Li,Rongfei Fan,Han Hu,Jie Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出投影降维（PDR）框架，通过稀疏随机投影将高维梯度压缩到低维子空间进行鲁棒聚合，将服务器计算复杂度降至最优的O(Mp)，同时通过子空间嵌入定理保证最优化收敛速率，仅以有界因子(1+ε)/(1-ε)放大拜占庭错误界限，实验表明可实现数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有拜占庭鲁棒聚合器在高维梯度聚合时计算开销巨大，且随模型规模增长而急剧增加，成为训练瓶颈。亟需一种通用加速框架来降低计算复杂度。

Method: 提出投影降维（PDR）框架，利用稀疏随机投影将梯度压缩到低维子空间，在此子空间中进行向量级距离计算和可靠性权重分配，实现鲁棒聚合。服务器计算复杂度降至O(Mp)，其中M为客户数，p为模型维度。

Result: 理论上：达到最优计算复杂度O(Mp)，非凸函数和强凸函数分别达到O(1/√T)和O(1/T)的最优收敛率，拜占庭错误界限仅被放大(1+ε)/(1-ε)倍。实验上：与现有聚合器集成后实现数量级加速且收敛性能高度竞争。

Conclusion: PDR是一种通用、高效、理论保障充分的拜占庭鲁棒聚合加速框架，以极小的鲁棒性代价换取巨大的计算效率提升。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables multiple clients to collaboratively train models without sharing raw data, but it is highly vulnerable to Byzantine attacks. Existing robust approaches can neutralize these threats but incur substantial computational overhead during high-dimensional gradient aggregation, an overhead that scales poorly with model size and increasingly dominates the training cost as modern models grow larger. To address this computational bottleneck, we propose Projected Dimensionality Reduction (PDR), a universal acceleration framework for vector-level distance-based robust aggregators, which performs robust aggregation by compressing gradients into a drastically smaller subspace via sparse random projection to efficiently compute reliability weights. This approach reduces the server computational complexity to an optimal $ \mathcal{O}(Mp) $, where $ M $ is the number of clients and $ p $ is the model dimension, matching the theoretical lower bound required merely to read the gradients. We establish convergence guarantees under standard FL assumptions in prior Byzantine-robust FL analyses. By leveraging the Subspace Embedding Theorem, we show that PDR achieves optimal convergence rates of $ \mathcal{O}(1/\sqrt{T}) $ for non-convex functions and $ \mathcal{O}(1/T) $ for strongly convex functions, where $ T $ denotes the number of iterations. Crucially, we mathematically demonstrate that this massive acceleration comes almost for free, merely inflating the inherent Byzantine error floor by a bounded, tunable factor of $ \frac{1+ε}{1-ε} $. Experimental results on benchmark datasets confirm that integrating PDR with existing aggregators yields orders of magnitude speedups in time efficiency while maintaining highly competitive convergence performance.

</details>


### [285] [Detecting Diffusion-Generated Time Series Under Generator Shift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28355)
*Zhi Wen Soi,Aditya Shankar,Gert Lek,Abele Mălan,Daniel Neider,Jian-Jia Chen,Lydia Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了白盒与黑盒方法在检测扩散模型生成的时间序列上的表现，发现白盒方法（基于重建的检测器）在生成器未知时表现不佳，而简单的黑盒分类器则表现出显著优势（F1=79.2，相对提升22.1%），表明时间序列上的检测不能直接套用图像领域的经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散生成的时间序列与真实数据难以区分，但该领域的检测研究尚不充分，尤其当生成器未知时。本文旨在系统探索白盒与黑盒检测方法在该问题上的表现。

Method: 对比白盒检测（基于重建的检测器，需访问生成器）与黑盒检测（仅基于原始信号的现成分类器），在分布内和生成器迁移场景下评估性能。

Result: 白盒检测在分布内有效，但在生成器迁移下失效；黑盒检测器平均F1达79.2，相对提升22.1%，TPR@1%FPR为57.2。

Conclusion: 扩散生成的时间序列检测不能直接迁移图像领域的经验，黑盒检测在当前设置下更有效；未来仍有多个开放方向值得探索。

Abstract: The boundary between real and diffusion-generated time series is becoming increasingly difficult to draw, yet detection in this domain remains underexplored, especially when the generator is unknown. We compare white-box detection, which requires access to the generator, against black-box detection, which operates on the raw signal alone. The white-box approach, a reconstruction-based detector adapted from the image domain, works well in in-distribution but breaks down under generator shift: reconstruction-based detection in images succeeds because large generic generators provide a near-universal reconstruction prior, and no analogous generator exists for time series. In contrast, a simple off-the-shelf classifier used as a black-box detector performs remarkably well, achieving an average F1 of 79.2, a 22.1% relative improvement over the white-box approach, and a TPR@1%FPR of 57.2. Diffusion-generated time series detection is therefore not a direct transfer of the image domain problem. This work provides the first systematic exploration of white-box and black-box detection for diffusion-generated time series. We close by identifying several open and promising directions.

</details>


### [286] [Score Based Error Correcting Code Decoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28358)
*Alon Helvits,Eliya Nachmani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SB-ECC，一种基于分数的纠错码软解码器，将解码视为连续时间去噪过程，使用神经网络定义概率流常微分方程，在42个码/SNR设置中39个达到最佳BER，平均SNR增益0.17dB，且可通过ODE求解器预算控制延迟-精度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际软解码在不同码族和码长下仍然具有挑战性，现有方法难以同时兼顾性能、泛化性和效率。

Method: 将解码建模为连续时间去噪过程，使用神经去噪器定义概率流ODE，迭代更新含噪信道观测值使其趋向有效码字，同时受奇偶约束引导。模型跨噪声水平训练，无需时间/SNR条件化，以原始带符号信道观测为输入学习连续去噪场。

Result: 在42个码/SNR设置中，SB-ECC在39个设置中取得最佳BER，平均SNR增益0.17dB，最大增益0.46dB。将求解器从Euler换为DPM可在保持-ln(BER)的同时平均减少8.86%（最高12.82%）的端到端解码时间。

Conclusion: SB-ECC是一种高效、通用且无需SNR估计的软解码方案，通过ODE求解器预算实现灵活的延迟-精度权衡，在多种码和SNR条件下显著优于现有方法。

Abstract: Error-correcting codes enable reliable communication, yet practical soft decoding remains challenging across code families and block lengths. We propose SB-ECC, a score-based decoder that casts decoding as continuous-time denoising. A neural denoiser defines a probability-flow ordinary differential equation (ODE) that iteratively updates the noisy channel observation toward a valid codeword, guided by parity constraints. The model is trained across noise levels without time/SNR conditioning, enabling inference without SNR estimation and supporting a direct latency accuracy trade off controlled by the ODE solver budget. We use the raw signed channel observation as input for learning a continuous denoising field. Across 42 code/SNR settings, SB-ECC achieves the best BER in 39/42 entries, with an average SNR gain of 0.17dB and a maximum gain of 0.46dB over the strongest competing baseline, we showed that swapping the solver from Euler to DPM preserves -ln(BER) while reducing end-to-end decoding time by 8.86% on average (up to 12.82%).

</details>


### [287] [LEIA: Learned Environment for Interactive Architected Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28368)
*Haiqian Yang,Yuan Cao,Markus J. Buehler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LEIA，一种面向超构材料的世界模型，能实时预测边界条件加载下的变形与应力场，并搭配MicroPlate基准测试，验证其在微观结构显式与隐式建模两种场景下的有效性，以及辅助超构材料设计搜索的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在游戏与机器人操控中表现良好，但物理工程领域仍面临挑战：真实材料具有非线性本构关系、历史依赖内变量、惯性动力学及多尺度层级结构，亟需能处理这些复杂性的交互式仿真工具。

Method: LEIA采用自回归方式对用户指定的加载条件逐步步进响应，支持大型三维非结构化网格，实时输出变形与应力场。同时构建了MicroPlate基准，涵盖两种微观建模范式：通过三维几何显式解析超构晶格，以及通过内自由度隐式建模微观结构变化的均质板。

Result: 在MicroPlate基准上，LEIA与四种基线方法进行了对比评估。此外，LEIA能够实现高效的候选生成与排序，支持基于快速代理引导的超构材料反设计搜索，且排序的应力精度经有限元真实验证。

Conclusion: LEIA可作为超构材料交互式仿真的有效世界模型，在保证应力精度的前提下大幅提升设计搜索效率，为物理工程中的快速迭代与反设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: World models have enabled interactive exploration of game environments and robotic manipulation, but physical engineering remains beyond their reach: real materials exhibit nonlinear constitutive laws, carry history-dependent internal state, undergo inertial dynamics, and may possess hierarchical structures spanning multiple length scales. We present LEIA (Learned Environment for Interactive Architected materials), a world model that lets engineers apply boundary conditions step by step and observe the resulting deformation and stress fields in real time. LEIA handles large three-dimensional unstructured meshes and generates autoregressive responses to user-specified loading. We introduce MicroPlate, a benchmark of architected plates spanning two regimes of microstructure modeling: architected lattices that resolve microstructure explicitly through three-dimensional geometry, and a homogeneous plate where microstructural change is modeled implicitly through internal degrees of freedom. MicroPlate is used to assess LEIA alongside four baseline methods across both regimes. Finally, we demonstrate that LEIA enables efficient candidate generation and ranking for fast surrogate-guided search for de novo designs of architected materials, with stress-accurate candidate ranking validated by finite element ground truth.

</details>


### [288] [Teacher-Student Representational Alignment for Reinforcement Learning-Driven Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28372)
*Meraj Mammadov,Pedro Zuidberg Dos Martires,Johannes Andreas Stork*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通过自监督对比学习构建共享嵌入空间的方法，在训练教师策略时防止其依赖特权状态信息，从而减小模仿学习中的不可约模仿间隙，无需RL微调即可提升学生策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人领域，从基于状态的强化学习策略进行模仿学习时，教师策略可以利用特权状态信息（学生无法从观测中推断出的信息），导致教师与学生之间存在不可约的模仿间隙。现有的RL微调方法需要全新的训练设置，成本高昂。

Method: 提出一种新算法，通过自监督对比学习训练一个共享嵌入空间，该空间隐藏智能体特定的观测信息。在训练教师策略的同时并行训练共享嵌入空间，并通过限制其梯度更新编码器网络来阻止教师提取私有信息，从而构造出天生可模仿的教师策略。

Result: 在多个示例领域上的评估表明，与最先进的基线方法相比，该算法能够实现更高的学生性能，并大幅减小模仿间隙。

Conclusion: 通过构造共享嵌入空间并限制教师策略对特权信息的依赖，可以在无需RL微调的情况下有效缩小模仿间隙，提升模仿学习中学生策略的性能。

Abstract: Imitation learning (IL) from a state-based reinforcement learning (RL) policy is a common approach to overcome the curse of dimensionality in complex and high-dimensional observation spaces prevalent in robotics. This paper addresses the irreducible imitation gap that emerges when teacher and student are learned in isolation, and the teacher policy has the liberty to rely on privileged state information that the student cannot infer from its observations. Instead of improving poor student performance with RL finetuning after IL, which often requires a whole new training setup, we propose a novel algorithm which learns a shared embedding space that hides agent-specific observations and thus trains imitable teacher policies by construction. We train the shared embedding space with self-supervised contrastive learning in parallel to the teacher policy and prevent it from extracting private information by limiting its gradients from updating the encoder networks. We perform evaluations on several example domains and compare to state-of-the-art baselines showing that our algorithm enables higher student performance with substantially reduced imitation gap.

</details>


### [289] [Meta-Attention: Bayesian Per-Token Routing for Efficient Transformer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28384)
*Alan Ferrari*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Meta-Attention框架，通过贝叶斯元控制器为每个token动态路由到最适合的注意力机制（全softmax、线性核或滑动窗口局部注意力），在Tiny LM基准上以25.1%的标准化FLOP成本达到可比性能，相比无先验基线降低34.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer对所有token和序列位置统一应用单一注意力机制，忽略了局部上下文差异和计算预算限制，导致计算效率低下。现有路由方法使用确定性或无先验学习路由，存在路由崩溃等问题。

Method: 设计贝叶斯元控制器，将每个token的机制选择视为计算感知狄利克雷先验下的后验推断；使用摊销变分后验q(α|x_t; φ)输出路由权重，通过ELBO目标联合优化任务性能和注意力机制成本；采用软到硬的渐进路由转换策略。

Result: Phase 1实验显示：硬路由下标准化FLOP成本25.1%（无先验基线为59.3%，降低34.2个百分点）；路由熵从55.8%降至43.3%（降低12.5个百分点），表明狄利克雷先验有效防止路由崩溃，而非贝叶斯模型退化为全注意力。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯路由方法在计算-性能权衡上优于确定性或无先验学习路由，且开销可忽略不计，为高效Transformer架构提供了新范式。

Abstract: Standard transformer architectures apply a single attention mechanism uniformly across all tokens and sequence positions, irrespective of local context or computational budget. We propose Meta-Attention, a framework that dynamically routes each token to the most appropriate attention strategy -- full softmax attention, linear (kernel) attention, or sliding-window local attention -- via a Bayesian Meta-Controller. Unlike prior routing approaches that use deterministic or prior-free learned routing, the Meta-Controller treats per-token mechanism selection as posterior inference under a compute-aware Dirichlet prior: routing weights are the output of an amortised variational posterior q(alpha | x_t; phi) trained with an Evidence Lower Bound (ELBO) objective that jointly encodes task performance and attention-mechanism cost. This design produces principled routing uncertainty estimates that govern the soft-to-hard routing transition, mitigates routing collapse without ad hoc load-balancing losses, and yields better compute-performance trade-offs than deterministic or prior-free learned routing at negligible overhead. Phase 1 empirical results on a Tiny LM benchmark confirm core predictions: the Bayesian controller's learned routing distribution implies a projected normalised FLOP cost of 25.1% under hard routing, vs. 59.3% for the prior-free baseline (-34.2 pp), and reduces routing entropy from 55.8% to 43.3% (-12.5 pp), demonstrating that the Dirichlet prior prevents routing collapse while the non-Bayesian model defaults to full attention. We present the Bayesian architecture, ELBO training objective, and a Phase 1 PyTorch prototype validating forward-pass correctness, posterior diversity, and a controlled ablation against a prior-free baseline. Code available at: https://github.com/KFEAL/meta-attention

</details>


### [290] [CLANE: Continual Learning of Actions on Neuromorphic Hardware from Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28387)
*Elvin Hajizada,Michael Neumeier,Edward Paxon Frady,Yulia Sandamirskaya,Axel von Arnim,Bing Li,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLANE是首个在Intel Loihi 2神经形态硬件上实现事件相机动作识别持续学习的端到端系统，通过脉冲CNN与新型Loihi 2模块（时间聚合层和定点归一化层）的结合，在THU E-ACT-50数据集上达到70.4%准确率，同时相比边缘GPU基线实现了超过100倍能耗降低和16倍延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AR/VR和机器人应用需要持续学习新动作且不遗忘旧类别，同时要求设备端处理和低延迟适应以保障隐私。事件相机和神经形态处理虽能提高视觉感知效率，但此前尚无系统在神经形态硬件上实现事件动作识别的持续学习流水线。

Method: CLANE结合脉冲2D CNN（时空特征提取）和CLP-SNN（片上学习头），并引入时间聚合层（Temporal Aggregation Layer）和定点归一化层（Fixed-point Normalization Layer）两个新型Loihi 2模块，将学习扩展至动作片段。

Result: 在THU E-ACT-50（50类真实世界数据集）持续学习任务上达到70.4%准确率，相比CNN+GRU+CLP边缘GPU基线实现超过100倍能耗降低和16倍延迟降低，通过三个评估级别的等算法跨平台基准测试验证。

Conclusion: 首次在神经形态硬件上实现了事件相机的持续动作学习，验证了神经形态计算在实时、低功耗持续学习场景中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Recognizing and continuously learning novel human actions without forgetting prior classes is a requirement for emerging AR/VR and robotics applications. For these applications, both on-device processing and learning are essential for privacy and low-latency adaptation. Event cameras address the efficiency of visual sensing with sparse, asynchronous output that is naturally compatible with neuromorphic processing. Yet no prior system has deployed a continual on-device learning pipeline for event-based action recognition using neuromorphic hardware. We present CLANE, Continual Learning of Actions on Neuromorphic Hardware from Event Cameras, deployed end-to-end on Intel Loihi 2. CLANE combines a spiking 2D CNN for spatiotemporal feature extraction with CLP-SNN as its on-chip learning head, extended to action clips via a Temporal Aggregation Layer and a fixed-point Normalization Layer, both novel Loihi 2 modules. On THU E-ACT-50, a 50-class dataset captured under real-world conditions, CLANE achieves 70.4% accuracy in a continual learning task while delivering more than 100x energy reduction and 16x lower latency over a sequential CNN+GRU+CLP edge GPU baseline, validated through iso-algorithm cross-platform benchmarking across three evaluation levels.

</details>


### [291] [ADWIN: Adaptive Windows for Horizon-Aware On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28396)
*Kun Liang,Chenming Tang,Clive Bai,Weijie Liu,Saiyong Yang,Yunfang Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出ADWIN框架，在在线策略蒸馏中自适应调整rollout长度，通过短前缀训练和延迟全展开审计，在保证性能的同时将训练成本降低最多4.1倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准全rollout在线策略蒸馏将每次更新与昂贵的完整轨迹生成绑定，且对后期位置的监督分配存在边际价值递减问题，导致计算效率低下。

Method: 提出ADWIN自适应窗口框架：将rollout长度视为在线可接受性决策，在教师锚定的短前缀上训练，同时使用延迟的全展开探测来审计前缀与全展开的对齐程度，并通过staleness控制自适应调整下一轮训练的前缀长度。

Result: 在数学和代码推理基准上（单任务、多任务、强到弱设置），ADWIN相比全rollout OPD和基于前缀的基线方法，在达到相当或更优准确率的同时，将端到端训练成本降低最多4.1倍。

Conclusion: 通过重新审视"有用监督视界"，发现学生诱导的rollout会偏离教师偏好的延续，而对齐的前缀可能已能保留长视界OPD更新方向，因此自适应调整rollout长度是一种有效的计算-精度权衡策略。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) transfers reasoning behavior by training a student on teacher feedback along student-generated trajectories, but standard full-rollout training ties every update to a costly completion and can over-allocate supervision to late positions with low marginal value for the current student. We revisit this assumption through the useful supervision horizon: student-induced rollouts can drift from teacher-preferred continuations, while aligned prefixes may already preserve the long-horizon OPD update direction. We propose ADWIN, an adaptive-window framework for OPD that treats rollout length as an online admissibility decision, training on short teacher-anchored prefixes while using delayed full-rollout probes to audit prefix--full alignment and adapt the next horizon with staleness control. Across math and code reasoning benchmarks in single-task, multi-task, and strong-to-weak settings, ADWIN improves the accuracy--compute trade-off over full-rollout OPD and prefix-based baselines, reducing end-to-end training cost by up to 4.1 times while achieving comparable or better accuracy.

</details>


### [292] [Revisiting Metafeatures to Explain Model Differences on Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28418)
*Markus Herre,Andrej Tschalzev,Sascha Marton,Christian Bartelt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文基于TabArena基准测试，系统研究了数据集元特征能否解释不同模型系列（神经网络vs树模型、非基础vs基础模型、以及不同基础模型之间）在表格预测任务上的性能差距。经过严格的统计检验和错误发现率控制，发现大多数元特征与性能差距的关联并不稳健，在留一数据集预测中表现有限，表明表格数据集的异质性和全局元特征方法的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着表格基础模型的兴起，传统模型在许多任务上仍然表现良好，因此如何为特定表格数据集选择合适的模型成为一个难题。作者希望通过研究数据集元特征是否能解释不同模型系列之间的性能差距，为模型选择提供理论依据。

Method: 利用TabArena基准测试结果，分析数据集级别的性能差距，并将其与模型无关的数据集描述符（元特征）进行关联。采用严格的统计检验和错误发现率控制，并进行留一数据集预测验证。

Result: (1) 神经网络与树模型之间的性能差距没有元特征通过错误发现率控制；(2) 非基础模型与基础模型之间的性能差距有一个稳健关联，但在留一数据集预测中无法泛化；(3) TabICLv2与TabPFN-2.6之间的性能差距有一个稳健关联能改善留一数据集预测。此外，元特征预测器在留一数据集分析中未能显著优于简单基线。

Conclusion: 表格数据集具有高度异质性，全局元特征方法在51个TabArena数据集上不够稳健，无法提供可靠的性能差距解释。

Abstract: With the rise of tabular foundation models alongside traditional models still performing well on many tasks, choosing the right model for a tabular dataset remains difficult. We investigate whether dataset meta-features can explain performance gaps between model families on tabular prediction tasks. Using the TabArena benchmark results, we analyze dataset-level performance gaps and relate them to model-agnostic dataset descriptors. After strict statistical tests with false discovery control, we find that (1) for neural network vs. tree gaps, no meta-feature survives false discovery control, (2) for non-foundation vs. foundation model gaps, one association is robust but does not generalize when tested in leave-one-dataset-out prediction, and (3) for TabICLv2 vs. TabPFN-2.6, one robust association also improves held-out prediction. Furthermore, we conduct a leave-one-dataset-out analysis and find that meta-feature predictors fail to improve meaningfully over a simple baseline. Overall, our results show the heterogeneity of tabular datasets and that global meta-feature approaches are not robust enough to offer explanations on the 51 TabArena datasets.

</details>


### [293] [Conveyance: A Versatile Framework for Learning in Structured Class Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28420)
*Yasser Taha,Grégoire Montavon,Nils Körber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Conveyance，一种适用于结构化类别空间的分类方法与损失函数，通过最大化不同类别划分上的两个独立间隔来处理类别间的图结构关系，在分层分类、序数回归和多实例学习任务中达到或超越专用基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准损失函数（如交叉熵）具有类别对称性，无法利用类别间的结构化关系，尤其是在存在结构化噪声时，这限制了模型在真实世界应用中的表现能力。

Method: 提出Conveyance损失函数，通过最大化两个独立间隔（针对不同的类别划分）来编码类别间的图结构关系，保留单调性和部分凸性等形式化性质，无需定义复杂联合分布或手动调整效用矩阵。

Result: 在分层分类、序数回归和多实例学习三个任务上，Conveyance均匹配或超越了专用基线的性能，验证了其作为统一解决方案的有效性。

Conclusion: Conveyance为结构化类别空间提供了一种统一且高效的学习方案，能够泛化到多种结构化分类任务，避免了对复杂联合分布建模或人工调参的需求。

Abstract: While machine learning (ML) architectures have evolved rapidly to account for complex data, loss functions like cross-entropy remain mostly structure-agnostic in many real-world applications. However, the `class-symmetric' nature of these standard losses fundamentally limits the ability of ML models to exploit structural relationships between classes, particularly when facing structured noise. We propose \textsc{Conveyance}, a new classification approach and associated loss function tailored to structured class spaces. It allows users to encode graph-like relations between classes without having to define complex joint distributions or manually tune utility matrices.Technically, our loss function operates by maximizing two separate margins over distinct class partitions, while preserving formal properties such as monotonicity and partial convexity. We demonstrate the versatility and effectiveness of our method by applying it to hierarchical classification, ordinal regression, and multiple instance learning. Across these tasks, \textsc{Conveyance} either matches or exceeds the performance of specialized baselines, thereby offering a unified solution for structured class spaces.

</details>


### [294] [Bilinear Coordinate Alignment for Training-Free Task-Vector Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28444)
*Jungyong Son,Jinwook Jung,Minhee Park,Sungyong Baik*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BiCo框架，通过双空间对齐（输入激活和输出梯度）实现任务向量在预训练模型间的零训练迁移，无需微调即可在新模型上复现性能，在视觉和语言任务上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当预训练模型更新版本时，已有的微调参数无法直接复用，必须重新进行昂贵的微调。现有基于任务向量的迁移方法存在性能差距，因为仅建立了部分对应关系（激活或梯度）而不足。

Method: 重新审视任务向量的形成过程，发现其可分解为输入激活和输出梯度的双线性累积交互。据此提出BiCo框架：通过双空间对齐（输入侧激活空间和输出侧梯度空间），利用少量校准数据的单次前向-后向传播估计正交Procrustes映射，无需参数更新即可迁移任务向量。

Result: 在多个计算机视觉和自然语言处理基准上，BiCo在模型宽度、深度和预训练配置不同的迁移场景中一致优于现有迁移方法。

Conclusion: 任务向量可以视为输入-输出双线性交互的累积，通过双空间对齐能够实现无需训练的、高效的任务向量迁移，显著缩小了与直接微调的性能差距。

Abstract: Fine-tuning large-scale pre-trained models is a recent prevalent paradigm for adapting general representations to specialized tasks. However, when a new version of a pre-trained model becomes available, expertise acquired through fine-tuning cannot be directly reused because it is tied to the parameterization of the original model, requiring another costly fine-tuning. To address this inefficiency, recent work uses task vectors, defined as the parameter difference between a fine-tuned model and its base model, to transfer expertise across models. While existing methods bridge disparate models by matching activations or gradients, a significant performance gap remains relative to direct fine-tuning, suggesting that these partial correspondences are insufficient. In this work, instead of viewing a task vector merely as a parameter offset, we revisit the formation of task vectors and show that they can be derived as accumulated bilinear interactions between input-side activations and output-side gradients. Motivated by this observation, we formulate task-vector transfer as a dual-space alignment problem and propose BiCo, a training-free framework for transferring task vectors through Bilinear Coordinate alignment. BiCo estimates orthogonal Procrustes mappings in both spaces using a single forward-backward pass on a small calibration set, without any parameter update. Across extensive computer vision and natural language processing benchmarks, BiCo consistently outperforms existing transfer methods across models that differ in width, depth, and pre-training configuration.

</details>


### [295] [Mitigating Adaptive Attacks against Reasoning Models with Activation Consistency Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28467)
*Avidan Shah,Jannik Brinkmann,Rico Angell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究一致性训练（ACT）在推理模型中的防御效果，发现激活层级的一致性训练（ACT）能有效抵御提示注入和越狱攻击，其防御机制表现为助理轮次边界上的线性激活偏移，且对自适应攻击具有鲁棒性，即使替换思维链仍能保持防御能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM推理能力增强，其扩展的思维链为对抗越狱和提示注入带来了新的防御挑战，需要研究更有效的安全训练方法。

Method: 研究了一致性训练的两个变体：输出层级（BCT）和激活层级（ACT），在五个推理模型上进行实验，使用干净提示和对抗性改写对进行自监督训练。

Result: ACT在提示注入防御中与其他基于训练的方法相当；在越狱场景中ACT对自适应攻击更鲁棒；防御机制表现为助理轮次边界上的线性激活偏移；即使思维链被替换为顺从轨迹，ACT仍能保持拒绝响应。

Conclusion: 监督内部表征是一种意外有效且可解释的安全训练方法，适用于推理模型的多种安全训练场景。

Abstract: As LLMs gain stronger reasoning capabilities, their extended chain-of-thought introduces new degrees of complexity for defending against adversarial jailbreaks and prompt injection. We study consistency training, a family of fine-tuning objectives that enforce identical behavior on clean prompts and adversarial rewrites, and evaluate its two main variants, output-level (BCT) and activation-level (ACT), across five reasoning models. We formulate both methods as a prompt injection defense and find ACT to be competitive with other training-based defenses while requiring only self-supervised pairs of clean and wrapped prompts. Our experiments also generalize both techniques within the jailbreak setting, demonstrating that ACT remains more robust to adaptive attacks. We also provide mechanistic evidence that ACT's defense against jailbreaks is encoded as a roughly linear shift in activation space at the assistant-turn boundary. After ACT training, we can recover a single steering direction that controls refusal on reasoning models with minimal effect on benign inputs. We find that ACT remains robust even when the model's chain-of-thought is replaced with a compliant trace from the undefended base model, pivoting to refuse prefilled jailbreaks. Together, these results suggest that supervising internal representations is a surprisingly effective and interpretable approach to various forms of safety training in reasoning models.

</details>


### [296] [Fitting Unknown Number of Hyperplanes with Manifold Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28501)
*Zhiqin Cheng,Yu Zhan,Mingjin Zhang,Lingbo Liu,Liang Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于流形优化的两阶段算法，用于拟合未知数量的超平面，通过黎曼期望最大化处理交点模糊性，并利用投影密度估计初始化实现全局收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理未知数量的超平面拟合问题时，常陷入局部最优解或缺乏几何一致性，该问题具有非凸、非可微和未知模型阶数的挑战性。

Method: 将问题转化为单位球面流形上的无监督学习任务；提出两阶段流形优化算法：Phase I采用带重尾核的黎曼EM过程估计后验概率，Phase II将软估计退化为硬匹配生成精确局部最优；引入投影密度估计初始化策略。

Result: 在几何精度和鲁棒性方面均优于现有最先进基线方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的流形优化框架有效解决了超平面拟合中的非凸优化和点分布歧义问题，在几何一致性和鲁棒性上取得显著提升。

Abstract: Fitting an unknown number of hyperplanes to data is a fundamental yet challenging problem in machine learning, characterized by its non-convexity, non-differentiability, and unknown model order. Existing approaches often struggle with local optima or lack geometric consistency. To address these limitations, we propose a novel framework based on Manifold Optimization. We reformulate the problem as an unsupervised learning task on the unit sphere manifold $\mathcal{S}^{\textbf{dim}-1}$. This formulation effectively handles the non-convex constraints and linearizes the distance measurement, rendering the gradient descent tractable. We propose a Two-Stage Manifold Optimization algorithm. In Phase I, we employ a Riemannian Expectation-Maximization process with a heavy-tailed kernel to robustly estimate posterior probabilities, effectively resolving the ambiguities of point distribution between intersecting hyperplanes. In Phase II, upon convergence of the soft estimates, the probabilistic weights degenerate into hard matching, generating a precise local optimum that strictly satisfies the geometric definition. Furthermore, we introduce a projected density estimation strategy for initialization to facilitate global convergence by significantly reducing the feature description space and search complexity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both geometric accuracy and robustness.

</details>


### [297] [Universal Time Series Generation with Neural Controlled Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28507)
*Torben Berndt,Elyes Farjallah,Leif Seute,Raeid Saqur,Benjamin Walker,Jan Stühmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出G-SLiCEs模型，证明结构化线性受控微分方程是通用时间序列生成器，并应用于路径空间上的流匹配，在概率预测和不规则网格任务上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有状态空间模型在序列通用性方面的研究主要聚焦于判别式设置，本文将其扩展至生成式时间序列建模，旨在证明SSM在生成任务中同样具备最大表达能力。

Method: 理论证明最大化表达能力的结构化线性受控微分方程(SLiCEs)是通用时间序列生成器；在此基础上提出G-SLiCEs模型，用于路径空间上的连续时间流匹配。

Result: G-SLiCEs在概率预测及下游任务中性能更优，且能泛化到任意观测网格，尤其在不规则网格上表现突出。

Conclusion: 最大化表达能力的连续时间模型在生成式时间序列建模中有效，兼顾性能优势与灵活观测能力。

Abstract: Recent work on the sequence universality of State Space Models (SSMs) has introduced efficient, maximally expressive continuous-time approaches for time-series modelling. While these works focus on discriminative settings, we extend this perspective to generative time-series modelling by proving that maximally expressive Structured Linear Controlled Differential Equations (SLiCEs) are universal time-series generators, in the sense that they can approximate the induced path laws of continuous causal pushforwards on compact latent sets in $W_\infty$. Building on these theoretical results, we propose Generative SLiCEs (G-SLiCEs), a maximally expressive continuous-time model for flow matching on path-space. Empirically, we show that expressivity improves performance in probabilistic forecasting and downstream tasks, while retaining the advantages of continuous-time models such as generalising to arbitrary observation grids. This is particularly beneficial for irregular grids, where fixed-grid models often struggle.

</details>


### [298] [Learning Theory of the SVRG: Generalization and Convergence Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28513)
*Yunwen Lei,Zimeng Wang,Xiaoming Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次通过算法稳定性视角，对SVRG方法在凸和强凸设定下建立了非平凡的泛化界，揭示了优化与泛化之间的相互作用，并得到了最优的过量风险界。分析框架可推广至其他方差缩减方法如SAGA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方差缩减方法的研究主要集中于收敛性分析，其泛化行为在很大程度上未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 通过算法稳定性视角，利用SVRG的算法结构建立凸和强凸设定下的尖锐稳定性界；将SVRG更新分解为类似SGD的步骤加上零均值校正项，并引入新的Lyapunov函数来吸收由参考点引起的额外梯度项。

Result: 建立了数据依赖的稳定性界（因为训练误差沿轨迹被纳入），并在凸和强凸两种设定下得到了最优的过量风险界。

Conclusion: 本文的分析框架可推广至其他方差缩减方法（如SAGA），为理解方差缩减方法的泛化行为提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Variance reduction (VR) methods employ stochastic gradients with decreasing variance, and they have been widely applied to solve large-scale optimization problems in machine learning because of their efficiency. Existing theoretical studies of VR methods are mainly focused on the convergence analysis, leaving the generalization behavior largely unexplored. In this paper, we bridge this gap by developing the first non-vacuous generalization analysis of the representative VR method: Stochastic Variance Reduced Gradient (SVRG), through the lens of algorithmic stability. In particular, we establish sharp stability bounds of the SVRG in both convex and strongly convex settings by exploiting its algorithmic structure. The obtained bounds are data-dependent, because the training errors are incorporated along the trajectory. Our analysis clarifies the interplay between optimization and generalization, leading to optimal excess population risk bounds in both settings. Our approach differs substantially from existing analyses of stochastic algorithms in the sense that we decompose the SVRG update as an SGD-like step plus a zero-mean correction term and then introduce novel Lyapunov functions to absorb the additional gradient terms induced by the reference points. Our analytical framework can be generalized to other VR methods, and we demonstrate the generalization by the well-known Stochastic Average Gradient Accelerated (SAGA) method.

</details>


### [299] [Stochastic Gradient Descent with Momentum is Algorithmically Stable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28517)
*Yunwen Lei,Zimeng Wang,Xiaoming Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过算法稳定性理论，对带动量的随机梯度下降（SGDM）的泛化性能进行了全面分析，建立了Polyak和Nesterov两种动量方案的紧的稳定性界，并推导了最优的过量群体风险界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献对SGDM的优化性质研究充分，但其泛化能力（尤其是在动量是否损害泛化性能的猜想上）仍缺乏理论理解。

Method: 引入统一的广义SGDM框架（涵盖Polyak和Nesterov动量），基于算法稳定性分析，建立平均模型稳定性界，并推导优化误差界，最终结合两者得到最优过量群体风险界。

Result: 得到了紧的稳定性界（适用于任意[0,1)的动量参数，不要求损失函数Lipschitz连续），以及最优的过量群体风险界。

Conclusion: SGDM在保证优化加速的同时，其泛化性能可以通过稳定性理论得到保证，动量参数不会必然损害泛化。
