Abstract: Stochastic gradient descent with momentum (SGDM) is one of the most widely used optimization algorithms in machine learning. While optimization properties of SGDM have been extensively studied in the literature, it remains insufficiently understood whether and when SGDM can generalize well to unseen data. In particular, it has been conjectured that while momentum accelerates training, it may degrade generalization. In this paper, we close this gap by developing a comprehensive generalization analysis of SGDM through the lens of algorithmic stability. More specifically, we introduce a generalized SGDM framework that encompasses both Polyak's and Nesterov's momentum schemes, and establish tight on-average model stability bounds for smooth and convex problems. Notably, the obtained bounds exploit small optimization error bounds along the trajectory, apply to any momentum parameter in the interval $[0, 1)$, and do not require the commonly assumed Lipschitzness of loss functions. We further derive optimization error bounds for the generalized SGDM, and combine them with our generalization analyses to obtain optimal excess population risk bounds for SGDM with both Polyak's and Nesterov's momentum.

</details>


### [300] [Stabilizing distribution-free probabilistic forecasts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28531)
*Jente Van Belle,Honglin Wen,Wouter Verbeke,Pierre Pinson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将预测稳定性与预测质量联合优化的分布自由概率时间序列预测方法，通过神经网络参数化的回归样条生成稳定的条件分位函数，可在不显著损失预测质量的前提下有效降低预测不稳定性，并针对分布的不同部分（如尾部）进行差异化稳定控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步预测随新观测值更新时，短预测时长虽提升质量，但导致预测不稳定（对同一目标期预测值变动），引发计划变更成本及信任下降；现有方法未在训练中联合优化预测质量与稳定性。

Method: 使用神经网络参数化的回归样条生成稳定的条件分位函数，将质量与稳定性指标联合优化，直接惩罚预测更新导致的分布差异，并可对预测分布不同区域（如中心vs尾部）赋予不同的稳定重要性权重。

Result: 在两个不同统计特性的数据集上，该方法能有效降低预测不稳定性，且不显著损失预测质量；能针对预测分布的特定部分（如尾部）定向施加稳定化效果。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了预测质量与稳定性的可控权衡，可针对下游任务（如库存管理关注上尾）定制稳定策略，提升了预测系统的实用性和可信度。

Abstract: Multi-step-ahead forecasts are often updated as new observations become available, since shorter forecast horizons typically improve forecast quality. However, such improvements come at the cost of forecast instability, i.e., variability in forecasts for the same target period. This instability can trigger costly changes to plans formulated based on the forecasts and may erode trust in the forecasting system. In this work, we integrate forecast stability alongside forecast quality into the training of distribution-free probabilistic time-series forecasting models, allowing us to control this trade-off. We propose a method for generating stabilized forecasted conditional quantile functions using regression splines parameterized by a neural network. This approach enables joint optimization of quality and stability, as it allows us to directly penalize dissimilarities arising from forecast updates. Furthermore, it allows assigning varying importance to stabilizing different parts of the forecast distributions (e.g., central parts vs. tails) to focus on the parts most relevant for the intended downstream use (e.g., the upper tail for inventory management). We empirically evaluate the proposed method on two datasets with different statistical properties and show that it can effectively reduce forecast instability without a substantial loss in forecast quality, and that it can target stabilization effort toward specific parts of the forecast distributions.

</details>


### [301] [Semi-Supervised Hypothesis Testing by Betting on Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28533)
*Yaniv Tenzer,Elad Tolochinsky,Yaniv Romano*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于预测的逐次假设检验框架（testing-by-betting），利用未标注数据增强检验效能。通过构建e统计量，在标签偏移或概念偏移假设下实现任意有效的序贯检验，即使预测不准确时仍能保持性质。实验表明该方法在二值数据上具有非平凡功效，并在大语言模型评估等任务中优于预测驱动推断等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在仅有少量标注数据（联合分布）但拥有大量未标注数据（X的边缘分布）的场景下，如何利用未标注数据来推断Y的分布以及Y|X的条件分布，从而提升假设检验的统计效力。

Method: 提出testing-by-betting框架，引入e统计量构建序贯检验；在标签偏移或概念偏移的分布假设下保证检验的任意有效性；通过理论分析证明二值数据下e统计量具有非平凡功效。

Result: 所提方法在预测不准确时仍保持任意有效性和非平凡功效；在模拟实验和大语言模型评估中，相比预测驱动推断等基线方法取得更优的检验效能，且即使在未标注数据有限、X与Y相关性弱（预测精度低）时增益依然存在。

Conclusion: 该框架有效利用未标注数据增强了序贯假设检验的统计能力，对预测质量具有鲁棒性，为实际应用中标注数据稀缺而未标注数据丰富的情形提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We introduce a testing-by-betting framework that leverages predictions on unlabeled data to enhance the power of sequential hypothesis testing. Given limited samples from the joint distribution of $(X,Y)$, and additional unlabeled samples from the marginal of $X$, we ask how unlabeled data can be used to hypothesize about the distribution of $Y$, and the conditional distribution of $Y\mid X$. We introduce an e-statistic and use it to construct a sequential test. Under standard distributional assumptions -- label shift or concept shift -- we establish that the test is anytime valid. Furthermore, we show that for binary data, the e-statistic has non-trivial power. Crucially, our approach retains these properties even when the underlying predictions are inaccurate. Through simulations and applications to large language models evaluation, we demonstrate power gains over baseline approaches, including prediction-powered inference. These gains persist even with relatively limited unlabeled data and when predictions have low accuracy due to weak correlation between $X$ and $Y$.

</details>


### [302] [High Performance, Low Reliability: Uncertainty Benchmarking for Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28554)
*José Lucas De Melo Costa,Fabrice Popineau,Arpad Rimmel,Bich-Liên Doan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过112个数据集的TALENT基准测试，发现表格基础模型（TFMs）虽然预测性能（AUC）优于梯度提升决策树（GBDTs），但其不确定性校准（条件覆盖率SSCS）更差，揭示了性能与不确定性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格基础模型的预测性能已有显著提升，但其可信度（尤其是不确定性量化）尚未得到充分研究，存在研究空白。

Method: 在TALENT基准的112个数据集上，对比TFMs、GBDTs和经典基线模型的预测性能（AUC）和保形预测下的条件覆盖率（SSCS），并在合成数据集上进一步分析效应增强的条件。

Result: TFMs的AUC最高，但SSCS低于GBDTs，存在性能-不确定性权衡；合成实验揭示了该效应加剧的特定数据场景。

Conclusion: TFMs推动了预测性能的前沿，但实现良好校准的不确定性仍是其可靠部署的重大挑战。

Abstract: Recent Tabular Foundation Models (TFMs) have demonstrated state-of-the-art predictive performance, often surpassing Gradient-Boosted Decision Trees (GBDTs). However, the trustworthiness of these models, particularly their uncertainty quantification, has been largely overlooked. We investigate this gap through an extensive study comparing TFMs, GBDTs, and classical baselines on the 112 datasets of the TALENT benchmark. Our results reveal a performance-uncertainty trade-off: although TFMs achieve the highest predictive performance, measured by AUC, they exhibit lower conditional coverage under conformal prediction, measured by SSCS, compared to GBDTs. Complementary experiments on synthetic datasets further characterize the regimes in which this effect intensifies. We conclude that while TFMs advance predictive frontiers, achieving well-calibrated uncertainty remains a major open challenge for their reliable adoption. Code is available at: https://github.com/jose-melo/high-performance-low-reliability

</details>


### [303] [A Multi-dimensional Framework for Evaluating Generalization in EEG Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28563)
*Aditya Kommineni,Emily Zhou,Kleanthis Avramidis,Tiantian Feng,Shrikanth Narayanan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个多维评估框架，在低资源条件下（少量标注数据、有限传感器、参数高效适应）评估EEG基础模型，发现基础模型在长上下文任务（睡眠阶段预测、心理健康分类）表现优异，但在短窗口BCI任务中与监督模型性能相当，且对通道约束的鲁棒性有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EEG基础模型通常在全微调、精心整理的基准数据集上评估，未反映生物医学领域的真实约束（如有限标注数据、传感器覆盖减少、参数高效适应），缺乏在低资源条件下的系统性评估。

Method: 提出一个多维评估框架，在低资源条件下对监督模型和EEG基础模型（LaBraM、CSBrain、CBraMod）在6个数据集上进行实证分析，涵盖长上下文任务（睡眠阶段预测、心理健康分类）和短窗口BCI任务。

Result: 1）EEG基础模型在长上下文任务（睡眠阶段预测、心理健康分类）上持续优于监督模型；2）在短窗口BCI任务上，监督模型尽管参数量更少但取得可比性能；3）现有基础模型对短窗口任务和通道约束条件鲁棒性有限。

Conclusion: 应使用多维评估协议在真实使用约束下表征模型行为，当前EEG基础模型在短窗口和通道受限场景下仍有局限。

Abstract: Evaluating foundation models under appropriate adaptation settings is essential for understanding the quality and transferability of the learned representations. Recent EEG foundation models have demonstrated promising transfer capabilities across tasks and datasets, motivating their growing use in neurotechnology and clinical applications. However, these models are typically evaluated under full fine-tuning on well-curated downstream datasets, a setting that does not reflect biomedical domain constraints such as limited labeled data, reduced sensor coverage, or parameter-efficient adaptation. In this work, we propose a multi-dimensional evaluation framework for assessing EEG models under realistic low-resource conditions. Empirical analysis of both supervised EEG models and recent EEG foundation models, including LaBraM, CSBrain, and CBraMod, across 6 different datasets is performed under the proposed multi-dimensional evaluation framework. We find that EEG foundation models consistently provide performance gains on long-context tasks such as sleep stage prediction and mental health state classification. In contrast, for short-window Brain Computer Interface style tasks, supervised models achieve comparable despite having substantially fewer parameters. Additional analyses demonstrate that current foundation models provide limited robustness to short-window tasks and channel constrained settings. Together, these findings motivate the use of multi-dimensional evaluation protocols that characterize model behavior under realistic use constraints.

</details>


### [304] [Semantic Optimal Transport for Sparse Autoencoder Feature Matching and Circuit Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28567)
*Tue M. Cao,Nguyen Do,My T. Thai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于分布式的框架，通过激活加权分布和Wasserstein距离统一度量跨层SAE特征的语义距离，解决了特征匹配和电路压缩两个难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀疏自编码器（SAE）已成为解释语言模型的核心工具，但跨层语义相似特征匹配和大规模特征电路压缩为可解释超节点这两个关键分析难以规模化。现有方法将二者视为独立问题，而本文认为它们本质上是同一更基础挑战——估计位于不同激活流形上的SAE特征之间的语义距离。

Method: 提出一个分布式框架：每个特征不由单一解码器向量表示，而是由表达该特征的隐藏状态上的激活加权分布表示。将这些分布投影到共享参考空间，使用Wasserstein距离进行比较，提供统一的跨层特征语义度量。理论上证明了该表示对激活重缩放不变、对扰动稳定、在有限样本边界条件下能恢复真实匹配。

Result: 实验上，该方法优于基于解码器向量和基于LLM的基线方法，能够捕捉相关特征之间的细微功能区分。并且能够自动将大规模特征电路压缩为可解释的超节点。

Conclusion: 本文成功将跨层特征匹配和电路压缩统一为语义距离估计问题，提出的分布式框架在理论上具有不变性和稳定性保证，在实证中表现优于现有方法，并能自动实现电路压缩。

Abstract: Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have become a central tool for interpreting language models. However, two key SAE analyses that remain difficult to scale are (1) matching semantically similar features across multi-layers and (2) compressing large feature circuits into interpretable supernodes. Although these have been treated as separate problems, we show that both are instances of a more fundamental challenge, which we frame as the estimation of semantic distances between SAE features that lie on different activation manifolds. We introduce a distributional framework for this problem, in which each feature is represented not by a single decoder vector like in the literature, but by an activation-weighted distribution over the hidden states that express it. By projecting these distributions into a shared reference space and comparing them with Wasserstein distance, our method provides a unified semantic metric for cross-layer feature comparison. We prove that our representation is invariant to activation rescaling, stable under perturbations, and recovers true matches under finite-sample margin conditions. Empirically, our method outperforms decoder-vector and LLM-based baselines and captures subtle functional distinctions between related features. Notably, our method compresses large feature circuits into interpretable supernodes automatically.

</details>


### [305] [Efficient Pre-Training of LLMs through Truncated SVD Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28573)
*Kaivan Kamali,Kajetan Schweighofer,Hormoz Shahrzad,Olivier Francon,Babak Hodjat,Risto Miikkulainen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TSVD提出了一种在训练过程中保持低秩和严格正交性的框架，通过基于谱能量的自适应秩选择和缓存机制实现正交性维护，在大幅降低计算开销的同时匹配或超越全参数基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型（LLM）的预训练成本日益高昂，现有低秩表示和正交权重矩阵方法要么依赖于静态秩选择，要么因计算成本过高而无法强制权重正交性，亟需一种既能动态调整秩又能高效维护正交性的方案。

Method: 提出TSVD框架：1）基于谱能量的启发式策略实现自适应秩选择；2）设计缓存机制在训练过程中维持权重的严格正交性；3）结合低秩表示与正交性约束进行预训练。

Result: 在多种模型规模上，TSVD匹配或超越了全参数基线的性能，同时显著降低了计算需求。

Conclusion: TSVD提供了一种有理论依据、实用且可扩展的高效LLM预训练路径。

Abstract: The massive scaling of Large Language Models (LLMs) has made pretraining increasingly cost-prohibitive. While low-rank representation and orthonormal weight matrices could in principle reduce parameter counts and computational overhead, most existing methods rely on static rank selection and do not enforce weight orthonormality due to high computational cost. This paper introduces TSVD, a framework that maintains low rank and strict orthonormality throughout the training process. It utilizes a spectral energy-based heuristic for adaptive rank selection, and a caching mechanisms to maintain orthonormality. Theoretical analysis justifies the advantage of the approach in pretraining dynamics and experiments across various model scales demonstrate that it is effective empirically. TSVD matches or exceeds the performance of full-parameter baselines while significantly reducing compute requirements. The approach thus offers a well-founded, practical, and scalable path toward efficient high-performance LLM pretraining.

</details>


### [306] [A Generalized Tikhonov Layer for Interpretable-by-design Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28578)
*Nicolas Tremblay,Benjamin Ricaud,Filippo Maria Bianchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Tikhonov层，一种可解释的图神经网络层，其传播矩阵为闭式解R=(p(L)+Q)^{-1}Q，通过可学习的节点重要性对角矩阵Q和多项式p(·)实现内置解释，在保持表达能力的同时提供全局感受野，并在图分类任务上媲美甚至超越黑盒基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图神经网络缺乏可解释性，模型决策过程不透明，难以理解模型依赖了哪些节点特征和拓扑信息进行预测。

Method: 设计Tikhonov层，传播矩阵为R=(p(L)+Q)^{-1}Q，其中L是归一化拉普拉斯矩阵，Q是可学习的正对角节点重要性矩阵，p(·)是可学习多项式。输出Rx是广义图Tikhonov问题的精确极小值解，通过深度Q网络生成重要性分数，保持表达能力的同时保持层本身透明。

Result: 在图分类基准测试中，该模型匹配（有时超越）黑盒基线模型，同时产生可解释且忠实的解释。

Conclusion: Tikhonov层实现了可解释性与表达能力的兼顾，其学习参数直接揭示预测所依赖的节点特征和图拓扑信息，且单层即可提供全局感受野，缓解过平滑和过挤压问题。

Abstract: We propose the Tikhonov layer, a graph neural network layer that is interpretable by design: once trained, its learned parameters directly reveal which node features and which aspects of the graph topology were leveraged for prediction. In practice, the layer's propagation matrix takes the closed-form $R = (p(L)+Q)^{-1} Q$, where $L$ is the normalized graph Laplacian, $Q = diag(q_1,...,q_n)$ a learnable diagonal matrix of positive node-importance scores, and $p(\cdot)$ a learnable polynomial. For any input feature $x$, the layer output $Rx$ is the exact minimizer of a generalized graph Tikhonov problem that trades off node-level data fidelity against a topology-driven regularization penalty. The learned pair $\{\{q_i\},p\}$ constitutes a built-in explanation: large $q_i$ indicates that node $i$'s own features drive the prediction, while small $q_i$ signals reliance on the local graph topology; the shape of $p$ reveals whether homophily, heterophily, or a band-pass response is exploited. Expressivity is preserved by routing complexity through a dedicated, arbitrarily deep Q-network that produces the importance scores, while the Tikhonov layer itself remains transparent. We prove that distinct node-importance matrices yield distinct propagation operators, structurally coupling the explanation to the computation. Additionally, the Tikhonov layer provides, in a single layer, a global receptive field, mitigating both oversmoothing and oversquashing. Experiments on standard graph classification benchmarks confirm that the model matches (and sometimes outperforms) opaque baselines while producing interpretable and faithful explanations.

</details>


### [307] [Outer-Momentum Restarting in High-Dimensional Two-Phase Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28585)
*Kristi Topollai,Allan Ma,Tolga Dimlioglu,Sui Jiet Tay,Anna Choromanska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在外动量优化器中引入周期性重置机制，通过丢弃陈旧动量同时保留内部循环进展，利用相位抵消效应，在理论上推导了模态重置收缩，并在语言模型预训练实验中验证了该方法能扩大外学习率和动量值的稳定范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiLoCo等通信高效的分布式优化器通过让工作节点执行多次本地更新后再聚合进展，减少了同步成本。外优化器作用于内优化循环诱导的有效谱，外动量控制着跨通信轮次本地进展的累积方式。本文旨在研究周期性重置外动量作为一种简单的补充机制，以控制这种外部记忆。

Method: 在线性化平方损失模型（预测空间残差在经验NTK下演化）中，推导了模态重置收缩，表明重置通过丢弃陈旧动量同时保留内部循环进展来利用相位抵消。在玩具实验验证预测的收缩行为后，在语言模型预训练中测试周期性重置的效果。

Result: 周期性重置外动量能扩大外学习率和动量值的稳定范围，在语言模型预训练实验中验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 周期性重置外动量是一种简单有效的补充机制，能增强分布式优化器的稳定性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Communication-efficient distributed optimizers such as DiLoCo reduce synchronization costs by letting workers perform many local updates before aggregating their progress with an outer momentum optimizer. Recent theory suggests that the outer optimizer acts on an effective spectrum induced by the inner optimization loop, and that the choice of outer momentum controls how progress from local updates is accumulated across communication rounds. We study periodic restarting of the outer momentum as a simple complementary mechanism for controlling this outer memory. In a linearized squared-loss model where prediction-space residuals evolve under the empirical NTK, we derive a mode-wise restart contraction showing that resets exploit phase cancellation by discarding stale momentum while preserving inner-loop progress. Toy experiments verify the predicted contraction behavior, and language-model pretraining experiments show that periodic restarts widen the stable range of outer learning rates and momentum values across communication periods.

</details>


### [308] [Thinned Mean Field Langevin Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28589)
*Zonghao Chen,Heishiro Kanagawa,François-Xavier Briol,Chris J. Oates,Lester Mackey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出KT-MFLD方法，通过核稀疏化技术将平均场Langevin动力学的粒子交互复杂度从O(N²)降至O(N^{3/2})，同时保持与原始方法相同的收敛保证（至多对数因子差异）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决平均场Langevin动力学（MFLD）中模拟N粒子相互作用系统时计算复杂度高达O(N²)的效率瓶颈问题。

Method: 受核稀疏化（kernel thinning）启发，提出KT-MFLD，其中每个粒子仅与大小为O(N^{1/2})的稀疏粒子核心集（coreset）交互，而非全部N个粒子。

Result: 计算复杂度从O(N²)降至O(N^{3/2})，且在温和正则条件下达到与MFLD相同的收敛保证（至多对数因子差异）。在师生神经网络训练、最大均值差异量化、后贝叶斯框架中的预测导向后验计算等任务上得到实证验证。

Conclusion: KT-MFLD在显著降低计算成本的同时保持了理论收敛性质，为大规模熵正则化概率分布优化问题提供了高效求解方案。

Abstract: Several important learning tasks can be formulated as minimizing an entropy-regularized objective over an appropriate space of probability distributions. Mean-field Langevin dynamics (MFLD) facilitate computation in this general context, casting the minimizer as the invariant distribution of a McKean--Vlasov process, which can be numerically discretized using $N$ particles and thus simulated. However, simulating this interacting particle system has computational complexity of order $N^2$. Motivated by recent research into \emph{kernel thinning}, we propose \texttt{KT-MFLD}, in which each particle interacts only with a thinned particle coreset of size $\mathcal{O}(N^{\frac{1}{2}})$. \texttt{KT-MFLD} thus reduces the computational complexity to order $N^{\frac{3}{2}}$ while, under mild regularity conditions, achieving the same convergence guarantees (up to logarithmic factors) as MFLD. Our theoretical analysis is empirically confirmed on tasks including the training of student-teacher neural networks, quantization with maximum mean discrepancy, and computation of predictively-oriented posteriors in a post-Bayesian framework.

</details>


### [309] [PLS in the Mirror of Self-Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28592)
*Jiangsheng,You*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文观察到偏最小二乘回归（PLS）可以被视为一种线性化的自注意力机制，从而将PLS纳入神经网络范式进行研究；同时，PLS中的降维和预测因子选择表明自注意力包含一定程度的维度归一化，有助于改进学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立PLS与自注意力机制之间的联系，将PLS纳入神经网络研究范式，并揭示自注意力机制中隐含的维度归一化特性。

Method: 通过理论分析，将偏最小二乘（PLS）重新解释为线性化的自注意力机制，从而在统一的框架下比较这两种方法。

Result: PLS可被等价地视为一种线性化的自注意力机制；自注意力机制内在地包含了一定程度的维度归一化，这与PLS的降维和预测因子选择功能相似。

Conclusion: PLS与自注意力机制之间存在深层联系，这种视角不仅为PLS提供了新的研究途径，也揭示了自注意力机制中维度归一化对学习性能提升的作用。

Abstract: This note provides an interesting observation on casting partial least square (PLS) as a linearized self-attention so that PLS may be studied within the neural network paradigm. On the other hand, the dimensionality reduction and selection of predictors in PLS may indicate that self-attention includes certain degree of dimensionality normalization toward improved learning.

</details>


### [310] [Transformers Provably Learn to Internalize Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28600)
*Yixiao Huang,Hanlin Zhu,Zixuan Wang,Jiantao Jiao,Stuart Russell,Somayeh Sojoudi,Song Mei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次从理论上分析了隐式思维链（ICoT），提出Log-ICoT课程学习框架，证明多层Transformer可在多项式样本内学习k-奇偶性，训练阶段数仅需O(log k)，在保持显式CoT样本效率的同时消除了推理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式CoT虽能提升Transformer样本效率，但生成推理步骤带来高昂推理开销；隐式CoT虽在经验上有望解决此问题，但其理论基础尚不清晰，缺乏严格的理论保证。

Method: 提出Log-ICoT课程学习方法：不同于标准ICoT逐个移除思维token，Log-ICoT以几何倍数方式分批移除（chunks），将训练阶段从k线性降至log k；理论分析证明L层Transformer在该课程下学习k-奇偶性仅需poly(n)样本，且L = log₂ k。

Result: Log-ICoT达到了与显式CoT相同的样本效率（多项式），同时消除了推理时的思维token生成开销；将训练阶段数从O(k)优化至O(log k)；多层Transformer实验验证了理论，可视化展示了推理过程如何逐步被深层网络吸收。

Conclusion: 隐式思维链在理论上可行且高效，Log-ICoT为消除CoT推理开销提供了理论支撑与实践指导，推动了从显式推理到隐式推理的理论理解。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting substantially improves the sample efficiency of transformers, reducing the complexity of tasks like parity learning from exponential to polynomial in the input length. However, generating explicit reasoning steps at inference is computationally expensive. Implicit Chain-of-Thought (ICoT) has emerged as a promising empirical remedy that trains models to internalize intermediate steps within their hidden states, but its theoretical foundations remain poorly understood. We give the first theoretical analysis of ICoT, proving that an $L$-layer transformer trained under our proposed Log-ICoT curriculum learns $k$-parity with $\mathsf{poly}(n)$ samples and $L = \log_2 k$ training stages. This matches the sample efficiency of explicit CoT while eliminating its inference overhead, and extends prior one-layer parity guarantees to multi-layer architectures. Compared to standard ICoT, which removes thinking tokens one at a time, Log-ICoT removes them in geometric chunks, reducing the number of stages from linear in $k$ to logarithmic. Experiments on multi-layer transformers confirm the theory and visualize how reasoning is progressively absorbed into deeper layers.

</details>


### [311] [Online Irregular Multivariate Time Series Forecasting via Uncertainty-Driven Dual-Expert Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28603)
*Haonan Wen,Hanyang Chen,Songhe Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对不规则多变量时间序列（IMTS）在线预测问题，提出Under-Cali框架，通过不确定性驱动的双专家校准机制，在保持源模型冻结的情况下实现轻量级在线自适应，显著提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IMTS预测方法在离线场景表现良好，但部署到线上时因数据分布动态变化导致性能显著下降。由于不规则采样破坏了时间连续性和周期性，无法直接利用规则MTS中成熟的在线学习技术，亟需针对IMTS的在线自适应方法。

Method: 提出Under-Cali框架，包含三个核心组件：1）不确定性估计器，作为核心控制信号联合管理推理与自适应过程；2）双专家校准模块，包含可靠专家与不可靠专家；3）自适应路由模块，根据不确定性高低将样本路由至不同专家：高不确定性样本交由不可靠专家校准，低不确定性样本保留在可靠专家。系统使用校准后的可靠样本更新可靠专家和不确定性估计器，用具有挑战性的样本更新不可靠专家，实现稳定高效的在线学习。源预测模型保持冻结，仅通过轻量级、模型无关的校准模块进行自适应。

Result: 在多个IMTS基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该方法在低计算成本下取得了一致且显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: Under-Cali通过不确定性驱动的双专家校准策略，有效解决了不规则MTS在线预测中的分布漂移问题，为IMTS在线自适应提供了一种高效、模型无关的解决方案。

Abstract: Irregular multivariate time series forecasting is critical in many real-world applications, where time series are irregularly sampled and exhibit dynamically evolving missingness patterns. Although existing methods perform well in offline settings, they often suffer from significant performance degradation when deployed online due to dynamic shifts in data distribution. Maintaining forecasting capability in such dynamic scenarios typically necessitates online adaptation techniques. Since irregular sampling fundamentally undermines temporal continuity and periodicity, we cannot leverage these widely studied characteristics from regular MTS for online learning. To this end, we study the problem of online IMTS forecasting and propose Under-Cali, an uncertainty-driven dual-expert calibration framework consisting of three core components: an uncertainty estimator, a dual-expert calibration module, and an adaptive routing module. We design an uncertainty estimator that serves as the core control signal to jointly manage inference and adaptation processes. In our framework, the uncertainty estimator first assesses uncertainty for each incoming batch. The adaptive routing module then directs samples with high uncertainty to the unreliable expert for calibration, while low uncertainty samples remain with the reliable expert. Subsequently, the system updates the reliable expert and the uncertainty estimator using well-calibrated reliable samples, and updates the unreliable expert with challenging samples, enabling stable and efficient online learning. Under-Cali keeps the source forecasting model frozen and performs adaptation only through a lightweight, model-agnostic calibration module, enabling efficient adaptation. Extensive experiments on IMTS benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements with low computational cost. Our code is available at https://github.com/HaonanWen/Under-Cali.

</details>


### [312] [Learning High-Dimensional Parity Functions with Product Networks using Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28612)
*Guillaume Larue,Louis-Adrien Dufrène,Quentin Lampin,Hadi Ghauch,Ghaya Rekaya*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种紧凑型乘积神经网络架构，结合伯努利输入数据稀疏性（p_e ≤ 1/N）和合适的超参数选择，能够高效学习高维奇偶函数，在N=100,000维度上实现收敛，并具有理论保证和多项式复杂度缩放规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经网络架构学习高维奇偶函数需要指数级样本复杂度，使得梯度优化在N较大时不可行，亟需更高效的方法。

Method: 采用紧凑型乘积神经网络架构，结合随机数据稀疏性（伯努利输入，p_e ≤ 1/N）和优化的超参数（p_e和学习率α），实现高效奇偶学习。

Result: 在N=100,000维度上实验验证了理论；发现了p_e和学习率α的最优超参数选择；证明了多项式复杂度缩放规律。

Conclusion: 建立了架构归纳偏置与数据稀疏性之间的基本联系，为神经算术、结构化推理、二元神经网络及自动化协议发现等机器学习应用开辟了新可能。

Abstract: Parity functions are fundamental Boolean operations with critical applications across machine learning, cryptography, and error correction. Yet, learning high-dimensional parity functions poses significant challenges: in a general setting, standard neural network architectures typically require exponential sample complexity, making gradient-based optimization intractable for large number of inputs $N$. We demonstrate that compact product-based neural architectures combined with stochastic data sparsity (Bernoulli inputs with $p_e \leq 1/N$) and appropriate hyperparameter choice enable efficient parity learning, with theoretical guarantees of convergence. Experiments validate our theory across dimensions up to $N = 100{,}000$, with empirical evidence showing optimal hyperparameter choices for $p_e$ and learning rate $α$, as well as polynomial complexity scaling laws. This work establishes fundamental connections between architectural inductive bias and data sparsity, opening new possibilities for neural arithmetic, structured reasoning, binary neural networks, and machine learning applied to automated protocol discovery.

</details>


### [313] [Random Process Flow Matching: Generative Implicit Representations of Multivariate Random Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28625)
*Julien Lalanne,David Picard,Lionel Boillot,Lina-María Guayacán-Carrillo,Leon Barens,Jean-Michel Pereira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RP Flow，一种基于Flow Matching的框架，通过神经隐式函数和随机傅里叶特征，从稀疏观测中学习信号分布并生成带校准不确定性的高质量样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型通常需要大量完整观测数据，但在实际场景中（如物理场重建）往往只有单一场的稀疏测量值，且需要可追溯的不确定性估计。传统概率方法（如GP）虽能提供不确定性但扩展性差，而现代深度生成模型难以在数据稀疏条件下保持校准的不确定性。

Method: RP Flow基于Flow Matching框架，将向量场表示为神经隐式函数。使用随机傅里叶特征学习可任意位置查询的隐式信号表示，通过集成采样编码不确定性。在源空间通过GP回归构建贝叶斯后验以生成高质量样本。

Result: 在挑战性条件下（高频、高稀疏度、高维度），RP Flow能生成逼真样本并提供校准的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: RP Flow标志着生成模型在数据稀缺且需保持不确定性可追溯的重建任务中的重要进展。

Abstract: Generative modeling provides a powerful framework for learning data distributions. These models initially relied on probabilistic methods such as Gaussian Processes (GP) for uncertainty-aware predictions and shifted towards larger trainable models to learn more complex distributions. In this work, we introduce Random Process (RP) Flow, a Flow Matching-based framework that represents the vector field as a neural implicit function. Unlike modern generative methods, our setting involves a single observed field, from which only sparse measurements are available. RP Flow uses Random Fourier Features to learn an implicit signal representation that can be queried at any arbitrary location from a limited set of observations, while encoding uncertainty through ensemble sampling. We propose constructing a Bayesian posterior by GP regression in the source space to generate high-quality samples. Our empirical results demonstrate that this framework generates realistic samples along with calibrated uncertainty estimates, even under challenging conditions such as high frequency, high sparsity, or high dimensionality. These findings position RP Flow as a milestone towards generative models for reconstruction tasks where data is scarce and uncertainty must remain traceable.

</details>


### [314] [When Interpretability Is Unequally Distributed: Fairness in Hybrid Interpretable Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28626)
*Ziba Jabbar Zare,Ulrich Aïvodji,Julien Ferry,Thibaut Vidal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 混合可解释模型通过将部分样本分配给透明组件、其余分配给黑箱模型，在准确性与可解释性之间实现灵活权衡。然而这可能导致某些群体系统性获得可解释决策，而其他群体被不成比例地路由至黑箱。本文形式化了"可解释性覆盖差异"(ICD)这一程序公平性问题，实验表明在中间透明度区间存在显著ICD，并证明简单约束可有效缓解ICD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合可解释模型在分配可解释性时可能存在程序公平性问题：某些人口统计学群体系统性地获得可解释决策，而其他群体被不成比例地路由至黑箱。现有研究缺乏对此类分配公平性的形式化刻画与系统性评估。

Method: 形式化定义了ICD（可解释性覆盖差异）作为混合可解释模型中路由决策的群体公平性度量；利用预测多重性工具，在四种混合可解释学习方法、三个标准公平性基准数据集和多个敏感属性上系统评估ICD；引入简单的覆盖率差异约束以缓解ICD。

Result: 在中间透明度区间（即可解释组件和黑箱组件均被积极使用）观察到显著的ICD；引入覆盖率差异约束可显著降低精确混合学习方法中的ICD，对准确率和稀疏性影响甚微；在某些设置下，缓解ICD还能同时改善标准算法公平性指标。

Conclusion: 混合可解释模型不仅应审计其预测公平性，还应审计其如何在个体和群体之间分配可解释性。ICD是一种应被关注的新维度程序公平性问题，且可以通过简单约束得到有效缓解。

Abstract: Hybrid interpretable models combine a transparent component with a black-box model by assigning some examples to the former and deferring the rest to the latter. While this design enables flexible tradeoffs between accuracy and interpretability, it also raises a distinct procedural fairness concern: some demographic groups may systematically receive interpretable decisions, while others are disproportionately routed to a black box.
  We formalize this issue as Interpretability Coverage Disparity (ICD), a demographic-parity-style measure applied to the routing decision of hybrid interpretable models. Using tools from predictive multiplicity, we study ICD across four hybrid interpretable learning methods, three standard fairness benchmark datasets, and multiple sensitive attributes. Our experiments reveal substantial ICD in intermediate transparency regimes, where both the interpretable and black-box components are actively used. We further show that simple coverage-disparity constraints can significantly reduce ICD in exact hybrid learning methods, with marginal impact on accuracy and sparsity. In several settings, ICD mitigation also improves standard algorithmic fairness metrics. These results show that hybrid interpretable models should be audited not only for predictive fairness, but also for how they allocate interpretability across individuals and groups.

</details>


### [315] [Single-Rollout Hidden-State Dynamics for Training-Free RLVR Data Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28631)
*Jianghao Wu,Jianfei Cai,Weiqiang Wang,Jin Ye,Daniel F. Schmidt,Yasmeen George*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHIFT提出了一种基于推理时隐状态动态的单次、无训练的数据选择方法，用于强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）。该方法通过计算推理引起的表征偏移（RIRS）作为实例效用的轻量级代理，并结合RIRS增强特征空间中的质量加权最远优先CoreSet过程来选择紧凑子集。在数学推理和医学QA基准测试中，SHIFT在超低预算下持续优于训练无关的多样性和难度/不确定性基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR对训练实例的选择高度敏感，而现有数据选择方法依赖于训练时的优化信号和/或需要对大量候选池进行奖励评估或获取真实答案，这在专业领域成本高昂且往往不可行。因此，需要在没有任何RL训练、标签或完整池奖励评估的情况下进行数据选择。

Method: SHIFT方法：(1) 对每个候选实例执行单次确定性推理展开；(2) 计算推理引起的表征偏移（RIRS），即从起始到结束的隐状态差值，作为实例效用的轻量级代理；(3) 在RIRS增强的特征空间中，通过质量加权的最远优先CoreSet过程选择紧凑子集，确保覆盖性。

Result: 在数学推理和医学QA基准测试中，SHIFT在超低预算下持续优于训练无关的多样性和难度/不确定性基线，提升了域内准确率并迁移到更难的评价设置。消融实验表明RIRS的覆盖性和质量加权贡献互补增益。

Conclusion: 基于推理时隐状态动态的SHIFT方法是一种有效的RLVR数据选择策略，无需训练和标签即可实现高质量选择，且RIRS并非简单的输入/输出长度统计所能解释。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) can yield large reasoning gains from very few training instances, yet its strong sensitivity to which instances are used makes data selection a central bottleneck. Most existing selection pipelines rely on training-time optimization signals and/or require access to verifiable rewards or ground-truth answers over large candidate pools, which is costly and often infeasible in specialized domains. We study RLVR data selection in a setting where selection must be performed before any RL training and without labels or reward evaluation on the full pool. We propose SHIFT, a one-shot, training-free selector based solely on inference-time hidden-state dynamics. For each candidate instance, SHIFT runs a single deterministic reasoning rollout and computes a reasoning-induced representation shift (RIRS) as the start-to-end hidden-state delta. SHIFT uses the RIRS magnitude as a lightweight proxy for instance utility and enforces coverage via a quality-weighted farthest-first CoreSet procedure in an RIRS-augmented feature space, producing compact subsets that scale to large unlabeled pools. Across mathematical reasoning and medical QA benchmarks under ultra-low budgets, SHIFT consistently outperforms training-free diversity and difficulty/uncertainty baselines, improving both in-domain accuracy and transfer to harder evaluation settings. Ablations show that RIRS-based coverage and quality-weighting contribute complementary gains, and analyses indicate that RIRS is not explained by simple input/output length statistics. Code is available at github.com/JianghaoWu/SHIFT.

</details>


### [316] [Augmenting Attention with Exponentially Decaying Memory Improves Query-Aware KV Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28640)
*Xiuying Wei,Caglar Gulcehre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了RAT+（一种循环增强注意力机制）能否提升现有查询感知稀疏推理方法的性能。实验表明，在多个稀疏预算下的八项大海捞针任务中，RAT+一致性地优于标准注意力方法，并且作者提出了两个假说来解释其优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文语言模型的推理效率至关重要，其中注意力计算和KV缓存访问是主要瓶颈。已有查询感知稀疏推理方法（如Quest、MoBA、SnapKV）试图缓解这一问题，但仍有改进空间。本文探索是否可以将RAT+的指数衰减记忆模块与这些方法结合，进一步提升稀疏推理的准确性。

Method: 在RAT+发布的检查点以及使用RAT+记忆模块继续预训练100亿token的OLMo2-7B模型上，将RAT+与三种代表性查询感知稀疏推理方法（Quest、MoBA、SnapKV）结合，在八个大海捞针任务上评估其与标准注意力基线的性能对比。此外，设计针对性实验验证两个解释性假说。

Result: RAT+在所有稀疏预算下，与Quest、MoBA、SnapKV三种方法结合时，均一致性地优于使用标准注意力的对应方法。该结论在RAT+原始检查点和OLMo2-7B继续预训练模型上均得到验证。

Conclusion: RAT+的指数衰减记忆模块能够有效提升查询感知稀疏推理方法的准确性，作者提出了两个假说解释这一提升，并通过实验支持了这些假说。

Abstract: Efficient inference is critical for long-context language models, where attention computation and KV-cache access dominate the cost. Recent work RAT+, introduces a recurrence-augmented attention backbone that enables flexible dilated attention at inference time. In this paper, we investigate whether this exponentially decaying memory can also improve existing query-aware sparse inference methods. Using representative methods including Quest, MoBA, and SnapKV, we show that RAT+ consistently improves accuracy over standard attention across sparse budgets on eight needle-in-a-haystack tasks. We validate these gains both on the released checkpoints from the RAT+ paper and on OLMo2-7B, which we continue pretraining with the added memory module for 10B tokens. Finally, we propose two hypotheses explaining why this memory module benefits query-aware sparse inference and design targeted experiments to support them.

</details>


### [317] [Interpretability-Guided Layer Selection over Subspace Projection: SAEs as Stethoscopes, Not Scalpels, for Raw Task Vector Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28649)
*Li Lei,Madalina Ciobanu,Qingqing Mao,Ritankar Das*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现将稀疏自编码器（SAE）用于大模型数学推理编辑时，直接投影任务向量到SAE特征子空间会导致约97%的修改能量丢失（信息瓶颈），效果无统计显著性。作者提出将SAE从"手术刀"重新定位为"听诊器"——仅用于层级别诊断而非干预级别过滤，通过SAE导出的特异性分数识别关键层注入未过滤的原始任务向量，在Minerva Math基准上将数论准确率从29.6%提升至39.4%（p=0.0007），7个数学科目中5个显著改善且无退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需要精准的模型编辑来增强特定领域能力，但传统微调计算成本高且会导致灾难性遗忘。SAE理论上允许在特征级别识别干预位置，但现有方法（直接投影到SAE特征空间）缺乏严格的定量评估，其有效性尚未得到验证。

Method: 作者首先严格评估了SAE引导的编辑流水线，发现投影操作存在几何不对齐问题：激活空间的SAE方向与权重空间的任务向量之间存在几何错位。然后提出"SAE作为听诊器而非手术刀"的新范式：用SAE计算每层的特异性分数（specificity score），仅将未过滤的原始任务向量注入得分高的层，而SAE不参与最终的向量过滤，仅用于指导在哪一层进行编辑。

Result: 直接投影到SAE特征子空间丢弃了约97%的修改能量，7个数学科目均无统计显著改善。相比之下，新方法在Minerva Math基准上：数论准确率从29.6%提升至39.4%（z=+3.41, p=0.0007），7个科目中5个显著提升，无任何科目显著退化。方法完全确定、无额外推理成本。

Conclusion: SAE直接用于特征过滤存在根本性的几何不对齐问题，但SAE作为层级别诊断工具具有实用价值。本文提供了可解释性引导模型编辑的原则性框架，未来工作可探索SAE诊断与其他编辑策略的结合。

Abstract: LLMs increasingly require surgical model editing to enhance domain-specific capabilities without incurring the computational cost or catastrophic forgetting associated with full fine-tuning. Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a promising tool in this setting, in principle allowing for feature-level identification of where to intervene. In this work, we rigorously evaluate an SAE-guided editing pipeline for mathematical reasoning on Gemma-3-4B-IT and uncover a fundamental failure mode: the intuitively appealing approach of projecting task vectors onto SAE feature subspaces acts as an information bottleneck that discards approximately 97% of the modification energy, yielding no statistically significant improvements across seven math subjects. We show that this failure stems from a geometric misalignment between activation-space SAE directions and weight-space task vectors. We then propose a shift in perspective: SAE as a Stethoscope, Not a Scalpel, where SAEs are used for layer-level diagnosis rather than intervention-level filtering. By injecting unfiltered raw task vectors only into layers identified by an SAE-derived specificity score, we improve Number Theory accuracy from 29.6% to 39.4% (z=+3.41, p=0.0007) on the Minerva Math benchmark; 5 of 7 math subjects significantly improved and none significantly degraded. Our method is fully deterministic, requires no additional inference cost, and provides a principled framework for interpretability-guided model editing.

</details>


### [318] [Applications of temporal graph learning for predicting the dynamics of biological systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28659)
*Manuel Dileo,Andrea Sottoriva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时间图神经网络的方法，通过构建伪时间分辨的基因调控网络来建模单细胞转录组数据的动态发育过程，在三个任务上超越了scGPT和scFoundation等基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单细胞基础模型（如scGPT、scFoundation）主要基于Transformer架构处理静态基因表达矩阵，未能显式建模细胞发育过程中的时序动态变化，而理解细胞状态的渐进涌现、分化和重组对研究发育和疾病进展至关重要。

Method: 从单细胞转录组数据出发，推断伪时间轨迹，将细胞离散化为发育快照，为每个快照重建一个基因调控网络，然后应用时间图神经网络来预测生物学状态。

Result: 在红细胞原肠胚形成和胰腺内分泌发生两个小鼠发育数据集上，图模型在基因表达预测、链路预测和出度中心性预测三个任务上均优于scGPT和scFoundation；时间图学习还能捕获非平凡的调控动态并识别时间上重要的基因枢纽。

Conclusion: 时间图学习是建模动态生物系统的有前景方向，可作为当前单细胞基础模型方法的补充范式。

Abstract: Biological foundation models have shown strong performance in single-cell representation learning by applying transformer architectures directly to gene-expression matrices. However, these approaches predominantly operate in static settings and do not explicitly model the temporal evolution of developmental programs in the cell. Modeling such dynamics is important for understanding how cellular states progressively emerge, differentiate, and reorganize during development or disease progression. In this work-in-progress paper, we investigate an alternative temporal graph-based perspective in which cellular states are represented through pseudotime-resolved gene regulatory networks and modeled as evolving graph structures over persistent gene identities. Starting from single-cell transcriptomic data, we infer pseudotime trajectories, discretize cells into developmental snapshots, reconstruct one gene regulatory network per snapshot, and apply temporal graph neural networks to forecast biological states. We evaluate this framework on two publicly available mouse developmental datasets, erythroid gastrulation and pancreatic endocrinogenesis, considering three complementary tasks: gene-expression forecasting, link prediction, and out-degree centrality prediction. Our results show that graph-based models outperform well-known foundation-model such as scGPT and scFoundation, suggesting that explicitly modeling evolving regulatory structure provides useful information beyond static pretrained representations. For link prediction and centrality forecasting, temporal graph learning captures non-trivial regulatory dynamics and enables the identification of temporally important gene hubs. Overall, our findings support temporal graph learning as a promising direction for modeling dynamic biological systems and as a complementary paradigm to current foundation model approaches in single-cell biology.

</details>


### [319] [Activation Steering for Synthetic Data Generation: The Role of Diversity in Downstream Safety Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28664)
*Vijeta Deshpande,Tootiya Giyahchi,Veena Padmanabhan,Leman Akoglu,Anna Rumshisky*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究激活引导(AS)能否为安全检测模型生成高质量训练数据。通过4个概念×2个模型×4种引导方法的内外评估，发现AS生成数据在3/4概念上优于提示生成数据，但仅有41/136配置有效。引入多样性作为新评估维度，发现引导强度增加会降低多样性。三个维度的调和平均数与下游AUROC更一致相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全检测模型需要大量违反HHH原则的示例以实现鲁棒泛化，但这类样本稀缺。激活引导(AS)是一种数据高效的目标概念对齐响应生成方法，但其能否生成高质量训练数据集尚未被验证。

Method: 采用内外双评估框架：内在评估引入样本级和集合级多样性作为新质量维度，联合评估引导成功（概念对齐）、连贯性和多样性；外在评估用AS生成数据替换HHH违规样本训练检测分类器。涵盖4个概念、2个模型、4种引导方法。

Result: AS生成数据在3/4概念上优于提示生成数据；仅41/136的AS配置优于提示生成；引导强度增加降低响应多样性；三个维度的调和平均数与下游AUROC的相关性比仅用成功率和连贯性更一致。

Conclusion: AS在合成数据生成方面具有提升安全检测的潜力，但下游效用存在于同时满足成功率、连贯性和多样性的狭窄区间。多样性是之前被忽视的关键调优轴，三个维度的调和平均数可作为调优AS超参数的实用启发式目标。

Abstract: Safety detection models require examples of HHH (Helpful, Harmless, Honest)-violating outputs for robust generalization, however such examples are scarce. Activation Steering (AS) has emerged as a data-efficient method for generating target-concept-aligned responses. We investigate whether AS can generate high-quality training datasets for downstream classifiers, a question that remains untested. We present a two-fold study with intrinsic and extrinsic evaluation across $4$ concepts $\times\,2$ models $\times\,4$ steering methods. Intrinsically, beyond the field-standard rubric of steering success (concept alignment) and coherence, we introduce sample- and set-level diversity as a quality axis previously absent from the literature, and find that increasing steering strength reduces response diversity. Extrinsically, we replace HHH-violating examples in the available training data with steered generations and fine-tune detection classifiers. AS-generated data results in a better classifier than the prompting-generated data on $3$ of $4$ concepts. However, only $41$ of $136$ AS configurations outperform prompting, indicating that downstream utility lies in a narrow regime that jointly satisfies success, coherence, and diversity. The harmonic mean of these three axes correlates with downstream AUROC more consistently across concepts than success and coherence alone, providing a practical heuristic target for practitioners tuning AS hyperparameters. Together, our results highlight the potential of AS in synthetic data generation for improving safety detection and identify diversity as a critical, previously overlooked axis for tuning AS.

</details>


### [320] [Optimal Data Acquisition for Reinforcement Learning: A Large Deviations Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28675)
*Mingjie Hu,Jian-Qiang Hu,Enlu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套统一的大偏差理论框架，用于无限时域强化学习中的数据采集问题，以指数衰减率作为效率指标，推导了马氏链下的变分刻画，并基于此提出了凸松弛和自适应数据采集策略，实现了接近鲁棒最优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在商业和医疗运筹等场景中，强化学习的数据采集效率面临严峻挑战，因为交互成本高、速度慢且常涉及人工参与。现有方法缺乏统一的理论框架来刻画和优化数据采集效率。

Method: 1) 基于大偏差理论，引入策略选择错误概率的指数衰减率作为效率度量；2) 推导马氏链下的变分刻画，得到嵌套优化问题；3) 提出显式约束的可处理凸松弛方法；4) 设计懒惰一步投影次梯度方法求解松弛问题，并构建自适应数据采集策略；5) 扩展至线性函数近似以提升可扩展性。

Result: 所提出的强化学习算法在最优性准则下达到接近鲁棒最优（常数因子内），数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文为无限时域强化学习中的数据采集问题提供了一个统一的大偏差理论框架，通过凸松弛和自适应策略实现了理论上保证且实际有效的数据采集方案。

Abstract: Data acquisition efficiency is a central challenge in deploying reinforcement learning in business and healthcare operations, where interactions are costly, slow, and often involve humans in the loop. This paper develops a unified large deviations framework for data acquisition in infinite-horizon reinforcement learning. We introduce the exponential decay rate of the policy-selection error probability as a principled efficiency metric and derive a variational characterization of this rate via large deviations theory for Markov chains, yielding a nested optimization problem. Based on this characterization, we formalize two complementary notions of optimality in terms of the optimal solution of the nested problem. Because the resulting program is implicit and generally intractable, we propose a tractable convex relaxation with explicit constraints. We then develop a lazy one-step projected subgradient method to solve the relaxed problem and use its iterates to construct an adaptive data acquisition policy. We prove that the resulting reinforcement learning algorithm is near-robustly optimal under our optimality criterion, up to a constant factor. Finally, we extend the framework to linear function approximation to improve scalability, and numerical experiments support the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [321] [History-aware adaptive reduced-order models via incremental singular value decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28684)
*Amirpasha Hedayat,Ali Mohaghegh,Laura Balzano,Cheng Huang,Karthik Duraisamy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于增量奇异值分解（iSVD）的自适应投影降阶模型（ROM）框架，通过在线修正快照更新基向量，使ROM能够适应偏离离线训练数据的动力学行为。在Burgers方程、Sod激波管和旋转爆震发动机问题上的实验表明，iSVD自适应方法在预测精度和计算效率上均优于现有基线方法，且主要计算成本来自与全阶模型的交互而非iSVD更新本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降阶模型（ROM）可以加速高维动力学仿真，但当在线动力学偏离离线训练数据所代表的区域时，其精度往往会显著下降。现有ROM缺乏有效的在线自适应能力，导致在长时间积分或工况变化时预测失效。

Method: 基于增量奇异值分解（iSVD）的投影自适应ROM框架：通过偶尔调用全阶模型获取修正快照，利用iSVD在线更新基向量；基向量更新自然传播到缩减算子和超缩减机制中，保持全参数化一致性；iSVD通过其演变的奇异结构保留历史动力学信息的编码历史。

Result: 1) Burgers方程问题：历史感知更新优于瞬时更新，iSVD整体性能最强；2) Sod激波管和旋转爆震发动机（RDE）问题：优势在更复杂的可压缩流设置中得以保持；3) RDE问题：iSVD自适应ROM在预测精度和计算效率上均优于当前最先进的Direct自适应ROM基线；4) 成本分析：主要在线成本来自与全阶模型的交互，iSVD更新本身可忽略。

Conclusion: iSVD是在线学习缩减子空间的有效机制，为实现预测时域比初始训练窗口长数个数量级的ROM指明了方向。

Abstract: Reduced-order models (ROMs) can accelerate high-dimensional dynamical simulations, but their accuracy often deteriorates when online dynamics leave the regime represented by offline training data. We develop a projection-based adaptive ROM framework based on incremental singular value decomposition (iSVD), in which occasional full-order operator evaluations provide correction snapshots for online basis updates. The intrusive ROMs considered here are fully parameterized by the basis, so each update naturally propagates to reduced operators and hyper-reduction machinery. Through its evolving singular structure, iSVD retains an encoded history of the observed dynamics and is history-aware in this sense. We study the method on three nonlinear problems of increasing complexity: the one-dimensional viscous Burgers equation, the Sod shock tube, and a stiff one-dimensional ten-species rotating detonation engine (RDE). The Burgers problem is used to analyze the method and compare iSVD with alternative basis adaptation rules, showing that history-aware updates outperform instantaneous updates and that iSVD gives the strongest overall performance. The Sod and RDE cases demonstrate that these advantages persist in more challenging compressible-flow settings. For the RDE problem, the iSVD adaptive ROM improves upon the current state-of-the-art Direct adaptive ROM baseline in both predictive accuracy and computational efficiency. A cost analysis shows that the dominant online cost comes from interacting with the full-order model to obtain correction snapshots, while the iSVD update itself is negligible. These results identify iSVD as an effective mechanism for online learning of reduced subspaces and suggest a path toward ROMs that remain predictive over horizons several orders of magnitude longer than their initial training window.

</details>


### [322] [Expressive Power of Floating-Point Neural Networks with Arbitrary Reduction Orders and Inexact Activation Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28704)
*Yeachan Park,Geonho Hwang,Wonyeol Lee,Sejun Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了浮点神经网络在通用浮点执行语义（包括任意归约顺序和带有界ulp误差的非精确激活实现）下的表达能力，提出了可区分性框架，证明了第一层区分所有不同输入对是通用表示能力的必要条件，并在温和条件下证明了该条件也是充分的，为Sigmoid、tanh、ReLU等广泛使用的激活函数建立了在更现实浮点执行模型下的通用表示能力结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络表达能力理论大多假设精确实数算术，但实际网络在有限精度浮点算术下执行，且实现依赖具体语义。已有浮点神经网络研究局限于高度受限的激活函数和理想化假设（如固定左到右归约顺序、正确舍入的激活实现）。因此需要研究在更通用的浮点执行语义下神经网络的表达能力。

Method: 引入了一个通用的可区分性框架，通过分析浮点神经网络能否区分每一对不同的输入来刻画其表达能力。首先证明第一层的可区分性是通用表示能力的必要条件，然后证明在激活实现的温和条件下，适当的可区分性也是充分条件。

Result: 该框架揭示了无法实现通用表示的一大类激活实现（扩展了先前孤立反例如正确舍入的余弦激活），并为Sigmoid、tanh、ReLU、ELU、SeLU、GeLU、Swish、Mish和sin等实际激活函数在更现实的浮点执行模型下建立了通用表示能力结果。

Conclusion: 本文为浮点神经网络在通用浮点执行语义下的表达能力提供了理论基础，表明在适当的可区分性条件下，许多实际激活函数的浮点实现仍具备通用表示能力，尽管浮点精度有限且执行语义复杂。

Abstract: Most existing expressivity theories for neural networks assume exact real arithmetic, whereas practical neural networks are executed under finite-precision floating-point arithmetic with implementation-dependent execution semantics. Recent works have begun studying the expressive power of floating-point neural networks, but existing results are limited to highly restricted activation functions and idealized assumptions such as fixed left-to-right reduction orders and correctly rounded activation implementations. In this work, we study the expressive power of floating-point neural networks under generalized floating-point execution semantics, including arbitrary reduction orders and inexact activation implementations with bounded ulp errors. We investigate when floating-point neural networks can represent arbitrary functions between floating-point domains exactly. To this end, we introduce a general distinguishability framework and show that the ability to distinguish every pair of distinct inputs in the first layer is necessary for universal representability. This characterization yields broad classes of activation implementations that are not universal representators, extending previous isolated counterexamples such as the correctly rounded cosine activation. We further prove that a suitable form of distinguishability is also sufficient for universal representability under mild conditions on the activation implementation. Using this framework, we establish universal representability results for a broad class of practical activation functions, including implementations of $\mathrm{Sigmoid}$, $\tanh$, $\mathrm{ReLU}$, $\mathrm{ELU}$, $\mathrm{SeLU}$, $\mathrm{GeLU}$, $\mathrm{Swish}$, $\mathrm{Mish}$, and $\sin$, under significantly more realistic floating-point execution models than previously known.

</details>


### [323] [Understanding Generalization and Forgetting in In-Context Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28705)
*Guangyu Li,Meng Ding,Lijie Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个上下文持续学习（In-Context Continual Learning）的理论框架，分析了预训练Transformer如何在单个prompt中处理多个顺序任务。通过研究线性和掩码线性自注意力机制，推导了顺序任务提示下的预测误差表达式，揭示了标准注意力机制会因统一或因果聚合历史上下文而导致任务间干扰和系统性偏差，并提供了偏差-方差-干扰的误差分解，解释了长提示中的顺序敏感性和性能退化现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ICL理论主要研究单任务场景，但真实场景中prompt往往包含多个异质任务序列，缺乏对LLM是否在推理过程中隐式执行持续学习的理论理解。

Method: 通过构建首个上下文持续学习的理论框架，聚焦线性和掩码线性自注意力机制，推导顺序任务提示下模型预测的误差表达式，并进行偏差-方差-干扰三因素误差分解。

Result: 标准注意力机制通过统一或因果聚合历史上下文，不可避免地导致任务间干扰和系统性偏差；揭示了历史上下文信息何时产生正迁移或确凿的负迁移；暴露了基于注意力的持续推理的基本局限性。

Conclusion: 该理论框架为长提示中的顺序敏感性和性能退化提供了理论解释，指出了注意力机制在持续推理场景中的固有缺陷。

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) derives its power from enabling Large Language Models to adapt to new tasks via prompt-based reasoning alone, entirely bypassing the need for parameter updates. Existing theories primarily study ICL in single-task settings, while real-world prompts often contain sequences of heterogeneous tasks, leaving a gap in understanding whether Large Language Models implicitly perform continual learning during inference. To bridge this gap, we propose the first theoretical framework for in-context continual learning, modeling how a pretrained Transformer processes multiple sequential tasks within a single prompt through shared attention mechanisms. Focusing on linear and masked linear self-attention, we derive error expressions for model predictions under sequential task prompts and analyze their generalization and forgetting behavior. Our results reveal that standard attention mechanisms inevitably induce intertask interference by uniformly or causally aggregating historical contexts, leading to systematic bias. We further provide a bias-variance-interference decomposition of prediction error, characterizing when historical in-context information yields positive transfer or provable negative transfer. This analysis exposes fundamental limits of attention-based continual inference and offers theoretical explanations for order sensitivity and performance degradation in long prompts.

</details>


### [324] [Stage-wise Distortion-Perception Traversal in Zero-shot Inverse Problems with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28711)
*Jiawei Zhang,Ziyuan Liu,Leon Yan,Zhenyu Xiao,Yuantao Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MAP-RPS框架，通过MAP估计阶段（低失真）与重噪后验采样阶段（提升感知质量）的两阶段设计，利用单一扩散模型在零样本逆问题中实现失真-感知权衡的灵活遍历，并扩展至潜空间版本LMAP-RPS以适用大规模预训练潜扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管扩散模型在零样本逆问题求解中取得成功，但如何在扩散逆算法中高效且原则性地实现失真-感知权衡（D-P tradeoff）的灵活遍历仍缺乏充分研究。

Method: 提出两阶段框架MAP-RPS：第一阶段进行MAP估计，逼近MMSE解以获得低失真初始化；第二阶段进行重噪后验采样，逐步提升感知质量。同时将方法扩展至潜空间得到LMAP-RPS，利用大规模预训练潜扩散骨干网络。

Result: 实验表明MAP-RPS和LMAP-RPS在多种任务上实现了更有效的D-P权衡遍历，同时作为实际逆问题的高效求解器也表现出强性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的两阶段框架为扩散模型逆问题求解中灵活控制失真-感知权衡提供了理论有效且实践高效的方案，潜空间扩展进一步提升了方法的通用性。

Abstract: The distortion-perception (D-P) tradeoff is a fundamental phenomenon of Bayesian inverse problems, which characterizes the inherent tension between distortion performance and perceptual quality. Enabling flexible traversal of the D-P tradeoff at inference time is crucial for practical applications. Despite the recent success of diffusion models in zero-shot inverse problem solving, efficient and principled strategies for D-P traversal in diffusion-based inverse algorithms remain inadequately characterized. In this paper, we propose a stage-wise framework for realizing D-P traversal using a single diffusion model in zero-shot inverse problems. Our proposed method, termed MAP-RPS, starts with an MAP estimation stage that approximates the MMSE solution and provides a low-distortion initialization, followed by a re-noised posterior sampling stage that progressively improves perceptual quality. We provide theoretical analyses for both stages, establishing the validity and effectiveness of the proposed design. Furthermore, we extend MAP-RPS to the latent space, yielding LMAP-RPS, which enjoys broader applicability by leveraging large-scale pre-trained latent diffusion backbones. Extensive experiments demonstrate that MAP-RPS and LMAP-RPS enable more effective D-P traversal on various tasks, while also exhibiting strong performance as efficient solvers for real-world inverse problems.

</details>


### [325] [BIRDNet: Mining and Encoding Boolean Implication Knowledge Graphs as Interpretable Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28739)
*Tirtharaj Dash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BIRDNet，一种通过稀疏例外二项检验挖掘布尔蕴含关系（BIRs）来构建稀疏、可解释神经网络的方法，在转录组和蛋白质组基准上，以微小精度代价（AUROC差距≤0.02）实现高达96倍参数压缩，且第一层规则可恢复已知生物学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识密集型领域的表格数据中常存在特征间的布尔蕴含关系（BIRs）作为潜在先验，现有方法大多依赖外部规则库或代理模型进行解释，缺乏直接从数据中挖掘结构先验并同时保持稀疏性与可解释性的神经符号模型。

Method: 1) 使用稀疏例外二项检验挖掘特征对间的布尔蕴含关系；2) 将挖掘出的蕴含关系构建为有向图（等价于2文字子句的命题规则库）；3) 将该图编码为分层神经网络的连接结构（BIRDNet），每个隐藏单元对应一条规则并仅连接两个特征；4) 通过稀疏性构造（每层最多2/d的权重活跃）和稳定的符号身份实现可解释性。

Result: 在六个转录组和蛋白质组基准上，BIRDNet与最强密集基线相比AUROC差距不超过0.02，精度损失微小，但活跃参数最多减少96倍。第一层规则可恢复多种癌症亚型和组织类型的已知生物学特征，包括典型扩增子、谱系定义共表达模块和免疫浸润标记。

Conclusion: BIRDNet通过从数据中挖掘结构先验而非依赖外部规则库，在保持极低精度损失的同时实现了高度稀疏（96倍参数压缩）和内在可解释性（无需代理模型即可直接读取规则），为知识密集型领域的神经符号学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Tabular data in knowledge-rich domains often carries a latent prior in the form of Boolean implication relationships (BIRs) between pairs of features. We mine such relationships with a sparse-exception binomial test. The mined implications form a typed directed graph, equivalent to a propositional rule base of 2-literal clauses. We encode this graph as the connectivity of a layered neural network, called BIRDNet, in which each hidden unit corresponds to one mined rule and binds only to its two features. We show two consequences of this design: First, the architecture is sparse by construction: at most $2/d$ of the weights in each BIR layer are active, where $d$ is the input dimension. Second, the model is interpretable: every trained unit keeps a stable symbolic identity, so rules can be read off the network without surrogate models. Unlike most neurosymbolic models, BIRDNet does not consume an external rule base; its structural prior is mined from the data. We evaluate BIRDNet on six transcriptomic and proteomic benchmarks. Our results show that BIRDNet stays within 0.02 AUROC of the strongest dense baseline, at a small accuracy cost, while using up to $96\times$ fewer active parameters than an architecture-matched dense MLP. First-layer rules recover known biological signatures across multiple cancer subtypes and tissue types, including canonical amplicons, lineage-defining co-expression modules, and immune-infiltration markers. Data and code are available at: https://github.com/MAHI-Group/BIRDNet.

</details>


### [326] [Extrapolative Weight Averaging Reveals Correctness-Efficiency Frontiers in Code RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28751)
*Kunhao Zheng,Pierre Chambon,Juliette Decugis,Jonas Gehring,Taco Cohen,Benjamin Negrevergne,Gabriel Synnaeve*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在代码强化学习中，通过嵌套单元测试覆盖度训练得到的检查点之间存在正确率-效率帕累托前沿，且外推权重平均可在不额外进行RL训练的情况下扩展该前沿，生成推理时可用的互补策略，在LCB/hard上将pass@250提升3.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性插值微调检查点可追踪竞争目标间的帕累托前沿，但外推式权重平均能否在不经额外RL训练的情况下将该前沿扩展到推理时可用的新检查点尚不清楚。

Method: 从共享初始化出发，在嵌套单元测试覆盖度下训练检查点（低覆盖度奖励对应小输入测试，高覆盖度奖励对应全量测试），通过低覆盖度与高覆盖度检查点之间的插值恢复前沿，并利用外推权重平均扩展该前沿。

Result: 外推权重平均可将前沿扩展到训练端点之外；该前沿及其外推延续在纯推理、工具使用和智能体编码三种推理设置以及32B和7B两种模型规模下均成立；外推检查点成为推理时可扩展的互补策略，集成后相比最佳单检查点在匹配采样预算下将LCB/hard上的pass@250提升3.3%。

Conclusion: 嵌套单元测试覆盖度在代码RL中诱导出一个前沿，外推权重平均能够导航、扩展并利用该前沿，从而在不增加RL训练成本的情况下提升推理性能。

Abstract: Linear interpolation between fine-tuned checkpoints has been shown to trace the Pareto front between competing objectives, but whether extrapolative weight averaging can extend such frontiers to new checkpoints useful at inference time, without additional RL training, remains unclear. We study this question in RL for competitive programming, where hidden unit tests under time and memory limits enforce both functional correctness and computational efficiency. Starting from a shared initialization, we train checkpoints under nested unit-test coverage: low-coverage rewards require passing smaller-input tests, while high-coverage rewards require passing progressively larger tests up to the full suite. This sweep reveals the emergence of a correctness-efficiency frontier: on hard problems, higher-coverage reward reduces optimization failures but increases correctness failures, leaving solve rate nearly unchanged. Interpolation between low- and high-coverage checkpoints recovers this frontier, while extrapolation extends it beyond the trained endpoints. Both the frontier and its extrapolative continuation appear across three inference settings, pure reasoning, tool use, and agentic coding, and across two model scales, 32B and 7B. At the problem level, moving along the frontier changes which problems are solved, making extrapolated checkpoints complementary policies in inference-time scaling. Ensembles with extrapolative weight averaging broaden coverage and improve pass@250 on LCB/hard by 3.3% over the best single checkpoint at matched sample budget. These results show that nested unit-test coverage in code RL induces a frontier that extrapolative weight averaging can navigate, extend, and exploit.

</details>


### [327] [LLM Zeroth-Order Fine-Tuning is an Inference Workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28760)
*Zelin Li,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM零阶微调本质上是推理主导的工作负载，通过将重复评分阶段迁移至推理运行时（vLLM）执行，在OPT-13B SST-2任务上实现8.13倍加速，并提出将零阶更新表示为动态适配器状态，为推理时训练开辟了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零阶微调实现在传统训练循环中执行ZO算法，但ZO算法的主要工作是重复评分（多次前向计算），这本质上是推理型负载，与训练循环的碎片化步骤存在工作负载-运行时错配，导致效率低下。

Method: 将ZO微调的重复评分阶段从训练循环中剥离，部署到推理运行时（vLLM）上执行；将ZO更新表示为动态适配器状态，使轻量级适配可以作为推理类工作负载调度。

Result: OPT-13B SST-2上LoZO微调：0.51估计训练小时 vs 基线4.15小时，8.13x加速；最终评估精度0.922，完整验证精度0.931。OPT-1.3B到OPT-13B核心步扩展实验：2.34x-7.72x加速。MeZO风格实验：追踪相似损失轨迹，加速最高2.55x。

Conclusion: ZO微调是推理主导的工作负载，通过运行时重组可获得显著加速；将ZO更新表示为动态适配器状态，为推理时训练提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Zeroth-order (ZO) fine-tuning is attractive for large language models because it replaces backpropagation with forward objective evaluations. Existing implementations nevertheless execute ZO algorithms inside conventional training loops, even though their dominant work is repeated scoring under nearby parameter states. This creates a workload-runtime mismatch: the algorithm asks for structured inference-style scoring, while the system exposes a sequence of fragmented training-loop steps. We show that LLM ZO fine-tuning is an inference-dominated workload and execute its repeated scoring phase through a serving runtime. On OPT-13B SST-2, the resulting vLLM execution path completes the 20k-step LoZO run in 0.51 estimated training hours versus 4.15 hours for the official LoZO baseline under the matched LoRA-only setting, an 8.13x speedup, while reaching 0.922 final evaluation accuracy and 0.931 final full-validation accuracy. In core-step scaling experiments across OPT-1.3B to OPT-13B, the same runtime reorganization gives 2.34x--7.72x speedups. A MeZO-style high-rank factorized experiment shows that the same runtime paradigm can track a MeZO-like loss trajectory while running up to 2.55x faster. More broadly, representing ZO updates as dynamic adapter states suggests a practical path toward inference-time training, where lightweight adaptation can be scheduled as an inference-like workload rather than as a separate training job.

</details>


### [328] [Multi-Mixer Models: Flexible Sequence Modeling with Shared Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28769)
*Kevin Y. Li,Asher Trockman,Ananda Theertha Suresh,Ziteng Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Oryx是一种混合架构模型，可以在序列维度上灵活切换注意力机制和线性递归机制，通过至少90%的参数共享，在保持高效生成的同时实现与Transformer相当的长上下文检索能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Softmax注意力虽然强大，但其内存随序列长度线性增长、计算量呈二次方增长。线性递归模型虽然计算和内存效率高，但在长上下文检索和上下文学习任务上表现不足。现有混合架构通常静态地交错或合并注意力和递归块，本文探索在序列维度上动态切换不同混合器的新方向。

Method: 提出Oryx混合模型，在序列处理过程中灵活切换不同混合器（如二次注意力和线性递归）。至少90%的参数在不同混合器之间共享，使得注意力和递归模式能在共享内部表示上运作。基于Mamba-2和Gated DeltaNet变体进行验证，最高训练至1.4B参数规模。

Result: 在固定token预算和混合训练策略下，Oryx在1.4B规模下所有实例均比基线模型在平均语言建模任务上提升至少0.7个百分点。在检索任务中，即使仅用不到10%的token运行注意力模式，Oryx也能达到与Transformer基线相当的性能。

Conclusion: 注意力和线性递归模型可以共享内部表示，序列轴上的混合（sequence-axis hybridization）是一个有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Softmax attention is the cornerstone of modern large language models, but its memory scales linearly and compute quadratically with sequence length. Linear recurrent models, such as linear attention and state space models, have become widely studied as alternatives to attention due to their linear compute and constant memory. While these sub-quadratic token mixing methods, or mixers, achieve promising efficiency gains and competitive results on a wide range of benchmarks, current linear recurrent models still lag behind on tasks that require long-context retrieval or in-context learning. A growing body of work studies hybrid architectures that attempt to mitigate these trade-offs by statically interleaving or merging attention and recurrent blocks. In this work, we explore a new axis of developing hybrid models: across the token sequence. We propose Oryx, a hybrid model that can, throughout a sequence, flexibly switch between different mixers, for example quadratic attention for rich context utilization and linear recurrences for efficient generation. Oryx ties at least 90% of its parameters across mixers, enabling attention and recurrent modes to operate over shared internal representations. We validate our design with Mamba-2 and Gated DeltaNet variants, up to 1.4B models. Under fixed token budgets and a mixed-training strategy, Oryx achieves comparable or better performance than its single-mixer baselines. At the 1.4B scale, all instances of Oryx outperform their respective baselines by at least 0.7 percentage points on averaged language modeling tasks. On retrieval tasks, Oryx achieves performance comparable to the Transformer baseline even when processing only a tiny fraction (<10%) of the tokens in attention mode. These results suggest that attention and linear recurrent models can share internal representations, and motivate sequence-axis hybridization as a promising direction.

</details>


### [329] [Learn from Weaknesses: Automated Domain Specialization for Small Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28775)
*Suji Kim,Kangsan Kim,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LearnWeak框架，利用更强的参考智能体识别小型计算机使用智能体的弱点，自动合成针对性训练数据，并引入区分规划与执行错误的误差感知训练目标，在OSWorld上平均提升11.6和11.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为每个软件领域部署大型专家智能体成本高昂，而小型开源智能体虽更适合专业化但性能较弱且存在领域特定失败。简单的大规模数据合成方法收效甚微，亟需更高效的专门化框架。

Method: 利用更强的参考智能体识别学生智能体的弱点，合成针对性任务并自动构建监督信号；引入误差感知训练目标，解耦规划错误与执行错误，实现行为级别的精确更新。

Result: 在OSWorld的8个领域上，相对于EvoCUA-8B和OpenCUA-7B分别平均提升11.6和11.1个百分点，且优于现有的自动轨迹生成与训练基线。

Conclusion: 证明了在数据合成和智能体训练中考虑学生自身弱点的重要性，为小型计算机使用智能体在不同领域的专门化提供了更原则化且高效的路径。

Abstract: Computer-use agents (CUAs) have recently made substantial progress, but deploying a separate large expert for each software domain remains expensive. Small open computer-use agents are more practical specialization targets, but they remain substantially weaker and exhibit uneven domain-specific failures. A straightforward remedy is to synthesize large-scale training data for the target domain, yet we find that this naive approach yields only marginal improvements. Building on this observation, we introduce LearnWeak, an annotation-free specialization framework for small computer-use agents that uses a stronger reference agent to identify the student's weaknesses in the target domain, synthesize targeted tasks, and construct supervision automatically. LearnWeak further introduces an error-aware specialization objective that disentangles planning and execution errors, enabling more behaviorally precise updates than broad uniform supervision. On OSWorld, LearnWeak achieves average gains of 11.6 and 11.1 percentage points over EvoCUA-8B and OpenCUA-7B, respectively, across eight domains. We also validate that our student-aware dataset generation and training approaches outperform existing autonomous trajectory generation and training baselines. Our work highlights the importance of student awareness in both data synthesis and agent training, pointing toward a more principled and efficient path for specializing small computer-use agents in diverse domains.

</details>


### [330] [Affective Music Recommendation: A Rollout-Based World Model for Offline Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28810)
*Audrey Chan,Aaron Labbé,Jacob Lavoie,Jordan Bannister,Arsène Fansi Tchango,Guillaume Lajoie,Laurent Charlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AMRS（情感音乐推荐系统），部署在LUCID健康平台，服务于临床用户（主要为神经认知障碍老年患者）和普通消费者。系统基于滚动世界模型（因果Transformer）学习用户行为与情感状态，使用行为克隆初始化策略，再通过直接偏好优化（DPO）进行离线微调，在冷启动条件下实现了对情感信号的有效预测，避免了贪婪优化导致的分布坍塌。该工作为在无法进行在线情感实验场景下的情感推荐提供了早期部署验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能性音乐应用（从消费品到临床干预）面临独特的推荐问题：成功与否取决于听者的情感状态，但在情感维度进行在线实验面临伦理约束，尤其是临床用户无法可靠地跳过歌曲或报告不适。因此需要一种无法在线实验的离线情感推荐方法。

Method: AMRS基于滚动世界模型（rollout-based world model），使用因果Transformer在日志收听数据上联合预测用户参与度、二元评分以及自我报告的效价（valence）和唤醒度（arousal）。世界模型既作为离线策略训练的仿真器，也作为部署前的压力测试工具。推荐策略通过行为克隆初始化，再使用直接偏好优化（DPO）根据可配置的多目标效用函数进行离线微调。

Result: 在严格的冷启动协议下，世界模型能够以可用的精度预测行为信号和情感信号；DPO在保持与基线相似的多样性特征、避免贪婪优化导致的分布坍塌的同时，改善了预测的效价和唤醒度。

Conclusion: 该工作为在线实验伦理上不可行的情感推荐场景提供了一种方法论验证，并已在真实平台上部署。

Abstract: Functional music applications, from consumer focus and sleep aids to clinical interventions, share a distinctive recommendation problem: success is defined by the listener's affective state, but online experimentation on emotion is ethically constrained, particularly for clinical populations who cannot reliably skip a song or report distress. We describe AMRS, the Affective Music Recommendation System deployed on LUCID's health-and-wellness platforms, which serve clinical users (primarily older adults with neurocognitive conditions) and consumer-wellness users across energize, focus, calm, and sleep modes. AMRS is built around a rollout-based world model: a causal transformer trained on logged listening data to jointly predict engagement, binary rating, and self-reported valence and arousal. The world model serves both as an in-silico simulator for offline policy training and as a stress-testing tool before deployment. A recommender policy initialized by behaviour cloning is fine-tuned offline with Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) against a configurable multi-objective utility function. Under a strict cold-start protocol, the world model predicts both behavioural and affective signals with usable fidelity; DPO improves predicted valence and arousal over the cloned baseline while maintaining a similar diversity profile and avoiding the distributional collapse produced by greedy optimization. We position the work as an early deployed validation of a methodology for affective recommendation when online experimentation is ethically untenable.

</details>


### [331] [PEFT-Arena: Understanding Parameter-Efficient Finetuning from a Stability-Plasticity Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.28819)
*Yangyi Huang,Ruotian Peng,Zeju Qiu,Jiale Kang,Yandong Wen,Bernhard Schölkopf,Weiyang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PEFT-Arena基准，从稳定性-可塑性困境出发评估参数高效微调方法，发现正交微调在帕累托前沿上表现最优，并通过权重空间和激活空间的几何分析解释差异，最后提出路径回退的后处理方法改进性能-保持权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT评估过度关注下游任务准确率，忽视了预训练能力的保持；需要从稳定性-可塑性困境的角度系统评估PEFT方法。

Method: 构建PEFT-Arena基准联合评估下游性能和通用能力保持；从权重空间（谱分析）和激活空间（表示保真度）两个几何视角分析PEFT更新；提出路径回退（path-wise rewinding）作为后处理改进方法。

Result: 不同PEFT方法呈现不同的稳定性-可塑性特征；在可比参数预算下，正交微调达到最优帕累托前沿；遗忘与非等距表示扭曲相关；最终SFT检查点往往越过更好的目标-保持权衡点。

Conclusion: PEFT评估应同时考虑下游适应与遗忘抵抗；路径回退可以有效改善性能与保持之间的权衡。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient finetuning (PEFT) has become the standard approach for adapting large language models, yet evaluations largely emphasize downstream accuracy while overlooking the retention of pretrained capabilities. We argue that PEFT should be assessed through the stability-plasticity dilemma: the trade-off between target-task adaptation and resistance to forgetting. We introduce PEFT-Arena, a benchmark that jointly measures downstream performance and general capability retention. Across methods, we find distinct stability-plasticity profiles; under comparable parameter budgets, orthogonal finetuning achieves the most favorable Pareto frontier. To explain these differences, we analyze PEFT updates from two geometric perspectives. In weight space, spectral analysis reveals how parameterizations interact with the pretrained singular-value structure. In activation space, retention metrics show whether finetuning preserves or distorts general-capability representations, with forgetting linked to non-isometric representation distortion. Finally, an analysis shows that final SFT checkpoints often overshoot a better target-retention operating point. Inspired by this, we present case studies of a post-hoc improvement with path-wise rewinding.

</details>


### [332] [LNN-PINN: A Unified Physics-Only Training Framework with Liquid Residual Blocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2508.08935)
*Ze Tao,Hanxuan Wang,Fujun Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LNN-PINN框架，通过在隐藏层引入液态残差门控架构，在不改变原有物理建模和优化流程的情况下提升PINN的预测精度，在四个基准问题上均取得更低的RMSE和MAE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PINN在复杂问题上预测精度有限，需要一种既能保持原有物理建模和优化流程、又能提升精度的架构改进方法。

Method: 在PINN的隐藏层映射中引入轻量级液态残差门控机制（Liquid Residual Gating），保持采样策略、损失组成和超参数设置不变，仅通过架构改进提升性能。

Result: 在四个基准问题上，LNN-PINN在相同训练条件下均降低了RMSE和MAE，绝对误差图进一步确认了精度提升；框架在不同维度、边界条件和算子特性下表现出强适应性和稳定性。

Conclusion: LNN-PINN提供了一种简洁有效的架构增强方案，能够显著提升物理信息神经网络在复杂科学与工程问题中的预测精度。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have attracted considerable attention for their ability to integrate partial differential equation priors into deep learning frameworks; however, they often exhibit limited predictive accuracy when applied to complex problems. To address this issue, we propose LNN-PINN, a physics-informed neural network framework that incorporates a liquid residual gating architecture while preserving the original physics modeling and optimization pipeline to improve predictive accuracy. The method introduces a lightweight gating mechanism solely within the hidden-layer mapping, keeping the sampling strategy, loss composition, and hyperparameter settings unchanged to ensure that improvements arise purely from architectural refinement. Across four benchmark problems, LNN-PINN consistently reduced RMSE and MAE under identical training conditions, with absolute error plots further confirming its accuracy gains. Moreover, the framework demonstrates strong adaptability and stability across varying dimensions, boundary conditions, and operator characteristics. In summary, LNN-PINN offers a concise and effective architectural enhancement for improving the predictive accuracy of physics-informed neural networks in complex scientific and engineering problems.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [333] [Calibrated Inference for the Conditional Average Treatment Effect in the Few-Placebo Regime via Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27473)
*Eichi Uehara*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究少对照组场景下的条件平均处理效应（CATE）估计问题。标准X-Learner方法结合贝叶斯第二阶段产生的置信区间覆盖不足，原因是回归目标继承了小样本臂的 nuisance 模型偏差，而标准双重稳健正交得分方法在该场景下也不可靠。作者提出 GP-CATE，用高斯过程分别建模各臂的结果曲面，使稀缺臂的不确定性直接进入后验而非作为未建模偏差，从而在校准覆盖方面优于Causal Forest和BART等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在药物试验、政策评估等场景中，常出现一个处理臂样本量远小于另一臂的情况（如不等分配试验或小留出A/B测试）。现有CATE估计方法在这种"少对照组"机制下无法提供校准的不确定性区间，导致决策依据不可靠。

Method: 作者首先理论分析指出X-Learner的贝叶斯扩展后验中心偏离真实效应，因其回归目标继承了小样本臂的偏差；双重稳健正交得分法因重叠度有限而要么方差过大要么有偏。为此提出GP-CATE：用高斯过程分别对每个臂的结果曲面建模，使稀缺臂的不确定性经由GP后验直接传播到CATE的后验分布中，而非通过一个单独估计的方差附加到有偏点估计上。

Result: 在合成和半合成基准上，GP-CATE实现了校准的覆盖率，而Causal Forest和BART等对比方法未能做到；代价是当数据信息不足时区间会适当变宽。

Conclusion: 少对照组场景下的CATE不确定性量化需要直接建模稀缺臂的不确定性，而非依赖点估计加独立方差的两阶段范式；GP-CATE提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Estimating how much an intervention helps a given individual the conditional average treatment effect (CATE) is increasingly central to decision-making in medicine, economics, and policy, where an estimate is most useful when accompanied by a calibrated uncertainty interval. We study the few-placebo regime, in which one treatment arm is much smaller than the other, as arises in unequal-allocation trials and small-holdout $A/B$ tests. The standard estimator in this setting is the X-Learner, and a natural way to obtain credible intervals is to make its second stage Bayesian. We show that these intervals under-cover: they contain the true effect less often than their nominal level. We trace this to a structural cause the X-Learner's regression target inherits the bias of a nuisance model fitted to the small arm, so the posterior is centered away from the true effect and we find that the standard remedy, regressing an orthogonal doubly-robust score, is also unreliable here, since the regime's limited overlap leaves the estimator either highly variable or, once stabilized, biased once more. Both consequences reflect a pattern that extends beyond causal inference: a separately estimated variance is attached to a point estimate of a hard-to-learn quantity, and the point estimate's bias is not captured by that variance. We propose GP-CATE, which models each arm's outcome surface with a Gaussian process, so the scarce arm's uncertainty enters the posterior directly rather than as an unmodelled bias. Across synthetic and semi-synthetic benchmarks, GP-CATE attains calibrated coverage where the estimators we compare against including Causal Forest and BART do not, at the cost of intervals that are appropriately wide when the data are uninformative.

</details>


### [334] [Stop Suppressing the Tail: Causal Inference for Extreme Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27474)
*Eichi Uehara*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的平均剂量-响应函数（ADRF）估计器，能够在连续处理下同时输出标准点估计和结构化尾部形状信息，通过基于中位数去中心化的尾部诊断（PDHTE+JK）打破循环依赖问题，在重尾数据上显著优于传统方法，并具备显式外推拒绝机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双机器学习（DML）方法为稳定均值而刻意压制极端值，但在金融收益、气候损失等高利害场景下，尾部极端事件恰是核心关注目标。同时，依赖模型残差读取尾部的方法存在循环依赖问题，使尾部形状推断因核心估计器切换（如Huber到Welsch）而剧烈变动。

Method: 提出ADRF估计器，包含尾部诊断PDHTE+JK：利用由先导中位数去中心化后的结果，评估每处理条件下的尾部形状，打破循环依赖，使诊断结果不依赖于核心方法的选择。输出包括尾部形状、深尾回报水平、条件短fall、恢复的均值ADRF，以及在极值模型不被数据支持时显式拒绝外推的机制。

Result: 与核加权分位数回归相比，在重尾面板数据上，深尾（α=0.001）回报水平MAE降低11%，条件短fall MAE降低25.5%；在样本稀缺（n≤2000）时MAE降低20-29%。在freMTPL2车险理赔数据上，成功触发对数理赔尺度上的显式外推拒绝，这是QR和仅损失DML无法做到的。

Conclusion: 该估计器在重尾数据场景下显著优于现有方法，不仅提供更准确的尾部推断，还具备数据驱动的外推自拒绝能力，为高利害因果推断提供了更可靠的工具。

Abstract: Estimating how an outcome responds to a continuous treatment (the Average Dose-Response Function, or ADRF) is a core causal-inference primitive. However, when outcomes possess heavy tails, standard robust double machine learning (DML) deliberately suppresses these extremes to stabilize the bulk average. In high-stakes settings, such as financial returns or climate losses, this omitted 1-in-1000 extreme event is the actual target quantity. Furthermore, current methods that read the tail from a model's residuals suffer from circular dependence, causing tail shape inferences to shift drastically based solely on whether the core estimator is switched between Huber and Welsch.The research proposes an ADRF estimator that emits a structured tail-shape output alongside the standard point estimate. Its tail diagnostic (PDHTE+JK) evaluates the per-treatment tail shape from the outcome centered by a pilot median, successfully breaking the circular dependence and rendering the diagnostic invariant to the choice of core method. The output encompasses four treatment-conditional quantities: tail shape $\hatξ(t)$, deep-tail return levels $\hat{Q}_α(t)$, conditional shortfalls $\hat{S}_α(t)$, the recovered mean ADRF, and an explicit refusal mechanism that declines extrapolation when extreme-value modeling is unsupported by the data. Compared to kernel-weighted quantile regression (QR), the proposed estimator reduces deep-tail ($α=0.001$) return-level MAE by 11% and conditional-shortfall MAE by 25.5% across a heavy-tailed panel. It also achieves a 20-29% MAE reduction in sample-scarce regimes ($n\le2000$). On freMTPL2 motor-insurance claims, it successfully triggered an explicit extrapolation refusal on the log-claim scale, which neither QR nor loss-only DML can produce.

</details>


### [335] [Iterative Causal Discovery: Per-Edge Impossibility Certificates, Tier-Aware Oracle Queries, and the $1+K$ Lower Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.27477)
*Eichi Uehara*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出RESOLVED协议，在连续数据的因果发现中为每条候选边附加不可行性证书，区分数据驱动的边方向与基于假设的方向，并通过五个可识别性层级（LSNM、IGCI、Stein、MDL、PEIT）和两种预言基元（元中心查询、子节点查询）在最多1+K次专家交互下恢复任意DAG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
